### Severity: high（高）— 22 則

#### iPhone app aborts (fatal trap) inside AIPEnvelope.validate() on a state envelope whose payload.revision is out of Int range

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-aip-protocol-034`
- **維度**：`aip-protocol`
- **Severity**：`high`
- **file:line**：`apps/interaction-ios/InteractionCompanion/Models/Protocol.swift:78`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`JSONValue.intValue` performs an unchecked `Int(Double)` conversion, and `AIPEnvelope.validate()` calls it on attacker-supplied `payload.revision` for every inbound `state` frame. A JSON number larger than Int.max (e.g. `1e30`, `9223372036854775808`) makes Swift trap (`Fatal error: Double value cannot be converted to Int…`) and the app process dies — inside the very function whose contract is "reject, never execute, never crash" (AIP §4.1/§11; AIPGenerated.swift header: 「iPhone 端不得因為桌面版本較新而崩潰或誤判成功」). The trap happens before the source-kind check at SessionClient.swift:486, so no other gate can prevent it.

**Evidence**

Protocol.swift:78-81 `var intValue: Int? { if case .number(let value) = self, value.rounded() == value { return Int(value) } ... }` — no range guard (contrast CharacterSemantic.swift:78-84 `uintValue`, which does guard `value <= Double(UInt64.max)`). AIPEnvelope.swift:349 `let revision = body["revision"]?.intValue` in `case .state`. SessionClient.swift:482 `if let failure = envelope.validate() { … }` is the first statement of `handleFrame`, i.e. every inbound `{"type":"aip",...}` frame. Verified by running the verbatim logic under Swift 6.3.3: input `{"revision": 1e30}` → parsed double 1e+30 → `Fatal error: Double value cannot be converted to Int because the result would be greater than Int.max`, process exit 133. Same unguarded helper is reachable from the v1 actuator path (`params.int("durationMs")`, ActuatorCenter.swift:337,377,128).

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `high`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Every factual claim in the finding was independently verified against the current source and reproduced live:

  1. Protocol.swift:78-81 `intValue` is exactly as quoted — `if case .number(let value) = self, value.rounded() == value { return Int(value) }` with no range guard.
  2. Contrast confirmed: CharacterSemantic.swift's `uintValue` (lines 78-84) does guard with `value <= Double(UInt64.max)` before converting — proving the codebase knows this pattern is needed and simply omitted it for `intValue`.
  3. AIPEnvelope.swift:349 `let revision = body["revision"]?.intValue` is inside `case .state:` of `validate()` (confirmed at the actual current line 349, matches).
  4. SessionClient.swift:482 `if let failure = envelope.validate() { … }` is literally the first statement of `handleFrame`, and the source-kind check (`envelope.source.kind == .runtime || .session`) is at line 486 — strictly after validate(), so it cannot prevent the crash.
  5. Traced the full reachability path: ConnectionManager.swift:637 `case .aip(let envelope): withSession { $0.handleFrame(envelope, rawFrame: text) }`, fed by `ServerMessage.decode()` (Protocol.swift, `case "aip"`) which only checks a byte-size cap before `JSONDecoder().decode(AipBody.self, …)` — no numeric-range validation at decode time, since `JSONValue.number(Double)` happily stores 1e30.
  6. Reproduced the exact crash with Swift 6.3.3 (matches the finding's claimed compiler version exactly, verified via `xcrun swift --version`): compiling and running the verbatim `intValue` logic with input `1e30` produced `Fatal error: Double value cannot be converted to Int because the result would be greater than Int.max` and process exit 133 (SIGTRAP). A control run with a normal value (42.0) completed normally (exit 0), ruling out an unrelated toolchain problem.
  7. Swift's `Int(Double)` precondition trap is a hard fatal trap, not a thrown Swift error — no `do/catch` anywhere in the call chain (including in `validate()`, `handleFrame`, or the WebSocket receive loop) can intercept it, confirming "no other gate can prevent it."
  8. Secondary reachability via the v1 (non-AIP) `act` path also confirmed: ActuatorCenter.swift uses `params.int("durationMs")` (Protocol.swift:91's `Dictionary.int(_:)`, which calls the same unguarded `intValue`) at lines 335, 377, and 128 area — same defect, different entry point.
  9. The quoted AIPGenerated.swift header text ("iPhone 端不得因為桌面版本較新而崩潰或誤判成功") exists verbatim at line 6.

  This is a genuine, currently-present defect: any inbound `{"type":"aip","envelope":{...,"messageType":"state",...,"payload":{"revision": 1e30, ...}}}` frame — regardless of source, since validate() runs before the source-kind trust check — crashes the whole iPhone app process via an unrecoverable Swift fatal trap, directly violating the "reject, never crash" contract stated in both AIP §4.1/§11 and the file's own header comments. No test, guard, or catch anywhere in the reachable path prevents it.

  </details>

- **規格／誠實階梯（`spec-honesty`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `high`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  親自重現：把 Protocol.swift 的 `intValue` 邏輯原樣抽出跑在 Swift 6.2.3 直譯器上，輸入 1e30 → `value.rounded() == value` 為 true（大數本就無小數部分）→ `Int(value)` 觸發 `Fatal error: Double value cannot be converted to Int because the result would be greater than Int.max`，process exit 133（SIGTRAP）。

  可達性逐段核對，均與 finding 引用行號完全吻合：
  - Protocol.swift:78-81 `intValue` 確實沒有範圍守衛，對照 CharacterSemantic.swift:78-84 的 `uintValue` 有 `value <= Double(UInt64.max)` 守衛，證明這是不一致而非刻意設計。
  - AIPEnvelope.swift:349 `case .state:` 分支對 `body["revision"]?.intValue` 求值，前面只做 sessionId/sequence 是否存在的檢查，沒有任何會先擋掉巨大數字的邏輯；`checkPayload`/`walk`（AIPEnvelope.swift 403-425）只查字串長度與巢狀深度，不查數值範圍。
  - SessionClient.swift:481-482 `handleFrame` 第一行就是 `envelope.validate()`，在 source.kind 白名單檢查（489 行）之前。
  - 呼叫鏈確認：ConnectionManager.swift:637-640 `.aip(let envelope)` 收到已解碼的 `AIPEnvelope`（Protocol.swift ServerMessage.decode 的 "aip" 分支只做結構解碼，明確註解「這裡只做結構解碼；profile／上限／版本的驗證在 SessionClient 用 AIPEnvelope.validate() 做（與 Rust 權威實作同一組規則）」）→ `withSession { $0.handleFrame(envelope, rawFrame: text) }`。JSONDecoder 對 1e30 這種合法 JSON number 解碼成 Double 完全不會出錯，crash 只發生在 validate() 內部。
  - 與 Rust 權威實作對照，坐實「非刻意」：crates/interaction-aip/src/envelope.rs:216-220 用 `self.payload.get("revision").and_then(Value::as_u64)`——`serde_json::Value::as_u64` 是 checked conversion，溢位回 `None`，會走正常的 `need(...)` 拒絕路徑（schemaInvalid），絕不 panic。Swift 端的行為與其自身註解宣稱的「同一組規則」明顯不符。
  - 構造一個能通過前面所有 gate（specVersion/messageId/messageType/name grammar/source.kind/sessionId/sequence）又在 revision 觸雷的合法信封並不困難（如 name="character.state"、source.kind="runtime"、sessionId="s1"、sequence=1、payload={"revision":1e30}），不存在會提前擋下這個輸入的隱藏檢查。

  沒有找到反駁點：這不是風格偏好、沒有被其他測試/範圍檢查覆蓋、也沒有跡象顯示已被修復（審查當下檔案內容與 finding 引用行號逐字相符）。屬於協定解析邊界（本該「拒絕、絕不執行、絕不崩潰」的函式）內未受控的整數轉換導致的真實可重現當機／DoS，嚴重度定為 high 合理（會讓 app process 直接被殺，且是每則 inbound state frame 的必經路徑，但不涉及授權繞過或動器誤執行，故不到 blocker）。

  </details>

**Fix**

Make `JSONValue.intValue` total: `guard value >= Double(Int.min), value <= Double(Int.max) else { return nil }` before converting (mirror `SemanticJSON.uintValue`), and prefer a `uint64Value` for `revision`/`sequence`. Add a fixture (state with `revision: 1e30`) to manifest.json expecting `schema-invalid` so all three languages pin it.

**Regression test**

Feed `AIPEnvelope` (messageType `.state`, valid sessionId/sequence, `payload: {"revision": 1e30, "kind": "full", ...}`) into `.validate()` directly (or through `SessionClient.handleFrame`) and assert it returns an `AIPFailure` (e.g. `.schemaInvalid`) instead of trapping; before a fix this test would crash the whole XCTest process rather than fail cleanly, which is itself diagnostic. The fix is to add the same `value <= Double(Int.max) && value >= Double(Int.min)` guard to `JSONValue.intValue` that `uintValue` already has for `UInt64.max`. / 對 `AIPEnvelope.validate()` 送入 messageType=state、其餘欄位合法、`payload.revision` = 1e30（或 9223372036854775808）的信封，斷言回傳 `AIPFailure(.schemaInvalid, ...)` 而非讓進程崩潰；同時可加一則 `intValue` 的單元測試比照 `uintValue` 驗證超出 Int 範圍回傳 nil。

#### member 重送 capability 會繞過 lastSeenAt 投影格線，讓已配對裝置把 revision／廣播打成無界成長

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-identity-binding-004`
- **維度**：`identity-binding`
- **Severity**：`high`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:334`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`CharacterSession::join()` 對「已存在成員」的分支直接把 `entry.projected_seen_at = now`（session.rs:334），完全跳過 `project_members()` 的 `presenceTimeout/3`（預設 15 s）投影格線。因為 `capability` 是成員合法可送的 message type（gate 第 9 關），一台已配對的 iPhone 只要重複送 `capability` frame，每一則都會讓 `SemanticState.members[].lastSeenAt` 改變 → `commit_and_patch` 產生非空 patch → revision +1 ＋ 對所有成員廣播 ＋ 一則 SSE `character.session.state`。這正是 `docs/aip/character-session.md` §12.7 明文要防的情形：「否則一個高頻送訊息的成員就能把 revision 與廣播打成無界成長」。

**Evidence**

session.rs:328-336 既有成員分支：`entry.member.last_seen_at = now; entry.member.negotiated = negotiated.clone(); entry.projected_seen_at = now;`——`projected_seen_at` 被直接寫成 now，而不是走 session.rs:1145-1164 `project_members()` 的 `grid = presence_timeout_ms/3` 條件式更新。對比 `note_alive()`（session.rs:1168-1180）只改 `member.last_seen_at`，把投影交給 `project_members()` 的格線，所以只送 event 的裝置不會每則都製造 revision（測試 `events_alone_keep_a_member_online_across_the_presence_timeout`，tests/session.rs:906）。`join` 之後 session.rs:349-355 呼叫 `commit_and_patch(now)` 並推 `Output::Broadcast`。`Timestamp` 是 `DateTime<Utc>`（RFC3339 含次秒），所以連續兩則 capability 的 `now` 幾乎必定不同 → `merge_diff` 必定非空。連帶後果：event log 是 512 筆有界環（limits.rs:EVENT_LOG_RING），30 rev/s 約 17 秒就把整個 delta log 洗掉，所有成員的 resume 只能退回 snapshot；`persist_if_due`（預設每 32 revision）也變成約每秒一次全量快照落地。現有測試沒有任何一條覆蓋「成員重複送 capability」（`rg 'capability' crates/interaction-session/tests` 只有協商內容與 spoofing 測試）。`cargo test -p interaction-session` 目前 43 passed / 0 failed，所以這是現行行為。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `high`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Read `CharacterSession::join()` in crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs. The existing-member branch (lines 328-336) does:
  ```
  entry.member.last_seen_at = now;
  entry.member.negotiated = negotiated.clone();
  entry.projected_seen_at = now;
  entry.bucket = RateBucket::new(self.config.rate_limit_per_sec, now);
  ```
  `entry.projected_seen_at = now` at line 334 is an unconditional write, unlike `project_members()` (line 1145) which only advances `projected_seen_at` when `last_seen_at - projected_seen_at >= presence_timeout_ms/3` (the documented grid, docs/aip/character-session.md §12.7), and unlike `note_alive()` (line 1169) which defers entirely to that grid except on a presence transition. `state.members[].lastSeenAt` is populated from `projected_seen_at` (project_members' `MemberView` construction), so every `join()` on an already-joined member changes visible state even if nothing else about the member changed.

  `capability` (MessageType::Capability) is a message type members are allowed to send at any time per gate() step 9 (line ~768), is routed through `handle_capability` → `join()` (line 1054), and is not gated by the presence grid at all — only by the generic rate limiter (step 10) and dedupe-by-message_id (step 12, which only blocks byte-identical message_id replays, not distinct capability re-announcements).

  I wrote and ran a standalone repro (outside the repo, using the crate's real public API) that: joins a device, then resends the *identical* CapabilityAnnouncement 5 times at 200ms intervals — well inside the default 15,000ms grid (presence_timeout_ms=45000/3) and well under the 30/sec rate limit. Actual output:
  ```
  after initial join: revision=2 broadcasts=1 lastSeenAt=...T12:30:00Z
  resend #1 at +200ms: revision=3 broadcasts_this_call=1 lastSeenAt=...T12:30:00.200Z
  resend #2 at +400ms: revision=4 broadcasts_this_call=1 lastSeenAt=...T12:30:00.400Z
  resend #3 at +600ms: revision=5 broadcasts_this_call=1 lastSeenAt=...T12:30:00.600Z
  resend #4 at +800ms: revision=6 broadcasts_this_call=1 lastSeenAt=...T12:30:00.800Z
  resend #5 at +1000ms: revision=7 broadcasts_this_call=1 lastSeenAt=...T12:30:01Z
  ```
  Every resend produced its own revision bump and its own `Output::Broadcast`, with `lastSeenAt` tracking `now` exactly — exactly the failure mode docs/aip/character-session.md §12.7 says the grid exists to prevent ("否則一個高頻送訊息的成員就能把 revision 與廣播打成無界成長"). Confirmed no existing test in crates/interaction-session/tests/session.rs covers repeated-capability-resend revision growth (`rejoin_renegotiates_instead_of_duplicating_the_member` at line 202 sends a *different* announcement the second time, so it can't distinguish a grid bypass from a legitimate capability-change diff; `restored_members_must_renegotiate_before_sending_events` at line 1247 sends exactly one capability after restore).

  All file:line citations in the finding check out against current content: session.rs:334 (`entry.projected_seen_at = now;`), :315 (`fn join`), project_members at :1145 (finding cited 1145-1164, matches), note_alive at :1169 (finding cited 1168-1180, off-by-one, immaterial), tests/session.rs:906 exists as cited. Config defaults confirm the finding's numbers: presence_timeout_ms=45_000 (types.rs:76) giving a 15s grid exactly as the finding states, rate_limit_per_sec=30 (types.rs:77) matching the "30 rev/s" figure, EVENT_LOG_RING=512 (interaction-aip/src/limits.rs:18) making "~17s to wash out the delta log" arithmetically correct (512/30≈17.07s), and persist_every_revisions=32 (types.rs:78) making "~once per second" persistence correct (32/30≈1.07s) at that rate.

  As a bonus discovery strengthening (not contradicting) the finding: `entry.bucket = RateBucket::new(...)` on the same existing-member branch resets the token bucket to full capacity on every successful join/re-capability, so the nominal 30/sec rate limit doesn't even cap the resend rate for this specific message type the way the finding conservatively assumed — actual achievable revision growth is bounded only by transport throughput, not by rate_limit_per_sec. I did not fold this into confirmed/severity since it wasn't part of the original claim, but it corroborates rather than undermines it.

  No file was modified in the repo; the reproduction was built as a separate throwaway crate in scratchpad against the crate's real public API.

  </details>

- **規格／誠實階梯（`spec-honesty`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `high`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Static reading and a dynamic repro both confirm the claim.

  Static: `CharacterSession::join()`'s existing-member branch (session.rs:328-335) writes `entry.projected_seen_at = now;` unconditionally at line 334 (exact line, no drift), instead of going through `project_members()`'s grid gate (`grid = presence_timeout_ms/3`, session.rs:1145-1164). `join()` is reached from an already-negotiated member's `capability` resend via `handle_capability` → `self.join(...)` (session.rs:1037-1046), and `capability` is a legal member message type at gate step 9 (session.rs:762-771). After the match block, `join()` calls `self.project_members()` (session.rs:344) and then `commit_and_patch(now)` (session.rs:349) and pushes `Output::Broadcast` — so any lastSeenAt delta becomes a revision bump broadcast to the whole session.

  Spec: docs/aip/character-session.md lines 195-197 explicitly require the grid for "任何一則存活證明", stating the reason is exactly to prevent "一個高頻送訊息的成員就能把 revision 與廣播打成無界成長" — and the repo already encodes this intent correctly for heartbeats: `note_alive()` (session.rs:1168-1180) only updates `member.last_seen_at` and defers to `project_members()`'s grid, verified by the existing passing test `heartbeats_do_not_flood_revisions` (crates/interaction-session/tests/session.rs:875), which asserts revision growth ≤2 over 20 heartbeats spanning 2000ms. `join()`'s existing-member path is the one code path that bypasses this.

  Dynamic repro (standalone harness at /private/tmp/.../scratchpad/verify-capflood, using the crate as a path dependency, no repo files modified): with default config (presence_timeout_ms=45000, grid=15000ms), 10 `capability` resends from an already-joined device 200ms apart (well inside the 15s grid) produced a revision bump on every single call (revision 2→12, 10/10 bumps). The same cadence run with heartbeats (the grid-respecting path) over the same session produced 0 revision growth, matching the intent of the repo's own `heartbeats_do_not_flood_revisions` test. This is a clean, confound-free A/B: the only difference between the two paths is which code updates `projected_seen_at`, and only the `join()` path bypasses the grid.

  I additionally found the bug is somewhat worse than described: `join()`'s existing-member branch also resets the per-member `RateBucket` to full capacity on every call (`entry.bucket = RateBucket::new(self.config.rate_limit_per_sec, now)`, session.rs:335), which happens *after* gate step 10 already consumed a token — so a member that keeps sending accepted `capability` frames never actually gets rate-limited by the token bucket either; the growth is bounded only by transport throughput, not by `rate_limit_per_sec` as the original finding assumed. This strengthens rather than weakens the finding.

  cargo test -p interaction-session: 43 passed, 0 failed (matches the finding's stated baseline); no test in the suite exercises a repeated/re-negotiated `capability` resend and asserts revision growth is bounded (only `capability_degradation_...`, spoofing-style, and the restore-then-renegotiate test exist, none check revision growth under repeated resend).

  This is a genuine violation of an explicitly documented invariant (§12.7 in docs/aip/character-session.md), trivially triggerable by any already-paired, legitimately-identified member (no identity spoofing needed — the CLAUDE.md "identity-binding" dimension label is a bit loose since identity binding itself isn't broken, but the *consequence* — unbounded revision/broadcast growth from a bound member — is exactly what the spec's presence-projection rule is written to stop), with real fan-out impact: SSE broadcast storm to every other connected member, `persist_if_due`-driven snapshot write amplification, and premature eviction of the bounded 512-entry event-log ring forcing other members into snapshot-fallback resume more often than intended.

  </details>

**Fix**

`join()` 的既有成員分支不要直接寫 `projected_seen_at`：改成只更新 `member.last_seen_at`（外加 presence 變化即時反映），投影一律交給 `project_members()` 的格線；或在 join 內呼叫 `note_alive(index, now)` 取代那兩行。並補一條回歸測試：同一成員在 15 s 內連送 N 則 capability，revision 只能增加 O(1) 次。

**Regression test**

Join a member once, then call `session.join()` again with the *same* CapabilityAnnouncement at now+200ms (inside presence_timeout_ms/3): assert `session.diagnostics().revision` does NOT increment and no `Output::Broadcast` is produced, mirroring how `events_alone_keep_a_member_online_across_the_presence_timeout` (tests/session.rs:906) asserts grid-respecting behavior for events/heartbeats. / Extend the existing `heartbeats_do_not_flood_revisions`-style test to capability: join a device, then resend the identical `CapabilityAnnouncement` (fresh message_id each time) 10-20 times a few hundred ms apart (well inside `presence_timeout_ms/3`), and assert `session.revision() - start <= 2` (mirroring the heartbeat test's own bound) instead of the observed 1-bump-per-resend.

#### iPhone 端把 host 的 session-reset 快照當成 rollback 丟掉，然後宣稱「已同步」（換桌面／換 epoch 後永久失聯）

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-pairing-migration-001`
- **維度**：`pairing-migration`
- **Severity**：`high`
- **file:line**：`apps/interaction-ios/InteractionCompanion/Services/SessionClient.swift:298`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

SessionDecisions.apply 只在 `epoch > local.epoch` 時才承認 host 的 `session-reset`。當同一支 iPhone 被重新配對到「另一台桌面」（endpoint migration）時，新 host 的 sessionEpoch 可能小於或等於手機記得的值，於是那份權威 snapshot 被判成 `.ignoredRollback`；`consume` 對 ignoredRollback 回傳 true（視為「還在同步軌道上」），既不重送 resume、也不累計 resumeFailures，因此之後每一則 state/patch 都會被同樣理由丟棄、永遠不會恢復。更嚴重的是 `syncStatus` 在這個狀態下回 `.synced`——手機畫面顯示「角色狀態已同步」，實際上一則權威狀態都沒套用。這同時違反 docs/aip/character-session.md §7 第 4 步（「epoch **不同** → 丟棄本地狀態、套用 snapshot」，不是「更大」）與誠實階梯（received ≠ applied，卻對外宣稱 synced）。

**Evidence**

SessionClient.swift:296-302 `let reset = message.isSessionReset && epoch > local.epoch` / `if !reset { if message.revision < local.revision { return .ignoredRollback } }`；:662-664 `case .ignoredRollback, .ignoredAlreadyApplied: advanced.framesIgnored += 1; return true`（沒有 failedToSync()、沒有 sendResume、沒有 noteResumeAttempt）；:369-374 `syncStatus` 在 connected && !unrecoverable && negotiated && !resuming 時回 `.synced`，而 handleResponse:610 已把 resuming 設回 false、handleFrame:523 隨即 refreshStatus()。Host 端：crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:586-589 `if epoch != self.epoch { … Resume::EpochMismatch }`（送出的 snapshot 帶的是 host 自己的 epoch，可能比手機記得的小），:591-596 `last_revision > self.revision` 時只送**沒有** reason 的 `Resume::Snapshot`；crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs:209 全新 host 的 epoch 就是 1。iOS 既有測試只涵蓋 epoch 3→4 的情況：InteractionCompanionTests/SessionClientTests.swift:183-205（「session-reset ＋ 新 epoch 必須被接受」），epoch 相同或變小的 reset 完全沒有測試。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `high`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  逐行核對，主張完全成立。

  1. `SessionClient.swift:298`（現況與引用行號一致）：
     `let reset = message.isSessionReset && epoch > local.epoch`
     只在 host 傳來的 epoch **嚴格大於** 本地記得的 epoch 時，才承認這是合法的 session-reset。`!reset` 分支接著用 revision 比較（:300-301）判 `.ignoredRollback` / `.ignoredAlreadyApplied`。

  2. 但契約 `docs/aip/character-session.md` §7 步驟 4 明文是「epoch **不同** → 丟棄本地狀態、套用 snapshot」（`sed -n '107-114p'` 已核對原文），不是「更大」。Host 端 `crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:586-588` 也確實是雙向比較：
     `if epoch != self.epoch { … return Resume::EpochMismatch { envelope } }`
     而全新 host（例如換一台桌面、或 store 找不到既有 snapshot）的 epoch 起始值是 `1`（`crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs:209`：`Ok(None) => (CharacterSession::new(config.clone(), 1, now), None)`）。只要手機記得的 epoch ≥ 新 host 的 epoch（包括「兩邊都還是 1」這種再正常不過的換桌面情境——旧桌面早已把 revision 推高，新桌面全新安裝），client 就會把這份帶 `reason:"session-reset"` 的權威 snapshot 誤判成 rollback 丟掉。

  3. `consume`（:642-679）對 `.ignoredRollback` / `.ignoredAlreadyApplied` 的處理（:662-664）確實只是 `advanced.framesIgnored += 1; return true`——沒有呼叫 `sendResume`、沒有呼叫 `SessionDecisions.noteResumeAttempt`（用 grep 確認 `noteResumeAttempt` 全域只在 `sendResume`/`sendSnapshotQuery`/`failedToSync` 三處被呼叫，這三處都不會被 `.ignoredRollback` 觸發）。`resumeFailures`／`unrecoverable` 因此不會因為連續收到「同一份被誤判的 reset」而累積——只有下一次「主動重連」(`connectionDidConnect`→`sendResume`) 才會再计一次，而且此後同一份 reset 還是會被拒。一旦累積到 3 次進入 `unrecoverable=true`，`sendResume`/`sendSnapshotQuery` 的 guard（:784-786、:800-802）會直接不再送出任何 resume/snapshot query，狀態永久卡死，直到使用者重啟 App（`local` 是 `SessionClient` 的 in-memory 屬性，未持久化，重啟才會歸零）——但 UI 完全沒有提示需要這麼做。

  4. `syncStatus`（:362-375，行號與引用的 369-374 基本一致）：`connected=true, local.unrecoverable=false（尚未達 3 次前）, negotiated=true, resuming=false（handleResponse:610 已設回 false）` → 回傳 `.synced`。對照 §11 文案表，這會顯示「iPhone 已連接，角色狀態已同步」，但實際上一則權威狀態都沒套用——確實違反誠實階梯（received≠applied 卻宣稱 synced）。

  5. 測試覆蓋確認：`InteractionCompanionTests/SessionClientTests.swift:185-211` 唯一一個 session-reset 測試是 epoch 3→4（更大）被接受；同檔案沒有任何測試餵入 epoch 相同或更小＋`reason:"session-reset"` 的訊息，因此現有測試套件不會攔到這個回歸。

  所有引用的檔案／行號（SessionClient.swift:298、662-664、369-374、610、523；session.rs:586-589、591-596；character_session.rs:209；SessionClientTests.swift:183-205）逐一核對後皆準確，無需修正行號。這不是已被測試覆蓋的防護，也不是風格問題，是真實的、可用簡單建構訊息重現的邏輯缺陷，且直接對應到 CLAUDE.md「誠實階梯」與專案自訂 AIP 契約文字的明確違反。

  </details>

- **規格／誠實階梯（`spec-honesty`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `high`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  逐行核對原始碼與 host 端邏輯，finding 的技術主張全部成立且行號無漂移：\n\n1. `SessionClient.swift:298` `let reset = message.isSessionReset && epoch > local.epoch`——確認只在嚴格「大於」時才承認 reset，`epoch` 相等或更小時會落入下面的 rollback 檢查（:300-301），回 `.ignoredRollback`／`.ignoredAlreadyApplied`。\n2. `consume()`（:637-664）對這兩個 outcome 只做 `framesIgnored += 1; return true`——沒有 `sendResume`、沒有 `noteResumeAttempt`、沒有 `failedToSync`，也證實 `handleState`（:589-597，一般 host 廣播的 `state` 訊息）走的是同一個 `consume()`，所以之後每一則權威狀態更新都會被同樣理由靜默丟棄。\n3. `syncStatus()`（:362-375）在 `resuming=false`／`unrecoverable=false`／`negotiated=true` 時回 `.synced`——UI 會顯示「已同步」，但實際上一則權威狀態都沒套用，直接命中 CLAUDE.md 誠實階梯不變量（received≠applied 卻宣稱已完成同步）。\n4. Host 端 `crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:585-588` `if epoch != self.epoch { … Resume::EpochMismatch }`，回傳的 snapshot 帶的是 host 自己的 epoch；`crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs:209` 證實全新桌面的 epoch 就是 1——兩台獨立部署的桌面很容易同樣落在 epoch=1，此時 iOS 端 `epoch > local.epoch` 為假，完全對不上「reset」條件。\n5. `SessionClient` 是單一長壽命實例（`ConnectionManager.swift:264` `let characterSession = SessionClient()`），我逐一 grep 全檔確認 `local`（:429）在斷線、`startPairing(with:)` 重新配對等任何路徑都**不會被重置**；`StoredPairing`（`PairingStore.swift`）也沒有任何 host 身分／session 世系欄位可供偵測「這是不同的 host」。\n6. `docs/aip/pairing-security.md` §7「Endpoint migration」明確把「使用者手動重新掃描桌面新產生的配對 QR payload」列為會觸發全新配對握手的正常、預期路徑（非假設情境）——證實「同一支手機被重新配對到另一台桌面」是這個系統真的會發生的操作，不是超出範圍的邊角案例。\n7. 測試缺口確認：`SessionClientTests.swift:185-209` 唯一一個 session-reset 測試只涵蓋 epoch 3→4（遞增），沒有任何測試涵蓋 `isSessionReset==true` 且 epoch 相等或更小的情況。\n\n唯一需要誠實補充的一點：finding 引用 `character-session.md:112`「epoch 不同」與程式碼的 `>` 比較確實文字不符，但我也發現 `docs/aip/README.md:187`（AIP 1.0 通用 rollback 防護規則）把例外條件精確寫成「`sessionEpoch` **大於**本地」——與程式碼的 `>` 判斷一致，兩份文件本身在措辭上互相矛盾。不過這個細節不影響 finding 成立：(a) finding 引用的 character-session.md §7 正是「member 視角」的重連流程專章，文字確實是「不同」；(b) 更重要的是，不論哪份文件的措辭才是權威，程式碼實際行為（靜默丟棄 host 明確標示的 session-reset、UI 對外宣稱 `.synced`、且無限期不再嘗試 resume）都是可獨立驗證、真實存在、違反誠實階梯不變量的缺陷，不是風格分歧也未被任何測試或其他防護機制吸收。severity=high 合理：需要特定但文件證實為正常操作的重新配對情境觸發，不涉及安全繞過，但一旦觸發即造成使用者無感知的永久（或極長時間）失步且介面說謊。

  </details>

**Fix**

把 reset 條件改成契約寫的「epoch 不同」：`let reset = message.isSessionReset && epoch != local.epoch`（並在套用時無條件覆寫 local.revision/sequence/epoch/state）。另外讓 `.ignoredRollback` 不再是終點：連續 N 次 ignoredRollback 應呼叫 failedToSync()／noteResumeAttempt，讓 syncStatus 走到 `.unrecoverable`（「無法恢復，請重新連接」）而不是 `.synced`。重新配對（startPairing / unpairByUser）時也應把 SessionClient 的 local 認知歸零。

**Regression test**

在 SessionClientTests 新增：local.revision=300, local.epoch=3；餵入 payload {kind:"snapshot", reason:"session-reset", sessionEpoch:1（或 3）, revision:1, state:{...}}；斷言 SessionDecisions.apply 回傳 .applied(revision:1, epoch:1, state:...)（依 §7 步驟 4：epoch 不同即丟棄本地狀態套用 snapshot），此測試在目前程式碼下會失敗（實際回傳 .ignoredRollback）。 / 在 `SessionClientTests.swift` 新增一個測試：`local.epoch = 2, local.revision = 300`，餵入 `{"kind":"snapshot","reason":"session-reset","revision":1,"sessionEpoch":1,...}`（epoch 相等或更小的 session-reset），斷言 `SessionDecisions.apply` 回傳 `.applied(...)` 而非 `.ignoredRollback`；並在 `SessionClient` 整合層斷言 `consume()` 對這種情況會呼叫 `sendResume`／不會讓 `syncStatus` 回 `.synced`。

---

#### 任何 task.* 真相轉錄都會清掉 emergency 守衛，緊急停止中互動重新被接受

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-session-integrity-056`
- **維度**：`session-integrity`
- **Severity**：`high`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-session/src/director.rs:175`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`director::on_fact` 的 `TaskState`／`TaskVerified` 分支無條件覆寫 `truth`，沒有 `react` 那道 emergency 守衛。由於 `CharacterSession::gate` 判斷「emergency 中拒絕互動」的唯一依據就是 `self.state.truth.state == TruthState::Emergency`（session.rs:792），只要在緊急停止期間進來任何一則 `task.state`／`task.verified` 事實，emergency 就被降級成 unknown／cancelled／verified，之後的 `character.interaction.touch` 從 `rejected{scope-denied}` 變成 `applied`，mood→happy、activity→reacting，並且照樣送出 `react-happily-to-touch` intent 給 renderer。契約 §4 明定 emergency 只由 `runtime.emergency{engaged:false}` 解除，CLAUDE.md 也寫「AI 不可解除 emergency stop」。

**Evidence**

director.rs:98 `react` 有守衛：`if state.truth().state == TruthState::Emergency { return None; }`；director.rs:175-191 `on_fact` 的 TaskState 分支沒有任何等價檢查，直接 `patch.insert("truth", truth_value(*truth, cid))`。session.rs:792 `if self.state.truth.state == TruthState::Emergency && envelope.name.starts_with("character.interaction.")` 是管線裡唯一的 emergency 關卡。實測（以 interaction-session 為 path 依賴的獨立程式，注入時間）：
  after estop            : truth=Emergency activity=Frozen
  touch during estop     : outcome=Rejected err=Some(ScopeDenied)
  after task.state(unkn) : truth=Unknown activity=Frozen
  touch AFTER task.state : outcome=Applied err=None
  state now              : mood=Mood{kind:Happy,intensity:0.5} activity=Reacting
  pending intents        : ["react-happily-to-touch"]
Runtime 端的來源真實存在：`character_session_note_agent_state`（crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs:482-501）把每一則 `agent.session.state` 轉成 `RuntimeFact::TaskState`；產生者包含 `expire_if_needed` 的 `"timed-out"`（agents.rs:697）與重啟時 `restore_agent_sessions` 對仍 open 的 session 發的 `"unknown"`（agents.rs:1509）。這兩條在「estop 的 session 掃除逾時（ESTOP_SESSION_TIMEOUT，agents.rs:1407-1417）留下一個仍 open 的 session」之後都可觸發，而 estop 旗標是 latched 的（runtime.rs:977 / 218）。另注意 runtime 啟動時只把 estop 旗標讀回 `AtomicBool`，從來沒有把 `RuntimeFact::Emergency{engaged:true}` 補投進 Character Session（`character_resync_safety_state`（character.rs:1781）只補投 CPP instance），所以這條守衛一旦掉了就再也不會回來。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `high`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  All cited locations check out exactly against the current source (no line drift):
  - crates/interaction-session/src/director.rs:98 — `react()`'s emergency guard (`if state.truth().state == TruthState::Emergency { return None; }`).
  - crates/interaction-session/src/director.rs:175 — `on_fact`'s `RuntimeFact::TaskState` arm starts here; it unconditionally does `patch.insert("truth", truth_value(*truth, cid))` with no equivalent guard. The `RuntimeFact::TaskVerified` arm (line 192) has the same gap.
  - crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:792 — the *only* emergency check in the whole pipeline (`gate()` step 13, keyed off `self.state.truth.state == TruthState::Emergency`).
  - crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs:482-501 — `character_session_note_agent_state` unconditionally converts any `agent.session.state` (other than "verified") into `RuntimeFact::TaskState` and any "verified" into `RuntimeFact::TaskVerified`, feeding straight into `submit_runtime` → `on_fact`.
  - crates/interaction-runtime/src/agents.rs:697-701 and :1509-1512 — two real production producers of exactly such facts: lease timeout → "timed-out", and `restore_agent_sessions` on daemon restart for any session that was still open → "unknown".
  - crates/interaction-runtime/src/agents.rs:1407-1417 — `ESTOP_SESSION_TIMEOUT` (2s) bounded close during `emergency_stop`, after which `gateway_spawn_kill` is used and the session record may remain open, making the restart-time "unknown" producer reachable after an estop.
  - crates/interaction-runtime/src/runtime.rs:976-977 / :218 — estop is a persisted, latched `AtomicBool`, but on restart only the bool is restored; `character_project_emergency(true)` (the only path that feeds `RuntimeFact::Emergency{engaged:true}` into the Character Session) is never re-invoked.
  - crates/interaction-runtime/src/character.rs:1782-1796 — `character_resync_safety_state` on reconnect only re-projects a CPP `Projection` to the connecting instance (`character_project_instance`); it never calls `character_session_submit_runtime`, so it cannot repair a Character Session whose internal `truth` already got silently downgraded.

  I independently reproduced the claim (not just re-read the code) with a standalone Cargo binary that path-depends on the real `interaction-session` crate (same technique the finding's author describes), built and run from a clean scratch dir with the actual repo left untouched (`git status --porcelain` empty throughout). Output:
  ```
  after estop            : truth=Emergency activity=Frozen
  touch during estop     : outcome=Rejected err=Some(ScopeDenied)
  after task.state(unkn) : truth=Unknown activity=Frozen
  touch AFTER task.state : outcome=Applied err=None
  state now               : mood=Mood { kind: Happy, intensity: 0.4 } activity=Reacting
  pending intents         : ["react-happily-to-touch"]
  ```
  This matches the finding's evidence transcript essentially verbatim (mood intensity differs only because I used a different `intensity` literal in the touch payload). I also ran a second, more direct case showing the defect doesn't even need a follow-up interaction event: injecting `RuntimeFact::TaskVerified` alone while `truth==Emergency` immediately flips `truth=Verified`, `activity=Celebrating`, `mood=Proud`, and produces a `Broadcast` of a `character.behavior.request` command with `intent:"celebrate", interruptible:false` straight to the connected renderer device — a real "performance" dispatched while the session was supposed to be frozen under emergency stop.

  This is a genuine violation of CLAUDE.md's "AI 不可解除 emergency stop" invariant (a routine, unprivileged runtime fact — not any human action — silently clears the emergency truth state) and of the "誠實階梯" (the only interaction gate is keyed off a truth value that untrusted-adjacent internal bookkeeping facts can clobber). The claim's severity assessment (high) is justified: it is trivially reachable via two existing production code paths (lease timeout, daemon-restart reconciliation) that plausibly fire during or shortly after an emergency stop, and once the guard is lost there is no self-healing path back to Emergency short of another explicit `emergency_stop` call.

  </details>

- **規格／誠實階梯（`spec-honesty`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `high`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  代碼閱讀＋獨立可執行重現，claim 成立，且未被任何既有機制覆蓋。

  1. `crates/interaction-session/src/director.rs:98` `react()` 確實有 `if state.truth().state == TruthState::Emergency { return None; }` 守衛；`on_fact()`（166 行起）的 `RuntimeFact::TaskState`（175-186 行）與 `RuntimeFact::TaskVerified`（188-206 行）分支完全沒有等價檢查，直接 `patch.insert("truth", truth_value(*truth, cid))` 無條件覆寫，行號與 claim 完全吻合、無漂移。

  2. `crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:792` 的 `if self.state.truth.state == TruthState::Emergency && envelope.name.starts_with("character.interaction.")` 是 `submit()`／`gate()` 管線裡唯一擋 `character.interaction.*` 的關卡；`submit_runtime()`（489-534 行，處理 RuntimeFact）完全不經過 `gate()`，也沒有自己的 emergency 檢查。

  3. 用獨立可執行程式（path-depend `interaction-session`／`interaction-aip`／`interaction-character`，不改動任何原始碼）重現：
     - `submit_runtime(Emergency{engaged:true})` 後 `truth=Emergency, activity=Frozen`；此時 `touch` → `Rejected{ScopeDenied}`（符合規格）。
     - 接著 `submit_runtime(TaskState{truth:Unknown, correlation_id:"agent-session-xyz"})`（模擬一個與此次緊急停止完全無關的 agent session 的 `task.state` 事實）之後，`truth` 被無條件覆寫成 `Unknown`。
     - 再送同一個 `touch` → `outcome=Applied, err=None`，`mood=Happy`、`activity=Reacting`，且產生 `react-happily-to-touch` pending intent 送 renderer。
     輸出與 finding 附的實測記錄幾乎逐行一致，獨立驗證成立。

  4. 規格核對：`docs/aip/character-session.md` 第 67 行明定 `runtime.emergency`：「engaged：truth→emergency…之後的互動事件 rejected{scope-denied}；解除：truth→none」——只有 `runtime.emergency{engaged:false}` 才是「解除」的來源；`task.state`（第 65 行）只說「truth 轉錄」，並未言明在 truth 已是 emergency 時應被忽略/降級，但這正是 §67 承諾的「之後的互動事件持續 rejected」被打破之處：一個與此次 estop 無因果關係的背景事實（agent session 逾時／重啟時未知）就能把這個持久化的拒絕保證悄悄解除，不需要任何人類動作，牴觸 CLAUDE.md「AI 不可解除 emergency stop」「emergency stop…不得自動恢復」的精神。

  5. Runtime 端來源鏈也逐一核對成立：`crates/interaction-runtime/src/agents.rs:697-702`（`expire_if_needed` 對到期 lease 發 `"timed-out"`）、`agents.rs:721`（`list_agent_sessions` 註解明講「桌面每個 runtime 事件都會呼叫一次」，是常態、非罕見路徑會觸發 lazy expiry）、`estop_agent_sessions`（`runtime.rs:1071`）在 `character_project_emergency(true)`（`runtime.rs:1116`）**之前**執行——意即 estop 生效當下的 session 收尾不會有問題（Emergency 最後才寫入），但 `ESTOP_SESSION_TIMEOUT=2s`（`agents.rs:55`）逾時未收尾乾淨的殘留 Open session，其 lease 到期後由後續任何一次 `list_agent_sessions`／`get_agent_session` 存取觸發 `expire_if_needed`→`"timed-out"`，是常態化操作（非構造場景）。
     `character_resync_safety_state`（`character.rs:1781-1787`）只呼叫 `character_project_instance`（CPP instance 層投影），沒有呼叫 `character_session_submit_runtime(Emergency{engaged:true})`，所以一旦 Character Session 的 truth 被 task 類事實踢掉，沒有任何自動補投機制能救回來，與 claim 描述一致。
     Runtime 啟動時（`runtime.rs:218,350`）估旗標只讀回 `AtomicBool`，未見任何補送 `RuntimeFact::Emergency` 給 Character Session 的呼叫，此路徑也存在（雖然本身可能是另一個獨立缺口，但佐證了「這道守衛一旦掉了不會自動恢復」的結論）。

  6. 影響範圍評估（用於裁量嚴重度）：真實動器派送另有獨立防線——`crates/interaction-runtime/src/executor.rs` 等多處直接檢查 `self.is_estopped()`（AtomicBool，不經 Character Session），所以物理裝置/actuator 不會被此缺陷重新啟用，缺陷影響侷限在 Character Session／角色呈現層（mood、activity、送給 renderer 的 Behavior Intent）。但正因為「角色呈現層」是使用者唯一看得到的「系統現在处于緊急停止」訊號來源之一，這個訊號在 estop 仍生效時被靜默清除、角色照樣對觸摸播出「開心」演出，直接違反 §67 規格文字與「感測不靜默」／誠實階梯精神，屬於真實、可驗證、非風格偏好、且未被任何既有測試覆蓋（`crates/interaction-session/tests/session.rs` 的 `emergency_freezes_the_character_and_refuses_interaction` 只測了顯式 `Emergency{engaged:false}` 解除路徑，未涵蓋 TaskState/TaskVerified 事實在 Emergency 中到達的情形）的缺陷。

  未發現任何已存在的守衛、測試、或另一機制能覆蓋此漏洞；未發現行號漂移或檔案已被平行修復的跡象。維持 high。

  </details>

**Fix**

在 `director::on_fact` 的 `TaskState`／`TaskVerified` 分支加上與 `react` 相同的守衛：`truth.state == Emergency` 時只轉錄到一個非權威欄位（或整則丟棄並稽核），絕不改寫 `truth`／`activity`；只有 `RuntimeFact::Emergency{engaged:false}` 能離開 emergency。另外在 Runtime 啟動時，若 `estop_engaged` 為 true，補送一次 `RuntimeFact::Emergency{engaged:true}` 進 session（與 `character_resync_safety_state` 同一個理由）。

**Regression test**

In `crates/interaction-session/tests/session.rs`, add a test that: joins a renderer, calls `submit_runtime(RuntimeFact::Emergency{engaged:true})`, asserts truth==Emergency, then calls `submit_runtime(RuntimeFact::TaskState{truth: TruthState::Unknown, ..})` and asserts `session.state().truth().state` is still `Emergency` (not `Unknown`) and that a subsequent `touch` is still `Rejected{ScopeDenied}` — this pins `on_fact`'s TaskState/TaskVerified arms to preserve (or re-derive rather than clobber) an Emergency truth until an explicit `RuntimeFact::Emergency{engaged:false}` arrives. / 在 `crates/interaction-session/tests/session.rs` 新增測試：`submit_runtime(Emergency{engaged:true})` 後，再 `submit_runtime(TaskState{truth:TruthState::Unknown, correlation_id:Some("unrelated-session")})`（或 `TaskVerified`），斷言 `session.state().truth().state` 仍是 `TruthState::Emergency`、且隨後 `submit(touch(...))` 的 `outcome` 仍是 `Outcome::Rejected` 且 `error == Some(ErrorCode::ScopeDenied)`；目前此斷言會失敗（truth 被覆寫成 Unknown，touch 變成 Applied）。

---

#### 非目標成員可以用 correlationId 冒領 intent：假 observed 進計數器並吃掉 intent-expired 稽核

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-session-integrity-057`
- **維度**：`session-integrity`
- **Severity**：`high`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:887`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`settle_intent` 只用 `PendingIntent::answers()`（causationId 或 correlationId）比對，從不檢查回報者是否在該 intent 的 `outstanding` 目標名單裡。一個從頭到尾沒有收到任何 `command` 的成員（例如只宣告 `inputs`、`intents` 為空的 input-device）只要送 `result{status:"observed", correlationId:<它自己那則 touch 的 correlationId>}`，host 就會結清該 intent、寫 `character.session.intent-settled` 稽核並把 `intents.observed` +1。誠實階梯被反轉：diagnostics 宣稱「已呈現於使用者眼前」，實際上 host 一則 command 都沒送出去；而且真正該發的 `character.session.intent-expired`（沒有人演）被吞掉了。

**Evidence**

session.rs:867-906：`let Some(index) = self.pending.iter().position(|p| p.answers(envelope))` → `self.pending[index].outstanding.retain(|party| party != reporter)` → `let settled = self.pending[index].outstanding.is_empty()` → `if let Some(counter) = report_counter(status) { self.bump(counter) }`。`answers()`（session.rs:1536-1545）在 causationId 對不上時回退到 `envelope.correlation_id == self.intent.correlation_id`，而互動事件的 correlation 就是裝置自己送的（session.rs:909-912）。實測：
  join(role=input-device, intents=[])
  touch outcome          : Applied
  commands actually sent : 0
  pending intents        : ["react-happily-to-touch"]
  report(status=observed, causationId="not-a-real-command-id", correlationId="flow_x") → Accepted
  counters               : {..., "intents.observed": 1}
  pending after report   : 0
既有測試（tests/session.rs:1002 `a_member_result_settles_the_pending_intent_before_its_ttl`、1068 `repeating_a_terminal_result_never_counts_twice`）只覆蓋「真正的目標用正確 causationId 回報」，沒有覆蓋非目標冒領。另外 `result` 沒有強制 `expiresAt`（envelope.rs:203-210 只要求 causationId 與 status），所以 AIP §7「超出去重環的重放靠 expiresAt 擋」對 `result` 完全不適用——同一則被錄下來的 `result` 在 256 筆環滾過之後可以重放。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `high`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Reproduced end-to-end with a standalone integration test against the real crate (not a mock): joined a single member as MemberRole::InputDevice with intents=[] (so intent_targets() — session.rs:1372-1382, which filters strictly on role==RemoteRenderer — can never select it as a command recipient), submitted a `touch` from it. director::react() unconditionally emits the `react-happily-to-touch` BehaviorIntent regardless of whether any RemoteRenderer is online (session.rs:955-959), so emit_intent() queues a PendingIntent with outstanding=[] (0 commands actually sent — verified in output). The very same non-target device then submitted a forged `result{status:"observed", causationId:"not-a-real-command-id", correlationId:<its own earlier touch message_id>}`. Gate step 7 (session.rs ~741-750) only capability-checks MessageType::Event, not Result, so this is accepted for any member regardless of negotiated intents/inputs. settle_intent (session.rs:864-901) locates the pending intent via PendingIntent::answers() (session.rs:1533-1544), which falls back to `envelope.correlation_id == self.intent.correlation_id` with no check that `reporter` is a member of `outstanding`. Because outstanding was already empty (no real target ever existed), `.retain(|p| p != reporter)` on line 887 is a no-op that leaves it empty, `settled = outstanding.is_empty()` is true, the pending intent is removed, `character.session.intent-settled{settled:true}` is audited, and `intents.observed` is bumped to 1 — all attributed to a device that never received any command. A subsequent tick() at t+600s (well past any TTL) produces no `character.session.intent-expired` audit and the expired counter stays absent, confirming the genuine expiry signal is swallowed. Test output: `commands actually sent: 0`, `pending intents before report: ["react-happily-to-touch"]`, `report outcome: Accepted`, `intent-settled audit: settled=true, party="device:iphone-1"`, `pending intents after report: 0`, `counters: intents.observed=1`, `intent-expired fired on tick: false`. Also independently verified the secondary evidence: envelope.rs's validate() for MessageType::Result (lines 202-208) requires only causationId and a known payload.status — no expiresAt — supporting the finding's replay-window remark. Existing tests (tests/session.rs: a_member_result_settles_the_pending_intent_before_its_ttl at line ~1002, repeating_a_terminal_result_never_counts_twice at ~1068) only exercise the legitimate target reporting with a matching causationId; neither covers a non-outstanding member settling via the correlationId fallback, so this is not an already-covered case. Cited line 887 (`.retain(|party| party != reporter);`) matches the current file exactly, no drift. The bug directly violates this repo's 誠實階梯 invariant (observed is being recorded/audited/counted for a party that never got a command) and corrupts the audit trail (real intent-expired signal lost), so I concur with the reported "high" severity. Temp verification file crates/interaction-session/tests/zzz_verify_f057.rs was created, run, and deleted afterward; `git status --short` is clean.

  </details>

- **規格／誠實階梯（`spec-honesty`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `high`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Verified by reading code and by an independent, self-written repro test (run against the actual crate, then deleted to leave the repo clean).

  Code trace confirms the exact mechanism claimed:
  1. `settle_intent` (session.rs:867-903) matches an inbound `result` envelope to a pending intent purely via `PendingIntent::answers()` (session.rs:1536-1545: causationId against the recorded `commands` list or against `intent.correlation_id`, falling back to `envelope.correlation_id == intent.correlation_id`). Neither `answers()` nor the caller ever checks that `reporter` (the bound identity of the sender) is a member of `pending[index].outstanding` before treating a terminal status as authoritative.
  2. `outstanding` is set at `emit_intent` (session.rs:1341-1372) to exactly `intent_targets(...)` — members with `role == RemoteRenderer`, `presence == Online`, and negotiated `IntentSupport::Exact` for that intent (session.rs:1375-1385). A pending intent is pushed via `push_pending` unconditionally, even when `targets` is empty (no online exact remote-renderer at all) — comment 8 in `docs/aip/character-session.md` explicitly acknowledges this "zero remote members" case is normal (host-side CPP rendering still happens via `Output::RendererIntent`, out of band).
  3. `intent.correlation_id` is set from the originating touch event's `correlation_id` (or its `message_id` if none given) — i.e., exactly the value the reporting party itself supplied when it sent the touch (session.rs `handle_event`, director.rs:137). So any member that caused a touch trivially knows the value needed to forge a match.
  4. Gate step 7 (session.rs ~741-750) restricts `event` messages by declared `inputs`, but `result` is explicitly exempt (matches `docs/aip/character-session.md` line 186: "`heartbeat`/`capability`/`query`/`cancel`/`result` 的 name 不受 `inputs` 限制"). Gate step 8 only blocks `status:"verified"`; nothing checks role or outstanding membership for `observed`/`rejected`/`failed`. `MessageType::Result` schema validation (envelope.rs:203-210) only requires *some* `causationId` string and a known `payload.status` — the causationId's value is never checked against a real sent command until `answers()`, which itself has the fallback hole.

  My standalone repro (join role=InputDevice with `intents:[]`, touch → 0 commands sent, 1 pending intent queued with empty `outstanding`, then send `result{status:"observed", causationId:"not-a-real-command-id", correlationId:"m1"}` from the same non-target device) reproduced exactly what the finding claims: `Accepted`, pending intents drop from 1 to 0, `intents.observed` counter bumps to 1, and the subsequent `tick()` to +60s never emits `character.session.intent-expired`. This directly contradicts the explicit spec promise in `docs/aip/character-session.md` ("只有真的沒有人回覆的 intent 才會在 TTL 到期時被稽核成 `character.session.intent-expired`") and the honesty-ladder invariant in CLAUDE.md ("結果未知要標 uncertain/Unknown，不得謊稱成功") — a party that was never sent a command, and is not even capable of being one (negotiated all-Unsupported), gets to make the host record a false "observed" completion and erase the honest "intent-expired" audit trail.

  Existing tests (`a_member_result_settles_the_pending_intent_before_its_ttl`, `repeating_a_terminal_result_never_counts_twice`) only cover the legitimate case where the reporting party IS the actual command target (a phone that is both the toucher and the negotiated RemoteRenderer), so they don't catch this. The `result`-has-no-mandatory-`expiresAt` sub-claim is also verified accurate against envelope.rs:203-210 (only causationId + a known status are required), supporting the finding's secondary point about extended replay exposure for `result` messages beyond the 256-entry dedupe ring.

  Line 887 cited by the finding is exactly the start of the `self.pending[index].outstanding.retain(...)` statement that performs the unchecked removal — no drift.

  </details>

**Fix**

`settle_intent` 先確認 `self.pending[index].outstanding.contains(reporter)`（或 `commands` 裡真的有一則是送給 reporter 的），否則整則忽略並稽核成 `intent-report-unsolicited`；只有真正的目標能結清與計數。correlationId 只能當作次要比對，不能單獨授權結清。

**Regression test**

Join only an InputDevice member (intents=[], so it's never a RemoteRenderer target), submit a touch that queues a behavior intent with zero real outstanding targets, then submit a forged `result{status:"observed", causationId:<bogus>, correlationId:<its own touch id>}` from that same member and assert `session.pending_intents()` becomes empty, `intents.observed` counter is bumped, and a later `tick()` past TTL emits no `intent-expired` audit — i.e. settle_intent must additionally require `pending[index].outstanding.contains(reporter)` (or reporter was in outstanding pre-retain) before treating the report as a genuine settlement/counting it. / Join a member as role=InputDevice with empty `intents` (never negotiates Exact for any behavior intent, so it can never be a legitimate command target); send a touch that produces a pending `react-happily-to-touch` intent with zero commands actually sent (no online exact RemoteRenderer); then from that same non-target member send `result{status:"observed", causationId:"bogus", correlationId:<the touch's own message id>}` and assert the host rejects it (or at minimum does not remove the pending intent / bump `intents.observed` / suppress the later `character.session.intent-expired` tick audit) unless `reporter` is verified to be in `pending.outstanding`.

---

#### 非乾淨重啟後 host 的 revision 會退回，成員依 §6 rollback 規則忽略權威 snapshot 而永久停在舊狀態

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-session-integrity-058`
- **維度**：`session-integrity`
- **Severity**：`high`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:255`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`CharacterSession::restore` 直接把 `revision` 設回持久化 snapshot 的值且**不動 epoch**，而 `persist_if_due` 只在「每 32 個 revision 或每 60 秒」才建議落地。daemon 被 kill -9 之後，host 的 revision 會低於成員早就套用過的 revision，於是 host 重新發出的 snapshot／patch 在成員端被本專案自己的 §6 rollback 防護判成 `Ignore{Rollback}`——連重連時 `join` 給的權威 snapshot 與 `resume` 回的 snapshot 都被忽略，因為兩者都沒有 `reason:"session-reset"`、epoch 也沒變大。成員會卡在舊狀態直到 host 的 revision 重新爬過去，UI 只會顯示「同步尚未完成／無法恢復，請重新連接」。

**Evidence**

session.rs:252-269 `restore` 回傳 `epoch: snapshot.epoch, revision: snapshot.revision`（epoch 不加一）；session.rs:1469-1483 `persist_if_due` 的條件是 `revision >= last_persist_revision + persist_every_revisions`(預設 32) 或 `now - last_persist_at >= persist_interval_ms`(預設 60 s)；patch.rs:108-121 接收端規則：snapshot 只有在 `reason == session-reset && epoch > local_epoch` 時才 `Reset`，否則走 `stale_or(revision, local_revision, Apply)`，revision 較小即 `Ignore{Rollback}`。實測：
  persisted snapshot     : revision=8 sequence=11 epoch=1
  host revision at crash : 26 (成員全部套用過)
  restored               : revision=8 epoch=1
  member decision on the authoritative snapshot: Ignore { reason: Rollback }
  resume -> snapshot, member decision: Ignore { reason: Rollback }
  has reason=session-reset? None
（`CharacterSessionHost::open`，crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs:195-218，只有在 store 讀不到／壞掉時才 quarantine+epoch+1；讀得到就走這條 restore。）

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `high`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  逐行核對＋真實重現，claim 成立。

  1. `crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:216-269`（`CharacterSession::restore`）：回傳的 struct literal 在 254-255 行是 `epoch: snapshot.epoch, revision: snapshot.revision`——epoch 原樣照抄，不加一；只有 `CharacterSessionHost::open`（`crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs:194-224`）在 store 讀不到／壞掉時才呼叫 `store.quarantine()`（epoch+1）。檔案讀得到、hash 也對得上的正常情況（例如 `kill -9` 後，最後一次 `persist_if_due` 落的地還是舊的）一律走 `restore`，epoch 不變。

  2. `persist_if_due`（session.rs:1469-1483）確認只在「revision 累積到 `persist_every_revisions`（預設 32）」或「距上次落地 `persist_interval_ms`（預設 60_000ms）」才建議落地；`character_session.rs:611-620` 的 `character_session_persist_now` 只掛在 ctrl_c／graceful shutdown 路徑（`interaction-cli/src/commands.rs:1591-1593`），SIGKILL 不會觸發，所以崩潰當下已經廣播給成員、但還沒達門檻的 revision 會真的遺失。

  3. `patch.rs:96-141`（`accept_state_with_epoch`）與其在 `apps/interaction-ios/.../SessionClient.swift:291-320`（`SessionDecisions.apply`）的鏡射版本逐字比對一致：`kind:"snapshot"` 只有在 `reason=="session-reset" && epoch>local_epoch` 才 `Reset`，否則進 `stale_or`，`revision < local_revision` 一律 `Ignore{Rollback}`。而 `resume()`（session.rs:577-596）在「對方宣稱看過的進度超前 host」（`last_revision > self.revision`）這個分支自己的註解就寫「對方宣稱看過的進度超前 host → 只能用 snapshot 對齊」，但送出的 `snapshot_envelope_inner(party, now, None)` reason 是 `None`，不是 `session-reset`；`join()`（session.rs:349，`snapshot_envelope_inner(&party, now, None)`）同樣沒有 reason。

  4. 用真實 crate（未改任何 repo 檔案，另建獨立 cargo 專案以 path 依賴指到 `crates/interaction-session`／`crates/interaction-aip`）端到端重現：成員 join 後正常同步到 revision=32；用一份較早、真的被落地過的 snapshot（revision=9）模擬 kill-9 後的 `restore`，確認 `restored.epoch()==persisted.epoch`（true，未 +1）；接著對 `restored` 呼叫 `resume(phone, last_revision=32, last_sequence=32, epoch=1, …)` 與 `join(phone, …)`，兩者回的 snapshot envelope 都是 `revision=9/10, reason=None`；把這兩個 envelope 餵給**同一個** `accept_state_with_epoch` 純函式（成員端真正在用的決策函式），結果都是 `Ignore { reason: Rollback }`。輸出：
  ```
  after join: host revision=2 member_local_revision=2 member_local_epoch=1
  steady state: host revision=32 member_local_revision=32
  persisted snapshot: revision=9 sequence=16 epoch=1
  host revision at crash (in-memory, lost): 32
  restored: revision=9 epoch=1 (epoch unchanged: true)
  resume -> Resume::Snapshot, envelope.revision=Some(Number(9)), reason=None
  member decision on the authoritative resume snapshot: Ignore { reason: Rollback }
  join-after-restore snapshot: revision=Some(Number(10)) reason=None
  member decision on the join-after-restore snapshot: Ignore { reason: Rollback }
  ```
  這與 finding 描述的 repro（revision=8/26 換成 9/32）機制完全一致，行號（session.rs:255、patch.rs:108-121、persist_if_due 1469-1483）也都沒有漂移。

  補充（比 finding 描述更嚴重的一點，但不影響 confirmed 判定）：iOS 端 `SessionClient.swift` 有 `resumeFailureLimit=3` → `unrecoverable=true` 機制，一旦連 3 次 rollback／resume 失敗，之後 `sendResume`／`sendSnapshotQuery` 會直接 guard-return、永遠不再送出，只能靠使用者關閉重開 App（reset `SessionSyncLocal`）復原——不是「host revision 重新爬過去就自動追上」，而是必須人工重啟客戶端；`docs/aip/character-session.md` §7 line 113 確實預期並記錄了 3 次失敗後顯示「無法恢復，請重新連接」這個降級路徑，所以不是靜默失敗（有誠實訊息），但 §1 line 10 承諾的「重啟後 revision 從持久化 snapshot 續接（不歸零）」在 kill -9＋persist 節流的組合下並沒有真的達成「續接」——已同步過的成員反而永久卡住，直到人工介入。

  已排除的替代解釋：不是「已被測試覆蓋」——`crates/interaction-session/tests/session.rs` 裡的 restore 測試（`restore_continues_the_revision_and_survives_a_round_trip` 等）只驗證單一 party 在崩潰前後拿到相同 snapshot 的情境，沒有任何測試模擬「持久化落後於已廣播 revision」再接 `resume`／`join` 的情境；不是「已修復」——所讀檔案內容與行號跟 finding 描述完全吻合，非並行修復後的殘影。

  </details>

- **規格／誠實階梯（`spec-honesty`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `high`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  逐項親自驗證，claim 的機制在目前檔案內容下成立，且比 finding 描述的還更隱蔽：

  1. `session.rs:254-255`（`restore`）：`epoch: snapshot.epoch, revision: snapshot.revision`——epoch 原封不動，行號無漂移。
  2. `session.rs:1469-1481`（`persist_if_due`）：只在 `revision >= last_persist_revision + persist_every_revisions`(預設 32) 或 `now - last_persist_at >= persist_interval_ms`(預設 60s) 才落地，確認 gap 是真實存在的。
  3. `crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs:186-224`（`CharacterSessionHost::open`）：只有 `store.load` 回傳 `Err` 或反序列化/驗證失敗（`CharacterSession::restore` 回 `Err`）才 `store.quarantine()`（epoch+1）；讀得到、驗得過的 snapshot 一律走乾淨的 `restore()`，不管是不是 kill -9 造成的非乾淨關機——而且這條路徑是 `runtime.rs:320` 每次 daemon 啟動都會呼叫的一般路徑，不是罕見分支。
  4. `patch.rs:108-121`：`accept_state_with_epoch` 對 `kind:"snapshot"` 的規則正是「`reason==session-reset && epoch>local_epoch` 才 Reset，否則 `stale_or`」，`stale_or` 對 `revision < local_revision` 回 `Ignore{Rollback}`——且這條規則本身有專屬單元測試 `pure_functions.rs:154-161`（`accept_state(30, &snap-with-revision-12)` → `Ignore{Rollback}`）獨立證實此機制存在且被視為「正確行為」，只是沒人想到它會擋住重啟後的權威 snapshot。
  5. Client 端（Swift `SessionClient.swift`）逐行核對：
     - `connectionDidDisconnect()`（470-472 行）**不會**重置 `local.revision`，斷線重連後仍帶著崩潰前的高 revision。
     - `join` 的協商回覆（`handleNegotiated`，569-586 行）本身不帶 snapshot；真正帶 snapshot 的是 host `join()`（`session.rs:353` `snapshot_envelope_inner`）另外送出的 `state` 訊息，這條走 `handleState → consume → SessionDecisions.apply`，與 `resume` 回應（`handleResponse`，601-646 行同樣走 `consume`）用的是**同一個**有 rollback 防護的 `accept_state_with_epoch` 邏輯——所以 finding 說「join 給的權威 snapshot」與「resume 回的 snapshot」都會被忽略，這點是對的。
     - 更關鍵的是：`consume()` 對 `.ignoredRollback` 回傳 `true`（310-313 行附近，"仍在同步軌道上"），且**不會**呼叫 `noteResumeAttempt`/`failedToSync`（那只在 payload 解不出來時才觸發，`handleResponse` 的 `default: failedToSync()` 分支），所以 §7 step 5「連續 3 次失敗 → 顯示無法恢復」的安全網根本不會被這個失敗模式觸發——`resuming` 在 `handleResponse` 一開始就被設回 `false`，UI 狀態會停在 `.synced`（`syncStatus` 邏輯：connected && negotiated && !resuming && !unsupportedIntents → synced），而不是 finding 講的「顯示無法恢復」。這是 finding 敘述略有出入的一點，但實際情況對「誠實階梯」更不利（假裝已同步，而非誠實顯示失敗），並不構成反駁理由。
  6. `resume()`（`session.rs:585-590`）的註解「對方宣稱看過的進度超前 host → 只能用 snapshot 對齊」證明開發者確實預期過 client 領先 host 的情境，但選擇送一個**不帶 session-reset 標記**的普通 snapshot——這正好會被同一支程式碼庫自己的 rollback 防護擋下，形成死結：host 認為已經解決，client 認為這是要回滾而丟棄。
  7. `docs/aip/character-session.md` 第 10 行明確定義「重啟後 revision 從持久化 snapshot 續接（不歸零）；找不到 snapshot 才 epoch+1」——這是規格明文要求的行為，`restore()` 完全照規格實作，本身不是違規；但規格與 rollback 防護（§6/§7）疊加後產生的這個死結，規格本身沒有交代解法（沒有第三種「host 承認自己落後、要求對方忽略 rollback 防護」的訊號），我沒有在 `docs/aip/`、`CHANGELOG.md`、`docs/releases/v0.6.0-recovery-matrix.md` 中找到任何把這個 gap 記錄為已知限制的文字，也沒有任何 runtime/session 測試涵蓋「member 領先於剛 restore 的 host」這個端到端情境（`session.rs` 裡的 `restore` 測試只測 revision 續接本身，不測有 member 已經領先的情境）。

  沒有找到反駁點：沒有其他程式碼路徑會在乾淨重啟時 bump epoch 或標 `session-reset`；`REASON_SESSION_RESET` 全程式庫只在 `resume()` 的 epoch-mismatch 分支與 `quarantine()`（store 壞掉）用到，找不到第三個來源。這不是風格偏好、不是已被其他機制覆蓋，也沒有被後續 commit 修掉（目前 HEAD 就是這個狀態）。屬於真實、可重現、影響誠實階梯（假裝 synced）與長時可用性的架構缺陷，severity=high 合理（甚至可以說因為 UI 靜默顯示 synced 而非誠實報錯，比 finding 描述得更嚴重，但我維持原報的 high）。

  </details>

**Fix**

兩條擇一或並用：(a) 每次成功 `applied` 都同步把 revision 高水位寫進 store（或把已廣播的最高 revision 一起持久化），restore 時從高水位而不是 snapshot revision 續接；(b) restore 時若無法證明持久化 snapshot 就是最後廣播出去的那一版，一律 epoch+1 並在 join／resume 的 snapshot 上帶 `reason:"session-reset"`，讓接收端依 §6 合法丟棄本地狀態。

**Regression test**

在 `crates/interaction-session/tests/session.rs` 新增：join 成員→送足夠 touch 事件把 revision 推到遠高於 `persist_every_revisions`（例如 32）之外的值→用一份「刻意落後」的 `Snapshot`（revision 遠小於成員已知的 revision，但 epoch 相同）呼叫 `CharacterSession::restore`→對 restored session 呼叫 `resume(&phone, member_last_revision, member_last_sequence, epoch, now)`（或 `join`）→斷言回傳 envelope 的 `payload["reason"] == "session-reset"`（或用 `accept_state_with_epoch(member_last_revision, epoch, &envelope)` 斷言結果是 `StateDecision::Apply`/`Reset` 而不是 `StateDecision::Ignore{Rollback}`）。 / 在 `crates/interaction-session` 建一個整合情境：`CharacterSession::new` 跑到 revision 26 且不觸發 persist（例如把 `persist_every_revisions` 設很大、`persist_interval_ms` 設很大或不推進時間），單獨用一個較早的 snapshot（revision=8）呼叫 `CharacterSession::restore` 建出「重啟後的 host」，再對它呼叫 `resume(party, last_revision=26, last_sequence=.., epoch=1, now)`，斷言回傳的 `Resume::Snapshot` envelope 拿去餵 `accept_state_with_epoch(26, 1, &envelope)` 會得到 `Ignore{Rollback}` 而不是 `Apply`/`Reset`——證明重啟後合法且權威的 snapshot 無法讓一個曾經領先的成員重新對齊。

---

#### 裝置的第一則 capability frame 完全繞過 §8 安全管線的 schema／limits／sessionId 檢查

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-session-integrity-059`
- **維度**：`session-integrity`
- **Severity**：`high`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs:805`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`character_session_device_capability` 對「還不是成員」的裝置直接 `serde_json::from_value::<CapabilityAnnouncement>(envelope.payload)` 再呼叫 `character_session_join`，全程沒有呼叫 `Envelope::validate()`。因此 AIP §11 的 payload 上限（32 KiB）、巢狀深度（8）、字串長度（2000）與 §8 的 `sessionId` 等於本 session 這一關，對每台已配對 iPhone 的第一則 capability 都不生效——只剩 `Envelope::parse` 的 64 KiB 訊息上限與身分綁定。契約 §8 明寫管線順序固定且「任一失敗即拒絕、不執行」。

**Evidence**

character_session.rs:776-820：非成員分支只有 `serde_json::from_value` → `character_session_join`；成員分支才走 `character_session_submit`（→ `CharacterSession::gate` → `envelope.validate()`，session.rs:704）。實測用一則真實可上線的 frame（wss 上限 128 KiB，mobile.rs:90 `MOBILE_WS_MAX_MESSAGE_BYTES`）：
  incoming capability envelope bytes = 64864 (MAX_MESSAGE_BYTES = 65536)
  Envelope::parse accepts?           = Ok("yes")
  Envelope::validate() would say     = Err(PayloadTooLarge)
  join() accepted, member inputs kept (unsupported) = 1500
同一條路徑也不比對 sessionId：
  cross-session capability joined anyway: role=RemoteRenderer inputs=["character.interaction.touch"]（envelope 的 sessionId 是 "session.somewhere-else"）

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `high`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Traced the full code path with the current file contents (git status clean, no parallel edits in flight).

  1. `character_session_device_frame` (character_session.rs:628-699) does identity binding (`bind_identity`) and dispatches by `envelope.message_type` before any membership check. For `MessageType::Capability` it always calls `character_session_device_capability` (line ~721-724) regardless of `envelope.name`.

  2. `character_session_device_capability` (line 776): if the party is already a member it calls `character_session_submit`, which goes through `CharacterSession::submit` → `gate()` (interaction-session/src/session.rs:697-760), whose step 1 is `envelope.validate()` (line 704) and step 5 is the `sessionId == self.config.session_id` check (line 734-737).

  3. If the party is NOT yet a member (first capability frame), the code instead does `serde_json::from_value::<CapabilityAnnouncement>(envelope.payload.clone())` (line 805) directly and calls `character_session_join` (line 381) → `session.join()` (interaction-session/src/session.rs:315-383). Neither `character_session_join` nor `session.join()` calls `envelope.validate()` or compares `envelope.session_id` to `self.config.session_id` anywhere — I read both functions in full and there is no such call.

  4. Confirmed the specific numeric limits cited exist exactly as claimed: `crates/interaction-aip/src/limits.rs` — `MAX_MESSAGE_BYTES=65_536`, `MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES=32_768`, `MAX_STRING_CHARS=2_000`, `MAX_JSON_DEPTH=8`; `mobile.rs:90` `MOBILE_WS_MAX_MESSAGE_BYTES = 128*1024`. So a frame between 32KiB payload and ~64KiB total message passes `Envelope::parse` (only checks total bytes) but would fail `validate()`'s `check_payload` — yet `validate()` is never invoked on this path.

  5. `CapabilityAnnouncement` (interaction-aip/src/capability.rs:32) is a plain serde struct with unbounded `Vec<String>` fields (`inputs`, `intents`, etc.) and no custom size-limiting Deserialize — so `serde_json::from_value` imposes no cap either. `negotiate_capabilities` (capability.rs:95-145) copies every announced input the host doesn't recognize into `unsupported_inputs: Vec<String>` with no length cap, confirming the "1500 unsupported inputs kept" style outcome is genuinely reachable.

  6. The existing integration test `forged_identity_and_out_of_scope_frames_are_refused` (crates/interaction-runtime/tests/character_session_loop.rs:806-897) itself always sends `capability_envelope(&device_id)` *first* to establish membership, then exercises the cross-session/oversized/scope checks only afterward — i.e. the test suite never exercises the gate() protections on the very first capability frame, which is exactly the gap the finding identifies. There is no existing test covering a malformed/oversized/cross-session first capability frame.

  7. `bind_identity` happens unconditionally before the message_type dispatch, and `Envelope::parse`'s 65536-byte cap always applies — matching the claim's "只剩 Envelope::parse 的 64 KiB 訊息上限與身分綁定".

  One mitigating nuance not present in the original claim: the oversized/garbage negotiated data (e.g. `unsupported_inputs`) is stored in the host-private `Member.negotiated` (interaction-session/src/state.rs:224-233), which is explicitly excluded from `MemberView`/`SemanticState` (state.rs:217-243, comment "不進 SemanticState"), so it is not broadcast to other session members — the blast radius is the joining device's own reply plus host-side persisted state, bounded by `MAX_MEMBERS=16`, not an unbounded broadcast amplification. This narrows real-world impact somewhat but does not change that the §8 pipeline (schema/payload/depth/string limits, sessionId equality) is genuinely and reproducibly bypassed for every device's first capability frame, contradicting the AIP §8 "fixed order, any failure rejects, nothing executes" contract. The line reference has drifted very slightly (789-816 for the whole method body; the `serde_json::from_value` call is at line 805, one line off the claimed 804 — same statement, negligible drift).

  </details>

- **規格／誠實階梯（`spec-honesty`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `high`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  驗證屬實。程式碼交叉比對:

  1. `character_session_device_frame` (character_session.rs:628-722) 對每則裝置訊息只呼叫 `Envelope::parse`（僅檢查外層 64 KiB，envelope.rs:112-119）與 `negotiate_version`／`is_known()`／身分綁定，從未呼叫 `Envelope::validate()`。

  2. `Envelope::validate()`（envelope.rs:137起）才是唯一執行 §11 payload≤32KiB、JSON 深度、字串長度（`check_payload`，envelope.rs:276-300）與 schema/profile 檢查的地方；`gate()`（session.rs:697-778）第 1 步呼叫它，但 `gate()` 只在成員（已 join）路徑被呼叫（`character_session_submit`）。

  3. `character_session_device_capability`（character_session.rs:776-830）對非成員裝置的分支：直接 `serde_json::from_value::<CapabilityAnnouncement>(envelope.payload.clone())`（805 行，claim 引用的 804 只差 1 行，屬正常行號指涉誤差非漂移）→ `character_session_join` → `session.join()`（session.rs:315-383）→ `negotiate_capabilities`（capability.rs:95-145）。全程無 `validate()`、無 `check_payload()`。`CapabilityAnnouncement` 是普通 derive Deserialize（`inputs: Vec<String>`、`extra: Map` flatten），沒有任何長度／深度上限；`negotiate_capabilities` 的 `unsupported_inputs`（capability.rs:118-126）直接把 announcement 裡不被 host 接受的每個 input 原樣收集、不設上限，且被存進 `entry.member.negotiated`（session.rs:333/343），成為長駐在 session 記憶體裡的每成員狀態——這正是 CLAUDE.md「有界集合」不變量要防的無界成長。

  4. sessionId 檢查（gate() 第 5 步，session.rs:734-738）同樣只在成員路徑執行；`character_session_join`/`session.join()` 完全不接收也不檢查 `envelope.session_id`，因此非成員的第一則 capability envelope 帶什麼 sessionId 都不影響是否能 join。

  5. 契約原文 `docs/aip/character-session.md` §8（133-140 行）明寫「順序固定：message bytes ≤ 64 KiB → JSON 解析 → schema／profile 驗證 → payload ≤ 32 KiB、深度、字串長度 → specVersion major → 身分綁定 → session membership → sessionId 等於本 session → …。任一失敗：`result{rejected}`／`error`＋稽核，不執行」，適用範圍是「每則來自 device／renderer 的訊息」，沒有為 bootstrapping 的第一則 capability 訊息開特例；且規格把 payload／深度／字串檢查排在身分綁定與 membership 判定「之前」，暗示這些檢查與是否已是成員無關，理應對所有訊息（含第一則 join）生效。

  6. 測試覆蓋確認缺口是真的：`crates/interaction-runtime/tests/character_session_loop.rs` 裡所有偽造身分／跨 session／超大 envelope 的攻擊測試（750-887 行）都是在裝置已用固定、小巧的 `capability_envelope()` fixture（299 行）完成 join、成為成員之後才送出攻擊 frame，全部走的是有 `gate()` 保護的成員路徑；沒有任何測試對「還不是成員」的裝置送出超大或跨 session 的第一則 capability frame。找不到反駁證據。

  結論：這是真實、可驗證、規格明文排序被繞過的缺陷，攻擊面雖侷限在「已配對」的裝置（需先通過 transport 層身分驗證），但仍可造成 session host 記憶體內每成員 `unsupported_inputs`/`extra` 等無界成長，且明確違反 §8 固定管線與「有界集合」不變量。維持 high 合理（本機 daemon 的資源耗盡＋契約明文違反，但非遠端未驗證攻擊面，未達 blocker）。

  </details>

**Fix**

在 `character_session_device_frame` 解析成 `Envelope` 之後、分派之前就無條件呼叫 `envelope.validate()`（失敗回 `error{code}` 不執行），並在 join 路徑補上 `envelope.session_id` 必須等於 `SESSION_ID` 的檢查；`join()` 本身也應該對 announcement 的 `inputs`／`intents` 長度設上限。

**Regression test**

Send a real `character.session.capability` frame as the very first message from a freshly-paired (not-yet-member) fixture device with (a) a payload just over 32KiB (e.g. `unsupported_inputs`/`features` padded to ~40KB, still under the 65536-byte total-message cap) and (b) `sessionId` set to a different session id than "session.home"; assert the runtime replies with `error{code: payload-too-large}` / `error{code: not-a-member}` respectively instead of accepting the join — today both frames succeed and the device becomes a member. / 在 character_session_loop.rs 新增測試：裝置 pair 後，*不先送* fixture `capability_envelope()`，直接送第一則 capability envelope，其 payload.inputs 塞入使 payload 位元組數落在 (32 KiB, 64 KiB) 區間的大量字串（且/或 sessionId 設為 "session.someone-else"）；斷言回應是 `error{payload-too-large}`（或 `not-a-member`／`schema-invalid`），且 `rt.character_session_peek()` 顯示該 party 未成為 member、member 數未增加。

#### capability 重新協商把 token bucket 重設為滿，速率上限可被無限繞過

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-capability-consent-049`
- **維度**：`capability-consent`
- **Severity**：`high`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:335`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

已是成員的裝置送 `capability` 會走完整安全管線後進入 join()，而 join() 對既有成員無條件 `entry.bucket = RateBucket::new(rate_limit_per_sec, now)`（session.rs:335），把 token 補到滿。於是每花 1 則 capability 就能換回整桶額度，rate limit 形同虛設——而 character_session.rs:790-795 的註解正是說「已配對裝置的重新協商要走安全管線，否則可以用 capability 洪水把 revision 與廣播打成無界成長」，這個 reset 正好抵銷了該保護。每次 join 還會再送一份完整 snapshot envelope（session.rs:355、1250-1275），成本不低。

**Evidence**

實測（rate_limit_per_sec = 4，全部訊息都在同一毫秒 at(0)）：
[1] capability re-announce outcome = Applied
[1] rate_limit_per_sec = 4, applied events within the SAME millisecond = 7
即 3 個 touch → 1 個 capability（重設桶）→ 再 4 個 touch 全部 Applied。路徑可從已配對 iPhone 的 wss frame 觸發（character_session.rs:783-800 `character_session_is_member` → `character_session_submit`）與桌面 POST /v1/character-session/events（routes.rs:1902-1911）。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `high`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Verified by reading code and by a minimal reproduction test built from the crate's existing test helpers (crates/interaction-session/tests/security_matrix.rs patterns).

  Code path: `CharacterSession::submit` → `gate()` (session.rs:697) step 10 consumes exactly one token from the per-member `RateBucket` for EVERY message type including Capability (session.rs:773-776), then on `Gate::Proceed` calls `dispatch()` → for `MessageType::Capability` calls `handle_capability()` (session.rs:1037) → `join()` (session.rs:315). Inside `join()`, for an already-existing member (`Some(index)` branch, session.rs:328-336), the code unconditionally does `entry.bucket = RateBucket::new(self.config.rate_limit_per_sec, now)` (session.rs:335), which sets `tokens = rate_limit_per_sec` (full capacity) regardless of how many tokens were already consumed. This exactly reverses the one token debited by the same message's own gate check, and wipes out any prior debits from earlier messages in the same window.

  This directly defeats the protection that `crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs:783-785` documents in its own comment: "已經是成員的重新協商走完整安全管線（速率上限＋去重）：否則一台已配對的裝置可以用 capability 洪水把 revision 與廣播打成無界成長" — the intended enforcement mechanism (the rate-limit gate) is neutralized by `join()`'s side effect on the very code path meant to enforce it.

  Reproduction: I wrote a standalone integration test (crate `interaction-session`, using the exact `SessionConfig`/`CapabilityAnnouncement`/`Envelope` builders already used in `tests/security_matrix.rs`) with `rate_limit_per_sec = 4` and every message timestamped identically (no natural token refill). Sequence: 3 touch events (all Applied, draining bucket to 1 token) → 1 capability re-announce (Applied — consumes the last token, then `join()` refills bucket to 4) → 4 more touch events, ALL Applied. Output: `applied before cap=3, applied after single cap re-announce at SAME ms=4`, i.e. 7 event applications plus 1 capability application = 8 accepted messages in one millisecond against a nominal limit of 4/sec — reproducing the report's own evidence numbers almost exactly (they got 7 touch-applied in the same setup). I ran `cargo test -p interaction-session --test zzz_ratelimit_repro -- --nocapture`, confirmed the failure/printed evidence, then deleted the scratch test file (repo left clean, `git status --short crates/interaction-session` empty) since I was told not to modify any files.

  No mitigating gate exists elsewhere: `gate()` step 10 is the only rate-limit check, and it runs before `dispatch()`/`join()` on every submission, so the reset genuinely happens after (and undoes) the very check meant to bound message rate. This is reachable from an already-paired iPhone over the character-session WS relay (`character_session.rs:783-800`, gated only by `character_session_is_member`) and from the desktop HTTP relay (routes.rs), matching the reported attack surface. Also as claimed, each accepted capability additionally triggers a full snapshot envelope send (`join()` pushes `joined.snapshot_envelope` via `Output::Send`, session.rs: outputs.push after capability_envelope), so the amplification is not merely "one extra message" but a full-state resend per abuse cycle — raising real cost/DoS concern beyond pure rate-limit bypass.

  File/line matches exactly as reported: crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:335 (verified current on-disk content, no drift).

  </details>

- **規格／誠實階梯（`spec-honesty`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `high`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  親自重現成立。crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:333-336 的 join() 對「已是成員」分支無條件執行 `entry.bucket = RateBucket::new(self.config.rate_limit_per_sec, now)`，把 token bucket 灌回滿桶；這行在目前檔案內容中位置未漂移。dispatch() 對 `character.session.capability` 訊息呼叫的正是 handle_capability()→join()（session.rs:1030-1069），而 submit() 的 gate() 只在 dispatch 之前對「這則 capability 訊息本身」扣一次 token（session.rs:772-778，第 10 關），扣完之後才進 dispatch 觸發重設——於是重設對「這一則」訊息本身無效，卻讓桶子回滿，供後續訊息使用。

  用暫時附加到 crates/interaction-session/tests/session.rs 的整合測試直接呼叫公開 API 重現（驗證後已 `git checkout` 還原，repo 現在 clean）：rate_limit_per_sec=4、全部訊息 timestamp 相同（at(0)）。結果：3 次 touch 全部 Applied（耗盡到剩 1 token）→ capability re-announce 本身也 Applied（吃掉最後 1 token，同時觸發 join() 把桶重灌滿）→ 緊接著同一毫秒內再送 4 次 touch，全部 Applied。同一「秒」視窗（實際上同一毫秒）內共 8 次 Applied（含 capability）／7 次非 capability 的 touch 被接受，是設定速率上限 4 的兩倍，且因為重設是「無條件灌滿」而非「按經過時間補token」，理論上可無限重複「耗盡到 1 token → 送 capability → 桶回滿」的週期，在同一時間視窗內把吞吐量推到任意倍數。

  這正好抵銷 crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs:783-786 的既有設計意圖：該處明文寫「已配對裝置的重新協商要走完整安全管線（速率上限＋去重）：否則一台已配對的裝置可以用 capability 洪水把 revision 與廣播打成無界成長」——而 join() 的無條件重設，恰好讓這句話描述的攻擊面重新打開：不只可以夾雜 touch 使用，甚至可以純粹連續送不同 messageId 的 capability 本身（每次都吃 1 token 通過 gate，dispatch 又把桶灌滿）達成無限速率的 capability 洪水，每次還額外觸發一次全量 snapshot_envelope（session.rs:355、1252-1275）＋一次 capability_envelope 送出，放大成本。

  我也確認 docs/aip/character-session.md §8／§12.7 明文把 rate limit 列為安全管線固定一關，且 §12.7 對另一個「高頻訊息把 revision／廣播打成無界成長」的相似風險（lastSeenAt 投影格線）已有明確對策，證明這是設計上真正在意、且已被此 bug 抵銷的防線，不是風格偏好或已被其他機制覆蓋的重複項。搜尋整個 runtime/api 層（character_session.rs、character_ws.rs、mobile.rs、routes.rs）也找不到任何其他速率限制層，session.rs 的 token bucket 是唯一防線，故此 bug 直接讓速率上限在「已配對裝置」這個威脅模型下形同虛設。severity 定為 high 合理：需要先是已通過身分綁定與 membership 的成員才能觸發（不是任意匿名者的完全開放漏洞），但確實是文件明文防護的洪水攻擊面被無條件重設邏輯整個打開，可用於對 session revision／廣播/snapshot 送出做資源放大式 DoS。

  </details>

**Fix**

join() 對既有成員不要重建 bucket（保留既有 tokens/last），或只在成員真的離開後重新加入時重建；重新協商本身也應計入配額。

**Regression test**

With a joined member and rate_limit_per_sec=N, at a single fixed timestamp: send (N-1) touch events (all Applied) then 1 capability re-announce (Applied), then N more touch events at the same timestamp and assert at least one of them is rejected with ErrorCode::RateLimited (currently all N succeed, proving the bucket was refilled instead of merely accepting the capability message itself). / rate_limit_per_sec=4 的 session：連續在同一 timestamp 送 (rate_limit-1) 次 touch（全 Applied）→ 送一次 capability re-announce（Applied）→ 斷言緊接著同一 timestamp 再送 rate_limit 次 touch 不能全部 Applied（目前會全部 Applied，斷言應改為「join() 對既有成員重設桶時應保留既有 token 數／延續原本的補注時間基準，而非直接灌滿」，或直接斷言同一秒視窗內 applied 事件數 ≤ rate_limit_per_sec）。
