#### 沒有收到過該 command 的成員可以把 Behavior Intent 結清成 observed，intents.observed 可被單一成員重複灌值

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-capability-consent-050`
- **維度**：`capability-consent`
- **Severity**：`high`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:867 (settle_intent), exploited via emit_intent:1341-1372 (outstanding: targets, no re-check of reporter membership) and answers():1536-1545 (causationId/correlationId only, no reporter identity check)`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

settle_intent() 只用 `answers()` 比對 causationId／correlationId，**不檢查回報者是否在 outstanding 名單裡**（session.rs:886-894）。而 intent 的 correlationId 就是裝置自己在 touch envelope 上填的 correlationId／messageId（director.rs:131 `correlation_id: event.correlation_id.clone()`），所以任何成員都能構造出對得上的 result。後果有二：(a) 目標名單為空（沒有任何 online remote-renderer，桌面單機就是這個情形）時，`settled = outstanding.is_empty()` 立刻為 true，任何成員送一則 `result{status:"observed"}` 就讓 host 記下「已結清、已 observed」，而 host 其實一則 command 都沒送出去；(b) 同一個成員用不同 messageId 重送終態回報，每次都會再 bump 一次 intents.observed——契約 §5 明寫「已結清的 intent 再收到 result → 忽略（不重複計數，重播灌不進計數器）」。observed 是誠實階梯上「對方說它演了」的證據，這條路讓非目標者偽造它。

**Evidence**

實測：
[2] intents.emitted=Some(1) intents.observed=Some(3) (only member A ever reported; B never did)  ← A 用 r0/r1/r2 三個 messageId 重送 observed，intent 仍 pending
[3] command envelopes actually sent to anyone = 0
[3] audit character.session.intent-settled {"intent":"react-happily-to-touch","party":"device:iphone-1","settled":true,"status":"observed"}
[3] intents.observed = Some(1) while nothing was ever sent
程式碼：session.rs:886 `.outstanding.retain(|party| party != reporter)`；session.rs:890 `let settled = self.pending[index].outstanding.is_empty()`；session.rs:893 `if let Some(counter) = report_counter(status) { self.bump(counter) }`（不論 reporter 是否為目標、是否已回報過）；session.rs:1536-1545 `answers()` 的 correlationId 備援。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `high`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Reproduced both halves of the claim with a temporary integration test appended to crates/interaction-session/tests/session.rs (run via `cargo test -p interaction-session --test session exploit_ -- --nocapture`, then reverted with `git checkout --` so the repo is left unmodified — confirmed via `git status --porcelain`).

  (a) Zero-target phantom settle: joined a single non-RemoteRenderer member ('intruder-1', role InputDevice, only negotiated the EVENT_TOUCH input). It sends a touch; director::react synthesizes a BehaviorIntent, but emit_intent's intent_targets() finds no online RemoteRenderer, so `commands sent = 0` and the PendingIntent is pushed with `outstanding: []` from birth. The same member then sends a Result envelope carrying only a forged causationId equal to the touch's public messageId ("m1") — satisfying answers()'s `causation == self.intent.correlation_id` fallback at session.rs:1539 — with status "observed". settle_intent has no check that `reporter` was ever a member of `outstanding`; since outstanding was already empty, `settled = outstanding.is_empty()` at session.rs:890 is true unconditionally, the pending entry is removed, and `report_counter("observed")` bumps `intents.observed` to 1 at session.rs:893-894. Actual test output: `commands actually sent to anyone = 0`, `pending_intents after touch = 1`, `report outcome = Accepted`, `pending_intents after forged report = 0`, `intents.observed = Some(1)` — i.e. a member that was never a command target caused an intent to be recorded as fully `observed` although zero commands were ever sent to anyone.

  (b) Same-reporter double-count on a still-pending intent: joined two RemoteRenderer members (A, B) that both become exact-support targets (`commands sent = 2`). Only A ever reports; it resends a terminal `observed` report three times with distinct messageIds/causationIds all resolving to the same pending intent via `answers()`. B never reports, so the intent legitimately never finishes (`outstanding` still contains B after every call, `settled` stays false, the entry is never removed from `self.pending`). Yet each of A's three resends independently reaches `report_counter(status)` and bumps `intents.observed`, because that bump is unconditional on `terminal_report(status)` and is not gated on whether this particular report actually shrank `outstanding` or on the entry finally settling. Actual test output: `pending_intents after 3x resend from A only = 1` (intent is honestly still open — B never answered) alongside `intents.observed = Some(3)` — i.e. the same reporter, restating a report the host has already acted on, inflates the honesty-ladder "observed" counter three-for-one while the underlying intent is still unresolved. This is a materially worse violation of docs/aip/character-session.md:90 ("已結清的 intent 再收到 result → 忽略，不重複計數") than the doc's own wording anticipates, because the counter inflation here happens even *before* settlement, on every terminal report regardless of whether `retain` actually removed a new party.

  Code-level root cause for both: `settle_intent` (session.rs:867-899) takes `reporter: &Party` but only ever uses it inside `outstanding.retain(|party| party != reporter)` (session.rs:888) and in the audit line — it never verifies `reporter` was present in `outstanding` before the call, and never tracks which reporters have already counted toward a terminal status for this intent. `answers()` (session.rs:1536-1545) matches purely on a self-declared, non-secret causationId/correlationId (the toucher's own messageId, visible to it and observable by anyone), with no cross-check against `bound_identity`/`intent_targets`. File:line for the core defect matches the finding exactly (session.rs:867); no recent fix is present — the file content and line numbers at HEAD (commit a94e3d5, clean tree) are exactly as described in the finding.

  </details>

- **規格／誠實階梯（`spec-honesty`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `high`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Independently reproduced both mechanisms the finding describes, using freshly-written test code (not relying on any other agent's in-flight scratch files) against the current, unmodified `crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs`.

  1) Zero-target phantom settlement: joined a single InputDevice-role member ("intruder-1") that never negotiates RemoteRenderer, submitted a touch (0 commands sent anywhere, `intent_targets()` empty, `pending_intents().len()==1`), then submitted a validly-formed `result{status:"observed"}` from that same non-target device with `causationId` set to the toucher's own messageId ("m1" — trivially known to the party who crafted it). Result: `reported.outcome == Accepted`, `pending_intents()` empties (`settled:true` in the audit), and `intents.observed` becomes `Some(1)` — even though zero command envelopes were ever sent to any party. This exactly matches evidence item [3].

  2) Counter re-inflation by a single already-reporting member: joined two RemoteRenderer members A and B (both get a command for one intent), then had A alone send three separate `result{status:"observed"}` envelopes (unique messageIds, real `causationId` = the actual command sent to A) while B never reports. Result: `pending_intents().len()` stays `1` (correctly reflecting that B never answered) but `intents.observed` climbs to `Some(3)` — one bump per resend from the same already-processed reporter, not gated on `settled` or on whether this reporter had already been removed from `outstanding`. This exactly matches evidence item [2] and directly contradicts the written contract at `docs/aip/character-session.md` §5: "已結清的 intent 再收到 result → 忽略（不重複計數，重播灌不進計數器）" (the intent-level protection only covers the case where the whole intent was already fully settled and removed from `pending`; it does not cover a per-reporter duplicate on a still-open, multi-target intent).

  Root cause confirmed by reading the code: `PendingIntent::answers()` (session.rs:1536-1545) matches on `causationId`/`correlationId` alone, with no check that `reporter` is actually a member of `self.pending[index].outstanding`. `settle_intent()` (session.rs:867) calls `.outstanding.retain(|party| party != reporter)` (a no-op if reporter isn't/no-longer present) and then unconditionally calls `self.bump(counter)` for any terminal status match, regardless of whether `retain` actually changed anything or whether `settled` just flipped true for the first time. This is a real violation of the honesty-ladder invariant in CLAUDE.md ("觀察未知要標 uncertain，不得謊稱成功") and of the file's own doc comment ("誠實階梯不變：`observed` 是『對方說它演了』"): a party that was never asked to perform the behavior (case 1) or that already reported once (case 2) can inflate the `intents.observed`/`intents.rejected`/`intents.failed` counters and the `character.session.intent-settled` audit trail, which are the only record operators have (via the diagnostics endpoint) that a behavior intent was actually acted on by its real target(s).

  File:line citations in the finding are essentially exact against current HEAD: `settle_intent` at session.rs:867 (exact), the retain/settled/bump block at session.rs:888-894 (finding said 886-894, off by ~2 lines, immaterial), `answers()` at session.rs:1536-1545 (exact). The director.rs citation of "131" is a minor drift — current file has `correlation_id: event.correlation_id.clone()` at lines 137 and 156 — but this is a supporting detail, not the locus of the bug.

  I verified this is not a preexisting/covered-and-passing regression test: I wrote my own test file, ran it, saw the exploit succeed, then deleted the file, leaving the repo tree exactly as found (`git status --short` clean). This is a freshly-introduced surface (the whole `settle_intent`/pending-intent mechanism was added in the same commit currently at HEAD, 6683403), and nothing in the existing (permanent) test suite exercises reporter-identity checking on `settle_intent`, so the gap is real and currently unguarded.

  This affects audit/counter integrity (the honesty ladder around `observed`), not the Policy Governor's safety gates or physical actuation directly — `verified` status is still correctly blocked at gate step 8 regardless of this bug, and no actual command execution is fabricated. That scopes the impact to "the system can be made to lie about whether a behavior intent was actually observed by its real target," which is a genuine, spec-contradicting defect but stops short of a safety-critical bypass — consistent with the claimed "high" (not "blocker") severity.

  </details>

**Fix**

settle_intent 先確認 `outstanding.contains(reporter)`（或 `commands` 裡有一則是送給 reporter 的），不在名單內就只稽核、不計數、不結清；intent 沒有任何目標時根本不要進 pending（見另一則 finding）。

**Regression test**

In interaction-session's session tests: (1) join a single non-RemoteRenderer member, have it touch (0 commands ever sent since no online RemoteRenderer target exists), then submit a Result{status:observed} from that same member with causationId=touch's messageId — assert the submission is Rejected or otherwise ignored (pending_intents stays non-empty, intents.observed stays None) instead of settling; (2) join two RemoteRenderer targets A and B, touch to get 2 commands pending, have only A resend a terminal observed report 3x with distinct messageIds — assert intents.observed increases by at most 1 total (not once per resend) and that the pending intent remains open until B (the still-outstanding target) actually reports. Fix direction: settle_intent must (i) reject/ignore reports whose reporter was never in the intent's original target set, and (ii) track which reporters have already been credited for this intent's terminal status so re-reports from an already-credited reporter neither remove anything new from outstanding nor bump the counter again. / Two-part regression: (a) join only an InputDevice-role member (never RemoteRenderer), touch to create a pending intent with zero outstanding targets, submit a `result{status:"observed"}` from that same member with `causationId` set to the touch's own messageId, and assert the result is rejected (or at least that it must NOT settle the intent / bump `intents.observed`) since that party was never in `outstanding`; (b) join two RemoteRenderer targets A and B, have only A send three terminal `result{status:"observed"}` reports with distinct messageIds and a real `causationId`, and assert `intents.observed` stays at `Some(1)` (not `Some(3)`) while `pending_intents()` still shows the intent open because B never reported — i.e. `settle_intent` must gate the `retain`/`bump` on `reporter` actually being present in `outstanding` at call time, not merely on matching `causationId`/`correlationId`.

---

#### 持久化檔壞到救不回 epoch 時，quarantine 產生的新 epoch 會與原本的 epoch 相同（0+1=1），成員收不到 session-reset

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-capability-consent-051`
- **維度**：`capability-consent`
- **Severity**：`high`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs:97-107（salvaged_epoch 的既有單元測試現在在 1184-1187，非 finding 引用的 1113，屬行號漂移，其餘引用行號準確）`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

quarantine() 用 salvaged_epoch() 從壞檔救 epoch，救不到就當 0，回傳 `salvaged.saturating_add(1)` = 1（character_session.rs:97-107）。但全新 session 的 epoch 也是 1（character_session.rs:209 `Ok(None) => CharacterSession::new(config.clone(), 1, now)`），而 epoch 從未被 bump 過正是最常見的情況。於是「檔案內容被清成 NUL／截成空檔／read_to_string 直接失敗」這幾種壞法之後，新 session 的 epoch 仍是 1、revision 歸 1，成員記得的 epoch 也是 1 → resume 不會走 EpochMismatch，成員拿到的是普通 snapshot，再被自己的 rollback 防護忽略（同上一則 finding）。契約 §1 要求「host 每次重建 session 時 epoch+1」「找不到 snapshot 才 epoch+1」，這裡實際上沒有 +1。另外，load() 對任何非 NotFound 的 I/O 錯誤回 PortError::Unavailable（character_session.rs:161-166），open() 的 `Err(_)` 分支照樣 quarantine：一次暫時性讀取失敗（權限、EIO、fd 用盡）就會把一份完好的 snapshot 改名丟棄，且 rename 失敗時直接 `remove_file` 刪掉（character_session.rs:103-105）。

**Evidence**

repo 內既有測試已釘住救不回來的情形：character_session.rs:1113 `assert_eq!(salvaged_epoch("not json at all"), 0);`、`assert_eq!(salvaged_epoch(r#"{"epoch":"nine"}"#), 0);`。搭配 character_session.rs:106 `salvaged.saturating_add(1)` 與 209 行新 session 的 epoch=1，可得「壞檔後 epoch 仍為 1」。character_session.rs:100-105：`let quarantined = self.path.with_extension("json.corrupt"); if let Err(e) = std::fs::rename(...) { ...; let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&self.path); }`。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `high`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  逐段驗證，主張成立。

  1. `quarantine()`（character_session.rs:97-107）：`salvaged_epoch()` 救不到值時回 0（unwrap_or(0)），最終回傳 `0.saturating_add(1) = 1`。`salvaged_epoch` 本身在完全非 JSON、或欄位型別不對時就是回 0 —— repo 既有單元測試已經釘住：`assert_eq!(salvaged_epoch("not json at all"), 0)`、`assert_eq!(salvaged_epoch(r#"{"epoch":"nine"}"#), 0)`（目前在 character_session.rs:1186-1187，行號較 finding 引用的 1113 有漂移，但斷言內容一致）。

  2. 全新 session（從未有任何持久化檔）在 `open()` 的 `Ok(None)` 分支同樣把 epoch 設成字面值 1（character_session.rs:209）。而正常運作中 epoch 只在 `CharacterSession::new`／`restore` 設定，session 存活期間從不被 mutate（`crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs` 內 `epoch` 欄位只在建構時賦值），所以只要沒發生過任何一次損毀，epoch 會永遠停在 1——這正是最常見情形。

  3. 因此「損毀且完全救不回 epoch」與「從未損毀過的健康 session」兩種情況，quarantine 後算出的新 epoch 都是 1，會撞在一起。

  4. 下游影響也查證屬實：`CharacterSession::resume()`（interaction-session/src/session.rs:576-589）先比 `epoch != self.epoch`，相等就不會走 `EpochMismatch`；接著因為 quarantine 後的新 session revision 重置為 `INITIAL_REVISION = 1`（session.rs:196, 29），必然小於曾經有過互動、revision 已推進的成員記得的 `last_revision`，於是落入 `last_revision > self.revision` 分支只回傳普通 `Resume::Snapshot`（reason=None，非 `session-reset`）。

  5. 這個普通 snapshot 送到成員端後，成員用同一套純函式 `accept_state_with_epoch`（interaction-session/src/patch.rs:91-134）判斷：沒有 `reason:"session-reset"`，所以直接落入 `stale_or(revision=1, local_revision=<較大值>, ...)`，因為 `revision < local_revision` 得到 `Ordering::Less → IgnoreReason::Rollback`（patch.rs:140-149）——也就是成員會把這份用來修正真相的 snapshot 當成落後訊息直接忽略，永久卡在損毀前的舊狀態，且沒有任何錯誤或警示浮出。iOS client（SessionClient.swift:214-224）證實 client 確實把本地 `epoch` 存起來、在 resume 請求中原樣送回 `sessionEpoch`，所以上述串接是可重現的真實路徑，不是憑空推論。

  6. 契約面：`docs/aip/character-session.md:10` 明文「找不到 snapshot 才 epoch+1」，且 quarantine() 自己的 doc comment（character_session.rs:94-96）明講意圖是「不靜默：epoch+1 讓所有成員在 resume 時拿到 session-reset，而不是默默對齊」——這條路徑剛好違反了作者自己寫下的意圖。

  7. 既有回歸測試 `an_unreadable_session_file_is_quarantined_and_never_silently_reused`（character_session_loop.rs:1365-1390）刻意用 `"{\"epoch\": 7, this is not json"` 讓 salvage 抓到 7、驗證變 8，完全沒有覆蓋「salvage 徹底失敗且真實前一個 epoch 剛好是 1」這個最常見、也是最危險的分支，證實這是一個測試沒蓋到的真實 gap。

  次要主張（load() 對任何非 NotFound 的暫時性 I/O 錯誤都直接 quarantine 一份可能完好的檔案，且 rename 失敗會 remove_file 刪掉）核對 character_session.rs:158-169、210-217、100-105 後也屬實，但這是附帶點，不影響主張的核心成立與否。

  整體只有一處可忽略的行號漂移（測試斷言引用的 1113 現為 1184-1187），核心程式碼行號（97-107、209）與 finding 描述完全吻合，不影響 confirmed 判斷。

  </details>

- **規格／誠實階梯（`spec-honesty`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `high`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Traced the full mechanism end-to-end in the current source, not just the cited lines, and it holds exactly as claimed.

  1. `quarantine()` (character_session.rs:97-106): re-reads the file, tries `salvaged_epoch(body)`, defaults to 0 on any failure, returns `salvaged.saturating_add(1)`. Confirmed at the cited lines, no drift.
  2. `salvaged_epoch()` (character_session.rs:276-293) returns 0 for unparseable content or a non-numeric `epoch` field — pinned by the repo's own tests (`salvaged_epoch("not json at all")==0`, `salvaged_epoch(r#"{"epoch":"nine"}"#)==0`).
  3. A brand-new session (`Ok(None)` branch, character_session.rs:209) also starts at `epoch=1` via `CharacterSession::new(config, 1, now)`.
  4. So any quarantine event where the epoch can't be salvaged (empty file, NUL-filled file, garbled JSON with no recoverable `epoch` digits, or a transient I/O error on `load()` at character_session.rs:161-166 which also feeds `quarantine()`'s own re-read) yields the exact same epoch value (1) as a genuinely fresh session — provided the epoch had never previously been bumped, which is the case for any deployment's first-ever corruption event.
  5. Downstream this is not cosmetic: `CharacterSession::resume()` (interaction-session/src/session.rs:582-589) only returns `Resume::EpochMismatch` (which carries `reason:"session-reset"`) when `epoch != self.epoch`. With the collision, a reconnecting member whose remembered `sessionEpoch` is 1 passes this check silently.
  6. It then falls to `last_revision > self.revision` (session.rs:590) — since the fresh/rebuilt session's revision resets to `INITIAL_REVISION=1` (session.rs:29, 196) while a previously-active member's remembered `last_revision` is almost always >1, this returns `Resume::Snapshot` with `reason=None` (not session-reset).
  7. On the receiving side, the canonical decision function `accept_state_with_epoch()` (interaction-session/src/patch.rs:92-131), explicitly documented as the logic shared by the Rust host, TS desktop, and Swift app, only treats the message as `Reset` when `reason=="session-reset" && epoch>local_epoch` (patch.rs:117). Since neither holds here, it falls through to `stale_or(revision=1, local_revision=N, Apply)` (patch.rs:52, 140-149), which returns `Ignore{reason: Rollback}` whenever the fresh snapshot's revision is lower than the member's locally-cached revision — exactly matching the finding's claim that the reset gets silently swallowed by the rollback guard. (In the edge case `last_revision==1==self.revision`, it's even worse: `Resume::Patches{envelopes: vec![]}`, i.e., zero signal at all.)
  8. This isn't a hypothetical path: docs/aip/character-session.md §7 (reconnect flow) explicitly describes this exact scenario — step 2 says "host 可能已重啟", step 3 has the member resend its remembered `{lastRevision, lastSequence, sessionEpoch}`, and step 4 states the epoch-mismatch check is *the* mechanism for detecting a host rebuild ("epoch 不同 → 丟棄本地狀態、套用 snapshot"). The spec's §1 also says epoch bumps "host 每次重建 session 時 +1" unconditionally on rebuild — a rebuild via quarantine is exactly such an event, yet the epoch value produced can coincide with an already-used value because it's scraped from the very file that's corrupted, rather than tracked independently.
  9. Confirmed the secondary point too: `load()`'s `Err(_) => Err(PortError::Unavailable)` (character_session.rs:162, catching any non-NotFound I/O error, e.g. permission/EIO) feeds the same `Err(error)` arm in `open()` (line ~209-217) which unconditionally quarantines — including the `remove_file` fallback at lines 103-105 if rename fails — so a transient read failure on an intact file can discard it.
  10. No mitigating mechanism exists: there is no separate, more-durable epoch counter (grep for "meta" storage found none — epoch lives only inside the same JSON blob that can be destroyed), and no unconditional periodic full-refresh push independent of the resume protocol that would later correct a rollback-ignored member.

  This is a real spec/invariant violation (breaks the epoch-mismatch/session-reset guarantee the honesty ladder and §7 depend on), not a style preference, not already covered by an existing test (the existing test `a_truncated_session_file_still_gives_up_its_epoch` and `store_note_never_carries_error_details_or_paths` only cover partially-salvageable corruption, not full epoch loss), and not fixed by any later commit (character_session.rs's most recent touching commit, 6683403, doesn't address this).

  </details>

**Fix**

epoch 不要只靠壞檔自身：另存一個只含 epoch 的小 meta（或用 store meta），每次開 session 時 `epoch = max(salvaged, persisted_meta) + 1`；救不回來時退回單調來源（例如上次啟動時間戳）而不是 0。並把 PortError::Unavailable 與 Corrupt 分開處理：暫時性 I/O 錯誤應該中止啟動或重試，不該隔離一份可能完好的快照。

**Regression test**

寫入一份合法 snapshot（epoch=1、經過幾次互動使 revision>1），把檔案內容整份改寫成不含 "epoch" 字串的垃圾位元組（模擬 NUL/截斷/非 UTF-8），重開 runtime 後斷言新 epoch 不等於 1（或至少斷言：用舊 epoch=1、舊 revision 呼叫 `character_session_resume` 必須拿到 `Resume::EpochMismatch` 而非 `Resume::Snapshot`）。 / Seed `character-session.json` with a fully-corrupt/epoch-less body (e.g. all-NUL bytes or `"not json"`) after first bumping revision past 1 via normal saves at epoch=1, restart `CharacterSessionHost::open`, then call `session.resume(&party, last_revision=<pre-crash revision>, last_sequence, epoch=1, now)` and assert it returns `Resume::EpochMismatch`, not `Resume::Snapshot`/`Resume::Patches`.

#### 匯入的 builtin:shape 角色被冒名成內建 ref-shape：adapter 丟掉 ctx.manifest／ctx.characterId

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-character-package-017`
- **維度**：`character-package`
- **Severity**：`high`
- **file:line**：`apps/interaction-desktop/src/character/adapters/index.ts:118`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`shape` 在 host builtin 白名單裡（Rust `CHARACTER_BUILTIN_ENTRYPOINTS` 與 TS `BUILTIN_ADAPTER_IDS` 都有），所以第三方可以匯入一個 `entrypoint:{kind:"builtin",id:"shape"}` 的角色；但 shape 工廠只傳 `container`，完全忽略 `ctx.manifest` 與 `ctx.characterId`，而 `ShapeCharacterAdapter` 建構子硬寫 `buildShapeCharacterManifest()`。結果匯入角色在執行期以內建 Reference Character `ref-shape` 的身分、顯示名（「參考形狀」）與能力集運作，且其 per-character 偏好與 `ref-shape` 共用同一個 key ——違反「每個共享狀態只有一個 owner」與 manifest 是身分權威的契約。

**Evidence**

adapters/index.ts:118-127 `registerBuiltinAdapter("shape", (ctx) => { const adapter = new ShapeCharacterAdapter({ ...(ctx.textHost ? { container: ctx.textHost } : {}) }); ... })`（ctx.manifest／ctx.characterId 未使用）；shape.ts:189 `this.manifest = buildShapeCharacterManifest();`（characterId 固定 "ref-shape"）。對照 text adapter 有收：text.ts:51 `characterId: opts.characterId ?? "plain-text"`，index.ts:100-116 明確把 `ctx.characterId`／`ctx.manifest?.displayName` 傳進去。可達路徑：character_store.rs:227-249 只要 entrypoint 在 host 白名單（含 "shape"）就允許匯入；gatewayWiring.ts:190 `if (!isBuiltinEntrypointId(entry.entrypoint))` 只擋非白名單；CompanionApp.tsx:1015-1039 `planAdapter` 對 shape 走 `return { entrypoint, ctx }`（meta 無 variants／requiresLegacyPackShape），CompanionApp.tsx:907 `manifestRef.current = built.adapter.manifest` → :1181-1182 `setCharacterId(manifest.characterId)` → :1191-1196 `adapterReconfigureFor(..., characterId: manifest.characterId)` → gatewayWiring.ts:366-372 `characterPreferencesFor(prefs, characterId)` 以 `all[characterId]` 取偏好，即匯入角色讀寫 `companionPreferences["ref-shape"]`。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `high`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  全鏈路逐檔核對，主張成立：

  1. Rust 白名單確有 "shape"：crates/interaction-runtime/src/character.rs:54-55 `CHARACTER_BUILTIN_ENTRYPOINTS = [ShuRigPack::ENTRYPOINT_ID, "shape", "sprite", "text"]`；TS 白名單同樣有：apps/interaction-desktop/src/character/adapterRegistry.ts:26 `BUILTIN_ADAPTER_IDS = ["shu-rig", "sprite", "text", "shape"]`。

  2. 匯入檢查（character_store.rs:227-249 `validate_import`）只擋兩件事：`is_bundled(character_id)`（characterId 撞到內建 id，如 "ref-shape" 本身）與 entrypoint 不在白名單。第三方可以合法送出 `characterId: "acme-blob"`、`entrypoint: {kind:"builtin", id:"shape"}` 的 manifest 並通過匯入——沒有任何規則要求「entrypoint=shape 的 manifest 其 characterId 必須是 ref-shape」。

  3. adapters/index.ts:118-127（行號與證據完全吻合）的 shape 工廠只讀 `ctx.textHost`，完全忽略 `ctx.manifest`／`ctx.characterId`；對照同檔 text 工廠（100-116）明確把 `ctx.characterId`／`ctx.manifest?.displayName`／`ctx.manifest?.description` 傳入，證明這不是設計慣例、是 shape 特有的遺漏。

  4. shape.ts:110-189，`buildShapeCharacterManifest()` 硬寫 `characterId: "ref-shape"`、`displayName: {"zh-TW":"參考形狀", en:"Reference Shape"}`；建構子 `this.manifest = buildShapeCharacterManifest()`（189 行），完全不接受外部注入的 manifest。

  5. 可達性鏈路逐段核實：
     - CompanionApp.tsx `planAdapter`（source.kind === "imported" 分支）確實正確組出 `ctx.characterId = entry.characterId`（真實匯入 id）與 `ctx.manifest = manifest`（真實匯入 manifest）——上游沒錯，錯在 shape 工廠丟掉這兩個欄位。
     - CompanionApp.tsx:907 `manifestRef.current = built.adapter.manifest` → 拿到的是 shape adapter 內部硬寫的 ref-shape manifest。
     - CompanionApp.tsx:1181-1182 `setCharacterId(manifest.characterId)`／1191-1196 `adapterReconfigureFor(..., characterId: manifest.characterId)`，這裡的 `manifest.characterId` 永遠是 "ref-shape"。
     - gatewayWiring.ts:337-340 `charNameFor`：使用者未自訂名稱時，UI 顯示名回退到 `manifest.displayName`，即顯示「參考形狀」而非匯入角色的真實 displayName。
     - gatewayWiring.ts:366-372 `characterPreferencesFor` 以 `all[characterId]` 取值，因此匯入角色讀寫的是 `companionPreferences["ref-shape"]`，與內建 Reference Character 共用同一個偏好 key——違反「每個共享狀態只有一個 owner」。

  6. 未被既有測試覆蓋：adapter-contract.test.ts 只直接 `new ShapeCharacterAdapter(...)` 或用泛用 ctx 呼叫 `createBuiltinAdapter`，未模擬「匯入角色→CompanionApp.planAdapter→shape 工廠」這條路徑；character-ref-shape.test.ts 只測內建 ref-shape 本身；companion-imported-characters.test.ts 沒有涉及 shape entrypoint 的匯入案例。找不到任何測試會在此路徑上失敗來擋住這個問題。

  結論：這是真實可重現的身分冒名／共享狀態單一 owner 違規，非風格問題、非已修復、非已被測試覆蓋。行號與檔案內容跟 finding 描述完全一致，無漂移。severity high 合理（使用者可見顯示名被冒名、且偏好資料互相污染，屬 UI 完整性與資料隔離缺陷；不涉及安全繞過或資料外洩，故不到 blocker）。

  </details>

- **規格／誠實階梯（`spec-honesty`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `high`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  逐段對照原始碼，claim 的每一步都成立且行號未漂移：

  1. `character_store.rs` `validate_import` (第227-249行區間) 只在 `is_bundled(&character_id)` 為真時拒絕 —— 即只擋「characterId 剛好是 ref-shape」，並不擋「characterId 是別的、entrypoint 是 builtin:shape」的組合。`shape` 確實在 host 白名單裡：Rust `CHARACTER_BUILTIN_ENTRYPOINTS`（crates/interaction-runtime/src/character.rs:55 `[ShuRigPack::ENTRYPOINT_ID, "shape", "sprite", "text"]`）與 TS `BUILTIN_ADAPTER_IDS`（adapterRegistry.ts:26）都含 "shape"。既有測試 `importing_a_bundled_reference_character_id_is_refused` 只測了 characterId="ref-shape" 且 entrypoint 是預設的 "sprite"（manifest_json 測試 helper 預設值），完全沒有覆蓋「別的 characterId + entrypoint builtin:shape」這條路徑——不是已被測試覆蓋的防護。

  2. `adapters/index.ts:118-127` 的 "shape" 工廠函式確實只傳 `container` 給 `ShapeCharacterAdapter`，完全沒用到 `ctx.manifest`／`ctx.characterId`；對照同檔案 100-116 行的 "text" 工廠，明確把 `ctx.characterId`／`ctx.manifest?.displayName` 等傳進去，證明這是 "shape" adapter 特有的缺陷而非設計常態。`adapterRegistry.ts` 對 `BuiltinAdapterContext.manifest` 的註解「已驗證的 CPP manifest（有就優先用）」進一步佐證這是違反介面設計預期的行為。

  3. `shape.ts:189` `constructor` 無條件執行 `this.manifest = buildShapeCharacterManifest()`，該函式硬寫 `characterId: "ref-shape"`、displayName「參考形狀」，`ShapeAdapterOptions` 也根本沒有 manifest/characterId 欄位可覆寫。

  4. `CompanionApp.tsx` 的 `planAdapter`（source.kind === "imported" 分支，約 1010-1030 行）確實把匯入角色自己的 `manifest` 與 `entry.characterId` 放進 `ctx`，證明管線本意是要把匯入角色的真實身分傳下去，但 "shape" 工廠沒接住。之後 `manifestRef.current = built.adapter.manifest`（約 907 行）、`wireAdapter` 裡 `setCharacterId(manifest.characterId)`／`adapterReconfigureFor(..., characterId: manifest.characterId)`（約 1181-1196 行）一律用 `built.adapter.manifest`（也就是 ShapeCharacterAdapter 硬寫的 ref-shape manifest），不是 ctx 裡的匯入 manifest。

  5. `gatewayWiring.ts:366-372` `characterPreferencesFor` 用 `all[characterId]` 取偏好，親眼確認就是這行為，會撞到 `companionPreferences["ref-shape"]`。

  結果：一個第三方 manifest（characterId 不是 "ref-shape"、entrypoint 為 `{kind:"builtin", id:"shape"}`）能通過匯入驗證，但執行期會被靜默置換成內建 Reference Character 的身分、顯示名與能力宣告，且與內建角色共用同一份偏好 key。這確實違反「manifest 是身分權威」與「每個共享狀態只有一個 owner」的不變量，也不是風格偏好、未被其他機制覆蓋、也未被既有測試涵蓋。severity=high 合理：使用者匯入的角色會冒用內建角色身分並污染/被污染共享的偏好狀態，屬於身分完整性缺陷（雖非資安層級的越權，但確實是規格明確禁止的行為）。

  </details>

**Fix**

shape 工廠改成把 `ctx.manifest`／`ctx.characterId`／`ctx.displayName` 傳進 `ShapeCharacterAdapter`，adapter 以傳入的 manifest（沒有才退回 `buildShapeCharacterManifest()`）作為身分與協商來源；或在 registry 增設「這個 adapter 只服務自己的 bundled 角色」的 meta 旗標，讓匯入這類 entrypoint 時 `importedCharacterSource` 誠實 loadFailure，而不是靜默冒名。並補一個測試：匯入 characterId="evil-shape"／entrypoint shape 後，adapter.manifest.characterId 必須是 "evil-shape" 或載入失敗。

**Regression test**

匯入一個 manifest：`characterId: "acme-blob"`、`entrypoint: {kind:"builtin", id:"shape"}`、自訂 displayName；經 CompanionApp.planAdapter → buildAdapter 走完整流程後，斷言 `manifestRef.current.characterId === "acme-blob"`（而非 "ref-shape"）且顯示名不是「參考形狀」/"Reference Shape"，並斷言其偏好讀寫的 key 是 "acme-blob" 而非 "ref-shape"。 / 匯入一份 characterId="my-circle"、entrypoint={kind:"builtin",id:"shape"} 的合法 manifest 並開啟該角色，斷言 `manifestRef`/`characterId` 狀態與 `adapterReconfigureFor` 收到的 characterId 等於 "my-circle"（而非 "ref-shape"），且 `companionPreferences` 讀寫的 key 是 "my-circle"。

#### sprite adapter 的 suspend() 會對 host machine 送 force clear-transient，抹掉受保護的安全 transient

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-renderer-lifecycle-028`
- **維度**：`renderer-lifecycle`
- **Severity**：`high`（原報 `critical`，經 verifier 調整）
- **file:line**：`apps/interaction-desktop/src/character/adapters/sprite.ts:111-115`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

視窗一隱藏（document.hidden），SpriteCharacterAdapter.suspend() 就會透過注入的 MixerRenderer 對 CompanionApp 的共享 machine 送出 `{type:"clear-transient", force:true}`，把只有 estop clear-all 才准清的 blocked／failed／unknown／requesting-consent 一起清掉；緊接著的 presence beat 會把 `transient: null` 回報給 Runtime。呈現層因此拿到了連 AI 可達的 presentation cancel 都拿不到的 force 權限，違反「角色呈現層沒有權限主權」與「安全訊息只能加嚴」。10 個內建角色有 5 個（shu-standard／shu-agile／shu-lazy／shu-lively／shu-minimal）走 sprite entrypoint。

**Evidence**

鏈路逐段可驗：(1) CompanionApp.tsx:1361 `if (document.hidden) gateway.suspend(PRIMARY_INSTANCE_ID);`，1364 緊接 `beat();`（beat 回報 `transient: machineRef.current.transient?.kind ?? null`，CompanionApp.tsx:1357）。(2) gateway.ts:571 `suspend()` → `guarded(..., (a) => a.suspend())`。(3) sprite.ts:111-115 `suspend() { this.suspended = true; this.renderer?.setAnimation("idle"); this.renderer?.pause?.(); }`。(4) 正式接線注入的 renderer 是 MixerRenderer：character/adapters/index.ts:93-94 `const renderer = ctx.mixer ? new MixerRenderer(real, ctx.mixer) : real; ... new SpriteCharacterAdapter({ pack, assetBase, renderer, scale })`，而 CompanionApp.planAdapter 的 base ctx 永遠帶 `mixer`（CompanionApp.tsx:975-981）。(5) mixerRenderer.ts:18-20 `setAnimation(name){ this.mixer.apply(machineEventForAnimation(name, frameSlice)); }`。(6) machine.ts:230-236 `case "idle": return { type: "clear-transient", force: true }`。(7) machine.ts:278-286 `if (!event.force && t && ... CLEAR_PROTECTED_TRANSIENTS.has(t.kind)) return state;` — force 為 true 就直接 `transient: null`；CLEAR_PROTECTED_TRANSIENTS = blocked/failed/unknown/requesting-consent（machine.ts:152-157）。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `high`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Traced the full chain and it holds exactly as claimed, with no guard anywhere breaking it:

  1. sprite.ts:111-115 `suspend(): void { this.suspended = true; this.renderer?.setAnimation("idle"); this.renderer?.pause?.(); }` — unconditional, no check on current transient/state.
  2. adapters/index.ts:92-94 — the real (production) wiring for the "sprite" entrypoint always wraps the real SpriteRenderer in `new MixerRenderer(real, ctx.mixer)` whenever `ctx.mixer` is present, and it is: CompanionApp.tsx:975-981 `planAdapter`'s `base` ctx always includes `mixer` (the always-present `mixerPort`), so `SpriteCharacterAdapter` in the actual app never gets the bare SpriteRenderer — it gets the MixerRenderer facade.
  3. mixerRenderer.ts:18-20 `setAnimation(name){ this.mixer.apply(machineEventForAnimation(name, frameSlice)); }`.
  4. machine.ts:230-236, `case "idle": ... return { type: "clear-transient", force: true };` — with an explicit comment claiming this force path is reached "only" by Runtime-dispatched intent or host-cancel-of-the-pinned-command, never by AI-reachable `cancel` (which doesn't pass force). This assumption is violated by sprite.ts's suspend(), which is neither — it fires purely from `document.hidden` (a plain tab/window visibility change), completely independent of Runtime dispatch or any pinned-command cancel.
  5. machine.ts:152-157/278-286: `CLEAR_PROTECTED_TRANSIENTS = {blocked, failed, unknown, requesting-consent}`; the `clear-transient` reducer only checks these when `!event.force`, so `force:true` wipes them unconditionally — confirmed both by reading the reducer and by an *existing* test (companion-gateway-wiring.test.ts:363-368) that explicitly demonstrates `facade.setAnimation("blocked")` → transient stays `blocked` (protected), then `facade.setAnimation("idle")` → `st.transient` becomes `null`, i.e. the mechanism itself is real and already proven to defeat the "blocked" protection through exactly this facade.
  6. CompanionApp.tsx:1359-1364 `onVisibility`: `if (document.hidden) gateway.suspend(PRIMARY_INSTANCE_ID); ... beat();` — `beat()` reports `transient: machineRef.current.transient?.kind ?? null` (CompanionApp.tsx:1349-1351) to the Runtime via `presentationHello`, immediately after suspend, so the false "not blocked/not waiting" report is exactly what the finding describes.
  7. gateway.ts:551-560 `guarded()`/`suspend()` add no state-aware gating — they only catch adapter exceptions.
  8. Cross-check against the sibling adapter: shu.ts:471-474 `SpriteCharacterAdapter`'s counterpart, `ShuCharacterAdapter.suspend()`, only calls `this.stage?.pause()` — it never touches `setAnimation`/the machine at all. This shows the sprite adapter's behavior is an outlier/oversight, not a deliberately shared cross-adapter design, reinforcing that routing suspend's cosmetic "go to idle pose" through the machine's safety-relevant `idle` event was not intended to carry force-clear semantics for this trigger.
  9. Confirmed the "5 of 10 characters" count: manifests with `"kind":"character-pack"` (shu-standard, shu-agile, shu-lazy, shu-lively, shu-minimal) map via `legacyPackKinds:["character-pack"]` (adapters/index.ts:42) to the `sprite` entrypoint; shu-maid/-dusk/-sakura are `character-rig` → `shu` entrypoint (unaffected); ref-shape/plain-text are `builtin`.
  10. No existing test exercises this exact scenario (suspend() while a protected transient is active through the MixerRenderer-wired adapter) — the two `character-adapters.test.ts` suspend tests use a bare `SpriteRenderer`/`FakeRenderer`, not `MixerRenderer`, so they never touch the machine/clear-transient path; the "viaIntent" tests in the same file only cover `perform()`, not `suspend()`. This is a genuine, unguarded gap, not a protection already covered/verified by tests.

  Not already fixed — files read fresh, line numbers match exactly as cited (sprite.ts:111-115).

  Severity: I'd correct "critical" down to "high". This is a real presentation-layer authority/honesty violation (force-clearing all four protected safety transients — blocked/failed/unknown/requesting-consent — plus a false `transient:null` reported to the Runtime) triggered by the mundane, extremely common act of switching tabs/apps, and it is reachable with zero AI/malicious involvement. But per this project's own invariant ("安全由 Rust Policy Governor 確定性強制"), the actual policy enforcement lives in the backend independent of this presentation/machine state, so this doesn't defeat real safety enforcement (unlike a "critical"-tier bypass) — it's a dishonest presentation/telemetry report, the same class of issue this repo's own prior finding director-pipeline-044 (non-force clearing of `requesting-consent` alone) rated "medium." This one is strictly broader (force, hits all 4 kinds) and more trivially triggered, so "high" fits better than either "medium" or "critical."

  </details>

- **規格／誠實階梯（`spec-honesty`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `high`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  逐段驗證整條證據鏈，全部在目前檔案內容中成立，行號無漂移：
  (1) CompanionApp.tsx:1361 `if (document.hidden) gateway.suspend(PRIMARY_INSTANCE_ID);` 緊接 1364 `beat();`，beat 內 1350-1351 讀 `machineRef.current.transient?.kind ?? null` 送進 `api.presentationHello(...)`。
  (2) gateway.ts `suspend()`（guarded→`a.suspend()`)。
  (3) sprite.ts:111-115 `suspend(){ this.suspended=true; this.renderer?.setAnimation("idle"); this.renderer?.pause?.(); }`——完全無條件，不判斷目前是否有 active transient。
  (4) adapters/index.ts:93-94 正式接線 `sprite` entrypoint 一律用 `ctx.mixer ? new MixerRenderer(real, ctx.mixer) : real`，而 CompanionApp.planAdapter 的 base ctx（CompanionApp.tsx ~975-987）永遠帶 `mixer: mixerPort`，此 mixerPort（CompanionApp.tsx ~634-650）的 `apply` 是同步寫回 `machineRef.current = reduce(...)`。
  (5) mixerRenderer.ts `setAnimation` → `this.mixer.apply(machineEventForAnimation(name, frameSlice))`。
  (6) machine.ts:230-236 `case "idle": ... return { type: "clear-transient", force: true }`。
  (7) machine.ts:152-157 `CLEAR_PROTECTED_TRANSIENTS = {blocked, failed, unknown, requesting-consent}`；278-286 的 reducer：非 force 才擋，force===true 一律 `transient: null`。

  關鍵補強證據（finding 未提到，但強化其成立）：
  - ShuCharacterAdapter.suspend()（shu.ts:471-474）只 `this.stage?.pause()`，完全不碰 machine／transient——與 SpriteCharacterAdapter 的行為不對稱，顯示 sprite.ts 這行是實作疏漏而非刻意設計。
  - machine.ts:233-235 的註解自己宣稱這條 force 路徑「只有 Runtime 派送的 intent／host 對『正在盯的那則命令』的 cancel 會走到」——但 sprite.ts 的 `suspend()` 既不是 Runtime 派送的 intent，也不是 `cancel(messageId)` 那種「對正在盯的命令」的操作（sprite.ts 的 `cancel()` 有正確地只在 `this.active.messageId === messageId` 時才觸發，符合註解描述），而是純粹由 `document.hidden` 觸發、與目前 active 命令完全無關的第三條路徑。這條路徑正是該註解自身假設之外、繞過 CLEAR_PROTECTED_TRANSIENTS 保護的漏洞。
  - companion-gateway-wiring.test.ts:329/365-366 已經測試「MixerRenderer.setAnimation('idle') 會清掉 blocked transient」這件事本身是刻意且經測試的行為（source: 對抗審查 director-pipeline-022 的既有結論），但沒有任何測試涵蓋「suspend() 在有 blocked/requesting-consent 等保護態時是否也會觸發這條 force 路徑」——character-adapters.test.ts 兩個 suspend 測試（454、482 行）都只用自有 renderer／FakeRenderer，從未注入 MixerRenderer，未觸及本 finding 描述的路徑，確認這是未被測試覆蓋的真實缺口。
  - 5/10 內建角色（shu-standard/agile/lazy/lively/minimal）manifest.json 的 entrypoint 確認為 "sprite"，claim 的角色清單與計數精確無誤。

  實際影響評估（用以校正嚴重度）：machine.ts 的 `transient`／CLEAR_PROTECTED_TRANSIENTS 是 apps/interaction-desktop/src/companion 這層舊 sprite 相容層的本機視覺狀態機，並非 docs/aip 或 docs/character-protocol/README.md 契約中定義的概念（grep 兩份契約皆無 "transient" 字樣）；真正權威的 CPP truthState／verified、FIXED_SAFETY_LINES、Rust Policy Governor 完全不受影響。suspend() 清掉的只是本機呈現狀態機＋presence heartbeat 回報的 `transient` 欄位，且 resume()（sprite.ts:117-123）若 `lastPlayed` 未被覆蓋、TTL 未到期，會用 `renderer.setAnimation(lastPlayed.resolution.animation, frameSlice)` 把它重新送回去、視覺上會恢復。但只要視窗維持隱藏（`setInterval(beat, 10_000)` 在背景分頁仍會跑），Runtime 在整段隱藏期間都會被回報「沒有 transient」，即使當下其實是 blocked／requesting-consent——這確實違反了 machine.ts 自己設計並在前一輪對抗審查（director-pipeline-044）強化的「非 force 不可清安全 transient」不變量精神，也與 CLAUDE.md「呈現層沒有權限主權」「感測不靜默」的原則衝突：呈現層不應該有比 AI 可達的 cancel 更大的清除權限。因此判定為真實缺陷，但因（a）不影響 Runtime 端 truthState／verified 的權威來源、（b）resume 後多半自我修復、（c）觸發需要「該角色走 sprite entrypoint且此刻恰有保護態」的特定組合，我將嚴重度由原稱的 critical 下修為 high（真實、可驗證、可在正式接線中觸發，但非不可逆的權威安全繞過）。

  </details>

**Fix**

suspend() 不要用 setAnimation("idle") 當「停下來」的手段；停止呈現應該只走 renderer.pause()。若真的需要讓混音器知道 adapter 暫停，另開一個不帶 force、且不能清安全 transient 的事件（或由 host 而非 adapter 發），並在 MixerRenderer 層擋掉任何來自 adapter 的 `force:true`。

**Regression test**

In companion-gateway-wiring.test.ts style: build a SpriteCharacterAdapter with a `MixerRenderer(fakeRenderer, {apply: ev => st = reduce(st, ev, now)})`; drive `st` to `{kind:"blocked", untilMs: now+60000}` via `facade.setAnimation("blocked")`, then call `adapter.suspend()`; assert `st.transient?.kind` is still `"blocked"` (currently it becomes `null`). / 仿 companion-gateway-wiring.test.ts 的 MixerRenderer 測試：建立一台真的 `reduce` 驅動的 machine，用 MixerRenderer 包一個假 real renderer 注入 SpriteCharacterAdapter；先 `perform()` 一個 `envelope.intent="blocked"`（或 request-consent）讓 machine transient 進入 CLEAR_PROTECTED_TRANSIENTS 且 `untilMs` 尚未到期，再呼叫 `adapter.suspend()`；斷言 machine 的 `transient?.kind` 仍是 "blocked"（不是 null）——目前程式碼會讓它變成 null，此斷言應失敗，證明 suspend() 不應無條件送 force clear-transient。

---

#### MixerRenderer 不轉送 pause()／resume()，sprite 角色 suspend／hide 從未真的停掉 rAF

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-renderer-lifecycle-029`
- **維度**：`renderer-lifecycle`
- **Severity**：`high`
- **file:line**：`apps/interaction-desktop/src/companion/mixerRenderer.ts:12`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

MixerRenderer 只實作 setAnimation／setReducedMotion／setMicroMotion／destroy，沒有 pause()／resume()。RendererBackend 把 pause／resume 宣告成選用（`pause?()`），所以 TypeScript 不會報錯，而 sprite adapter 用的是 `this.renderer?.pause?.()` 這種選用呼叫 —— 正式接線下這兩行是純 no-op。結果是 CPP §7「看不見就不畫、不排 rAF」對 5 個內建 sprite 角色完全沒有生效：視窗隱藏（gateway.suspend）時真正的 SpriteRenderer rAF 迴圈照跑到底。同一段 hide() 的 `if (this.canvas) this.canvas.style.visibility = "hidden"` 也是死碼，因為工廠沒有把 canvas 傳給 SpriteCharacterAdapter（index.ts:94 只傳 pack/assetBase/renderer/scale），`this.canvas` 恆為 null。

**Evidence**

mixerRenderer.ts:12-33 全類別沒有 pause/resume；renderer.ts:64-75 `RendererBackend` 把 `pause?(): void; resume?(): void;` 宣告為選用；sprite.ts:104-123 `hide(){ ... this.renderer?.pause?.(); }` / `suspend(){ ... this.renderer?.pause?.(); }` / `resume(){ ... if (this.visible) this.renderer?.resume?.(); }`；index.ts:92-94 注入的是 MixerRenderer。既有測試給了假的信心：character-adapters.test.ts:482-495「注入的 renderer：suspend／hide → pause，resume／show → resume（可選方法）」用的是自帶 pause/resume 的 FakeRenderer（同檔 49-70），而 companion-gateway-wiring.test.ts:346-378 明確斷言門面只轉送 `["reduced:true", "micro", "destroy"]`，沒有任何 pause/resume 覆蓋。CompanionApp 也沒有別的地方對 rendererRef 呼叫 pause（grep `\.pause` 只命中 audio context 與 api.pauseSet）。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `high`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  親自讀碼＋寫最小回歸測試證實兩個子論點都成立，且行號與敘述精準：(1) mixerRenderer.ts:12-33 的 MixerRenderer 類別確實只轉送 setAnimation(→machine event)/setReducedMotion/setMicroMotion/destroy，沒有 pause/resume 方法；renderer.ts:64-75 的 RendererBackend 把 pause?/resume? 宣告為選用，讓 TS 對 `this.renderer?.pause?.()` 這種呼叫完全不報錯。(2) 生產接線（index.ts:89-94）對 5 個內建 sprite 角色（shu-agile/shu-lazy/shu-lively/shu-minimal/shu-standard，kind=character-pack）一律以 `ctx.mixer ? new MixerRenderer(real, ctx.mixer) : real` 建 renderer，而 CompanionApp.tsx 的 planAdapter()（980-989 行）對每個 entrypoint 都無條件塞 `mixer`，所以正式路徑下 adapter 內部持有的一定是 MixerRenderer facade，不是 real SpriteRenderer。CompanionApp 的 visibilitychange handler（1359-1362 行）只呼叫 `gateway.suspend/resume(PRIMARY_INSTANCE_ID)`，這會路由到 gateway.ts:572-577 → adapter.suspend()/resume()（sprite.ts 104-123 行）→ `this.renderer?.pause?.()`；因為 this.renderer 是沒有 pause 方法的 MixerRenderer，這是純 no-op。CompanionApp 對 rendererRef.current（=真正的 SpriteRenderer，index.ts 回傳的 `renderer: real`）自己從未直接呼叫 pause/resume（grep 全檔案只有 setAnimation/setReducedMotion/setMicroMotion/destroy/blinkNow）。

  我寫了一支最小回歸測試，完全比照 index.ts 的生產接線（real SpriteRenderer → 包成 MixerRenderer → 注入 SpriteCharacterAdapter，不用測試裡才有的 FakeRenderer），跑完後刪除：載入圖片後 rAF pending=1；呼叫 a.suspend() 與 a.hide() 後 real.isPaused() 仍是 false、pending 仍是 1；再往前 tick 兩次，draws 從 1 變 3——證明真正的 rAF 迴圈完全沒被停掉，會無限跑下去。這與 shu-rig adapter（shu.ts:464-478）形成對照：shu-rig 的 hide/suspend 直接呼叫 `this.stage?.pause()`（stage 就是真正的 StageRenderer，沒有中間 facade），確實停 rAF——證明 sprite 路徑的失效不是設計如此，而是 MixerRenderer 這層 facade 漏轉送。

  既有測試給假信心的說法也查證屬實：character-adapters.test.ts:482-495『注入的 renderer：suspend／hide → pause…』用的是自帶 pause/resume 的 FakeRenderer（449-470 行），companion-gateway-wiring.test.ts:346-378 的 MixerRenderer 測試明確斷言真正的 renderer 只收到 `["reduced:true","micro","destroy"]`（連 pause 都没測，因為 MixerRenderer 根本沒有這個方法可呼叫）；character-adapters.test.ts 裡唯一真正驗證 SpriteRenderer pause/destroy 生命週期的測試（同檔標題含『CPP §7』的 describe block）用的是 `new SpriteCharacterAdapter({..., canvas: h.canvas})`（沒有注入 renderer，adapter 自己建 SpriteRenderer，完全不經過 MixerRenderer）——生產接線唯一會用到的『注入 MixerRenderer』組合，沒有任何測試覆蓋。

  canvas 死碼子論點也查證屬實：index.ts:94 的 `new SpriteCharacterAdapter({ pack, assetBase, renderer, scale })` 確實沒有傳 canvas 欄位，SpriteAdapterOptions.canvas 為 undefined → constructor 裡 `this.canvas = opts.canvas ?? null` 恆為 null，sprite.ts hide()/show() 裡 `if (this.canvas) this.canvas.style.visibility = ...` 恆不執行。

  CPP 文件 §7（README.md:268-291）沒有逐字寫『看不見就不畫、不排 rAF』，這句話是程式碼註解自己標的設計意圖（sprite.ts 對應行、mixerRenderer.ts 檔頭註解都寫明『真正的畫面由 host 的 syncPose 依 pose() 驅動』），但 §7 crash/斷線那條確實把『釋放 rAF』列為必要行為，且 shu-rig 對稱路徑證明這是全平台期望的行為，不是過度延伸。

  結論：這是真實、可重現、生產路徑會觸發的缺陷——5 個內建 sprite 角色在視窗隱藏或 adapter suspend 時，畫面停止渲染但底層 rAF 迴圈仍以裝置更新率無限跑下去（每個 rAF tick 仍持續依 fps 節流 drawImage），持續耗用 CPU/GPU 直到 destroy()。severity 定為 high 合理：不是資料損毀或安全繞過（故不到 blocker），但確實是背景資源洩漏型效能缺陷，且被測試套件的假信心掩蓋，值得判定為 high。

  </details>

- **規格／誠實階梯（`spec-honesty`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `high`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  親自逐行核對，claim 的每一項技術細節都成立，找不到反駁點：

  1. mixerRenderer.ts:12-33 — `MixerRenderer` 只實作 `setAnimation`（轉給 mixer.apply）、`setReducedMotion`、`setMicroMotion`、`destroy`（轉給 `this.real`），全類別確實沒有 `pause`/`resume`。

  2. renderer.ts:70-74 — `RendererBackend` 介面把 `pause?(): void; resume?(): void;` 宣告為選用，所以 `MixerRenderer implements RendererBackend` 編譯不會報缺方法。介面上的註解「可選——不是每個後端都有自己的迴圈（注入的假 renderer 可以省略）」講的是「沒有迴圈的假 renderer 可以省略」，但 `MixerRenderer` 包的 `real` 恰好是「有真迴圈」的 `SpriteRenderer`，屬於本該轉送卻沒轉送的情況。

  3. sprite.ts：`hide()`(107-108)／`suspend()`(112-114)／`resume()`(117-122)／`show()`(99-101) 都用 `this.renderer?.pause?.()` / `this.renderer?.resume?.()` 這種雙重選用呼叫；當 `this.renderer` 是 `MixerRenderer` 時 `.pause`/`.resume` 是 `undefined`，`?.()` 靜默跳過，是真正的 no-op。

  4. index.ts:89-96（sprite 工廠）：`const real = new SpriteRenderer(...)`；`const renderer = ctx.mixer ? new MixerRenderer(real, ctx.mixer) : real;`；`new SpriteCharacterAdapter({ pack, assetBase, renderer, scale })` —— 確認建構參數沒有傳 `canvas`，所以 `this.canvas` 恆為 `null`（sprite.ts:65），`show()/hide()` 裡 `if (this.canvas) …visibility…` 確實是死碼。

  5. CompanionApp.tsx:980-987（`planAdapter`）：`base` context 無條件帶 `mixer: mixerPort`，不分角色種類；因此正式路徑下 sprite 角色一定走 `new MixerRenderer(real, ctx.mixer)` 分支，`real` 分支在生產環境不會被觸發。

  6. renderer.ts 裡 `SpriteRenderer.pause()/resume()`（149-162）才是真正取消/恢復 `requestAnimationFrame` 的地方；`loop()`（181-）內部沒有任何 `document.hidden` 或可見性檢查，也沒有其他機制會停止已排程的 rAF。`dispose()`（sprite.ts:184-190）因為 `ownsRenderer = !opts.renderer = false`（工廠總是傳入 renderer），也不會呼叫 `renderer.destroy()`；真正的 `destroy()` 只由 CompanionApp 在整個元件卸載／換角色時直接呼叫 `rendererRef.current?.destroy()`（CompanionApp.tsx:965-966、1083-1084），完全繞過 adapter 的 pause/resume 語意，且全程搜尋不到任何地方對 `rendererRef` 呼叫 `.pause()`。

  7. CompanionApp.tsx:1361 `if (document.hidden) gateway.suspend(PRIMARY_INSTANCE_ID);` 是唯一會觸發 `suspend()` 的生產路徑，之後 rAF 迴圈在視窗被隱藏期間持續執行到下一次 `document.hidden` 翻轉或元件卸載，並非布局／CSS 面的問題。

  8. 兩份測試引用皆屬實：character-adapters.test.ts:481-495（"注入的 renderer：suspend／hide → pause..."）用的是自帶 `pause()/resume()` 的 `FakeRenderer`（同檔 49-70），繞過了 `MixerRenderer`，給出的是 adapter 語意層的假信心，並未覆蓋工廠實際組裝出的 `MixerRenderer` 轉送路徑；companion-gateway-wiring.test.ts:346-373 則明確斷言只有 `["reduced:true", "micro", "destroy"]` 會轉給真正的 renderer，等於已經用測試把「pause/resume 不轉送」的現況釘死成"預期行為"，但這與同一介面上「CPP §7 hide／suspend：停掉 rAF」的設計意圖矛盾——是遺留的組裝缺陷而非刻意設計。

  9. 影響範圍：`public/packs/` 下確有 5 個 `character-pack`（sprite）內建角色（shu-standard／shu-lively／shu-maid／shu-maid-sakura／shu-maid-dusk），全部走同一個 sprite 工廠，claim「5 個內建 sprite 角色」屬實。

  CPP README 第 7 節文字本身只在斷線／crash／goodbye 明文要求「釋放…rAF」，「看不見就不畫、不排 rAF」的措辭是程式碼註解對 hide/suspend 生命週期的內部工程約定，不是 README 逐字條款，但該約定同時寫在 `RendererBackend.pause?`（renderer.ts:71）與 `SpriteRenderer.pause()`（renderer.ts:145）兩處註解裡，是這個子系統自訂並自我宣告的契約，工廠組裝卻沒有兌現，屬實際、可重現的資源管理缺陷（視窗隱藏／suspend 期間 60fps canvas rAF 迴圈跑到底），不是風格偏好，也沒有被其他機制覆蓋或已修復。維持原報告的 high 嚴重度。

  </details>

**Fix**

MixerRenderer 補上 `pause(){ this.real.pause?.(); }` 與 `resume(){ this.real.resume?.(); }`；並讓 sprite 工廠把 canvas 一併傳給 SpriteCharacterAdapter，或把可見性交給 host。測試面補一個「注入 MixerRenderer 時 suspend 真的停掉底層 rAF」的案例。

**Regression test**

比照 index.ts 的 sprite 工廠接線——construct real SpriteRenderer、用 `new MixerRenderer(real, mixerStub)` 包一層、把這個 facade 當 `renderer` 注入 `new SpriteCharacterAdapter({pack, assetBase, renderer})`（不要用自帶 pause/resume 的 FakeRenderer，也不要省略 renderer 讓 adapter 自建）；載入 sheet 後呼叫 `adapter.suspend()`／`adapter.hide()`，斷言 `real.isPaused() === true` 且 rAF pending 為 0（目前會失敗：仍是 false／1，並且再 tick 仍持續 drawImage）。 / 用 index.ts 的真正工廠路徑（`registerBuiltinAdapter("sprite", ...)` 而非直接 new SpriteCharacterAdapter 注入 FakeRenderer）建出 adapter，對 mixer 注入的 real SpriteRenderer spy 其 `requestAnimationFrame`／`pause`，呼叫 `gateway.suspend(id)` 後斷言 real renderer 的 `pause()` 被呼叫且不再排程下一個 rAF frame。
