#### capability 訊息會把該成員的 rate-limit token bucket 重置成滿桶，成員可自行解除 §8 第 10 關的速率上限

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-identity-binding-005`
- **維度**：`identity-binding`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:335`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`join()` 在既有成員分支執行 `entry.bucket = RateBucket::new(self.config.rate_limit_per_sec, now)`（session.rs:335），而 `RateBucket::new` 直接把 tokens 設成滿容量（session.rs:1578-1583）。`capability` 本身是成員可送的 message type，經 gate 第 10 關只消耗 1 個 token，接著 `handle_capability` → `join` 就把桶子補滿。因此成員只要在訊息流中插入 capability，就能無視 `rate_limit_per_sec`：每插一則 capability 就多拿 `rate_limit_per_sec` 則額度，時間完全不用經過。

**Evidence**

session.rs:772-777 gate 第 10 關：`if !self.members[index].bucket.take(now, self.config.rate_limit_per_sec) { return Err(GateFailure::rejected(ErrorCode::RateLimited)); }`；session.rs:1578-1583 `fn new(per_sec, now) -> Self { Self { tokens: f64::from(per_sec.max(1)), last: now } }`（滿桶）；session.rs:1112-1118 `handle_capability` 對成員一律呼叫 `self.join(bound_identity.clone(), &announcement, now)`，進入既有成員分支重建 bucket。現有測試 `rate_limit_is_a_token_bucket_on_injected_time`（tests/session.rs:444-480）只送 touch，沒有測 capability 交錯，所以這條旁路未被鎖住。iPhone transport 另有 `MOBILE_MAX_INBOUND_PER_SEC = 30`（mobile.rs:92）剛好等於預設 `rate_limit_per_sec = 30`（types.rs:77），所以預設值下 transport 仍擋得住；但只要 host 把 session 速率調嚴（`normalized()` 允許低到 1，types.rs:93），這個上限就名存實亡，而且 HTTP 的 `/v1/character-session/events` 路徑沒有任何 transport 層速率上限。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Verified every element of the claim against the current file content (no drift) and, more importantly, empirically reproduced the bypass:

  1. crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:335 — `join()`'s existing-member branch: `entry.bucket = RateBucket::new(self.config.rate_limit_per_sec, now);` — confirmed verbatim, correct line.
  2. session.rs:1578-1583 — `RateBucket::new` sets `tokens: f64::from(per_sec.max(1))`, i.e. full capacity, not a partial/prorated refill. Confirmed.
  3. session.rs gate step 10 (currently lines 771-777) — every inbound envelope, `capability` included, consumes exactly 1 token via `self.members[index].bucket.take(now, self.config.rate_limit_per_sec)` before dispatch. `MessageType::Capability` is listed among gate-eligible types at line ~768. Confirmed.
  4. session.rs:1037-1054 `handle_capability` calls `self.join(bound_identity.clone(), &announcement, now)` for a bound (already-connected) identity, landing in the existing-member branch that resets the bucket. Confirmed.
  5. `negotiate_capabilities` (crates/interaction-aip/src/capability.rs:95) is a pure/stateless function with no idempotency or replay guard — the same `CapabilityAnnouncement` payload can be resent indefinitely and will renegotiate successfully every time, so an attacker doesn't need new capability content, just resends. Confirmed.
  6. Wrote a minimal reproduction test in crates/interaction-session/tests/session.rs (then reverted — repo left clean, `git status --porcelain` empty) that: configures `rate_limit_per_sec: 2`, joins one member, then at a SINGLE fixed timestamp (zero elapsed time) alternates `touch` events with `capability` resends. Result: all 10 touch events were `Outcome::Applied` and all 10 capability resends were also `Outcome::Applied`, at zero elapsed time, against a 2/sec configured limit — i.e., unbounded throughput at a single instant. This is a direct, working exploit of the exact mechanism the finding describes, not merely a theoretical code-reading concern.
  7. Supporting evidence also checks out: mobile.rs:92 `MOBILE_MAX_INBOUND_PER_SEC = 30`; types.rs:77 default `rate_limit_per_sec: 30`; types.rs:93 `normalized()` clamps `rate_limit_per_sec` to `[1, 1_000]` so a host can legitimately configure a much stricter per-second cap than the mobile transport's fixed 30/sec pre-filter, at which point this session-level bypass becomes the only limiter — and it's broken. The HTTP route `POST /v1/character-session/events` (routes.rs:1900) forwards straight to `character_session_submit` with no visible additional transport-level throttle, so for HTTP-originated members this in-session bucket is the sole rate limiter.

  Severity: the finding's "medium" rating is reasonable — it's not a full authentication/consent bypass (identity binding, gate steps 1-9 and 11-13 all still apply; a member must already be a validly-bound member of the session to exploit it), but it does let an already-joined member completely defeat the per-member rate limiter (§8 gate step 10) via a cheap, repeatable, stateless message type, enabling unbounded message/event throughput from a single member — a real DoS/resource-exhaustion and fairness violation against the stated invariant. No existing test (`rate_limit_is_a_token_bucket_on_injected_time`, tests/session.rs:445) exercises capability interleaving, so this path is genuinely uncovered.

  </details>

**Fix**

重新協商不要重置 bucket（保留既有 `entry.bucket`），或只在「第一次加入」時建立 bucket；若真的需要在重連時放寬，改成明確的、有界的補償量而不是整桶重填，並補測試：capability 交錯不得提高每秒可送訊息數。

**Regression test**

In a session configured with a small rate_limit_per_sec (e.g. 2), join one member, then at a single fixed timestamp alternate `touch` events with `character.session.capability` resends carrying the member's existing announcement; assert that once the count of Applied touches exceeds `rate_limit_per_sec` at that same instant (zero elapsed time), the test fails — i.e. add an assertion that a member cannot get more than `rate_limit_per_sec` applied non-capability messages through per second regardless of interleaved capability resends (e.g. by having `join()`'s existing-member branch top up the bucket via the normal `take`/elapsed-time accrual path, or cap the refill at the member's current token level, rather than unconditionally rebuilding it via `RateBucket::new`).

#### iPhone transport 的身分不符稽核與錯誤 envelope 直接回顯未驗證、無長度上限的攻擊者可控字串

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-identity-binding-006`
- **維度**：`identity-binding`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs:704`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`character_session_device_frame` 在身分綁定失敗時，把**沒有經過 `Envelope::validate()`** 的 `envelope.source.kind` 與 `envelope.name` 原封不動寫進 audit（character_session.rs:704-713），並把未消毒的 `envelope.message_id` 當成 `causationId` 塞進回送的 error envelope（:690、:698、:717）。`interaction-session` 內部特地用 `safe_party()`／`safe_name()`／`safe_id()` 做這件事（session.rs:1664-1691），transport 綁定層卻整個繞過去。結果：(a) 一台已配對的 iPhone 每則訊息可把最大約 64 KiB 的任意字串寫進 audit（audit 無截斷、無保留期），(b) 送回去的 error envelope 自己就違反 AIP §1／§11（`causationId` 超過 `MAX_ID_CHARS = 128`、可含控制字元／空白），`Envelope::validate()` 會拒絕它。

**Evidence**

character_session.rs:628-720 的流程只呼叫 `Envelope::parse`（envelope.rs:110-122，只量 `MAX_MESSAGE_BYTES = 65536`，明確註明「不做 profile 驗證」）、`negotiate_version`、`message_type.is_known()`，之後就到 :703 的 `bind_identity`；`envelope.validate()`（會做 `check_id(messageId)`、`is_valid_name(name)`、`check_payload`）在這條路徑上從未被呼叫。:707-712 的 audit detail 直接寫 `"claimedKind": envelope.source.kind`（`PartyKind::Unknown(String)` 是 `#[serde(untagged)]`，message.rs:86-88，會原樣序列化任意字串）與 `"name": envelope.name`。:717 `Some(envelope.message_id.clone())` 未過同一函式開頭 :635-644 已經寫好的消毒器（`filter(|id| ... <= MAX_ID_CHARS && !control && !whitespace)`）——該消毒版本 `causation` 只用在 parse 之前的三個分支。回覆送出時走 mobile.rs:3572-3575 `send(&out_tx, json!({"type":"aip","envelope": reply}))`，不經 `send_aip` 的 `envelope.encode()` 上限檢查，也不經 `validate()`。audit 寫入端 interaction-storage/src/lib.rs:969-977 直接 `detail.to_string()` 存進 SQLite，全檔沒有任何 `DELETE FROM audit`／截斷。對照：tests/session.rs:68-80 `assert_outputs_valid` 要求每一則上線 envelope 都要 `validate()` 通過——這條 transport 路徑產出的 envelope 不在該斷言範圍內。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  逐行核對 crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs 704-720（行號與原始 finding 完全吻合，無漂移）：函式開頭 635-644 已經建好一個經消毒的 `causation`（長度 ≤128、無控制/空白字元），但 identity-mismatch 分支（690/698/717）改用未過該消毒器的 `envelope.message_id.clone()` 直接塞進 `character_session_error(...)` 當 causationId；`character_session_error`（1015-1033）只呼叫 `.with_causation(id)`（envelope.rs:89，純字串賦值，不驗證），從未呼叫 `Envelope::validate()`。`Envelope::parse`（envelope.rs:112-122）明確只量 65536 bytes、不做 profile 驗證，因此 message_id／name／source.kind 在到達 identity-mismatch 分支前完全未經 check_id／is_valid_name 檢查。704-712 的 audit 呼叫把 `envelope.source.kind`（PartyKind 的 Unknown 變體以 variant-level `#[serde(untagged)]` 保留原字串，message.rs:83-88）與 `envelope.name` 原封不動寫入，interaction-storage/src/lib.rs:969-977 的 `audit()` 直接 `detail.to_string()` 存 SQLite，repo 內搜尋不到任何 `DELETE FROM audit`／截斷／保留期邏輯。回覆送出路徑 mobile.rs:3572-3574 用的是本地閉包 `send`（3244-3251，只做 `v.to_string()` 後 `send_timeout`），完全繞過 `send_aip()`（1207-1224）的 `envelope.encode()` 65536-byte 上限檢查與 validate。對照組：interaction-session/src/session.rs 的 safe_id／safe_name／safe_party（1664-1690）在同一情境（1331 等）一律先消毒再塞 causation／audit，證明這套紀律在別處存在、只是 transport 層 character_session.rs 沒套用。既有測試 forged_identity_and_out_of_scope_frames_are_refused（character_session_loop.rs:806-889）只用短字串（"iphone-someone-else"、"runtime"）偽造身分，從未餵過超長／含控制字元的 messageId 或任意 source.kind 字串，也沒有對回覆 envelope 呼叫 validate()；interaction-session/tests/session.rs 的 assert_outputs_valid（68-80）只涵蓋 interaction-session crate 自己的 Output，不涵蓋這條 runtime 端 transport 路徑。所有機制、行號、常數（MAX_MESSAGE_BYTES=65536、MAX_ID_CHARS=128）均與 finding 描述精確吻合，且要求攻擊者已是配對過的合法裝置（一致地被歸類為 medium 而非 high/critical）。

  </details>

**Fix**

在 `character_session_device_frame` 於 `Envelope::parse` 成功後、任何 audit／回覆之前先呼叫 `envelope.validate()`（失敗即回固定錯誤碼），並讓所有 audit／causation 一律走 `safe_id`／`safe_name`／`safe_party` 等價的消毒（或把這些 helper 從 interaction-session 提升成 interaction-aip 的公開函式共用）。回送前對 envelope 再做一次 `validate()`／`encode()`，送不出去就誠實丟棄並記固定稽核。

**Regression test**

在 character_session_loop.rs 的 forged_identity 測試中送一則 source.kind 為 'x'.repeat(60000)（或含控制字元）且 messageId 亦超過 128 字元／含空白的 envelope，斷言 (a) 回傳的 error envelope 本身能通過 Envelope::validate()（目前會因 causationId 超長/含非法字元而失敗），(b) audit_tail 裡對應那筆 aip.identity-mismatch 的 detail 中 claimedKind／name 長度被截斷在合理上限內。

#### 遠端成員自報的 role 未經任何驗證即被採信，一台 iPhone 宣稱 host-renderer 就能讓一般模式顯示「已同步」，但它永遠收不到任何 Behavior Intent

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-identity-binding-007`
- **維度**：`identity-binding`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-aip/src/capability.rs:100`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`negotiate_capabilities` 對 `role` 完全不做交集或與 Transport 綁定身分的相容性檢查——`let role = announcement.role.unwrap_or(MemberRole::Observer);`（capability.rs:100）原樣採信。因此一台 `kind: device` 的已配對 iPhone 可以宣稱自己是 `host-renderer`（AIP §4.2 詞彙中屬於可信桌面 host 的角色）。該 role 會被寫進權威狀態 `SemanticState.members[].role`（session.rs:330／340，state.rs:217-222），廣播給所有成員並投影到控制中心；而 `intent_targets()`（session.rs:1375-1385）只把 Behavior Intent 送給 `MemberRole::RemoteRenderer`。結果是：桌面一般模式顯示「iPhone 已連接，角色狀態已同步」，但 Runtime 一則 intent 也不會送給那台手機——由裝置自報的資料決定了誠實文案。

**Evidence**

capability.rs:100 `let role = announcement.role.unwrap_or(MemberRole::Observer);`（協商演算法對 intents／inputs／syncClass 都做交集，只有 role 例外）。session.rs:1375-1385 `intent_targets`：`entry.member.role == MemberRole::RemoteRenderer && presence == Online && negotiated.intents.get(intent) == Some(&IntentSupport::Exact)`。apps/interaction-desktop/src/statusProjection.ts:962 `canPresent: item["role"] === "remote-renderer" || item["role"] === "host-renderer"`；:1036-1050 `const online = remote.filter(m => m.presence === "online"); if (online.length > 0) return online.every(m => m.canPresent) ? project("synced") : project("partial-capability");`；:807-811 `synced` 文案為「iPhone 已連接，角色狀態已同步」。同一段程式也顯示 §11 的「協商後有 unsupported intent →『部分能力目前不可用』」這一列根本無法被滿足：`MemberView`（state.rs:217-222）只帶 `party／role／presence／lastSeenAt`，協商出來的 `intents` 支援表只留在 host 私有的 `Member.negotiated`（state.rs:225-233，註解明寫「不進 SemanticState」），UI 拿不到，`CharacterSyncSignals`（statusProjection.ts:881-896）也沒有任何 unsupported-intent 欄位——`partial-capability` 目前唯一的來源就是這個自報 role。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  All cited evidence checks out verbatim against the current source, and I traced the full chain end-to-end myself:

  1. capability.rs:100 `let role = announcement.role.unwrap_or(MemberRole::Observer);` — confirmed exact line/text. `negotiate_capabilities` intersects/mins spec version, sync_class, intents, inputs, and limits, but `role` is taken from the remote announcement with zero cross-check against anything (not against `Party.kind`, not against transport identity). Grepped every `MemberRole` usage in the whole repo (`rg -n "MemberRole" --type rust .`) — the only place role is ever validated against anything is the single filter in `intent_targets` (`role == MemberRole::RemoteRenderer`); there is no code anywhere that restricts which role a given `PartyKind` (e.g. `Device`) may declare.

  2. Traced the real iPhone transport path: `crates/interaction-runtime/src/mobile.rs` → `character_session_device_frame` (character_session.rs:625) binds `party = Party::device(device_id)` and calls `bind_identity(&party, &envelope.source)` — but per `docs/aip/README.md` §5, that check only binds the envelope's `source` (party kind/id) to the transport-authenticated device identity. It says nothing about, and does nothing to, the `role` field inside the `capability` payload, which is deserialized directly from `envelope.payload` in `character_session_device_capability` (character_session.rs:801-812) and passed straight to `session.join()`. So a paired iPhone (bound identity `device:<id>`) can send `capability{role:"host-renderer", ...}` and it passes identity binding cleanly while smuggling an unverified role.

  3. session.rs:330/340 (`entry.member.role = negotiated.role` / `role: negotiated.role` in the new-member branch) — confirmed exact, this negotiated role is what's stored.

  4. state.rs:217-222 `MemberView{party,role,presence,last_seen_at}` (doc comment explicitly: "不含協商結果——那是 host 私有") and state.rs:225-233 `Member{...,negotiated}` with comment "不進 SemanticState" — confirmed exact line numbers and content. `project_members()` (session.rs:1145) builds `SemanticState.members` purely from this stripped `MemberView`, so the self-reported `role` reaches the shared/broadcast state but the negotiated per-intent support table never does. Diagnostics (session.rs:155-163, `Diagnostics.members: Vec<MemberView>`) confirmed to carry the same stripped shape — so even the diagnostics endpoint never exposes unsupported-intent data.

  5. `intent_targets` (session.rs:1375-1385) confirmed to filter strictly on `MemberRole::RemoteRenderer` — a member with self-declared `HostRenderer` role is excluded from `sends`, so it will never receive a Behavior Intent command over the wire, permanently.

  6. statusProjection.ts:962 `canPresent: item["role"] === "remote-renderer" || item["role"] === "host-renderer"` and :1046-1049 `online.every(m => m.canPresent) ? project("synced") : project("partial-capability")` and :808-811 headline "iPhone 已連接，角色狀態已同步" — confirmed exact. `CharacterSyncSignals` (statusProjection.ts:881-896) confirmed to carry no unsupported-intent field, and `CharacterSyncCard.tsx:315-322` confirmed to build `signals` only from `enabled/failedReads/revoked/connected/diagnostics.storeNote` — nothing from negotiated intents. This is also root-caused: no code path exists (state broadcast nor diagnostics) that could feed such a field even if the frontend wanted to.

  7. docs/aip/character-session.md §11 (lines 165-169) confirms the contract explicitly promises a `partial-capability` row triggered by "協商後有 unsupported intent" — a promise the current wiring cannot fulfill by any means other than the self-reported-role side channel, exactly as the finding argues.

  8. Checked existing tests (`crates/interaction-session/tests/session.rs`): `HostRenderer` role is only ever exercised for `Party::human_surface("desktop")`, never for `Party::device(...)` — confirming this device/host-renderer combination is untested, and not an intentionally-covered/accepted trust boundary.

  Net effect verified: a paired iPhone (`kind: device`) can send `role: "host-renderer"` in its `capability` announcement; the runtime accepts it verbatim, projects it into the authoritative `SemanticState.members[].role`, and the desktop's general-mode UI reads that self-reported role as proof of "已同步" (green/ok tone) — while the runtime's own intent-routing logic simultaneously guarantees that device receives zero Behavior Intents, forever. This is a real violation of the project's own 誠實階梯/不得謊稱成功 invariants (CLAUDE.md), reproducible purely by reading the code path end-to-end with no fixes pending. Severity "medium" is reasonable: it is a trust/honesty defect in a status projection, not a data-integrity or privilege-escalation bug (the `role` field grants no extra send permissions — permission gating in `gate()` step 7 is via the separately-declared, equally-unverified `inputs` array, a distinct and broader issue not claimed here).

  </details>

**Fix**

協商時把 role 與 Transport 綁定身分對齊：`PartyKind::Device`／`Renderer` 的宣稱一律夾到 `remote-renderer｜input-device｜observer`，`host-renderer` 只允許 `PartyKind::HumanSurface`（不符就降級並稽核，不拒絕連線）。同時把「是否有 unsupported intent」以布林形式投影進 `MemberView`（不外洩 intent 名單），讓 §11 的 partial-capability 由真正的協商結果決定而不是由 role 推測。

**Regression test**

In `crates/interaction-session/tests/session.rs`, join a `Party::device("iphone-1")` with `announcement(MemberRole::HostRenderer, &["celebrate"], &[])`, then submit a touch that emits an intent the member declared support for; assert `sends(&submission.outputs)` is empty (the device never receives the command) while separately asserting `session.members()` shows that device's `role == MemberRole::HostRenderer` and `presence == Online` — proving the state a downstream UI would read as "capable" is disjoint from the runtime's actual intent-delivery target set.

---

#### character session 檔案救不回 epoch 時新 epoch 固定為 1，與成員記得的 epoch 相同（註解宣稱不會發生）

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-pairing-migration-002`
- **維度**：`pairing-migration`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs:106`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`quarantine()` 的註解與 `salvaged_epoch` 的註解都宣稱「救不回來就當 0——新 epoch 至少是 1，不會與任何成員記得的 epoch 相同」。這個宣稱是錯的：全新 host 的 epoch 本來就是 1（:209 `CharacterSession::new(config.clone(), 1, now)`），所以一台從未經歷過隔離的桌面，它的成員記得的 epoch 就是 1；當這台桌面的 `character-session.json` 壞到 `salvaged_epoch` 救不回任何數字時，隔離後的新 epoch 也是 1，revision 卻從 1 重新起算。結果 resume 走的是 `epoch == self.epoch` 這條路（不是 EpochMismatch），host 只送一份**沒有 session-reset**、revision 比成員小的 snapshot——正好落進上一則 finding 的靜默丟棄路徑，兩邊都不會察覺（桌面依 §11 顯示「iPhone 已連接，角色狀態已同步」）。

**Evidence**

character_session.rs:94-106 `fn quarantine` → `salvaged.saturating_add(1)`；:274-293 `fn salvaged_epoch`，救不回時回 0；:209 `Ok(None) => (CharacterSession::new(config.clone(), 1, now), None)`。既有單元測試自己釘住了 0 這一支：:1186 `assert_eq!(salvaged_epoch("not json at all"), 0);`——實跑 `cargo test -p interaction-runtime --lib a_truncated_session_file_still_gives_up_its_epoch` → `1 passed`。Host resume 分支：crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:586（epoch 相同就不送 session-reset）與 :591-596（`last_revision > self.revision` 只送普通 snapshot）。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Verified end-to-end by reading the actual code (no line drift from the cited citations) and executing the existing unit test. (1) character_session.rs:209 — the `Ok(None)` branch confirms a session that has never been quarantined starts at epoch=1, which is the steady-state epoch for any host that hasn't previously hit corruption (i.e. the common case, not a rare one). (2) character_session.rs:97-107 `quarantine()` computes `salvaged_epoch(&body).saturating_add(1)`; `salvaged_epoch` (lines 276-293) falls back to 0 whenever the corrupted body doesn't contain the `"epoch"` substring at all — confirmed by running `cargo test -p interaction-runtime --lib a_truncated_session_file_still_gives_up_its_epoch` (1 passed), whose own assertion `assert_eq!(salvaged_epoch("not json at all"), 0)` proves the 0-fallback path exists for exactly this kind of severe corruption (truncation, binary garbage, or anything that erases the epoch field's text). 0+1=1, which is identical to the untouched host's epoch, directly falsifying the code comment's claim that a salvage-miss default "won't ever match what a member remembers" — it matches precisely the most common member-remembered epoch (1). (3) Read interaction-session/src/session.rs:576-597 `resume()`: when `epoch == self.epoch` (true in this collision case) and `last_revision > self.revision` (true, since the freshly-quarantined session restarts at INITIAL_REVISION=1 per session.rs:29/196 while the member remembers a higher pre-corruption revision), the code takes the `Resume::Snapshot` branch with `reason: None` at line 592, not `Resume::EpochMismatch`'s session-reset branch (lines 586-588). `snapshot_envelope_inner` (session.rs:1252-1281) only inserts the `reason`/`session-reset` key when `Some` is passed — confirmed by reading its body — and character_session.rs:952-957 confirms the runtime only injects the `session-reset` reason string for the `EpochMismatch` arm, never for `Snapshot`. So the finding's full mechanism is real: a silent epoch collision on quarantine routes resume down the no-reset Snapshot path, handing the client a lower revision with no reset marker. This is a genuine flaw in the code comment's reasoning, not a duplicate/style/already-fixed issue, and every cited line number matches the current file content exactly.

  </details>

**Fix**

epoch 不能只靠檔案救回：把 epoch 另外持久化在 store meta（或 audit）並取 `max(salvaged, last_known) + 1`；救不回時至少從一個保證未用過的起點（例如以 unix 秒為底）出發。另外，`last_revision > self.revision` 的 `Resume::Snapshot` 也應帶上 `session-reset` 之類的明確理由，讓接收端知道這是合法的倒退而不是重播攻擊。

**Regression test**

Seed character-session.json with a legitimate epoch=1 snapshot at revision=50 (a never-quarantined host that's been running a while), overwrite the file with non-JSON garbage containing no "epoch" substring, reopen via CharacterSessionHost::open (expect resulting epoch==1, revision==1), then call session.resume(party, last_revision=50, last_sequence=.., epoch=1, now) and assert it returns Resume::Snapshot (not EpochMismatch) with no "reason" key in the payload — proving the silent same-epoch rollback.

---

#### 被新連線取代（superseded）的收尾略過高風險受器強制停用，重連在 45 s idle timeout 內即可保住 iphone.mic-level

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-pairing-migration-003`
- **維度**：`pairing-migration`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-runtime/src/mobile.rs:3782`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

mobile.rs 開頭的不變量寫著「手機斷線 → …高風險受器（iphone.mic-level）由桌面端強制 disabled，重連不自動恢復」。但 superseded 這條收尾路徑完全跳過 `mobile_disable_high_risk_receptors`。iOS 的重連退避上限是 15 s（ConnectionManager.swift `backoffMaxSeconds = 15`），桌面的 idle timeout 是 45 s（IDLE_TIMEOUT_DEFAULT_MS），所以「socket 斷掉 → 手機在舊 handler 還沒逾時前就重連上」是常態而非邊界：新連線在 `mobile_attach_conn` 取代舊表項並 cancel 舊 close token，舊 handler 因為裝置仍在 `devices` 而判成 superseded，於是受器保持 enabled——「重連不自動恢復」被繞過，之後手機只要單邊打開麥克風就能直接推 mic-level，不需要人類在桌面重新啟用。同一條路徑也讓 `was_active` 為 false，先前發出的 `sensor.started` 永遠等不到對應的 `sensor.stopped`，而該手機卻已從 `status.activeSensors` 消失（新 ConnState 的 mic_since 是 None），事件面與 status 不一致。

**Evidence**

mobile.rs:3143-3155 `mobile_attach_conn` 插入新 ConnState 後 `old.close.cancel()`；:3259-3266 舊 handler 的 `close.cancelled()` 分支以 `devices.contains_key(id)` 判斷 → 裝置仍在 → `closed_by_server = Some("superseded")`；:3705-3712 `was_active` 因 `c.conn_id == conn_id` 不成立而為 `(false,false)`，所以 :3719-3730 的 `sensor.stopped` 事件不會發；:3782 `Some("superseded") => {}` 明確跳過 :3783-3788 與 :3789-3794 兩條會呼叫 `mobile_disable_high_risk_receptors` 的分支。既有測試 tests/mobile_loop.rs:930-964 `high_risk_receptor_forced_off_on_disconnect_and_stays_off` 只涵蓋「舊連線已完全關閉後才重連」，沒有涵蓋 superseded。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Verified end-to-end by reading the current file (HEAD 8c5ea58, one commit after the reviewed 6683403 — no relevant change in between).

  1. Invariant, as claimed: mobile.rs:15 "手機斷線 → ... 高風險受器（`iphone.mic-level`）由桌面端強制 disabled，重連不自動恢復。" (also restated at :119-120, :2533-2534, :2598).

  2. The bypass is real and deterministic, not merely probable:
     - `mobile_attach_conn` (:3127-3155) holds the `conns` write lock, `insert`s the NEW ConnState (mic_since always reset to `None`) which atomically replaces the OLD entry, and only *after* that swap is complete calls `old.close.cancel()` (:3153-3154). So by construction, by the time the old handler's `close.cancelled()` arm runs, `conns` is guaranteed to already hold the new connection's entry — this is not a race, it fires on every same-device reconnect while the old entry is still registered.
     - Old handler's `close.cancelled()` arm (:3256-3266): `still_paired` (device still in `self.mobile.devices`) ⇒ `closed_by_server = Some("superseded")`, with the code's own comment framing it as "同一台手機的新連線仍在：不是斷線".
     - Tail cleanup (:3705-3716): `conns.get(&device_id)` now returns the *new* connection's state; `c.conn_id == conn_id` (old handler's own id) is false ⇒ falls to `_ => (false, false)`, so `was_active = false`.
     - Final dispatch (:3781-3796): `Some("superseded") => {}` — verified exact line 3782, matching the finding's citation — skips both branches (:3783-3788 and :3789-3794) that call `mobile_disable_high_risk_receptors`. Since that function (:2535-2560) mutates the *global* registry state via `self.registry.set_receptor_enabled(&id, false)` (not per-connection), skipping it leaves `iphone.mic-level` enabled registry-wide across the reconnect.
     - `mobile_active_sensors`/`ingest` path confirms the consequence is real: `ingest()` (runtime.rs:614-643) only gates on registry-enabled state (`self.registry.receptor(...).await?`) plus, for `iphone.mic-level` specifically, an independent session-consent check (`mobile_receptor_consented`, tied to `current_session()`, not to the WS connection) — so if a consent-granting session is already active when the network blip happens (the ordinary case for an in-progress mic-streaming use), the new connection's fresh `iphone.mic-level` observations sail straight through with no re-enable step.
     - Status/event inconsistency also confirmed: new ConnState's `mic_since: None` means `mobile_active_sensors()` (:2772-2810, via `mic_streaming_devices`) drops the device from `status.activeSensors` immediately on the swap, while no `sensor.stopped` was ever emitted for it (`was_active=false` skips the :3719-3730-equivalent block) — an event/status split exactly as claimed.

  3. Numeric claims confirmed: `IDLE_TIMEOUT_DEFAULT_MS = 45_000` (mobile.rs:59); iOS `backoffMaxSeconds: Double = 15` (ConnectionManager.swift:298, doubling from 1s) — so ordinary reconnects land well inside the 45s window, making "superseded" the common outcome of a network blip rather than a rare edge.

  4. Existing coverage does not touch this path: `tests/mobile_loop.rs:930` (`high_risk_receptor_forced_off_on_disconnect_and_stays_off`) uses `drop(ws)` (a real close, `closed_by_server=None`) followed by a completely separate later reconnect — it never exercises the `superseded` branch. `rg -n "superseded" crates/interaction-runtime/tests/` finds no hits, matching the finding.

  5. Independent corroboration: `docs/releases/v0.6.0-recovery-matrix.md` (written earlier in this same v0.6.0 effort) already flags, with matching line references (`mobile.rs:3157,3635` under an earlier commit — now drifted to :3130/:3264/:3782, consistent with normal commit drift, not evidence of a fix), that the `superseded` branch is "僅程式碼存在，無任何測試斷言" and recommends v0.6.0 prioritize adding tests for it — but that document only flags the *coverage* gap, not the specific safety-property bypass this finding identifies (the forced-disable being skipped, and the resulting event/status inconsistency), so this finding is not a duplicate of an already-fully-described, already-fixed, or already-test-covered issue.

  Everything in the claim — the code paths, the exact line numbers, the deterministic mechanism, the numeric backoff/timeout comparison, and the absence of test coverage — checks out against the current source.

  </details>

**Fix**

superseded 也要視為「這條有效連線消失」：在該分支呼叫 `mobile_disable_high_risk_receptors(device_id, "superseded")`（新連線本來就要人類重新啟用），並在舊 ConnState 的 mic_since 為 Some 時補一則 `sensor.stopped{reason:"superseded"}`；或在 `mobile_attach_conn` 取代舊表項的當下就做這兩件事（那裡拿得到 old.mic_since）。

**Regression test**

Pair a device, enable+consent `iphone.mic-level`, drive a mic observation so `mic_since` is set on the live connection; without closing that socket, open a second WS and re-authenticate with the same `deviceId`/token (triggering the `superseded` branch on the first handler); then assert `rt.registry.receptor(&iphone_mic_level).await` is still `Err` (currently it stays `Ok`, i.e. the receptor is never force-disabled) and that a `sensor.stopped` audit/event exists to pair the earlier `sensor.started` before the device silently drops out of `status.activeSensors`.

#### host 自己送出的 capability 協商回覆可以超過 AIP payload 上限，且 unsupported_inputs 無上限存進成員紀錄

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-session-integrity-060`
- **維度**：`session-integrity`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:1284`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`negotiate_capabilities` 把對方宣告的所有未支援 `inputs` 原封不動收進 `NegotiatedCapabilities.unsupported_inputs`（沒有數量上限），`capability_envelope` 再把整包序列化成回覆 payload，而這個 envelope 從來沒有被 `validate()` 過（它是 `JoinOutcome.capability_envelope`，不是 `Output`，測試的 `assert_outputs_valid` 看不到它）。結果是 host 送出一則違反自己 §11 上限的訊息：符合規範的接收端必須拒絕它，等於協商靜默失敗。

**Evidence**

session.rs:1284-1303 `capability_envelope` 直接 `serde_json::to_value(negotiated)` 當 payload，沒有任何大小檢查；tests/session.rs:70-86 `assert_outputs_valid` 只驗 `Output::Send`／`Output::Broadcast`。capability.rs:118-125 `unsupported_inputs.push(input.clone())`（且 `contains` 是 O(n²) 線性掃描）。實測：
  announced inputs                   = 1500
  host capability reply bytes        = 65068
  host capability reply validates?   = Err(PayloadTooLarge)
  host capability reply encode()?    = Ok(65068)   // 仍然會被送上線
  negotiated unsupported_inputs kept = 1500

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  逐項核實主張，全部成立，且用最小化 repro 直接重現：

  1. `crates/interaction-aip/src/capability.rs:118-125` `negotiate_capabilities` 對 `announcement.inputs` 逐一分類進 `inputs`／`unsupported_inputs`，兩個 `Vec<String>` 都沒有數量上限（唯一的去重用 `contains`，O(n²) 但不影響本結論）。`CapabilityAnnouncement.inputs` 本身（capability.rs:44-45）也只是 `#[serde(default)] Vec<String>`，沒有自訂 Deserialize 長度檢查。

  2. `crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:1284-1303`（行號與現況一致，無漂移）`capability_envelope()` 直接 `serde_json::to_value(negotiated)` 當 payload，中間沒有呼叫 `check_payload`／`validate()`，也沒有任何大小檢查。

  3. `JoinOutcome.capability_envelope`／`snapshot_envelope` 是獨立欄位，不是 `Output` — `join()`（session.rs:311-380）回傳它們時完全繞過 `Vec<Output>`。`crates/interaction-session/tests/session.rs:70-86` 的 `assert_outputs_valid` 只 match `Output::Send`／`Output::Broadcast`，結構上看不到這兩個欄位（另一個測試 helper `join()`，session.rs:128-146，確實會對它們呼叫 `.validate()`，但沒有任何既有測試餵入夠大的 `inputs` 觸發門檻，所以這條路徑目前沒被測試蓋到）。

  4. 親自重現（暫時在 `crates/interaction-session/tests/session.rs` 加一個 `#[test]`，`cargo test -p interaction-session` 跑完後用 `git checkout` 還原、`git status` 確認乾淨）：用 1500 個唯一 input 字串 join，結果：
     announced inputs = 1500
     host capability reply bytes = 41058
     host capability reply validates? = Err(PayloadTooLarge)（超過 32768）
     host capability reply encode()? = Ok(41058)（在 65536 之內，代表走 `Envelope::encode()` 檢查的傳輸路徑仍會把它送出去）
     negotiated unsupported_inputs kept = 1500（全部保留，無上限）
     數字與原始 finding 給的 65068 不同（我用的字串長度不同所致），但質性結論完全一致：validate() 失敗、encode() 成功、collection 無界。

  5. 額外交叉檢查（強化而非推翻主張）：production 中至少有一條真實外部路徑會撞到這個洞——`crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs:805-819` `character_session_device_capability` 在裝置「第一次 join」時，直接把 `envelope.payload` 反序列化成 `CapabilityAnnouncement`，中間只經過 `Envelope::parse()`（只檢查整包 ≤65536 bytes），從未呼叫 `.validate()`（32768 payload 上限、per-string 2000 字、深度 8 都沒被強制）；回傳的 `capability_envelope` 再被 `crates/interaction-runtime/src/mobile.rs:3244` 的本地 `send` closure（`v.to_string()` 直接塞進 outbound channel）送出——這條路徑連 `.encode()` 都沒呼叫，比 finding 描述的還寬鬆。這證實「host 自己送出的協商回覆缺乏大小把關」不是紙上談兵，而是有可達的外部輸入路徑。

  沒有找到任何已存在的上限檢查、schema-level 陣列長度驗證，或下游強制 `.validate()` 的守門邏輯能推翻此結論；也沒有跡象顯示此問題已被近期 commit 修復（cited 行號與現況完全吻合）。歸類為 session-integrity／有界集合 違規成立。

  </details>

**Fix**

`negotiate_capabilities` 對 `unsupported_inputs`（與 `inputs`／`intents`）設固定上限（例如 32 筆）並改用 BTreeSet；`capability_envelope`（以及所有 host 產生的 envelope）在回傳前跑一次 `validate()`，超限就降級成只帶計數的摘要。同時把 capability envelope 納入 `assert_outputs_valid` 的覆蓋範圍。

**Regression test**

在 `crates/interaction-session/tests/session.rs` 新增測試：構造一個 `CapabilityAnnouncement`，`inputs` 塞入遠超正常規模（例如 1500+ 個不重複字串，host 都不支援），呼叫 `session.join(...)`，斷言 `outcome.capability_envelope.validate().is_ok()`（目前會是 `Err(PayloadTooLarge)`，測試應先失敗以釘死缺陷，修復後轉為通過）；同時斷言 `outcome.negotiated.unsupported_inputs.len()` 不超過某個明確上限（例如與 `MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES`／已知 input 白名單掛鉤的常數），修復方式可以是在 `negotiate_capabilities` 對 `unsupported_inputs`（以及必要時 `inputs`）做數量截斷＋標記 `truncated:true`，或在 `capability_envelope()` 組完 payload 後呼叫 `check_payload` 失敗就降級成一則明確的 `error{payload-too-large}` 而不是硬送出違規訊息。

---

#### restore() 只驗 snapshot 自洽的 hash，攜帶未知欄位的被污染 state 會被原樣重新廣播給所有成員

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-session-integrity-061`
- **維度**：`session-integrity`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:225`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`restore` 用 `state_hash(&snapshot.state) != snapshot.hash` 判斷竄改，但 hash 是對 snapshot 自己的 state 算的——只要污染者一併重算 hash 就通過。`SemanticState` 反序列化會忽略未知欄位，`violates_limits` 只看 mood intensity 與成員數，而 `state_json` 保留的是**原始（含污染鍵）的 JSON**，於是 `snapshot()`／`state{kind:"snapshot"}` 會把攻擊者塞的鍵連同一個「合法」的 hash 一起發給每個成員（含 iPhone renderer），直到下一次 commit 才被 merge patch 的 `null` 清掉。

**Evidence**

session.rs:225-232（hash 比對＋`violates_limits`）、session.rs:259 `state_json: snapshot.state.clone()`；state.rs:303-307 `violates_limits` 只檢查 `mood.intensity` 與 `members.len()`；`SemanticState` 沒有 `deny_unknown_fields`（state.rs:248-260）。實測：
  restore accepted poisoned snapshot: revision=2 epoch=1
  republished snapshot state = {"activity":"idle",...,"evilKey":{"safetyText":"all clear"},...}
既有測試 tests/session.rs:1283 `restore_rejects_tampered_snapshots` 只覆蓋「改了 state 但沒有重算 hash」的情形。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Verified every link in the claim by reading the code and by reproducing it live:\n\n1. state_hash = canonical_hash(&Value) in crates/interaction-aip/src/lib.rs:55-59 hashes the raw JSON as given (key-sorted, whole-tree) — not a hash of a re-serialized/typed SemanticState. So it only proves \"this JSON matches this hash\", not \"this JSON conforms to SemanticState's schema\".\n2. SemanticState (crates/interaction-session/src/state.rs:247-259) has no #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]; violates_limits (state.rs:302-307) checks only mood.intensity range and members.len(), never unknown top-level keys.\n3. restore() (session.rs:216-263) sets state_json: snapshot.state.clone() — the raw, attacker-controllable JSON — while CharacterSession::new() (session.rs:187-208) always derives state_json via state_value(&state), a serialize of the typed struct that structurally cannot carry unknown keys. This asymmetry is real and is the root cause.\n4. I wrote and ran a temporary integration test (crates/interaction-session/tests/session.rs, added then git-checkout-reverted, working tree now clean per `git status --short`) that: builds a snapshot, injects an `evilKey`, recomputes state_hash over the polluted JSON (self-consistent, as the claim says), calls CharacterSession::restore (succeeds), then calls session.resume(&phone, 0, 0, epoch, ...) — which hits Resume::Snapshot — and asserts on the outgoing envelope's payload[\"state\"].\n   Actual output:\n   restore accepted poisoned snapshot: revision=2 epoch=1\n   republished snapshot state = {...,\"evilKey\":{\"safetyText\":\"all clear (forged)\"},...}\n   This matches the finding's own cited evidence verbatim.\n5. Root cause of *why* resume() leaks it while join()/handle_capability don't: resume() (session.rs:577-621) builds its reply directly via snapshot_envelope_inner → self.state_json (session.rs:1252-1265) with NO prior commit_and_patch call, whereas join() (session.rs:349-358) calls self.commit_and_patch(now) — which recomputes state_json from the typed state via state_value(&self.state), scrubbing unknown keys — before building its own snapshot_envelope. So a reconnecting member that calls resume() (§7 step 1) before any member renegotiates capability receives the poisoned state_json verbatim, with a hash that checks out.\n\nCaveat (does not falsify the claim, only bounds its severity): Snapshot only ever originates from the local state/character-session.json file, written 0600 owner-only by write_owner_only() in crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs:109-134 (confirmed via grep — CharacterSession::restore has exactly one production caller, CharacterSessionHost::open). So the \"poisoner\" must already have local same-OS-user file-write access to the daemon's state dir, which is a fairly strong precondition. The finding is about a code-level integrity/mechanism gap (hash is self-consistency-only, not schema-authoritative; state_json isn't re-derived/sanitized on restore before being served to resume() callers), which is exactly what it claims — not about a network-remote attacker. severity: medium (as filed) is reasonable given the local-write precondition; I would not raise it to high, but the mechanism itself is real, reproducible, not already covered by the existing restore_rejects_tampered_snapshots test (which only covers state changed WITHOUT recomputing the hash), and not already fixed in the current file content (line numbers checked against current session.rs and they match: restore() spans ~216-263, state_hash comparison at line ~225, state_json: snapshot.state.clone() at line 259).

  </details>

**Fix**

restore 時以 `serde_json::from_value::<SemanticState>` 的結果重新序列化成 canonical state（丟掉未知鍵），並用重新序列化後的值計算／比對 hash 與設定 `state_json`；`SemanticState` 加 `#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]`，讓污染在反序列化階段就變成 `InvalidState`。

**Regression test**

In a fresh session, join a member, snapshot it, inject an unknown top-level key into the snapshot's JSON state, recompute state_hash over the polluted JSON so restore()'s hash check passes, call CharacterSession::restore, then call resume() for that member with last_revision/last_sequence below current (forcing Resume::Snapshot) before any commit happens — assert the returned envelope's payload[\"state\"] does NOT contain the injected key (currently it does).

#### 一般模式「已同步／部分能力目前不可用」用的是成員自報的 role，不是協商後的 intent 支援度

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-capability-consent-052`
- **維度**：`capability-consent`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-aip/src/capability.rs:100（其餘引用行號含 statusProjection.ts:962 準確）`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

契約 §11 的判定條件是「協商後有 unsupported intent → 『部分能力目前不可用』」，但 characterSyncMembers() 的 canPresent 只看 `item["role"] === "remote-renderer" || "host-renderer"`（statusProjection.ts:962），而 role 是成員在 capability 宣告裡自己填的（capability.rs:99 `let role = announcement.role.unwrap_or(MemberRole::Observer)`，join 時原樣寫進 members 投影）。協商結果（哪些 intent 是 unsupported）是 host 私有、不進 SemanticState，桌面根本拿不到。因此一台宣告 role=remote-renderer 但一個 intent 都不支援（全部協商成 unsupported）的裝置，會被投影成綠色的「iPhone 已連接，角色狀態已同步」；反過來說 partial-capability 這條文案只有在裝置自報 input-device／observer 時才會出現，判定訊號整個接錯。這正是 §8 必測清單裡「renderer capability spoofing 不影響他人」要防的事：宣稱影響了人類看到的能力結論。

**Evidence**

statusProjection.ts:962 `canPresent: item["role"] === "remote-renderer" || item["role"] === "host-renderer"`；statusProjection.ts:1045-1049 `if (online.length > 0) { return online.every((m) => m.canPresent) ? project("synced") : project("partial-capability"); }`。rg 顯示 canPresent 沒有其他來源（唯一寫入點就是 962 行）。實測 host 端：宣告 intents=[] 的 remote-renderer 成功 join 且 members 投影 role=remote-renderer，但 intent_targets() 為空、一則 command 都不會送給它（harness [3] `command envelopes actually sent to anyone = 0`）。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  逐段追蹤程式碼，主張的每一環都成立：

  1. `crates/interaction-aip/src/capability.rs` `negotiate_capabilities()`：`let role = announcement.role.unwrap_or(MemberRole::Observer);`（目前行號 100，非 99，只有 1 行漂移）。role 完全是成員自己宣告的值（或缺省 Observer），跟後面逐一計算的 `intents` 交集（`Exact`／`Unsupported`）完全獨立、互不影響——一個宣告 `role: remote-renderer, intents: []` 的成員，協商出來 `negotiated.role == RemoteRenderer` 但 `negotiated.intents` 裡每個 host intent 都是 `Unsupported`。

  2. `crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs::join()`：`entry.member.role = negotiated.role`——原樣把這個自報／未經 intent 支援度調整的 role 寫進 member。

  3. `crates/interaction-session/src/state.rs`：`SemanticState.members: Vec<MemberView>`，型別定義正上方明寫「§3 `members[]`：共享狀態裡的成員投影（**不含**協商結果——那是 host 私有）」。`MemberView` 只有 `party/role/presence/last_seen_at`，沒有任何 per-intent 支援度欄位。`Diagnostics.members` 也是同一個 `Vec<MemberView>`（`session.rs:158`），連進階模式的診斷端點都拿不到 unsupported intents。`capability_envelope()`（`session.rs:1284`）帶著完整 `negotiated`（含每個 intent 的支援度）送出，但 `.with_target(to.clone())` 只送給剛 join 的那一方自己，不會廣播給其他成員（例如桌面的 host-renderer/observer）。因此「協商後有哪些 intent unsupported」這個信號，在目前的架構下**沒有任何文件化管道**能到達桌面。

  4. `apps/interaction-desktop/src/statusProjection.ts:962`：`canPresent: item["role"] === "remote-renderer" || item["role"] === "host-renderer"`，`characterSyncMembers()` 的輸入就是上面那個不含協商結果的 `MemberView` 投影，`canPresent` 因此只能看自報 role，這是它在架構上唯一能看到的東西。`projectCharacterSession()`（1045-1049 行）：`online.length>0` 時 `online.every(canPresent) ? synced : partial-capability`。

  5. `apps/interaction-desktop/src/test/statusProjection-session.test.ts:268` 的測試標題直接寫「只能收發輸入的裝置標成『演不出角色』（partial-capability 的來源）」——現有測試套件把「partial-capability 的唯一來源」釘死在 role=input-device，而不是契約 §11 講的「協商後有 unsupported intent」。這證實了實作與 `docs/aip/character-session.md` §11 那張文案表（「協商後有 unsupported intent →『部分能力目前不可用』」）確實不一致：目前的訊號是「自報角色」，不是「協商結果」。

  6. `intent_targets()`（`session.rs:1375`）用的才是真正的協商結果：`role == RemoteRenderer && presence == Online && negotiated.intents.get(intent) == Exact`。所以一個宣告 `remote-renderer` 但 `intents=[]` 的裝置，在 `emit_intent()` 時對任何 intent 都拿不到 target，真的一則 command 都不會送出——這與 finding 描述的 harness 觀察（「command envelopes actually sent to anyone = 0」）在邏輯上完全吻合，且我已從程式碼直接驗證出因果鏈，不需要另外起 daemon 才能確認。

  結論：這是一個真實、可用程式碼證據重現的落差——一般模式「已同步」徽章的判定訊號用的是成員自報、協商前就存在的 role，而非契約 §11 定義的協商後 intent 支援度；而且協商後的 intent 支援度目前在架構上完全不會傳到桌面（不進 SemanticState、不進 diagnostics、capability envelope 只單播給宣告者自己），所以桌面連「正確判定」的原始資料都拿不到。這正好命中本次審查要抓的「誠實階梯／宣稱不等於已驗證」與「架構邊界」兩個維度。

  唯一需要更正的地方是引用行號：`crates/interaction-aip/src/capability.rs` 裡 `let role = announcement.role.unwrap_or(MemberRole::Observer);` 目前在第 100 行，不是 99 行（漂移 1 行，可能是後續其他行被加入所致）；`statusProjection.ts:962` 行號完全準確。

  </details>

**Fix**

把每個成員的協商摘要（例如 `unsupportedIntents: n` 或 `canPresent: bool`）由 Runtime 從 host 私有的 NegotiatedCapabilities 投影到 /v1/character-session 的回應（不必進 SemanticState 也行，diagnostics 之外另開一個一般模式可用的最小欄位），前端據此判定，不要用自報 role。

**Regression test**

Rust 端：`CharacterSession::join()` 一個 `CapabilityAnnouncement{ role: Some(RemoteRenderer), intents: vec![] }`（其餘 intents 全落 Unsupported）後，斷言 `intent_targets("celebrate")`（或任何 host intent）回傳空 vec、且該 session 的 `SemanticState.members` 序列化出的 `role` 仍是 `"remote-renderer"`，藉此釘死「self-declared role 使 intent_targets 為空，但 MemberView 對外仍呈現 remote-renderer」這條落差；TS 端：`characterSyncMembers()` 餵入 `role: "remote-renderer", presence: "online"` 但沒有任何額外「unsupported intents」欄位可用，`projectCharacterSession()` 仍回傳 `"synced"`，斷言這與契約 §11「協商後有 unsupported intent → partial-capability」的期望不符（目前無法讓這個情境判成 partial-capability，因為協商結果根本不在輸入資料裡）。

---

#### 沒有任何目標的 Behavior Intent 照樣掛進 pending，TTL 到期被記成 intents.expired 與 intent-expired 稽核

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-capability-consent-053`
- **維度**：`capability-consent`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:1356`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

emit_intent() 不論 intent_targets() 是否為空都呼叫 push_pending（session.rs:1345-1362）。桌面單機（只有 host-renderer 成員，intent_targets 只收 remote-renderer，session.rs:1382-1391）是預設拓撲，於是**每一次互動**產生的 intent 都會在 intent_ttl 後被 tick() 計成 intents.expired 並寫一則 `character.session.intent-expired` 稽核（session.rs:625-634），但 host 從來沒有把這個 command 送給任何人。契約 §5 說「只有真的沒有人回覆的 intent 才會在 TTL 到期時被稽核成 intent-expired」——沒有人被問過不等於沒有人回覆，這個計數器因此在最常見的拓撲下永遠是假訊號，也讓「遠端 renderer 沒回應」這件事無法從證據裡讀出來。

**Evidence**

實測（成員宣告 intents=[]，等同沒有可派送目標）：
[4] audit character.session.intent-expired {"count":1}
[4] intents.expired = Some(1) (no command was ever sent to any member)
程式碼：session.rs:1345 `let targets = self.intent_targets(&intent.intent);`、1356 `self.push_pending(PendingIntent{ intent, outstanding: targets, commands, .. })`（targets 可能為空，無條件 push）；session.rs:625-634 tick 的過期計數。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  親自讀碼＋寫最小可重現測試證實主張成立。session.rs:1341-1367 的 emit_intent()：`let targets = self.intent_targets(&intent.intent);`（1345）算出 RemoteRenderer 目標後，1356 呼叫 `self.push_pending(PendingIntent{ intent, outstanding: targets, commands, .. })`，不論 targets 是否為空都無條件執行；intent_targets()（1374-1384）只挑 role==RemoteRenderer 且 online 且協商 Exact 的成員，HostRenderer 從不計入。settle_intent()（902-924）只在收到成員 result 時把該成員從 outstanding 移除，一開始 outstanding 就是空的 PendingIntent 沒有任何「立刻結清」路徑，只能等 tick()（624-634）用 `pending.intent.expires_at > now` 掃除，一律計 C_INTENTS_EXPIRED 並發 AUDIT_INTENT_EXPIRED，不分辨「送了沒人回」與「根本沒送給任何人」。

  關鍵：這條路徑不是理論邊角案例。director::react()（director.rs 90-140）是純函式，收到 touch/dismiss 一律回傳 BehaviorIntent，完全不知道有沒有 RemoteRenderer 存在。而桌面視窗在 `/v1/character/hello` 協商後就是以 `MemberRole::HostRenderer`＋`HOST_INTENTS`（react-happily-to-touch/celebrate/settle/idle）＋`HOST_INPUTS`（touch/dismiss）加入 session（interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs:531-549，`character_session_join_desktop`），這正是本專案「桌面單機、沒有外接 iPhone/renderer」的真實預設拓撲。

  我寫了一個獨立探針測試（借用 tests/session.rs 既有 fixture 慣例，跑完立刻刪除、`git status` 乾淨無殘留）：只加入一個 HostRenderer 成員（intents=HOST_INTENTS、inputs=[EVENT_TOUCH]，與 runtime 實際註冊完全對齊），送一則 touch。實測輸出：
    submission outcome = Applied
    sent_commands.len() = 0   （沒有 RemoteRenderer，什麼 command 都沒送出）
    pending_intents = 1       （emit_intent 仍無條件掛進 pending，outstanding=[]）
    tick 到 TTL 之後：expired_audit fired = true，intents.expired counter = Some(1)

  這完全複現 finding 敘述的行為：從沒送出任何 command，卻被計成 intents.expired 並稽核 character.session.intent-expired。對照契約 docs/aip/character-session.md:92「只有真的沒有人回覆的 intent 才會在 TTL 到期時被稽核成 character.session.intent-expired」——上下文（88 行的結清表格）把這句話定位在「有人被問過但沒回覆」對比「有人被問且回覆了就提早結清」，不是「根本沒人被問」。現有測試（tests/session.rs 的 presence_times_out_and_expired_intents_are_dropped、an_unanswered_intent_still_expires_and_is_audited）都用 device_announcement()——它宣告 role=RemoteRenderer，所以那些測試裡「沒人回覆」其實是「command 真的送出去、對方沒回」，跟本 finding 描述的「command 從未送出」是不同情境，沒有測試涵蓋到後者，因此這不是已被測試覆蓋的防護、也沒有被修掉。

  結論：finding 的程式碼引用（1356、intent_targets 1374 一帶、tick 625-634 一帶）行號無漂移、機制描述精確，且我以真實生產拓撲（HostRenderer-only）而非假設情境重現，證實在最常見的單機桌面拓撲下，每一次觸摸互動都會在 intent_ttl 後產生一則假的 intent-expired 稽核與計數，讓這個訊號在最常見拓撲下永遠是雜訊，無法用來判斷「遠端 renderer 真的沒回應」。屬於誠實稽核軌跡失真的真實缺陷，非資安/資料外洩等級，medium 合理。

  </details>

**Fix**

targets 為空時不要建立 pending（host renderer 仍照送 Output::RendererIntent），或改用 `intents.dropped`／新增 `intents.no-target` 之類的獨立計數，讓 intents.expired 只描述「送出去了但沒人回」。

**Regression test**

只加入一個 role=HostRenderer 的成員（不加入任何 RemoteRenderer）、送一則 touch 事件觸發 emit_intent，斷言 outputs 中沒有任何 Output::Send（沒有目標可送）；目前行為是 pending_intents().len()==1，tick 到 TTL 後仍會產生 character.session.intent-expired 稽核與 intents.expired=1——回歸測試應斷言在 targets 為空時不建立 PendingIntent（或改用不同語意的計數，如 intents.no-target，明確標示「從未派送」而非「派送後無回應」），使該計數/稽核只反映真的送出 command 卻沒收到終態回覆的情況。

---

#### 緊急停止只清掉 host 自己的 pending，不對已經拿到 command 的 renderer 送 character.behavior.cancel

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-capability-consent-054`
- **維度**：`capability-consent`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:510`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

submit_runtime() 處理 `RuntimeFact::Emergency{engaged:true}` 時只做 `cancelled = self.pending.len(); self.pending.clear();`（session.rs:510-513），沒有像成員發起的 cancel 路徑那樣對每個目標送 `character.behavior.cancel`（對照 handle_cancel，session.rs:986-994）。iOS 端收到 truth=emergency 的 state patch 時也不會清空 nowPlaying／queue（SessionClient.swift:640-676 的 consume() 只更新 local/presentation；只有 connectionDidConnect/Disconnect 才 `queue.removeAll()`，447/469 行）。因此緊急停止當下手機上正在播、且 interruptible=false 的 celebrate 會整段播完，播完還會回一則 observed（SessionClient.swift:560-565）——host 已經把 pending 清掉，這則回報對不到任何東西。契約 §4 對 engaged 的定義是「取消所有 pending intent」，實作只取消了 host 這一側的帳。

**Evidence**

session.rs:508-513：
```
if matches!(fact, RuntimeFact::Emergency { engaged: true }) {
    cancelled = self.pending.len();
    self.pending.clear();
}
```
（同一函式後續只產生 state 廣播與稽核，沒有 Output::Send 的 cancel envelope）；對照 session.rs:986-994 handle_cancel 會 `for party in self.intent_targets(..) { outputs.push(Output::Send{ .. cancel_envelope .. }) }`。SessionClient.swift:447/469 是唯二的 `queue.removeAll()`，都在連線生命週期，不在 state 處理路徑。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  逐項親自驗證，主張成立，行號無漂移：

  1. crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:510-513 現狀與引用完全一致：
     ```
     if matches!(fact, RuntimeFact::Emergency { engaged: true }) {
         cancelled = self.pending.len();
         self.pending.clear();
     }
     ```
     整個 `submit_runtime` 函式在此分支之後只推 `Output::Broadcast`（state patch）與兩則 `Output::Audit`（`character.session.emergency`／`character.session.truth`），**沒有任何 `Output::Send`** 針對被清掉的 pending intent。

  2. 對照組 `handle_cancel`（session.rs:983 起，迴圈在 1007-1013）：對每個被取消的 intent 呼叫 `self.intent_targets(&intent.intent)` 逐一組 `character.behavior.cancel` 的 `Output::Send`。`PendingIntent`（session.rs:1524 起）本身就存了 `outstanding: Vec<Party>` 這種「送給了誰」的資訊——Emergency 分支明明可以複用同一套機制卻沒有用，證實不是「資訊不夠做不到」而是路徑漏了。

  3. 契約 `docs/aip/character-session.md` §4 逐字寫「engaged：…、取消所有 pending intent、…」，是唯一權威定義；現有測試 `crates/interaction-session/tests/session.rs::emergency_freezes_the_character_and_refuses_interaction` 只斷言 `session.pending_intents().is_empty()`（host 端 bookkeeping 清空），完全沒斷言任何 `Output::Send` 的 cancel envelope 被送出——測試沒蓋到這個缺口。

  4. iOS 端逐一核對：
     - `SessionClient.swift` 的 `consume()`（642-676 行處理 state patch）只更新 `local`／`presentation`，不碰 `nowPlaying`／`queue`。
     - 全 repo 唯二呼叫 `cancelIntents(matching:)` 清空 `nowPlaying`／`queue` 的地方是 `handleCommand` 收到 `SessionNames.behaviorCancel`（第 709-710 行）；`queue.removeAll()` 只另外出現在 `connectionDidConnect`（447-448）與 `connectionDidDisconnect`（469-470）。全 app 沒有任何程式碼在 `truth == .emergency` 時主動清空播放佇列（grep 全 repo 的 `isEmergency`／`cancelIntents` 使用點已確認）。
     - `crates/interaction-session/src/director.rs:205`：`task.verified` 產生的 `celebrate` intent 確實是 `interruptible: false`（唯一非 interruptible 的 1.0 intent）。
     - `CharacterView.swift` 的 `startPlayback`／`play()` 對 `.celebrate` 固定播 700ms（`playbackMs`，`CharacterSemantic.swift:576`），播完才呼叫 `session.intentDidFinishPlaying`；`SessionClient.swift:560-565` 的 `intentDidFinishPlaying` 送出 `status: .observed`。這條路徑不受 emergency 影響，只受 `character.behavior.cancel` 取消。
     - Host 端 `settle_intent`（session.rs 900 行區）對「對不到 pending」的 result 直接 `return Vec::new()`——證實這則遲到的 observed 確實「對不到任何東西」，雖不致產生錯誤，但坐實了 `cancelledIntents` 稽核數字只是host 自己清單的計數，並未真的讓對方停下來。

  综合：緊急停止當下已送達 remote-renderer 的 `celebrate`（interruptible:false）會照樣播完 700ms 才回報，佇列中尚未播放的 intent 也要等到下一次收到明確 cancel 才會清空——而 emergency 路徑從不送這則 cancel。這與 handle_cancel 的行為不對稱，也與契約「取消所有 pending intent」的文字有落差，且稽核 `cancelledIntents` 描述的只是 host 內部帳目被清空，而非真的通知到對方，帶有輕微的誠實階梯疑慮。就事論事屬於真實、可重現的缺陷，非測試已覆蓋、非已修復、非主觀風格问题。

  </details>

**Fix**

emergency engaged 時沿用 handle_cancel 的派送流程，對每個仍在 outstanding 的成員送 `character.behavior.cancel`；iOS 端在套用到 truth==emergency 的狀態時主動 `playback?.cancel()` 並清空 queue／nowPlaying。

**Regression test**

在 crates/interaction-session/tests/session.rs 新增：join 一個 remote-renderer 協商支援 celebrate → submit_runtime(TaskVerified{...}) 產生 pending celebrate intent 並送出 command（可從 outputs 找到 Output::Send{envelope.name=="character.behavior.request"}) → 再 submit_runtime(Emergency{engaged:true}) → 斷言 outputs 中存在一則 Output::Send{ to: 該 renderer, envelope.name == "character.behavior.cancel" }；目前程式碼下此斷言會失敗（找不到任何 cancel Output::Send），證明缺陷成立。

---

#### host 接線層仍用小樞專屬 helper 決定 variant，架構守門測試抓不到

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-character-package-018`
- **維度**：`character-package`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`apps/interaction-desktop/src/companion/CompanionApp.tsx:1017`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`docs/aip/reference-character.md` §5 宣稱「CompanionApp.tsx 沒有為 ref-shape 加過任何分岔：它只呼叫 createBuiltinAdapter(entrypoint, ctx) 並讀 meta」，但 CompanionApp 實際上以 `meta?.variants` 為條件呼叫小樞專屬的 `rigPaletteForImported`／`rigPaletteFor`／`importedRigPack`。任何**非小樞**的 adapter 只要在 meta 宣告 `variants`，host 就會把小樞的預設配色 `maid-classic` 當成它的初始 variant 塞進去；若清單沒夾帶 manifest，host 還會替它捏造一份 `kind:"character-rig"` 的舊 pack。這是角色專屬知識殘留在 host 接線層，且 `architecture-no-entrypoint-switch.test.ts` 只 grep `=== "<id>"` 字面比較，完全抓不到這種以 import 形式殘留的耦合。

**Evidence**

CompanionApp.tsx:1017 `const variant = meta?.variants ? rigPaletteForImported(manifest) : null;`；:1034 `return { entrypoint, ctx: { ...ctx, legacyPack: importedRigPack(entry, variant) } };`；:1048 `variant: meta?.variants ? rigPaletteFor(manifest) : null,`；import 來源 CompanionApp.tsx:113/124/125（經 gatewayWiring.ts:259-265 的 @deprecated re-export 指向 character/adapters/shu.ts）。shu.ts:271-285 `rigPaletteForImported()` 對非小樞 variants 直接 `return DEFAULT_SHU_RIG_PALETTE`（"maid-classic"，shu.ts:105）；shu.ts:291-305 `importedRigPack()` 固定回 `kind: RIG_PACK_KIND`("character-rig")、`palette: isShuRigPalette(palette) ? palette : DEFAULT_SHU_RIG_PALETTE`。守門測試 architecture-no-entrypoint-switch.test.ts:31-43 只比對 `[!=]== "<adapter id>"` 與 `case "<id>":`，第三個 case（:80-92）也只掃 `src/character/*.ts`，不掃 `src/companion/`，因此 CompanionApp 與 settingsTransfer.ts:124（`if (!SHU_RIG_PALETTES.includes(palette)) throw`）的小樞耦合全部漏網。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Verified line-by-line against the current tree (no drift — line numbers in the finding match exactly).

  1. CompanionApp.tsx:1013-1050 (function that builds BuiltinAdapterContext) gates variant/legacyPack computation on the generic-looking `meta?.variants` flag but then calls shu-specific helpers:
     - :1017 `const variant = meta?.variants ? rigPaletteForImported(manifest) : null;`
     - :1034 `legacyPack: importedRigPack(entry, variant)` (only when `!manifest && variant !== null`)
     - :1048 `variant: meta?.variants ? rigPaletteFor(manifest) : null,`

  2. These three functions are imported from `./gatewayWiring` (CompanionApp.tsx:113/124/125), which in turn just re-exports them from `character/adapters/shu.ts` with an explicit `@deprecated 改 import character/adapters/shu` comment (gatewayWiring.ts:250-265) — the repo's own code already flags this as shu-specific knowledge sitting in the shared layer.

  3. `shu.ts` confirms the hard-coded shu vocabulary: `SHU_RIG_PALETTES = ["maid-classic","maid-dusk","maid-sakura"]` (shu.ts:102), `DEFAULT_SHU_RIG_PALETTE = "maid-classic"` (shu.ts:105), `isShuRigPalette` only accepts those three names (shu.ts:108-110). `rigPaletteForImported` (shu.ts:271-285) checks a manifest's `x-legacy`/`legacy.palette`, `preferencesSchema` default, and `variants[0]` — all gated through `isShuRigPalette` — and falls back to `DEFAULT_SHU_RIG_PALETTE` ("maid-classic") whenever none matches. So a manifest belonging to a wholly different adapter (whose `meta.variants` are e.g. `["v1","v2"]`, not shu palette names) would deterministically get `"maid-classic"` stuffed into `ctx.variant`. `importedRigPack` (shu.ts:291-305) hard-codes `kind: RIG_PACK_KIND` = `"character-rig"` (shu.ts:192) — the legacy shu pack kind — for any entry it's called on.

  4. `BuiltinAdapterMeta.variants` (adapterRegistry.ts:59-63) is a generic, adapter-declared field with no shu-specific typing — any future `registerBuiltinAdapter` call (e.g. a second variant-bearing character) could set it, and CompanionApp's context-builder would immediately route through the shu helpers regardless. Confirmed via `adapters/index.ts`: today only `SHU_META` declares `variants: SHU_RIG_PALETTES` (index.ts:31); `SHAPE_META` (the ref-shape reference adapter) declares no `variants`, so the bug is currently dormant for ref-shape specifically — but that's exactly why the doc's narrow claim ("沒有為 ref-shape 加過任何分岔") reads as true while the broader architectural leak the finding describes is real and would break for the very next variant-bearing adapter, contradicting docs/aip/architecture-boundaries.md §4's stated target ("CompanionApp 只呼叫 createAdapter(entrypoint)；白名單＝registry keys").

  5. Confirmed the guard-test blind spot: `architecture-no-entrypoint-switch.test.ts` test 1 (`literalBranches`, :29-43) only regexes for literal `[!=]== "<id>"` / `case "<id>":` against the four adapter-id strings — a function call like `rigPaletteForImported(manifest)` is invisible to it. Test 3 (:80-92, the "no shu|maid|character-rig substring" scan) only reads `readdirSync(join(SRC, "character"))` — a non-recursive top-level listing of `src/character/*.ts` — it never touches `src/companion/*.ts(x)` at all, so it wouldn't catch this even if the substrings matched. Both claims about the test's coverage gap are accurate.

  6. Also confirmed the supporting evidence: settingsTransfer.ts:124 `if (!SHU_RIG_PALETTES.includes(palette)) throw new Error(...)` inside `companion/settingsTransfer.ts`, imported via `SHU_RIG_PALETTES` from `../character/adapters/shu` (line 10) — another shu-vocabulary dependency living in the generic companion layer (though this one is guarding the `companionFamiliars` feature, which is arguably shu-specific by design, it still adds to the pattern the finding describes).

  Net: the finding accurately identifies that host-layer context-building logic in CompanionApp.tsx still branches on adapter-declared metadata to invoke character-specific (shu) helper functions rather than delegating variant-defaulting to the adapter itself, that this would silently misassign shu's default palette / fabricate a shu-shaped legacy pack for any other adapter that opts into `meta.variants`, and that the existing architecture guard test's regex/scope genuinely cannot detect this class of coupling. No sign the file was fixed in parallel — the code at the cited lines matches the claim exactly.

  </details>

**Fix**

把「初始 variant 怎麼算」與「沒有 manifest 時要不要造 legacy pack」下放到 adapter 工廠（工廠已經收得到 `ctx.manifest`／`ctx.legacyPack`／`ctx.characterId`），CompanionApp 只傳 prefs 的 variant 字串；或在 `BuiltinAdapterMeta` 增加 `defaultVariantFor(manifest)`／`legacyPackFor(entry)` 由各 adapter 提供。守門測試補一條：`src/companion/**` 不得從 `character/adapters/**` import。

**Regression test**

Register a throwaway second adapter via `registerBuiltinAdapter` with `meta.variants = ["alpha","beta"]` (non-shu names) and no `requiresLegacyPackShape`; resolve an `imported` CharacterSource whose manifest has no matching legacy/preferencesSchema palette (or has no manifest body at all) and assert the built `ctx.variant` is NOT `"maid-classic"` / `ctx.legacyPack.kind` is NOT `"character-rig"` — today's code fails this because `rigPaletteForImported`/`importedRigPack` are called unconditionally whenever `meta?.variants` is truthy.

#### 匯入上限 32 MB 但讀取上限 8 MB：8–32 MB 的 sprite 角色匯入成功卻永遠載不起來

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-character-package-019`
- **維度**：`character-package`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`apps/interaction-desktop/src-tauri/src/character_store.rs:36`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

匯入路徑允許單一資產大到 min(manifest.resourceLimits.maxAssetBytes, 32 MB)，UI 的硬上限也是 32 MB；但讀回資產的 `asset_data_url` 硬性拒絕超過 8 MB。因此一個宣告 `maxAssetBytes: 33554432`、附 20 MB sheet 的 sprite 角色會「匯入成功」（對話框顯示完成、清單列為 valid），之後每次選用它都在 `desktop.characterAsset` 失敗、退回文字角色並只顯示固定文案「角色載入失敗，改用文字顯示」，使用者拿不到真正原因。這違反誠實階梯（accepted ≠ 可用），且兩個上限沒有任何一處交叉檢查。

**Evidence**

character_store.rs:34 `MAX_TOTAL_IMPORT_BYTES = 32*1024*1024`；:36 `MAX_ASSET_DATA_URL_BYTES = 8*1024*1024`；:204-209 `effective_max_asset_bytes = manifest.resource_limits.max_asset_bytes.min(MAX_ASSET_BYTES_CEILING)`（CEILING 亦為 32 MB，manifest.rs:18）；:537-542 `if meta.len() > MAX_ASSET_DATA_URL_BYTES { return Err(...) }`。UI：CharacterLibrary.tsx:281 `MAX_ASSET_BYTES_HARD = 32*1024*1024`，:332 `const limit = Math.min(validation.manifest.resourceLimits.maxAssetBytes, MAX_ASSET_BYTES_HARD)` —— 只擋 32 MB，不擋 8 MB。失敗傳播：CompanionApp.tsx:1028 `const sheetUrl = await desktop.characterAsset(...)` 拋出 → :912-914 `catch` → :920 `fallbackToText(gateway, renderScale, "load failed")` → 顯示 gatewayWiring.ts:43 的固定文案。既有測試 `rejects_oversize_assets_per_manifest_limit_and_total`(:706) 與 `asset_data_url_rechecks_path_size_and_magic`(:820) 各測一邊，沒有測到兩個上限的落差。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  逐一核對過程式碼與測試，claim 完全成立且行號皆準確：

  1. character_store.rs:34 `MAX_TOTAL_IMPORT_BYTES = 32*1024*1024`；:36 `MAX_ASSET_DATA_URL_BYTES = 8*1024*1024`——兩個獨立常數，數值確認相差 4 倍。

  2. character_store.rs:204-209 `effective_max_asset_bytes()` = `manifest.resource_limits.max_asset_bytes.min(MAX_ASSET_BYTES_CEILING)`，而 `MAX_ASSET_BYTES_CEILING`（crates/interaction-character/src/manifest.rs:18）= 32 MB。`validate_import()`（:290, :309）只用這個 32 MB 上限做單檔與總量檢查，全程未引用 `MAX_ASSET_DATA_URL_BYTES`——匯入路徑完全不知道讀取路徑有 8 MB 這條線。

  3. character_store.rs:538 `asset_data_url()` 對磁碟上的檔案硬性拒絕 `meta.len() > MAX_ASSET_DATA_URL_BYTES`（8 MB），且此函式是唯一把已匯入資產讀成 data URL 給前端顯示的路徑（CompanionApp.tsx:1028 `desktop.characterAsset(...)`）。

  4. 前端同樣沒有交叉檢查：CharacterLibrary.tsx:281 `MAX_ASSET_BYTES_HARD = 32*1024*1024`；:332 `limit = Math.min(manifest.resourceLimits.maxAssetBytes, MAX_ASSET_BYTES_HARD)`——只擋 32 MB。manifest.ts:505 `num("maxAssetBytes", 0, LIMITS.maxAssetBytesCap)`，`maxAssetBytesCap`（protocol.ts:745）= 32 MB，同樣不含 8 MB 檢查。

  5. 決定性反證測試：`character-protocol.test.ts:175-182` 的既有測試明確斷言 `maxAssetBytes: 16_000_000` 搭配宣告 `bytes: 9_000_000`（9 MB，落在 8–32 MB 缺口內）驗證結果為 `ok === true`——這是專案自己的測試，直接證明前端把這個本該之後讀不回來的組合判為合法。

  6. CPP 契約文件本身（docs/character-protocol/README.md:82）寫明「單一資產 bytes ≤ resourceLimits.maxAssetBytes（上限 32 MB）」，代表宣告到 32 MB 是協定允許、非邊角案例。

  7. 既有兩個測試各打一半：`rejects_oversize_assets_per_manifest_limit_and_total`(:706-726) 只測 1024 bytes 的小上限＋斷言 `MAX_TOTAL_IMPORT_BYTES == MAX_ASSET_BYTES_CEILING`；`asset_data_url_rechecks_path_size_and_magic`(:820-844) 是匯入一個 64 bytes 小檔後「在磁碟上直接覆寫成 >8MB」才觸發拒絕，並非透過 `import()` 走一個 8–32MB 之間的合法資產再讀取——沒有任何測試覆蓋『import() 對 8–32MB 資產回傳 Ok，隨後 asset_data_url() 回傳 Err』這個端到端缺口。

  8. 失敗訊息確實被壓平：CompanionApp.tsx 在 buildAdapter 失敗時 `console.error` 記下真正原因，但使用者可見的只有 gatewayWiring.ts:43 的固定文案 `CHARACTER_LOAD_FAILED_LINE = "角色載入失敗，改用文字顯示"`，UI 不會告知是超過 8 MB 讀取上限。

  此為協定允許、程式碼路徑真實存在、無任何測試釘住的落差，符合誠實階梯『accepted ≠ 可用』的不變量違反。medium 嚴重度合理（可用性／誠實性缺陷，非安全漏洞、非資料損毀）。

  </details>

**Fix**

在 `validate_import` 就以 `MAX_ASSET_DATA_URL_BYTES` 為單檔上限（或把 `asset_data_url` 改成串流／分塊讀取），並讓 UI 的 `limit` 取同一個常數；若刻意保留兩層，匯入時對超過讀取上限的資產至少要回一個明確的錯誤或警告，而不是等到載入才變成無說明的 fallback。

**Regression test**

以 manifest 宣告 `maxAssetBytes: 16_000_000` 匯入一個 10 MB（>8MB 且 ≤32MB）合法 PNG 資產，斷言 `import()` 回傳 `Ok`，再呼叫 `asset_data_url()` 斷言其回傳 `Err` 且訊息含 '8 MB'——證明匯入成功但永遠讀不回來。

---

#### adapter contract 的「資源清理」測試是空的：四個 case 都不會排任何 timer／rAF，斷言恆真

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-renderer-lifecycle-032`
- **維度**：`renderer-lifecycle`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`apps/interaction-desktop/src/test/adapter-contract.test.ts:333`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

檔頭宣稱「資源清理：timer／rAF／DOM listener 數在 dispose 後回到 dispose 前的水位」，但四個 test case 建出來的 adapter 沒有一個會呼叫 requestAnimationFrame 或 setTimeout/setInterval，所以 `expect(rafHandles.size).toBe(0)` 與 `expect(vi.getTimerCount()).toBe(0)` 在 dispose 前後都恆為 0，證明不了任何事。實際擁有 rAF 迴圈的兩條正式路徑（shu-rig 自建 StageRenderer、sprite 自建 SpriteRenderer）完全沒被涵蓋，連 ownsStage/ownsRenderer=true 的 dispose 分支都跑不到。

**Evidence**

adapter-contract.test.ts:129-133 shu case 注入 `new StageRenderer(stubCanvas(), "maid-classic", 1, { autoStart: false, ... })`；stage.ts:497-500 建構子 `if (opts?.autoStart !== false) { this.looping = true; this.loop(); }` — autoStart:false 時 loop() 從未被呼叫，raf 永遠是 0；且 shu.ts:419-421 `if (!this.stage && this.canvas) this.stage = new StageRenderer(...)` 因為 stage 已注入且 canvas 為 null 而跳過，dispose 走的是 `else this.stage?.pause()`（shu.ts:620-627）。sprite case（同檔 140-144）注入 FakeRenderer，sprite.ts:74-79 同理跳過自建 SpriteRenderer，dispose 因 ownsRenderer=false 不 destroy。text/shape 兩個 adapter 完全沒有 requestAnimationFrame／setTimeout（grep 全 src 的 requestAnimationFrame 只命中 CompanionApp、rig/renderer.ts、companion/renderer.ts、rig/stage.ts、perf-entry）。測試現況：`npx vitest run src/test/adapter-contract.test.ts` → 31 passed。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Verified every link in the claim by direct file inspection at apps/interaction-desktop/src/test/adapter-contract.test.ts:333 (matches cited line exactly):

  1. The "資源清理" test (lines 333-378) stubs requestAnimationFrame/cancelAnimationFrame and spies on addEventListener/removeEventListener, then for each of the 4 CASES calls `perform()` + `dispose()` and asserts `vi.getTimerCount()===0` and `rafHandles.size===0`.

  2. shu-rig case (lines 126-134) constructs `StageRenderer(stubCanvas(), "maid-classic", 1, { autoStart: false, ... })`. In stage.ts:487-503, the constructor only sets `this.looping = true; this.loop()` `if (opts?.autoStart !== false)` — with autoStart:false this branch is skipped entirely, so no rAF is ever scheduled by this instance, before or after dispose. No other constructor code calls requestAnimationFrame (verified lines 483-503).

  3. shu.ts:411-413 sets `this.stage = opts.stage ?? null; this.ownsStage = !opts.stage;` — since the test injects a stage, ownsStage=false. initialize() (line 423-424) `if (!this.stage && this.canvas)` skips creating its own stage because this.stage is already set. dispose() (lines 624-626) does `if (this.ownsStage) this.stage?.destroy(); else this.stage?.pause();` — takes the pause() branch, never touching StageRenderer's real destroy/rAF-cancel path. resume()/pause() calls during perform also can't start the loop since `looping` stays false and StageRenderer.resume() (stage.ts:569-578) early-returns via `if (!this.paused || this.destroyed) return;` since paused starts false.

  4. sprite case (lines 140-144) injects a `FakeRenderer` (a stub with no-op methods, lines 96-106). sprite.ts:67-68 sets `ownsRenderer = !opts.renderer` → false. dispose() at sprite.ts:188 only calls `this.renderer?.destroy()` `if (this.ownsRenderer)` — skipped, so the real `SpriteRenderer` (which does own a genuine rAF loop, confirmed at companion/renderer.ts:187 `this.raf = requestAnimationFrame(this.loop)`) is never constructed or exercised by this test case at all.

  5. text.ts and shape.ts adapters: grepped both files for setTimeout/setInterval/requestAnimationFrame — zero matches in either file, confirming neither has any timer/rAF resource to leak or clean up.

  6. Ran `npx vitest run src/test/adapter-contract.test.ts` → "31 passed (31)", matching the finding's cited evidence and consistent with the assertions being vacuously true (0===0) rather than exercising real cleanup.

  Every specific evidentiary claim in the finding (line numbers, code snippets, mechanism, grep result, test pass count) checks out exactly against the current file contents — nothing has drifted or been fixed since the finding was written. The test's docstring promise ("timer／rAF／DOM listener 數在 dispose 後回到 dispose 前的水位") is not actually exercised for either of the two adapters that own a real rAF loop, because both test cases inject fakes/pre-built instances that bypass the adapters' self-construction (and thus self-destruction) branches.

  </details>

**Fix**

至少加一組「adapter 自建 renderer」的 case（shu-rig 用 autoStart 預設的 StageRenderer + 真 canvas stub、sprite 用 canvas 而非注入 renderer），先斷言 dispose 前 rafHandles.size > 0，再斷言 dispose 後歸零；否則這條斷言只是裝飾。

**Regression test**

For the shu-rig case, drop the injected `stage` option (or set `autoStart: true`) so ShuCharacterAdapter constructs and owns its own StageRenderer via initialize(); similarly for sprite, omit the injected `renderer` so SpriteCharacterAdapter builds a real SpriteRenderer — then dispose() must actually call destroy()/cancelAnimationFrame on both, and the existing rafHandles.size/vi.getTimerCount() assertions would newly have something real to prove (and would fail today if cleanup were broken).

---

#### CharacterGateway.handshake 對 adapter.onInput() 沒有 try/catch，違反「任何 adapter 例外都不會擲出 Gateway」並留下卡在 negotiating 的殭屍實例

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-renderer-lifecycle-033`
- **維度**：`renderer-lifecycle`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`apps/interaction-desktop/src/character/gateway.ts:453`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

gateway.ts 檔頭第 12 行寫「任何 adapter 例外都不會擲出 Gateway」，且 initialize／negotiate／negotiated／dispose／cancel／tick／show/hide/suspend/resume/reconfigure 全部都有 try/catch，唯獨第 453 行的 `inst.adapter.onInput(...)` 沒有。adapter 的 onInput 一擲例外：例外直接穿出 registerInstance／renegotiate／reattach；實例被留在 instances 表裡、state 卡在 "negotiating"（不在 LIVE_STATES，永遠收不到 intent 也不會被 sweep 處理）；adapter 沒有被 dispose；而且前一行 452 已經先呼叫過舊的 unsubscribe，`inst.unsubscribe` 仍指向那個已用掉的函式。同一段的 negotiate catch 分支（436-445）也漏了 `inst.unsubscribe?.()`，renegotiate 失敗時舊訂閱的解除函式就此遺失。

**Evidence**

gateway.ts:446-456：`inst.negotiated = negotiated; try { inst.adapter.negotiated?.(negotiated); } catch (e) { this.audit(...) } inst.unsubscribe?.(); inst.unsubscribe = inst.adapter.onInput((event) => this.ingestInput(inst.instanceId, event)); inst.lastSeenAt = this.now(); this.lifecycle(inst, "ready");` — onInput 這一行沒有保護。對照 gateway.ts:399-406（initialize 有 catch 並 `this.instances.delete(instanceId)`）與 433-445（negotiate 有 catch 並 dispose）。後果可驗：registerInstance 第二次用同一個 instanceId 會在 gateway.ts:366-368 因 `existing.state !== "disposed"` 直接擲 "character instance already registered"。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  逐行核對 apps/interaction-desktop/src/character/gateway.ts 目前內容，claim 完全成立：

  1. 檔頭第 10 行明文承諾「任何 adapter 例外都不會擲出 Gateway」。initialize（399-406，有 try/catch 並 delete instance）、negotiate（433-445，有 try/catch 並 dispose+lifecycle→disposed）、negotiated?()（447-450，有 try/catch）都遵守此不變量；唯獨第 453 行 `inst.unsubscribe = inst.adapter.onInput(...)` 完全沒有保護。onInput 是 CharacterAdapter 介面（adapter.ts:105）的必要方法（非 optional），簽章上就是可執行任意 adapter 程式碼的同步呼叫。

  2. handshake() 的三個呼叫點——registerInstance（406）、renegotiate（470）、reattach（516）——全部沒有包 try/catch，onInput 拋出的例外會原樣往外穿出這三個公開 API。

  3. 寫了最小重現測試（暫時放入 src/test/ 跑完即刪除，git status 確認乾淨）：一個 onInput 直接 throw 的 fake adapter，呼叫 `gw.registerInstance(...)`。結果：
     - 例外確實穿出 registerInstance（未被吞掉）。
     - `gw.getInstance("zombie-1")?.state` 停在 `"negotiating"`——對照 LIVE_STATES（gateway.ts:227-234）不含 "negotiating"，該實例既不算 live 也不是 disposed，形同殭屍。
     - 第二次用同一 instanceId 呼叫 registerInstance，確實在 366-368 因 `existing.state !== "disposed"` 擲出 `"character instance already registered: zombie-1"`，與 claim 描述的後果完全吻合。
     - adapter.dispose() 在此路徑上確實從未被呼叫（handshake 對 onInput 拋出沒有任何 dispose/lifecycle 收尾）。

  4. 452 行 `inst.unsubscribe?.()` 確實先於 453 行執行；若 453 拋出，`inst.unsubscribe` 賦值敘述未完成，欄位仍停留在舊的（已被呼叫過一次的）unsubscribe 函式，與 claim 描述一致。

  5. 另核對 negotiate 的 catch 分支（436-445，renegotiate 失敗路徑）：確實只呼叫 `inst.adapter.dispose()`，沒有呼叫 `inst.unsubscribe?.()`，舊訂閱的解除函式在該分支被遺失，與 claim 附帶的次要主張相符。

  6. 搜尋既有測試（src/test/character-gateway.test.ts）：FakeAdapter.onInput 的實作（140 行附近）永遠不拋例外，"throw" mode 只影響 perform()，找不到任何測試覆蓋「onInput 拋例外」情境，確認這是真正的測試/防護空隙而非已被覆蓋的重複主張。

  行號、程式碼片段、後果鏈完全對得上 finding 描述，且用可執行測試獨立驗證了因果關係，判定 confirmed。severity medium 合理：這是文件明訂不變量的真實違反，且會造成永久卡死的殭屍實例＋資源（訂閱／adapter）洩漏，但觸發條件需要 adapter 本身在 onInput 呼叫時同步拋例外，內建 adapter（shu/sprite/text/shape）目前實作都不會拋，故非立即可被外部輸入觸發的 blocker，維持 medium 適當。

  </details>

**Fix**

把 452-453 包進 try/catch，失敗時比照 negotiate 分支：lifecycle → "disposed"、audit、`inst.adapter.dispose()`（自身再包 try）、`inst.unsubscribe = null`，再往外擲；negotiate 的 catch 分支也補上 `inst.unsubscribe?.(); inst.unsubscribe = null;`。

**Regression test**

用一個 onInput() 同步 throw 的 fake CharacterAdapter 呼叫 gw.registerInstance()：斷言例外會被 gateway 吞掉並改走既有的 audit＋dispose＋lifecycle("disposed"/"crashed") 收尾路徑（而非穿出呼叫者），且 gw.getInstance(id)?.state 不應停留在 "negotiating"，隨後用同一 instanceId 再次 registerInstance 應該成功而不是拋 "already registered"。
