#### 出貨路徑（live 模式）讀不到權威狀態時完全沒有自動重試：卡片永遠停在 pending 的「同步尚未完成」，契約 §7.5 的「無法恢復，請重新連接」只能靠人按三次

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-general-mode-ux-024`
- **維度**：`general-mode-ux`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`apps/interaction-desktop/src/components/CharacterSyncCard.tsx:206`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

CompanionPage 永遠傳 sessionEvents，所以 live=true、pollKey 固定為 0，效果 A 只在掛載／使用者按「重新檢查」／patch 接不上（realign）時才重新 GET。後端一時讀不到時：failedReads 停在 1，畫面停在 tone=pending 的「同步尚未完成／還在對齊角色狀態」（暗示正在進行中的事其實沒有在進行），而且後端恢復後只要 session 沒有新的 patch 就永遠不會自己回到正確狀態；契約 §7.5「連續 3 次失敗 → 無法恢復，請重新連接」在這條路徑上不會自動達成，必須使用者連按三次「重新檢查」。

**Evidence**

CharacterSyncCard.tsx:206 `const pollKey = live ? 0 : refreshKey;`，:207-233 效果 A 的 deps 是 `[pollKey, reload, realign, commitSession]`（commitSession 是 useCallback([]) 穩定值），失敗時 `commitSession(null)` 並只把 failedReadsRef +1；:181 `const live = sessionEvents !== undefined;`；App.tsx:970 `<CompanionPage refreshKey={refreshKey} events={events} />`（events 恆為陣列，故 live 恆真）。判定門檻在 statusProjection.ts:1033 `if (signals.failedReads >= 3) return project("unrecoverable")`，而 :1034 讀不到就是 `syncing`（tone pending）。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  用親自讀碼＋最小重現測試雙重驗證，主張成立，行號未漂移。

  1. App.tsx:224 `const [events, setEvents] = React.useState<RuntimeEvent[]>([])` 恆為陣列（never undefined），App.tsx:970 `<CompanionPage refreshKey={refreshKey} events={events} />` 恆傳入；CompanionPage.tsx:309 `<CharacterSyncCard ... sessionEvents={events} />` 原樣轉傳。CharacterSyncCard.tsx:181 `const live = sessionEvents !== undefined` 在真實 App 路徑下恆為 true，:206 `pollKey = live ? 0 : refreshKey` 因而恆為 0。

  2. Effect A（:207-233）deps 是 `[pollKey, reload, realign, commitSession]`；live 模式下唯一能讓它重跑的是使用者按「重新檢查」（:372 `setReload`）或 SSE patch 接不上觸發的 `setRealign`（:299）。沒有任何 `setInterval`／`setTimeout` 對 effect A 做定時重試——已對整個 character_session.rs 確認 `publish_character_session_state` 只在真的有 session 狀態變更（Output::Emit）時才會呼叫，後端沒有心跳/週期性 re-broadcast 全量 snapshot 的機制（crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs:1071/1100/1104）。

  3. 寫了一支最小重現測試（render `<CharacterSyncCard refreshKey={0} advanced={false} sessionEvents={[]} />`，`characterSessionSnapshot` 恆丟錯），跑在真實 vitest 環境下：掛載後畫面卡在「同步尚未完成」，等待 3 秒真實時間（無按鈕、無新 SSE 事件）後，`characterSessionSnapshot` 仍只被呼叫過 1 次，畫面既沒有自動重試、也沒有依 statusProjection.ts:1033 的 `failedReads >= 3` 門檻升級成「無法恢復，請重新連接」。測試通過（1 passed），證實「連續三次失敗才顯示無法恢復」在 live 模式下不會自動發生——必須使用者手動觸發（按鈕或某個會讓 patch 對不上的新事件）恰好 3 次。測試檔案是暫時性重現腳本，驗證完已刪除，`git status --porcelain` 確認 repo 乾淨、未留下任何變更。

  4. 唯一能自動觸發 realign 的路徑，是「之後又有一則新的 `character.session.state` patch/snapshot 事件送達」——但如評述所述，這依賴 session 真的又發生一次狀態變更（例如裝置互動、presence 改變），backend 暫時讀不到權威狀態本身並不保證會有新事件產生；這正是原始 finding 中「只要 session 沒有新的 patch 就永遠不會自己回到正確狀態」所描述的條件，經查證成立。

  5. 也確認既有測試（character-sync-card.test.tsx:204-212）驗證「連續三次失敗→無法恢復」時用的是**非 live 模式**（沒有傳 `sessionEvents`，靠 `refreshKey` re-render 模擬），live 模式那組測試（:280 起）只測 SSE patch/snapshot 對齊與節流，完全沒有測「live 模式下讀取失敗會不會自動重試／自動升級」這個情境——確認這是既有測試沒有覆蓋到的真實缺口，不是「已被測試覆蓋的防護」。

  結論：finding 對程式邏輯與契約落差的描述精確且可重現，屬於真實缺陷。severity=medium 合理（UX 誠實性與契約承諾未兌現，但非資料完整性或安全層級的破口）。

  </details>

**Fix**

live 模式下對讀取失敗加有界退避重試（例如 5s／15s／30s 上限，附 cancel），讓 failedReads 能自然累積到 3；或在 SSE 斷線／逾時（transport 已知的重連狀態）時把卡片降級成明確的「讀不到」而不是 pending 的「同步尚未完成」。

**Regression test**

render `<CharacterSyncCard sessionEvents={[]} .../>`（live 模式）、mock `api.characterSessionSnapshot` 恆丟錯，等待數秒後斷言 `characterSessionSnapshot` 仍只被呼叫 1 次、畫面仍是「同步尚未完成」而非「無法恢復，請重新連接」；接著模擬「有新 SSE patch 事件送達但 baseRevision 對不上」以觸發一次 `realign`，斷言呼叫次數變 2 且需要恰好第 3 次讀取失敗（不論是使用者按「重新檢查」或另一則接不上的 patch）才會顯示「無法恢復，請重新連接」。

---

#### 連接頁的手機卡只看 presence：online 一律寫成「角色同步：已同步」，與角色頁對同一台裝置的 partial-capability 自相矛盾

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-general-mode-ux-025`
- **維度**：`general-mode-ux`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`apps/interaction-desktop/src/pages/connect/PhoneDeviceCard.tsx:323（finding 原引用 322，行號小漂移；statusProjection.ts:1093 本身無漂移）`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

characterSyncDeviceLine 在成員的 presence === "online" 時硬回「角色同步：已同步」，完全不看 role／canPresent／能力降級。同一份快照下，一台 role=input-device（或協商出 unsupported intent）的裝置在角色頁的同步卡是 warn 的「部分能力目前不可用」，在連接與權限頁的手機卡卻是「角色同步：已同步」——兩個一般模式畫面對同一台裝置給出互相矛盾的結論，而契約在這一頁強調的正是「連上 ≠ 已同步」。

**Evidence**

statusProjection.ts:1083-1096 characterSyncDeviceLine：`if (presence === "online") return "角色同步：已同步";`，函式全程沒有讀 role；對照 :962 canPresent 與 :1047 partial-capability 的判定。呈現點：apps/interaction-desktop/src/pages/ConnectPage.tsx:446 `syncLine={characterSyncDeviceLine(characterSession.data ?? null, m.deviceId)}` → pages/connect/PhoneDeviceCard.tsx:322 `{syncLine && <div className="small">{syncLine}</div>}`。測試覆蓋為零：rg「角色同步」在 src/test/connectPage.test.tsx 沒有任何命中。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  親自驗證，主張成立。

  1. 逐行核對 apps/interaction-desktop/src/statusProjection.ts:1083-1096 的 characterSyncDeviceLine：函式只讀 party.kind/party.id/presence，全程未讀 item["role"]，第 1093 行原文為 `if (presence === "online") return "角色同步：已同步";`，與引用完全一致。

  2. 對照同檔 projectCharacterSession（:1022-1053）：對 remote members 先算 canPresent（:962 `item["role"] === "remote-renderer" || item["role"] === "host-renderer"`），online 但非全員 canPresent 時回傳 "partial-capability"（headline「部分能力目前不可用」，:824）。

  3. 呈現點核實：ConnectPage.tsx:446 `syncLine={characterSyncDeviceLine(characterSession.data ?? null, m.deviceId)}` → PhoneDeviceCard.tsx:323（原引用寫 322，off-by-one，屬引用行號小漂移，不影響實質）`{syncLine && <div className="small">{syncLine}</div>}`；角色頁 CompanionPage.tsx 用同一顆 characterSessionSnapshot 快照餵給 CharacterSyncCard.tsx:323 的 projectCharacterSession，兩處資料源相同。

  4. 寫最小回歸腳本（vitest，臨時檔已刪除）用同一份 snapshot（單一裝置 `{party:{kind:"device",id:"d1"}, role:"input-device", presence:"online"}`）同時餵給兩個函式：
     - characterSyncDeviceLine → "角色同步：已同步"
     - projectCharacterSession → state="partial-capability", headline="部分能力目前不可用"
     實測輸出即如此，證明同一快照、同一裝置在兩頁得到互相矛盾的一般模式文案。

  5. 契約 docs/aip/character-session.md §11 的文案表本身把「有 online 遠端成員→已同步」與「協商後有 unsupported intent→部分能力目前不可用」列為互斥的不同狀態，projectCharacterSession 正確實作了這個優先序（canPresent 檢查），但 characterSyncDeviceLine 完全繞過了 canPresent／role，違反同一契約在同一頁面強調「連上 ≠ 已同步」的意圖（PhoneDeviceCard.tsx 322 行上方註解也明寫「角色同步（AIP Character Session §11）：連上 ≠ 同步，兩件事分開說」，但函式本身沒做到 canPresent 這一段區分）。

  6. 測試覆蓋：rg 全 repo 對 "characterSyncDeviceLine" 在 src/test/ 下零命中；connectPage.test.tsx（1061 行）對 "PhoneDeviceCard"／"characterSession" 也零命中，證實這個矛盾目前無任何測試防護。

  severity=medium 合理：純 UI 一般模式文案矛盾，不涉及安全繞過或資料外洩，但確實違反「誠實階梯」與本頁契約強調的「連上 ≠ 已同步」，且完全零測試覆蓋，值得標記並修。

  </details>

**Fix**

讓 characterSyncDeviceLine 走與卡片同一套判定（把 members 投影成 CharacterSyncMember 後再產生單台裝置的句子），能力降級時輸出「角色同步：部分能力目前不可用」而不是「已同步」，並補一支連接頁的守門測試。

**Regression test**

用同一份 snapshot（單一遠端 member: role="input-device"（或任何非 remote-renderer/host-renderer role）、presence="online"）分別呼叫 characterSyncDeviceLine(snapshot, deviceId) 與 projectCharacterSession(snapshot, characterSyncMembers(snapshot, names), signals)：斷言兩者傳達一致的同步狀態（例如 characterSyncDeviceLine 應在該裝置 canPresent=false 時回傳非「已同步」的文案，如「角色同步：部分能力目前不可用」），而非目前的「已同步」vs「partial-capability」互相矛盾。

---

#### 多裝置時「連上但尚未同步／已撤銷」的事實被綠色「已同步」蓋掉

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-general-mode-ux-026`
- **維度**：`general-mode-ux`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`apps/interaction-desktop/src/statusProjection.ts:1053`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

projectCharacterSession 只在「一個遠端成員都沒有」時才看 signals.connectedButNotSynced／revokedDevice。只要有任一台裝置 online，卡片就回 tone=ok 的「iPhone 已連接，角色狀態已同步」，另一台已經連上這台電腦、卻還不是 session 成員（送不出互動也收不到狀態）的手機完全不會在角色頁被提到——對那台手機而言畫面上的句子是假的，違反 general-mode-ux.md §3「連上 ≠ 已同步」「撤銷不是回到空狀態」的原則。

**Evidence**

statusProjection.ts:1045-1056：online.length > 0 直接 return synced／partial-capability，`if (signals.revokedDevice || signals.connectedButNotSynced)` 在 :1053 才被檢查，之前的每一條分支（synced／partial-capability／reconnecting／offline）都會先 return。訊號本身確實算得出來且是多台裝置的集合：components/CharacterSyncCard.tsx:314-322 `connectedButNotSynced: connected.some((id) => !synced.has(id))`、`revokedDevice: revoked`；connectedDeviceIds／deviceNames 都是多台裝置的陣列（同檔 :125-147）。撤銷後 provider 列會留著 Revoked 狀態（crates/interaction-runtime/src/mobile.rs:2509 `.transition(&pid, ProviderState::Revoked, ...)`），所以兩種訊號都可能與另一台 online 裝置同時成立。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  親自讀了 statusProjection.ts:1030-1055 的完整分支順序：disabled → failedReads>=3 → sessionState===null → 認不得的 presence → storeReset → `online.length > 0`（:1046，直接 return synced／partial-capability）→ reconnecting → `remote.length > 0` return offline → 只有到這裡（remote 完全是空陣列時）才在 :1053 檢查 `signals.revokedDevice || signals.connectedButNotSynced`。這與 finding 的敘述完全吻合，行號無漂移。

  驗證了訊號與資料模型都支援 finding 描述的情境確實可達，不是憑空假設：
  1. `connectedButNotSynced` 由 CharacterSyncCard.tsx:314-322 算出，來源是 `connected`（`mobileStatus().devices[].connected`，來自 crates/interaction-runtime/src/mobile.rs:2404 對每一台已配對裝置各自查其 WS 連線）與 `synced`（`characterSyncMemberDeviceIds`，只讀權威 session snapshot 的 `members` 陣列）之間的差集——這個訊號本身是「跨所有裝置」全域算的，不是只在無成員時才有意義。
  2. `crates/interaction-runtime/src/mobile.rs` 的 `self.mobile.devices` 是 HashMap（可配對多台），`MOBILE_MAX_CONNS` 允許多條並發連線，`mobile_status()` 對每台已配對裝置各自回報 connected；session 成員資格則要靠裝置在 WS 連上之後另外送 AIP capability announcement 才會被 `character_session_join`（crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs:381,806-816）寫進 `members`——所以「WS 連著、但還沒加入 session」是真實可達的中間狀態，不是理論上不可能。
  3. 已撤銷裝置：`mobile_revoke`（mobile.rs:2445 起）會把裝置整個從 `self.mobile.devices` 移除並斷線、同時把 provider 轉成 Revoked（2509 附近）並呼叫 `character_session_leave` 退出成員——這與 finding 引用的證據一致：revoke 後 provider 列留著 Revoked，而該裝置不再是 session 成員。
  4. `apps/interaction-desktop/src/test/statusProjection-session.test.ts` 裡所有帶 `revokedDevice:true` 或 `connectedButNotSynced:true` 的測試案例，members 參數一律傳空陣列 `[]`（例如 :160、:166、:231），完全沒有「members 內已有一台 online 成員＋這兩個訊號同時為 true」的組合，證實這個共存分支目前確實未被任何測試涵蓋，也未在程式碼中被處理。

  同時檢查了緩解因素，確認不足以推翻 finding 的具體範圍：「連接與權限」頁另有 `characterSyncDeviceLine`（statusProjection.ts:1083-1095）逐裝置正確判斷（不在 members 裡就回「角色同步：尚未同步（需要在手機上重新確認）」），且已在 ConnectPage.tsx:446／PhoneDeviceCard.tsx 接上——所以該事實並非在整個 App 裡完全查無蹤跡。但 finding 的主張本來就精準限定在「角色頁」（CharacterSyncCard／`projectCharacterSession` 的卡片標題），而角色頁的 `remote` 清單只來自 `members.filter(m => m.remote)`（statusProjection.ts:1036，即 session 成員），連著但未加入 session 的第二台手機不會出現在這個清單裡（見 CharacterSyncCard.tsx 裡 `remote.map` 只渲染 session 成員），這一點 finding 講得沒錯：對那台手機而言，角色頁上完全沒有任何一行提到它，卡頭卻是綠色「iPhone 已連接，角色狀態已同步」。這與 general-mode-ux.md §3 開頭原則「空狀態 ≠ 成功」及其具體例句「連上 ≠ 已同步」「撤銷不是回到空狀態」的精神確實不一致（該文件舉的例子雖然聚焦在「remote.length===0」的單裝置情境，但沒有任何文字把這個原則侷限在單裝置情境，而程式碼行為卻把它侷限在單裝置情境）——這是一個真實、可重現、目前無測試覆蓋的邏輯缺口，不是重複／風格／主觀偏好，也不是已被其他防護覆蓋或已修復的問題。

  </details>

**Fix**

在 headline 之外加一行逐台事實（例如「另有 1 台裝置尚未同步」），或在 connectedButNotSynced 為真時禁止 tone=ok（降為 warn 的「部分裝置尚未同步」），使綠色只在「所有已連接的裝置都真的同步」時出現。

**Regression test**

projectCharacterSession(snapshot(), [member({presence:"online", canPresent:true})], signals({connectedButNotSynced:true})) 應該回傳能反映「還有一台裝置尚未同步／需要重新確認」的狀態（例如 partial-capability 或新的複合狀態），而不是目前會回傳的純 synced／ok。

---

#### 成員可重複灌爆 intents.observed／rejected／failed 計數，甚至為從未收到的 command 回報「已呈現」

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-evidence-honesty-010`
- **維度**：`evidence-honesty`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:887-898`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`settle_intent()` 在收到終態 `result` 時，既不檢查回報者是否真的在 `outstanding` 名單裡，也不檢查它是否已經回報過；只要該 pending intent 還沒被完全結清（還有其他成員未回覆），同一個成員用新的 `messageId`（避開去重環）重送 `result{status:"observed"}` 就會每次都 `self.bump(counter)`。這違反 `docs/aip/character-session.md` §5「已結清的 intent 再收到 result｜忽略（不重複計數，重播灌不進計數器）」與 `session.rs:867-869` 的註解「重播的終態回報因此不會重複計數」——那句話只在**單一成員**的情況下成立。

**Evidence**

`session.rs:873-905`：`position(|pending| pending.answers(envelope))` 只比對 `causationId`／`correlationId`（`PendingIntent::answers`，:1536-1545），完全沒有 `outstanding.contains(reporter)` 的前置檢查；接著 `self.pending[index].outstanding.retain(|party| party != reporter)`（:887-889）對已移除／從未在名單內的回報者是 no-op，`settled` 因此維持 false、pending 不被 `remove`，但 `if let Some(counter) = report_counter(status) { self.bump(counter); }`（:896-898）無條件執行。`handle_report`（:836-861）對每一則 `result` 都呼叫 `settle_intent`，`gate()` 只用 `messageId` 去重（256 環）與 rate limit，`result` 的 `name` 不受協商 `inputs` 限制（`docs/aip/character-session.md` §12.2）。具體情境：裝置 A 觸摸角色（A 產生並知道 `correlationId`），但 A 把該 intent 協商成 `unsupported` 因此不在 `outstanding`（只有裝置 B 在），A 仍可送 `result{status:"observed", correlationId: X}` 反覆推高 `intents.observed`——一台從未被派送 command 的裝置宣稱自己「已呈現」，且該數字會出現在 `GET /v1/character-session/diagnostics` 與桌面進階模式的「連接診斷」。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  親自在 crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs 追蹤程式碼並寫最小回歸測試（複製進 crates/interaction-session/tests/、cargo test 執行、驗證後刪除，git status 確認乾淨）證實兩條路徑：

  1) 同一個已在 outstanding 名單內的成員可重複計數：settle_intent（867-905）在 retain(|party| party != reporter)（888-889）之後，無論 retain 有沒有真的移除任何人，都無條件執行 if let Some(counter) = report_counter(status) { self.bump(counter); }（894-898）。測試：兩個 RemoteRenderer（A、B）都被列為 outstanding；A 用兩個不同 messageId（各自通過 A 自己的去重環）對同一個未結清 intent 送 result{status:"observed"}——B 全程未回報——結果 intents.observed = 2（實測輸出：intents.observed = Some(2)），正確語意應為 1（既有測試 repeating_a_terminal_result_never_counts_twice 只涵蓋「單一目標、intent 已完全結清」，沒覆蓋「還有其他成員未回覆」這個多目標未結清窗口）。

  2) 從未被派送 command 的成員可偽造「已呈現」：director::react（director.rs 116-135）把 BehaviorIntent.correlation_id 直接設成觸發它的 InteractionEvent.correlation_id（=該事件自己的 correlationId 或退回 messageId），也就是觸發互動的那個成員自己就知道這個 correlationId。intent_targets（session.rs 1374-1385）只挑 MemberRole::RemoteRenderer 且 negotiated Exact 的成員進 outstanding；一個 MemberRole::InputDevice（只送 touch，不是 renderer）觸發的 intent，它自己永遠不在 outstanding 裡。PendingIntent::answers（1532-1545）在 causation_id == intent.correlation_id 時就直接判定「這則 result 是在回這個 intent」，不檢查回報者是否曾被列為目標。gate()（697-793）除了擋 status=="verified"（第8關）外，對 Result 訊息完全沒有角色或「必須是這個 intent 的目標成員」的檢查（envelope.validate() 只要求 Result 訊息帶任一合法 causationId 字串，不驗證它對應到真的送出去的 command）。測試：InputDevice 角色的 touch-sensor 觸摸角色後，從未被送任何 command，卻用 result{status:"observed", causationId:"m-touch-1"}（m-touch-1 正是它自己那則 touch 事件的 messageId，也正是產生出的 intent 的 correlationId）取得 outcome=Accepted，intents.observed 從 0 灌到 1（實測輸出：fake report outcome=Accepted / after: Some(1)）。

  intents.observed／intents.rejected／intents.failed 經 Diagnostics.counters（session.rs 700-711）由 GET /v1/character-session/diagnostics（crates/interaction-api/src/routes.rs:1855-1860）對外，並在桌面進階模式的「連接診斷」卡片（apps/interaction-desktop/src/components/CharacterSyncCard.tsx:376-390）逐筆渲染，確實會把這個灌爆的數字呈現給人看。這違反 docs/aip/character-session.md 第88、90行「名單空了就結清…計數」／「已結清的 intent 再收到 result → 忽略（不重複計數，重播灌不進計數器）」背後「同一個回報不得重複計數」的精神，也違反 session.rs 註解宣稱的「重播的終態回報因此不會重複計數」——該保證只在單一目標、intent 立刻完全結清的情況下成立。

  行號沒有漂移：867 settle_intent 開頭、873-877 position 比對、887-889 retain（finding 引用的錨點）、890-892 settled/remove、894-898 無條件 bump，皆與現況一致。

  </details>

**Fix**

在 `settle_intent` 進入終態分支前先確認 `self.pending[index].outstanding.iter().any(|p| p == reporter)`；不是待覆成員就只寫稽核、不動 `outstanding`、不 `bump`。並補一則 `security_matrix.rs` 測試：兩個成員時，同一成員重送 observed 只能讓 `intents.observed` +1。

**Regression test**

兩個 RemoteRenderer（A、B）協商 exact 支援同一 intent；A 觸摸產生 intent（outstanding=[A,B]）；A 用 causationId 相同、messageId 不同的兩則 result{status:observed} 連送兩次（B 全程不回報）；斷言 diagnostics().counters["intents.observed"] 仍為 1（現況為 2）。另加一測試：MemberRole::InputDevice 的成員觸摸角色後，從未被送任何 command，用 result{status:observed, causationId:<自己那則 touch 的 messageId>} 送出；斷言不應被 Accepted／不應讓 intents.observed 計數（現況會被 Accepted 且計數為 1）。

---

#### iOS 開啟 Reduced Motion 時 react-happily-to-touch 完全沒有任何呈現，卻仍回報 result{observed}

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-evidence-honesty-011`
- **維度**：`evidence-honesty`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`apps/interaction-ios/InteractionCompanion/Views/CharacterView.swift:205 (bodyColor now at 240-245, was cited as 239-248 — 1-line drift, immaterial)`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`docs/aip/iphone-companion.md` §4 的表格宣稱 Reduced Motion 開啟時 `react-happily-to-touch` 是「不縮放，只換顏色」，但程式碼裡這個 intent 的**全部**效果都包在 `if !reduceMotion` 之內，沒有任何與該 intent 綁定的換色邏輯；`bodyColor` 只由 `presentation.tone`（來自 state patch 的 mood，與 intent 無關，即使 intent 被丟棄也照樣變）與 `celebrating`（只有 celebrate 會設）決定。結果是：Reduced Motion 開啟時這個 intent 的播放是一段純 `Task.sleep`，什麼都沒演，`play()` 仍回 true → `intentDidFinishPlaying` → `sendResult(status: .observed)`。`observed` 在契約裡的定義是「呈現完成」（`iphone-companion.md` §4 第 4 點、`SessionClient.swift:10,559`），這裡是沒有呈現卻宣稱呈現。

**Evidence**

`CharacterView.swift:205-211`：`case .reactHappilyToTouch: if !reduceMotion { withAnimation(...) { pulse = 1 + 0.12 * CGFloat(playing.intensity) } }` —— 分支內只改 `pulse`，沒有任何顏色語句；`CharacterView.swift:239-248` `bodyColor` = `celebrating && !isEmergency ? .yellow : Self.color(for: presentation.tone)`，`celebrating` 只在 `case .celebrate`（:212-216）被設為 true。`play()` 之後無條件 `try await Task.sleep(...)` 並 `return true`（:221-229），`startPlayback` 於 `completed` 為 true 時呼叫 `session.intentDidFinishPlaying(messageId:)`（:195-198），該函式 `sendResult(causationId:status:.observed…)`（`SessionClient.swift:560-562`）。對照 `docs/aip/iphone-companion.md:107`「不縮放，只換顏色」與 §10 把「真機 Reduced Motion 實機行為」標為 implemented-unverified——目前連模擬器層級都沒有測試斷言這個表格列。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Read the current file directly and reproduced every step of the claim:

  1. `CharacterView.swift:201-231` `play(_:)` — the `.reactHappilyToTouch` case (lines 205-211) contains only `if !reduceMotion { withAnimation(...) { pulse = ... } }`. There is no `else` branch and no other statement in that case that touches color, opacity, or any other visual property when `reduceMotion` is true — so when Reduced Motion is on, the entire case body is a no-op.
  2. `bodyColor` (lines 240-245, one line off from the finding's 239-248 but same content) is `celebrating && !isEmergency ? .yellow : color(for: presentation.tone)`. `celebrating` is set to `true` only inside `.celebrate` (line 215) — never inside `.reactHappilyToTouch`. `presentation.tone` comes from mood state independent of which intent is playing (confirmed via grep — no code path ties `reactHappilyToTouch` to any color mutation). So there is no "change color instead of scaling" behavior for this intent at all, contradicting the doc's claim.
  3. `grep -n "reduceMotion|reactHappilyToTouch|PlayingIntent|pulse" CharacterView.swift` confirms `pulse` is the only state variable touched in that case, and only under `!reduceMotion`.
  4. `play()` unconditionally does `try await Task.sleep(...)` then `return true` (unless cancelled) regardless of which branch executed — so a Reduced-Motion playback of `reactHappilyToTouch` is a bare sleep with zero rendering.
  5. `startPlayback` (lines 190-198) calls `session.intentDidFinishPlaying(messageId:)` when `play()` returns true; `SessionClient.swift:560-562` `intentDidFinishPlaying` calls `sendResult(causationId:, status: .observed, ...)`. So the client reports `observed` (contract meaning: presentation genuinely completed, per doc §4 point 4) even though nothing was presented.
  6. `docs/aip/iphone-companion.md` line 107 (§4 table) literally states for `react-happily-to-touch` under Reduced Motion: "不縮放，只換顏色" — directly contradicted by the code, which does neither scaling nor coloring in that state.
  7. `grep -rln "reduceMotion|ReduceMotion|reactHappilyToTouch" apps/interaction-ios` returns only the three source files (CharacterView.swift, CharacterSemantic.swift, SessionClient.swift) — no test file references these symbols, confirming the finding's secondary claim that this table row/behavior is untested even at the simulator/unit level.

  This is a genuine violation of the repo's 誠實階梯 invariant (claiming `observed` — "呈現完成" — when no presentation occurred) and a real accessibility gap (Reduced-Motion users get zero feedback for touch), not a style or duplicate concern. The finding's file, line ranges, and quoted code are accurate (line 240 vs cited 239 is a 1-line drift, immaterial).

  </details>

**Fix**

二選一並讓文件與程式碼一致：(a) Reduced Motion 下實作一次與該 intent 綁定的短暫色彩脈衝（例如 `reacting` 期間的高亮），仍回 `observed`；或 (b) Reduced Motion 下對 `react-happily-to-touch` 回 `result{status:"rejected", code:"unsupported-capability"}`（或 `substituted` 語意）並修改 §4 表格，不回 `observed`。並補一則 `SessionClientTests` 斷言。

**Regression test**

A view/unit test that sets `accessibilityReduceMotion = true`, plays a `react-happily-to-touch` intent, and asserts `bodyColor` (or some observable presentation state) actually changes during playback — today it would fail because no color/visual change occurs, yet the test could also assert the client still emits `observed`, exposing the same honesty-ladder violation.

---

#### CHANGELOG [Unreleased] 明文宣稱 wave 2 的四項「尚未落地」，但四項都已在 HEAD 上

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-evidence-honesty-012`
- **維度**：`evidence-honesty`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`CHANGELOG.md:15`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

CHANGELOG 的 v0.6.0 段落引言寫「本段只記錄已提交並經回歸的事實；尚未落地的項目（Runtime Session Host、iPhone `aip` frame、桌面同步 UI、iOS Session client、對抗審查）在落地前不會出現在這裡」。這四項功能（前四項）已經全部提交在目前分支上，CHANGELOG 因此對讀者陳述了一個可驗證為假的事實，並且這些已落地功能在 CHANGELOG 裡**完全沒有條目**——沒有證據等級、沒有已知限制，違反 CLAUDE.md「已知限制記錄在 CHANGELOG.md 與 docs/acceptance-evidence.md」的規則。

**Evidence**

`git log --oneline -- CHANGELOG.md` 顯示最後一次更動是 `336a6b6 docs(changelog): open the v0.6.0 Foundation section with the landed wave-1 facts`。之後落地的 commit：`e71ab45 feat(runtime): host the authoritative Character Session and bind it to the iPhone wire protocol, HTTP, SSE and CLI`（Session Host＋iPhone `aip` frame）、`f28bb84 feat(desktop): show character sync state in plain language and cover the iPhone journey end to end`（桌面同步 UI，`src/components/CharacterSyncCard.tsx` 實際存在）、`012ff69 feat(ios): add the AIP Character Session client and render the character from semantic state`（`InteractionCompanion/Services/SessionClient.swift` 實際存在）。CHANGELOG 內文的回歸數字也只到「wave 1 之後」（Rust 945／vitest 1302），而 `cargo test -p interaction-session` 本次實跑已是 14+13+7+43 = 77（CHANGELOG 寫 70）。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Verified directly against the repo at HEAD (8c5ea58).

  1. CHANGELOG.md:15-16 currently reads: "本段只記錄**已提交並經回歸**的事實；尚未落地的項目（Runtime Session Host、iPhone `aip` frame、桌面同步 UI、iOS Session client、對抗審查）在落地前不會出現在這裡。" — confirmed present verbatim via sed/grep.

  2. `git log --oneline -- CHANGELOG.md` shows the file's last edit is 336a6b6 ("open the v0.6.0 Foundation section with the landed wave-1 facts"). `git diff 336a6b6 HEAD -- CHANGELOG.md` is empty, confirming the file has not been touched since, while HEAD (8c5ea58) sits 11 commits ahead of 336a6b6.

  3. `git log --oneline 336a6b6..HEAD` shows the four named items landed in real, substantive commits:
     - `e71ab45 feat(runtime): host the authoritative Character Session and bind it to the iPhone wire protocol, HTTP, SSE and CLI` — verified via grep: `crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs` defines `pub struct CharacterSessionHost` (line 177) wired into `runtime.rs:119/318-355`; `crates/interaction-runtime/src/mobile.rs` uses `interaction_aip::Envelope`/`Party::device(...)` at multiple call sites (session touch/leave over the iPhone wire).
     - `f28bb84 feat(desktop): show character sync state in plain language ...` — `apps/interaction-desktop/src/components/CharacterSyncCard.tsx` exists.
     - `012ff69 feat(ios): add the AIP Character Session client ...` — `apps/interaction-ios/InteractionCompanion/Services/SessionClient.swift` and `apps/interaction-ios/InteractionCompanionTests/SessionClientTests.swift` both exist.

  4. Grepping the whole CHANGELOG.md for these four items' names finds zero Added/Changed/known-limitation entries for any of them — every hit is either the disclaimer line itself or unrelated "對抗審查" references from older v0.5 sections. So the four landed features have no evidence-graded entry anywhere in the file, exactly as the finding states.

  This is a genuine, reproducible documentation-honesty defect: the file's own stated policy ("尚未落地的項目...在落地前不會出現在這裡") is falsified by the current repo state, and CLAUDE.md's rule that landed work/known-limitations be recorded in CHANGELOG.md is violated for these four items. The regression-number staleness detail (wave-1-only numbers) is also consistent with the file being frozen at 336a6b6 while HEAD has moved on.

  </details>

**Fix**

在 v0.6.0 段補上 wave 2 的 Added／Changed 條目（Session Host、`aip` frame、四條 HTTP 路由＋SSE＋CLI、桌面同步卡、iOS Session client），逐項標證據等級（fixture／simulator／browser），刪掉或改寫第 15-16 行那句已被推翻的宣稱，並更新回歸數字為 wave 2 之後的實跑值。

**Regression test**

A doc-lint check that asserts: for every commit reachable from HEAD whose subject matches known 'landed' feature keywords referenced in CHANGELOG's own 'not yet landed' parenthetical list, CHANGELOG.md must contain a corresponding dated/evidence-graded entry — fails today because e71ab45/f28bb84/012ff69 landed but CHANGELOG.md still lists their features as not-yet-landed with no entries.

#### ARCHITECTURE.md 與 v0.6.0-migration.md 仍以「rg 搜尋不到對應實作」為由宣告桌面同步 UI 與 iOS Session client 未落地

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-evidence-honesty-013`
- **維度**：`evidence-honesty`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:275`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

ARCHITECTURE.md §6「進行中，未驗證」與 v0.6.0-migration.md §9「桌面 UI／iOS client：待 wave 3 落地後補（TODO）」都以「本次任務在 `apps/interaction-desktop/src`／`apps/interaction-ios` 搜尋不到對應實作」為證據，宣告桌面 §11 人話與 iOS Session client 不存在。這兩項現在都存在且已被 e2e／vitest／XCTest 覆蓋，因此這兩份文件對現況的陳述為假；migration notes 也因此漏掉使用者升級時真正會看到的變更（角色頁新增「同步」卡、iPhone App 新增 Session 行為）。

**Evidence**

`docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:275-284` 與 `docs/releases/v0.6.0-migration.md:99-108` 的原文（兩者最後一次更動都是 `aa9c36b`，早於 `f28bb84`／`012ff69`）。反例：`apps/interaction-desktop/src/statusProjection.ts:807-861` 的 `CHARACTER_SYNC_PROJECTION` 十句人話（含「iPhone 已連接，角色狀態已同步」）、`apps/interaction-desktop/src/components/CharacterSyncCard.tsx`、`apps/interaction-desktop/e2e/character-session.spec.ts`；`apps/interaction-ios/InteractionCompanion/Services/SessionClient.swift`、`InteractionCompanionTests/SessionClientTests.swift`（實測 28 個 `func test`）。本次實跑 `pnpm vitest run src/test/regressions-v06-general-mode.test.tsx src/test/character-sync-card.test.tsx src/test/statusProjection-session.test.ts` → 50 passed / 0 failed。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Verified every factual claim in the finding directly against the working tree at HEAD (8c5ea58, git status clean):

  1. docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:275-284 (§6 "進行中，未驗證") and docs/releases/v0.6.0-migration.md:99-108 (§9 "待 wave 3 落地後補") both currently state, verbatim, that no desktop sync UI text and no iOS Session client exist, citing `rg -n "characterSession|CharacterSession" apps/interaction-desktop/src apps/interaction-ios` finding only `aip/generated.ts` type-mirror hits as proof.

  2. Re-running that exact search today gives 64 hits, not the "type definitions only" the docs claim — confirming the docs' own stated evidence is now stale.

  3. Both docs were last touched by commit aa9c36b (2026-09-04 21:46:39). The implementations they claim don't exist were added afterward, same-timestamp commits f28bb84 and 012ff69 (2026-09-04 23:26:09):
     - `apps/interaction-desktop/src/statusProjection.ts:807-809` — `CHARACTER_SYNC_PROJECTION.headline = "iPhone 已連接，角色狀態已同步"` exists verbatim, the exact string the docs say is missing.
     - `apps/interaction-desktop/src/components/CharacterSyncCard.tsx` exists and is imported/rendered in the real page `apps/interaction-desktop/src/pages/CompanionPage.tsx:17,309` — not dead code.
     - `apps/interaction-desktop/e2e/character-session.spec.ts` exists.
     - `apps/interaction-ios/InteractionCompanion/Services/SessionClient.swift` exists and is wired live: `ConnectionManager.swift:264` owns `SessionClient()`, `CharacterView.swift:26` consumes it via `@EnvironmentObject` — not dead code.
     - `InteractionCompanionTests/SessionClientTests.swift` contains exactly 28 `func test` (verified by grep -c).

  4. Ran the cited vitest suites myself: `pnpm vitest run src/test/regressions-v06-general-mode.test.tsx src/test/character-sync-card.test.tsx src/test/statusProjection-session.test.ts` → 3 files, 50/50 tests passed, matching the finding's cited numbers exactly.

  Every piece of evidence in the finding — file paths, line numbers, commit hashes, ordering, test counts — checks out. The docs' "not implemented" claim, sourced from a search the docs themselves quote, is demonstrably false as of HEAD, and the underlying features are genuinely landed and wired into the real app (not orphaned files), not merely "already fixed" mid-review — the docs commit predates the feature commits by ~1h40m, so this is a real evidence-honesty defect in the docs as committed, still present in the current file content I read.

  </details>

**Fix**

把 ARCHITECTURE §6 的兩條移到「已落地」並改標 HEAD commit；在 migration notes 新增「§10 桌面 UI 變更」「§11 iPhone App 變更」兩節（含證據等級：browser fixture／simulator），刪掉已完成的 TODO 條目。

**Regression test**

A doc-freshness check that runs the exact `rg -n "characterSession|CharacterSession" apps/interaction-desktop/src apps/interaction-ios` command cited in ARCHITECTURE.md/migration.md and fails CI if the hit count exceeds the "type-definitions-only" baseline the docs assert, forcing the docs to be updated alongside the code that falsifies their claim.

#### 沒有遠端 renderer 時，每一個 Behavior Intent 都被記成「沒有人回覆」而過期，drop-if-offline 被誤報成 intents.expired

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-reconnect-recovery-043`
- **維度**：`reconnect-recovery`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:1356`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`emit_intent` 先算 `intent_targets()`（只挑 online 且把該 intent 協商成 exact 的 remote-renderer），即使結果是空的，仍無條件把 intent 掛進 `pending`（`outstanding` 為空）。空的 outstanding 沒有任何成員能回 result，`settle_intent` 永遠不會被觸發，於是 `tick` 一定把它算成過期：`intents.expired` +1 並發 `character.session.intent-expired` 稽核。桌面單機（沒有配對手機，也就是預設情境）下每一次互動都會產生一筆假的「intent 過期」。這違反 character-session.md §5「只有**真的沒有人回覆**的 intent 才會在 TTL 到期時被稽核成 `character.session.intent-expired`」，也讓 §10 的 diagnostics 分不出「手機收到卻不回」與「根本沒有手機」——而 §12.8 明說 `character.behavior.*` 是 drop-if-offline，§10 本來就有 `intents.dropped` 這個計數器可用。

**Evidence**

session.rs:1341-1372 `emit_intent`（:1345 `let targets = self.intent_targets(...)`；:1356 `self.push_pending(PendingIntent { outstanding: targets, ... })` 無空值判斷）；:1375-1385 `intent_targets` 過濾 `RemoteRenderer` + `Online` + `IntentSupport::Exact`；:626-633 `tick` 把所有超過 `expires_at` 的 pending 一律 `bump_by(C_INTENTS_EXPIRED)` + 發 `AUDIT_INTENT_EXPIRED`；:867-906 `settle_intent` 只從 `handle_report`（成員 result）進入。實跑 probe（只有桌面 host-renderer 在 session 裡，送一次 touch 後 tick）輸出：`Send outputs (commands to members) = 0 / pending intents = 1 / intent-expired audits = [Object {"count": Number(1)}] / counters = {..., "intents.emitted": 1, "intents.expired": 1}`。既有測試 tests/session.rs:1048 `an_unanswered_intent_still_expires_and_is_audited` 一律先 join 一台 remote-renderer，沒有覆蓋零目標的預設情境。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Verified end-to-end by reading the code and by an independent runtime probe.

  1. Code reading confirms the mechanism exactly as claimed:
     - `emit_intent` (session.rs:1341-1372) computes `targets = self.intent_targets(&intent.intent)` (line 1345) and unconditionally calls `self.push_pending(PendingIntent { outstanding: targets, ... })` at line 1356 — no check for `targets.is_empty()`.
     - `intent_targets` (1375-1385) filters to `MemberRole::RemoteRenderer` + `Presence::Online` + negotiated `IntentSupport::Exact` only. A `HostRenderer` (the desktop's own window, joined via `character_session_join_desktop` in crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs with `role: Some(MemberRole::HostRenderer)`) never matches this filter.
     - `tick` (session.rs:624-632) does `self.pending.retain(|pending| pending.intent.expires_at > now)` and unconditionally counts every entry that ages out as `C_INTENTS_EXPIRED` + `AUDIT_INTENT_EXPIRED`, regardless of whether `outstanding` was ever non-empty.
     - `settle_intent` (867-906) is reachable only from a member's `result` report (via `handle_report`); with an empty `outstanding` list there is no member to report, so it can never fire.
     - Damning corroboration: the `PendingIntent` struct's own doc comment (session.rs:1519-1520) states the invariant explicitly: "`outstanding` 是還沒回報終態的目標成員；空了就代表這個 intent 已經結清，不必再等 TTL" (empty outstanding means already-settled, no need to wait for TTL) — yet `emit_intent`/`push_pending` violate this invariant by pushing an already-"settled-by-definition" (empty-outstanding) intent into the TTL-tracked `pending` queue anyway.

  2. Independent runtime reproduction: built a standalone probe binary (path-dependency on the real crates, no repo files modified) that joins only a `HostRenderer` desktop party (no `RemoteRenderer`/iPhone), submits one touch, and ticks past `intent_ttl_ms`. Actual output:
     `Send outputs (commands to members) = 0`, `pending intents = 1`, `intent-expired audits = [{"count":1}]`, counters `{"intents.emitted":1, "intents.expired":1}` — reproducing the finding's cited evidence almost verbatim.

  3. Spec conflict confirmed: docs/aip/character-session.md:92 states "只有真的沒有人回覆的 intent 才會在 TTL 到期時被稽核成 character.session.intent-expired", and §12.8 (line ~221) explicitly documents `character.behavior.*` as drop-if-offline with a distinct `intents.dropped` counter existing in §10 diagnostics specifically for this class of case. The zero-target (no remote renderer at all — the default single-device desktop configuration) case is semantically "dropped" (nothing was ever sent), not "expired" (sent but unanswered), yet the code funnels it through the expiry path and mislabels it.

  4. Test-coverage gap confirmed: both existing pending/expiry tests in crates/interaction-session/tests/session.rs (`presence_times_out_and_expired_intents_are_dropped` at line 857, `an_unanswered_intent_still_expires_and_is_audited` at line 1048) call `join(&mut session, &phone, &device_announcement(), ...)` with `MemberRole::RemoteRenderer` first — neither covers the zero-remote-renderer default case. grep of docs/releases/v0.6.0-test-matrix.md, docs/acceptance-evidence.md, and CHANGELOG.md turned up no mention of this scenario as a known/accepted limitation.

  All cited file/line references (session.rs:1356, 1341-1372, 1375-1385, 626-633≈624-632, 867-906) match the current file content with no material drift.

  </details>

**Fix**

`emit_intent` 在 `targets.is_empty()` 時不要掛 pending：直接 `bump(C_INTENTS_DROPPED)` 並發一則 `character.session.intent-dropped{reason:"no-online-renderer"}` 稽核（drop-if-offline 的誠實記法），只有真的送出去給至少一個成員的 intent 才進 `pending` 等 TTL。

**Regression test**

Join only a `HostRenderer` desktop party (no `RemoteRenderer`), submit one `character.interaction.touch`, then `tick(now + intent_ttl_ms + ε)`: assert no `character.session.intent-expired` audit fires (or that it is instead counted/audited as `intents.dropped`), and that `diagnostics().counters["intents.expired"]` stays `None`/0 while `intents.emitted` is 1 — mirroring `an_unanswered_intent_still_expires_and_is_audited` but with zero RemoteRenderer members.

#### Presence::Reconnecting 沒有任何產生者：契約 §11 與桌面 UI 的「iPhone 正在重新連線」永遠不會出現

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-reconnect-recovery-044`
- **維度**：`reconnect-recovery`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-session/src/state.rs:126`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`Presence::Reconnecting` 這個變體在整個 Rust 程式碼裡只被定義與 `as_str` 比對，沒有任何一行把成員設成 Reconnecting。Runtime 的兩個 presence 寫入點只會寫 Online 或 Offline（transport 斷線寫 Offline、任何已驗證 inbound 訊息寫 Online）。因此 character-session.md §11 的「遠端成員 presence=reconnecting → 『iPhone 正在重新連線』」這一列不可能被觸發：手機在 1s→15s 退避重連期間，桌面一律顯示「iPhone 暫時離線」。桌面 statusProjection 與其 vitest 都用手寫 fixture 餵 `presence: "reconnecting"`，因此測試全綠也不會發現這條路徑沒有生產者。

**Evidence**

`grep -rn 'Presence::Reconnecting' --include='*.rs'` 全 repo 只有一個命中：crates/interaction-session/src/state.rs:134（`as_str` 的 match arm），零個賦值點；state.rs:120-128 定義 `enum Presence { Online, Reconnecting, Offline }`。寫入點：crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:433（`presence()` 用呼叫端給的值）、:1175-1178（`note_alive` 只寫 Online）、:641（`tick` 只寫 Offline）；crates/interaction-runtime/src/mobile.rs:3747-3751 斷線時只傳 `Presence::Offline`。消費端：apps/interaction-desktop/src/statusProjection.ts:1051 `if (remote.some((m) => m.presence === "reconnecting")) return project("reconnecting")`，:911 文案「重新連線中」，測試 src/test/statusProjection-session.test.ts:130 用手寫 member fixture。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Exhaustively verified. `grep -rn 'Presence::Reconnecting' --include='*.rs' .` across the whole repo returns exactly one hit: crates/interaction-session/src/state.rs:134, the `as_str()` match arm. A full listing of every non-test reference to the `Presence` type (18 sites across interaction-session and interaction-runtime) confirms the only places that ever *write* `.presence` are: `session.rs:331/341` (join → always `Online`), `session.rs:429` (`presence()` setter — takes whatever caller passes), `session.rs:641` (`tick()` timeout → always `Offline`), `session.rs:1176` (`note_alive` → always `Online`), and `character_session.rs:395/447` (desktop join → always `Online`). Tracing every caller of the `presence()` setter (`character_session.rs:423` and `:586`, plus one test): `:586`'s `desired` is a binary `if desktop_online { Online } else { Offline }`; `:423` is `character_session_presence(party, presence)`, whose only production caller is `mobile.rs:3747-3751`, which hardcodes `interaction_session::Presence::Offline` on disconnect. No AIP inbound message, reconnect-backoff timer, or adapter path ever constructs `Reconnecting`. Meanwhile docs/aip/character-session.md:128 and :166 both document `reconnecting` as an expected, distinct presence value with its own UI text ("iPhone 正在重新連線"), and apps/interaction-desktop/src/statusProjection.ts:1051/911 plus the vitest at src/test/statusProjection-session.test.ts:130-136 implement/test that consumer path purely against a hand-built fixture (`presence: "reconnecting"`) that production code never produces. This is a real, verifiable dead code path / unfulfilled contract clause, exactly as claimed, with all cited file:line references accurate as of current file contents.

  </details>

**Fix**

要嘛讓 transport 在斷線後、成員還在 stale 寬限期內時寫 `Presence::Reconnecting`（mobile.rs 斷線收尾改送 Reconnecting，超過門檻才由 tick 轉 Offline），要嘛把這個變體與 §11 那一列一起刪掉——不能留一個永遠不會發生的狀態，讓 UI 測試給出假的覆蓋率。

**Regression test**

In interaction-session's session.rs unit tests (or a runtime integration test), simulate a transport disconnect→reconnect-attempt window (e.g. mobile.rs's backoff loop) and assert that at some point during that window `session.members()` reports the device's `presence == Presence::Reconnecting`; today this assertion is unreachable because nothing ever sets that variant.

#### iOS：resume 失敗計數不隨重連歸零，且第 3 次 resume 根本沒送出就宣告「無法恢復，請重新連接」

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-reconnect-recovery-045`
- **維度**：`reconnect-recovery`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`apps/interaction-ios/InteractionCompanion/Services/SessionClient.swift:782`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`sendResume`（與 `sendSnapshotQuery`）先呼叫 `noteResumeAttempt` 再 `guard !local.unrecoverable else { return }`，所以第 3 次嘗試只增加計數、**不會真的送出 resume**——使用者看到「無法恢復」時實際只做了 2 次 round-trip。更嚴重的是 `connectionDidConnect`／`connectionDidDisconnect` 都不重設 `local.resumeFailures` 與 `local.unrecoverable`（只重設 negotiated／queue／resuming），所以失敗計數會跨連線累積：前一條連線已經失敗 2 次時，重連後的第一次 resume 就直接被吞掉並顯示「無法恢復，請重新連接」——但契約 §11 給使用者的指示正是「重新連接」，而重新連接本身並不會清掉這個狀態（只有成功套用一次 state 才會由 `noteSyncSucceeded` 清除）。

**Evidence**

SessionClient.swift:778-793 `sendResume`：:782 `SessionDecisions.noteResumeAttempt(&local)` 在 :785 `guard !local.unrecoverable else { note("連續無法補齊角色狀態，需要重新連接"); return }` 之前；:795-808 `sendSnapshotQuery` 同一形狀；:322-327 `noteResumeAttempt` 在第 3 次把 `unrecoverable` 設 true。:443-464 `connectionDidConnect` 只重設 `negotiated`/`unsupportedIntents`/`nowPlaying`/`queue`/`resuming`；:467-473 `connectionDidDisconnect` 同理；`rg 'resumeFailures|unrecoverable'` 顯示唯一的清除點是 :330-333 `noteSyncSucceeded`（由 :616 空 patches 與 :652 applied 呼叫）。ConnectionManager.swift:672 在 auth-ok 後呼叫 `connectionDidConnect()`。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  逐行核對 apps/interaction-ios/InteractionCompanion/Services/SessionClient.swift 目前內容，claim 完全成立：

  1. sendResume（778-793）與 sendSnapshotQuery（795-808）都是「先呼叫 SessionDecisions.noteResumeAttempt(&local)（782/799，遞增 resumeFailures 並在達門檻時把 unrecoverable 設 true），再 guard !local.unrecoverable else { note(...); return }（785/801）」。resumeFailureLimit = 3（第 71 行）。逐次模擬：第 1 次呼叫 resumeFailures 0→1 未達門檻，正常送出；第 2 次 1→2 未達門檻，正常送出；第 3 次 2→3 達門檻→unrecoverable=true，guard 立刻命中並 return，這一次從未組出 envelope、也沒呼叫 sendAip。使用者看到「連續無法補齊角色狀態，需要重新連接」時，實際只發生了 2 次真正的 round-trip，第 3 次只是計數器自我觸發，這是 guard 順序（先記數再擋）造成的 off-by-one，與 docs/aip/character-session.md §7 步驟 5「連續 3 次失敗→顯示無法恢復」的字面意圖（應該是真的試了 3 次都失敗）不符。

  2. connectionDidConnect（443-464）只重設 negotiated/unsupportedIntents/nowPlaying/queue/resuming；connectionDidDisconnect（467-473）同樣只重設這五項，兩者都完全不碰 local.resumeFailures／local.unrecoverable。全檔掃描 resumeFailures|unrecoverable，唯一會清除這兩個欄位的地方是 noteSyncSucceeded（330-333），只在真正成功套用一次狀態時才被呼叫（consume() 652 行、handleResponse 的空 patches 分支 616 行）。單純斷線／重連本身不會呼叫它。

  3. characterSession 是 ConnectionManager 的 let 屬性（ConnectionManager.swift:264），整個連線管理器生命週期只建立一次，重連不會 new 出新的 SessionClient（不會重置 local）。enterConnected()（auth-ok 之後）在 ConnectionManager.swift:672 呼叫 withSession { $0.connectionDidConnect() }，而 connectionDidConnect 內若 local.revision > 0 會呼叫 sendResume(now:)（461 行）。

  綜合 1+2+3：若前一條連線已經因兩次 needsResume/needsSnapshot（consume() 662-674 行觸發）把 resumeFailures 推到 2（未到門檻，仍真送），連線在第 3 次之前掉線，connectionDidDisconnect 不清計數；重連成功後 connectionDidConnect 呼叫的第一次 sendResume 就是全域第 3 次呼叫→立即撞門檻→guard 擋下、完全不送這次 resume、直接顯示「無法恢復，請重新連接」。這與 §11 文案給使用者的建議動作（重新連接）自相矛盾：照做後第一次嘗試就可能被吞掉並立刻宣告失敗，且除了整個 App 重啟（換出新的 SessionClient 實例）沒有任何路徑能清掉這個跨連線累積的失敗計數。

  搜尋 InteractionCompanionTests/SessionClientTests.swift 及全 repo 的 SessionClient( 確認：現有測試只單元測試了純函式 SessionDecisions.noteResumeAttempt 本身（218-234 行），從未以 SessionClient 物件＋mock transport 驗證 sendResume/connectionDidConnect/connectionDidDisconnect 三者交互下的真實送出次數與跨連線計數行為，這個缺陷沒有被任何現有測試覆蓋或保護。

  finding 引用的所有行號（778/782/784/785、795/799/801、322-327、330-333、443-464、467-473、616、652、ConnectionManager.swift:672）逐一比對目前檔案內容全部精確吻合，沒有行號漂移，也沒有跡象顯示已被修復。

  </details>

**Fix**

`connectionDidConnect` 一開始就把 `local.resumeFailures = 0; local.unrecoverable = false`（新連線＝新的一輪嘗試，符合 §11 給的「請重新連接」指示）；`sendResume`／`sendSnapshotQuery` 改成先 `guard !unrecoverable`、真的送出去之後才 `noteResumeAttempt`，讓「連續 3 次失敗」對應到 3 次真的送出過的嘗試。

**Regression test**

用 mock SessionTransport（記錄每次 sendAip 呼叫並回報 isConnected=true/boundDeviceId 固定）建構真正的 SessionClient，把 local.revision 設為非 0 後：連續讓 consume() 回傳兩次 .needsResume 觸發兩次 sendResume，斷言 sendAip 被呼叫 2 次且 syncStatus 仍非 .unrecoverable；接著呼叫 connectionDidDisconnect() 再呼叫 connectionDidConnect()，斷言這次 sendAip 的呼叫次數真的變成 3（重連後的這次 resume 有被真的送出），而不是直接跳過送出就把 syncStatus 判定為 .unrecoverable。

#### 依賴邊界測試只釘住 tokio：FORBIDDEN 宣告 9 個套件，遞移檢查只斷言 1 個

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-runtime-boundaries-063`
- **維度**：`runtime-boundaries`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`tests/e2e/tests/dependency_boundaries.rs:170`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`docs/aip/architecture-boundaries.md` §1 宣稱「`cargo tree -p interaction-session` 與 `-p interaction-aip` 的結果由 tests/e2e/tests/dependency_boundaries.rs 釘住」，但該檔只對「直接宣告」比對 9 個禁用套件，遞移檢查（唯一真正走 dependency graph 的測試）只斷言 tokio 一個。純 crate 透過 path 依賴（`interaction-session` → `interaction-character`）間接拉進 axum／reqwest／hyper／tungstenite／rumqttc／serialport／btleplug 這 8 個之一時，兩個測試都會綠燈通過。

**Evidence**

line 14 `const FORBIDDEN: [&str; 9] = ["tokio","axum","tauri","tungstenite","rumqttc","serialport","btleplug","reqwest","hyper"];`；line 81 `for banned in FORBIDDEN` 只在 `pure_crates_declare_no_transport_or_runtime_dependencies`（讀 Cargo.toml 文字）裡使用；`pure_crates_do_not_pull_tokio_transitively` 走完整 graph 後只有一句 line 170 `assert!(!reached.contains("tokio"), ...)`。實測 `cargo test -p interaction-e2e --test dependency_boundaries -- --nocapture` → 2 passed，未印 SKIPPED（遞移檢查有實際執行）。`interaction-session/Cargo.toml` 的 `interaction-character = { path = "../interaction-character" }` 不在 FORBIDDEN 內，其下游變動不受任何斷言保護。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Read tests/e2e/tests/dependency_boundaries.rs in full. FORBIDDEN has exactly 9 entries (line 14-24) and is referenced exactly once in the whole file (grep confirms), inside `pure_crates_declare_no_transport_or_runtime_dependencies` (line 81 `for banned in FORBIDDEN`), which only parses the [dependencies]/[build-dependencies] text of the two PURE_CRATES' own Cargo.toml files — it never looks at what those crates' own dependencies (e.g. interaction-character) declare, so it cannot catch a forbidden package introduced indirectly.

  The only test that actually walks the full `cargo metadata` dependency graph (`pure_crates_do_not_pull_tokio_transitively`) computes the complete transitive closure into `reached`, but then asserts only `!reached.contains("tokio")` (line 170) — none of the other 8 forbidden names (axum, tauri, tungstenite, rumqttc, serialport, btleplug, reqwest, hyper) are checked against `reached` anywhere in the file.

  Verified `crates/interaction-session/Cargo.toml` does depend on `interaction-character` via a path dependency (not in PURE_CRATES, so its own manifest is never scanned by the direct-declaration test), confirming the described attack/regression path is structurally real: if interaction-character (or anything else interaction-session/interaction-aip pull in) ever gained axum/reqwest/hyper/tungstenite/rumqttc/serialport/btleplug as a dependency, `reached` would contain it but no assertion would fire, and the direct-declaration test also wouldn't catch it since that package name never appears literally in interaction-session's/interaction-aip's own Cargo.toml.

  Ran `cargo test -p interaction-e2e --test dependency_boundaries -- --nocapture` → `2 passed; 0 failed`, no SKIPPED line printed, exactly matching the finding's evidence — confirming the transitive test executes for real (not silently skipped due to `cargo metadata` failing) and currently passes only because interaction-character today happens not to pull in any forbidden crate (verified its own Cargo.toml has no such deps) — i.e. the gap is real but not currently triggered, which matches how the finding is worded (a latent coverage gap, not a claim that a violation exists today).

  Also verified docs/aip/architecture-boundaries.md lines 17-19 do say `cargo tree -p interaction-session` 與 `-p interaction-aip` 的結果由 tests/e2e/tests/dependency_boundaries.rs 釘住, for the full forbidden list (tokio, axum, Tauri, SwiftUI, WebSocket/BLE/MQTT/Serial libs, etc.) — so the doc's claim of full pinning is indeed overstated relative to what the code enforces. No other test in the repo covers this transitively (grep for the forbidden crate names elsewhere in tests/e2e/tests/ turns up only this same file). Line numbers cited in the finding (14, 81, 170) all match the current file exactly — no drift.

  </details>

**Fix**

把 line 170 改成對 FORBIDDEN 全部 9 項 loop 斷言（`for banned in FORBIDDEN { assert!(!reached.contains(banned), ...) }`），並把測試名改成 `pure_crates_do_not_pull_transport_or_runtime_crates_transitively`。

**Regression test**

Temporarily add `reqwest = { workspace = true }` (or any other FORBIDDEN-but-tokio-excluded package) as a dependency of interaction-character, run `cargo test -p interaction-e2e --test dependency_boundaries`, and observe both tests still pass (green) despite interaction-session now transitively pulling a banned transport crate — proving the transitive assertion only guards tokio.

---

#### `ProviderCapabilityRegistry::high_risk_receptors()` 在正式程式碼中無任何呼叫端；stop-all-sensors 仍硬接 mobile

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-runtime-boundaries-065`
- **維度**：`runtime-boundaries`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-runtime/src/providers.rs:216`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`docs/aip/architecture-boundaries.md` §4.1 表格宣稱高風險受器清單改由「provider 宣告」驅動，實作註記 2 更宣稱「行為上的耦合（要補發哪些事件、哪些算確認停止）已經全部走 `SensorStopOutcome`」。實際上 registry 的 `high_risk_receptors()` 只有測試呼叫；`sensors.rs::stop_all_sensors` 唯一詢問的來源仍是 `self.mobile_stop_sensors(...)`，補發事件用的受器 id 也是 `mobile.rs` 自己的 `mobile_high_risk_receptors()`。任何非 mobile 的 provider 即使宣告了高風險受器，也不會被 stop-all／estop 掃到，而 `StopAllSensorsReport.stopped` 只看 mobile 裝置的結果，會回報 `true`（謊稱全部已停）。

**Evidence**

`rg -n 'high_risk_receptors' crates/ apps/ --glob '*.rs'` 的正式碼命中只有定義（providers.rs:103/140/144/149/216/220）與 mobile.rs 自己的 `mobile_high_risk_receptors()`；讀取端 providers.rs:1278、mobile_loop.rs:3876、providers_loop.rs:1384/1408 全在測試。sensors.rs:10 `use crate::mobile::MobileStopOutcome;`；sensors.rs:255-261 `stop_all_sensors` → `.mobile_stop_sensors(actor, "mobile.stop-sensors", "stop-all-sensors")`，`stopped: devices.iter().all(|d| d.confirmed_stopped())`（`devices` 只含 mobile）。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  驗證所有引用行號與行為皆與目前檔案內容相符，主張成立且未被修復：

  1) `rg -n 'high_risk_receptors\(\)'` 全 repo（含 apps/、docs/）顯示 `ProviderCapabilityRegistry::high_risk_receptors()`（providers.rs:216-222）在正式碼中零呼叫端——唯一呼叫是測試斷言 providers.rs:1278（`registry.high_risk_receptors()`），以及自我定義。`docs/releases/v0.6.0-migration.md:99` 甚至把它列成新增 API 卻沒有任何生產呼叫端佐證。

  2) `sensors.rs::stop_all_sensors`（sensors.rs:255-267）唯一資料來源是 `self.mobile_stop_sensors(actor, "mobile.stop-sensors", "stop-all-sensors")`；`StopAllSensorsReport.stopped = devices.iter().all(|d| d.confirmed_stopped())` 只看這個 mobile-only 的 `devices: Vec<MobileStopOutcome>`。

  3) 補發「可能還在擷取」事件所需的受器 id——`MobileStopOutcome::sensor_ids()`（mobile.rs:806-810，`impl SensorStopOutcome for MobileStopOutcome`）——呼叫的是 `mobile_high_risk_receptors()`（mobile.rs:207，mobile.rs 自己的函式），完全不經過 `ProviderCapabilityRegistry`，與文件表格宣稱的「受器 id 來自 provider 宣告的高風險受器清單」（architecture-boundaries.md 表格＋實作註記 2）在機制上不符——那份清單的『來源』是 mobile.rs 硬寫的函式，不是查表。

  4) 目前只有 companion 與 mobile 兩個 provider 在生產路徑呼叫 `declare_provider_capabilities`（providers.rs:438-439，`Runtime::init_providers()`），且只有 mobile 宣告了 `high_risk_receptors`；declarative adapter（Serial/MQTT/BLE）完全沒有呼叫 `.declare(...)` 或參與 `high_risk_receptors()` 聚合。這代表：任何未來新增、宣告了高風險受器的非 mobile provider，stop-all-sensors／estop 掃描都不會問到它，`stopped` 欄位仍只以 mobile 為準——與誠實階梯「未確認的來源要誠實標 uncertain」的不變量相牴觸（雖然目前系統裡尚不存在這樣的第二個 provider，此風險目前是潛伏而非已觸發）。

  文件的『實作註記 2』確實有揭露 `StopAllSensorsReport.devices` 型別仍是 `Vec<MobileStopOutcome>`（wire 契約、本輪範圍外），但緊接著宣稱『行為上的耦合（要補發哪些事件、哪些算確認停止）已經全部走 SensorStopOutcome』，這句話掩蓋了『stop_all_sensors 到底該問誰』這一層依然完全硬編 mobile、且 registry 聚合方法根本沒人呼叫的事實——finding 對此落差的描述準確，行號亦與目前檔案內容一致，未見漂移，也未見已被修復的跡象。

  </details>

**Fix**

要嘛讓 `stop_all_sensors` 迭代 `capability_declarations().high_risk_receptors()` 對應的來源（需要一個 `SensorSource` port），要嘛把 `high_risk_receptors()` 標成尚未接線的預留 API 並在 CHANGELOG／acceptance-evidence 誠實記為已知限制，不要在架構文件宣稱耦合「已經全部」移除。

**Regression test**

在 ProviderCapabilityRegistry 上額外 declare 一個非 mobile 的 fake provider（含 high_risk_receptors 但不參與 mobile_stop_sensors），呼叫 Runtime::stop_all_sensors 後斷言：其宣告的高風險受器 id 有出現在補發的 SensorStopUncertain 事件中，且 StopAllSensorsReport.stopped 在該 provider 未確認停止時應為 false——目前這個斷言會失敗，因為 stop_all_sensors 完全不知道這個 provider 存在。

---

#### builtin entrypoint 白名單在 Rust 與 TS 各有一份，無跨語言一致性測試

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-runtime-boundaries-066`
- **維度**：`runtime-boundaries`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-runtime/src/character.rs:54`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`CHARACTER_BUILTIN_ENTRYPOINTS`（Rust，gate `/v1/character/hello` 與 adapter 註冊的 manifest 驗證）、`BUILTIN_ADAPTER_IDS`（TS，gate `manifest.ts` 驗證與 `createBuiltinAdapter`）、以及架構守門測試裡第三份 `ADAPTER_IDS`，三份清單各自手寫且順序都不同，沒有任何測試比對它們。只在 TS 端註冊第五個 builtin adapter 時，Runtime 會用 `ManifestErrorCode::Entrypoint` 拒絕該角色的 manifest（hello 協商與 Tauri 匯入都失敗），而 TS 端測試全綠——正是 §4 想避免的「加角色要改 host」以另一種形式復發。

**Evidence**

crates/interaction-runtime/src/character.rs:54 `pub const CHARACTER_BUILTIN_ENTRYPOINTS: [&str; 4] = [ShuRigPack::ENTRYPOINT_ID, "shape", "sprite", "text"];`；apps/interaction-desktop/src/character/adapterRegistry.ts:26 `export const BUILTIN_ADAPTER_IDS = ["shu-rig", "sprite", "text", "shape"] as const;`；apps/interaction-desktop/src/test/architecture-no-entrypoint-switch.test.ts:29 `const ADAPTER_IDS = ["shu-rig", "sprite", "text", "shape"];`。Rust 端 gate：character.rs:975、character.rs:1263 皆 `validate_manifest(bytes, …, &host_validation_limits())`；Tauri 端 character_store.rs:216、431 用同一份 `host_limits()`。`crates/interaction-runtime/tests/character_host_registry.rs:20-23` 只把 Rust 清單釘給一個寫死的 Rust literal，不讀 TS。repo 已有讀 Rust 原始碼做跨語言釘死的先例（hitRegions.test.ts、companion-gateway-wiring.test.ts 讀 src-tauri/src/*.rs），此處沒有。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Verified all cited evidence directly:

  1. Three hand-maintained lists exist with different orderings, exactly as claimed:
     - Rust: `crates/interaction-runtime/src/character.rs:54-55` — `CHARACTER_BUILTIN_ENTRYPOINTS: [&str; 4] = [ShuRigPack::ENTRYPOINT_ID, "shape", "sprite", "text"]` (order: shu-rig, shape, sprite, text)
     - TS: `apps/interaction-desktop/src/character/adapterRegistry.ts:26` — `BUILTIN_ADAPTER_IDS = ["shu-rig", "sprite", "text", "shape"]` (order: shu-rig, sprite, text, shape)
     - TS test literal: `apps/interaction-desktop/src/test/architecture-no-entrypoint-switch.test.ts:29` — same values as adapterRegistry but a third, independently-typed literal.

  2. Confirmed the Rust-side gate is single-sourced correctly: both `crates/interaction-runtime/src/character.rs:975` (hello negotiation) and `:1263` (adapter registration), plus Tauri's `character_store.rs:216` and `:431`, all call through to the same `character_host_registry().validation_limits()` → `CHARACTER_BUILTIN_ENTRYPOINTS`. This matches the architecture doc's own claim (`docs/aip/architecture-boundaries.md` §4 note 2) that "Tauri 匯入路徑與 Runtime 不可能漂移" — true only within Rust.

  3. Confirmed TS validation (`character/manifest.ts:788`, `opts.builtinWhitelist ?? builtinEntrypointIds()`) resolves to the *same TS* `BUILTIN_ADAPTER_IDS`, i.e., TS-side manifest validation (used by `character-manifests.test.ts`, `adapter-contract.test.ts`) is entirely self-referential and never touches the Rust list.

  4. Confirmed `crates/interaction-runtime/tests/character_host_registry.rs:19-23` pins `CHARACTER_BUILTIN_ENTRYPOINTS` against a hardcoded Rust `vec![...]` literal only — no read of any TS file.

  5. Grepped the whole TS test suite and the Rust crates for any file reading `character.rs`/`CHARACTER_BUILTIN_ENTRYPOINTS` from TS, or any Rust test reading `adapterRegistry.ts`/`BUILTIN_ADAPTER_IDS` — zero hits in both directions. So no test anywhere ties the two lists together.

  6. Confirmed the repo does have precedent for exactly this kind of cross-language pinning test (`hitRegions.test.ts:354` reads `src-tauri/src/lib.rs`; `companion-gateway-wiring.test.ts:279` reads `src-tauri/src/character_bridge.rs`), but neither of those reads `crates/interaction-runtime/src/character.rs`, so the precedent doesn't cover this specific list.

  7. The failure mechanism the finding describes is real and directly traceable: adding a 5th id to `BUILTIN_ADAPTER_IDS` + registering its TS factory + adding a manifest would pass every TS test (`adapter-contract.test.ts` only checks TS-internal `builtinEntrypointIds() === registeredBuiltinAdapterIds() === BUILTIN_ADAPTER_IDS`, and `character-manifests.test.ts` validates against the same TS whitelist) while Rust's `host_validation_limits()` — unaware of the new id — would reject the manifest with `ManifestErrorCode::Entrypoint` at both `/v1/character/hello` and Tauri import, exactly as claimed.

  This is a genuine, verifiable test-coverage gap, not a style/subjective nit, and it isn't covered by any existing guard in the repo.

  </details>

**Fix**

在 vitest 加一個測試讀 `crates/interaction-runtime/src/character.rs` 抽出 `CHARACTER_BUILTIN_ENTRYPOINTS` 的字面 id（含 `ShuRigPack::ENTRYPOINT_ID` 的實際值）並 `expect(new Set(rust)).toEqual(new Set(BUILTIN_ADAPTER_IDS))`，順便把 architecture 測試的 `ADAPTER_IDS` 改成 import `BUILTIN_ADAPTER_IDS`。

**Regression test**

A vitest test that reads `crates/interaction-runtime/src/character.rs` as text, regex-extracts the `CHARACTER_BUILTIN_ENTRYPOINTS` array literal, and asserts its id-set equals `new Set(BUILTIN_ADAPTER_IDS)` (order-independent) — mirroring the pattern already used by `hitRegions.test.ts` for `src-tauri/src/lib.rs`.

---

#### CI 關卡只要求「HEAD 現有的 check-runs 全綠」，不要求四個必需 job 都在場

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-release-provenance-077`
- **維度**：`release-provenance`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`scripts/release-verify.sh:65`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

CI gate 抓 HEAD 的 check-runs 後只斷言「沒有任何一筆不是 success」，從未斷言 `ci.yml` 的四個 job（rust / desktop-backend / frontend / e2e）都存在。只要有 ≥1 筆綠色 check-run 就會印出「CI checks for HEAD all success ✔」。因此 job 被改名、被刪掉、或被 path filter／`if:` 條件擋成根本沒建立 check-run 的情況，這道關卡不但不會擋，還會給出全綠結論——包括 e2e（真 daemon＋Playwright）這種最貴、最容易被「暫時拿掉」的 job。

**Evidence**

scripts/release-verify.sh:61 `CONCLUSIONS=$(gh api "repos/{owner}/{repo}/commits/${SHA}/check-runs" --jq '.check_runs[] | "\(.name)=\(.conclusion)"' ...)`；:62-64 只在完全抓不到時失敗；:65-66 `echo "$CONCLUSIONS" | grep -vqE '=success$'; NOTGREEN=$?` / `[[ "$NOTGREEN" == "1" ]]; gate "CI checks for HEAD all success" $?` — 全程沒有任何必需 job 名單。對照 .github/workflows/ci.yml:12 `rust`、:39 `desktop-backend`、:64 `frontend`、:90 `e2e` 四個 job。次要證據：`gh api` 未帶 `--paginate`，預設每頁 30 筆，未來 job 數成長會靜默截斷。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Read scripts/release-verify.sh:58-70 directly (cat -n). The CI gate builds CONCLUSIONS from gh api check-runs as \"name=conclusion\" lines and asserts only `! echo \"$CONCLUSIONS\" | grep -vqE '=success$'` — i.e. \"no line present that isn't =success\". There is no enumeration of required job names anywhere in the script (repo-wide grep for REQUIRED_JOBS / job-name lists in scripts/ and .github/ turned up nothing but the script's own check-runs call). Traced the shell logic concretely: if one job (e.g. e2e) never produces a check-run for HEAD — renamed, deleted, or suppressed by an `if:`/path filter — while the remaining jobs still post `=success`, then CONCLUSIONS has only those lines, `grep -v '=success$'` matches nothing, exits 1, NOTGREEN=1, `[[ "$NOTGREEN" == "1" ]]` is true (status 0), and `gate` prints `✔ CI checks for HEAD all success`. This is a real silent-pass path, exactly as claimed. Cross-checked ci.yml job positions cited in the evidence (rust:12, desktop-backend:39, frontend:64, e2e:90) — all match exactly via grep. Secondary claim (no --paginate on the gh api call) also verified true, though currently low-impact with only 4 check-runs. Nothing in the file or repo mitigates this; the gap is genuine and matches the cited line numbers with no drift.

  </details>

**Fix**

在腳本內寫死必需 check 名單（或從 ci.yml 的 job 名稱產生），逐一斷言「存在且 == success」，缺席即 fail 並列出缺哪一個；同時加 `--paginate`。

**Regression test**

Feed release-verify.sh's CI-gate block a synthetic CONCLUSIONS variable containing only \"rust=success\\ndesktop-backend=success\\nfrontend=success\" (e2e omitted) and assert the gate reports failure, not \"all success\" — today it reports success.

---

#### interaction-agent-gateway 的版本號脫離 release 流程，並以 0.3.0 洩漏給外部 Codex agent

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-release-provenance-078`
- **維度**：`release-provenance`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-agent-gateway/Cargo.toml:3`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

三個 crate 用寫死的自有版本（`interaction-agent-gateway` 0.3.0、`interaction-adapter-declarative` 0.2.0、`adapters/media` 0.2.0）而非 `version.workspace = true`。`release-prepare.sh` 只改根 `Cargo.toml` 第一個 `version =`（即 `[workspace.package]`），`release-verify.sh` 與 `golden.rs::versions_are_in_sync` 也都只檢查四個檔案，所以這三個版本永遠停在舊值、且沒有任何測試會發現。其中 `interaction-agent-gateway` 的版本會被送到外部 agent：v0.6.0 發布後，Codex 端看到的 `clientInfo.version` 仍是 `0.3.0`。

**Evidence**

crates/interaction-agent-gateway/Cargo.toml:3 `version = "0.3.0"`；crates/interaction-adapter-declarative/Cargo.toml:3 `version = "0.2.0"`；adapters/media/Cargo.toml:3 `version = "0.2.0"`（其餘 crate 皆 `version.workspace = true`）。根 Cargo.toml:30 `[workspace.package]` 下 `version = "0.5.1"`。scripts/release-prepare.sh:23-30 `bump_toml()` 用 `re.subn(r'(?m)^version = "[^\"]+\"$', ..., count=1)` 只改第一筆，且只對 `Cargo.toml` 與 `apps/interaction-desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.toml` 呼叫。scripts/release-verify.sh:27-32 只比對四處。tests/e2e/tests/golden.rs:135-168 `versions_are_in_sync` 同樣只比對 workspace / src-tauri Cargo.toml / tauri.conf.json / package.json。洩漏點：crates/interaction-agent-gateway/src/codex.rs:295 `json!({"clientInfo": {"name": "adaptive-interaction", "version": env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION")}})`。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Verified every element of the claim against current file contents (2026-09-05, main @ a94e3d5, worktree clean). (1) crates/interaction-agent-gateway/Cargo.toml:3 is a hardcoded `version = "0.3.0"` — not `version.workspace = true` — and so are crates/interaction-adapter-declarative/Cargo.toml:3 (`0.2.0`) and adapters/media/Cargo.toml:3 (`0.2.0`); every other workspace crate manifest checked uses `version.workspace = true`. Root Cargo.toml:30 `[workspace.package]` currently sits at `version = "0.5.1"`. (2) scripts/release-prepare.sh's `bump_toml()` regex-substitutes only the first `^version = "..."$` line (count=1, asserted with a hard `assert`) and is invoked on exactly two files: root Cargo.toml and apps/interaction-desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.toml — it never touches the three per-crate manifests. (3) scripts/release-verify.sh's "versions in sync" gate reads V_CARGO/V_TAURI/V_CONF/V_PKG from exactly those same four sources (root Cargo.toml, src-tauri Cargo.toml, tauri.conf.json, package.json) and never references interaction-agent-gateway or the other two crates. (4) tests/e2e/tests/golden.rs `versions_are_in_sync` performs the identical four-file comparison — same blind spot, confirmed by reading the function body. (5) A repo-wide grep confirms no script anywhere mentions interaction-agent-gateway in a version context, and CHANGELOG.md's own documented invariant ("四處版本必須相同") literally enumerates only the four synced files, matching the gap exactly. (6) The leak point checks out in context: crates/interaction-agent-gateway/src/codex.rs:295 sends `env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION")` — which Cargo resolves from the crate's own manifest, i.e. 0.3.0, not the workspace/release version — inside the JSON-RPC `initialize` handshake to the spawned external Codex app-server process, genuinely visible to that external agent as `clientInfo.version`. Every cited file:line matches current content with no drift, and no test or script anywhere closes this gap for the three affected crates, so it will silently persist through v0.6.0 and beyond.

  </details>

**Fix**

三個 crate 改成 `version.workspace = true`（若刻意獨立版本，就在 `versions_are_in_sync` 內把它們列成允許清單並斷言其值）；並在 `release-verify.sh` 加一道「workspace 內沒有非 `version.workspace = true` 的 crate，除非在白名單」的檢查。

**Regression test**

Extend tests/e2e/tests/golden.rs::versions_are_in_sync to also assert every non-Tauri crate manifest under crates/ and adapters/ either declares `version.workspace = true` or has a literal version equal to the workspace version, so interaction-agent-gateway / interaction-adapter-declarative / adapters-media can't silently drift again.

#### Release 先建立非草稿 release 再開始建置，任一平台失敗就留下資產不全的已發布版本，且無任何後置檢查

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-release-provenance-079`
- **維度**：`release-provenance`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`.github/workflows/release.yml:18`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`create-release` 是第一個 job，立即以 `releaseDraft: false` 的形式把 Release 公開，之後 `cli`（4 個平台）／`desktop`（3 個 OS）／`extras` 才並行建置上傳，且兩個 matrix 都設 `fail-fast: false`，最後沒有任何 job 驗證 18 項資產都到齊。後果有二：(a) 建置期間 Release 已經可見，`get.sh`／`self update` 抓到的是資產尚未上傳的版本，`curl -fsSL` 直接 404；(b) 某平台建置失敗時 Release 仍維持已發布、綠燈以外的訊號只有 Actions 頁面，使用者端看到的是「這個版本沒有我的平台」。文件自己也承認這一格沒有測試。

**Evidence**

.github/workflows/release.yml:18-33 `create-release` 在 `cli`/`desktop`/`extras` 的 `needs:` 之前執行，`gh release create ... --generate-notes --verify-tag`（無 `--draft`）；:38-39 與 :100-101 皆為 `strategy: fail-fast: false`；:150 `tauri-action` 參數 `releaseDraft: false`；整份 workflow 沒有 `needs: [cli, desktop, extras]` 的收尾 job。docs/releases/v0.6.0-recovery-matrix.md:207 已自承「`release.yml` 在 `v*` tag push 後須產出固定 18 項資產…【無測試：本次未見任何自動化測試驗證 workflow 本身的產出數量】」。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  親自讀了 .github/workflows/release.yml 全文（174 行）與 docs/releases/v0.6.0-recovery-matrix.md:207，逐項核對 finding 的每個具體引用：(1) create-release job（第18行起）確實無 --draft，`gh release create ... --generate-notes --verify-tag` 直接建立公開 release；cli/desktop/extras 三個 job 都用 `needs: create-release`（分別在 37/99/154 行），故 create-release 確實先跑。(2) cli matrix（39行）與 desktop matrix（101行）都是 `fail-fast: false`，跨 4+3 個平台。(3) desktop job 的 tauri-action 148 行確為 `releaseDraft: false`。(4) 檔案在 174 行結束於 extras job，grep 全檔 `needs:` 只有三個各自指向 create-release，沒有任何以 `needs: [cli, desktop, extras]`（或等效）收尾、驗證 18 項資產到齊的 job。(5) docs 引用逐字核對 207 行內容完全吻合，本身承認「無測試驗證 workflow 產出數量」。結論：Release 在建置期間即已公開、任一平台失敗不影響已發布狀態、且無後置完整性檢查，finding 描述的機制與後果均成立，且行號與現況檔案完全一致（無漂移）。

  </details>

**Fix**

`create-release` 改為 `--draft`，新增 `finalize` job（`needs: [cli, desktop, extras]`）用 `gh release view --json assets` 斷言 18 項資產齊全後再 `gh release edit --draft=false`；matrix 保留 `fail-fast: false` 但讓 `finalize` 在缺資產時 fail 並讓 Release 停留在 draft。

**Regression test**

在 CI 中新增一個 `needs: [create-release, cli, desktop, extras]` 的收尾 job，對 tag 觸發後的 release 呼叫 `gh release view <tag> --json assets` 並斷言恰好有 18 個 asset 名稱與預期 pattern 相符，否則以非 0 exit code 失敗（並可選擇性刪除/標記該不完整 release）。
