#### Linux aarch64 被安裝器與 CLI 宣告為支援平台，但 release.yml 從未建置該 target

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-release-provenance-080`
- **維度**：`release-provenance`
- **Severity**：`medium`
- **file:line**：`.github/workflows/release.yml:41`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`release.yml` 的 CLI matrix 只有四個 target，沒有 `aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu`；但 `get.sh` 與 `interact-ai self update` 都把 Linux/aarch64 對應到該 triple 並照樣去下載。實際效果是 Linux ARM64（樹莓派、AWS Graviton、ARM 容器——正是這個「裝置 runtime」最可能落地的機器）使用者拿到的是 `curl: (22) HTTP 404` 或 `download failed (HTTP 404). Private repo?` 這種誤導性訊息，而不是「此平台未提供預編譯檔，請從原始碼建置」。`cmd_install_desktop` 也把 Linux 硬編成 `_amd64.AppImage`，同樣打不到。

**Evidence**

.github/workflows/release.yml:41-49 matrix include 僅 `x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu`、`aarch64-apple-darwin`、`x86_64-apple-darwin`、`x86_64-pc-windows-msvc`。crates/interaction-cli/src/selfmgmt.rs:20-28 `target_triple()`：`("linux", "aarch64") => Ok("aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu")`（:25），且 :71-72 對真正不支援的平台才 `bail!("...build from source...")`。scripts/get.sh:70 `Linux/aarch64) TRIPLE="aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu" ;;`（:71 的 build-from-source 分支同樣走不到）。selfmgmt.rs:500 `format!("interaction-control-center_{bare}_amd64.AppImage")` 對所有 Linux 一律 amd64。旁證：docs/releases/v0.5.1-release-readiness.md 記載的 18 資產組成為「4 個 CLI 封裝＋4 個 .sha256＋6 個桌面 bundle＋…」，即歷史 Release 確實只有 4 個 CLI target。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `medium`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Verified every element of the claim directly against current source (no working-tree changes to the three files touched, confirmed via `git status --short`):

  1. `.github/workflows/release.yml:41-49` — the `cli` job's matrix has exactly 4 targets: `x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu`, `aarch64-apple-darwin`, `x86_64-apple-darwin`, `x86_64-pc-windows-msvc`. No `aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu` anywhere in the file, and there is no other workflow in `.github/workflows/` (only `ci.yml` and `release.yml` exist) that could build it.

  2. `crates/interaction-cli/src/selfmgmt.rs:20-27` — `target_triple()` explicitly maps `("linux", "aarch64") => Ok("aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu")` at line 25, so it returns success rather than reaching the `bail!("unsupported platform …; build from source")` arm at line 27 (which only triggers for truly unmatched os/arch combos, e.g. linux/armv7).

  3. `scripts/get.sh:70-71` — same pattern: `Linux/aarch64) TRIPLE="aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu" ;;` is matched before the catch-all `*) echo "unsupported platform…build from source"` branch, so a Linux ARM64 user never sees the honest "build from source" message; they get a `TRIPLE` value that resolves to an asset name (`interact-ai-${VERSION}-aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu.tar.gz`) release.yml never uploads.

  4. `crates/interaction-cli/src/selfmgmt.rs:126-146` (`download_asset`) — confirmed the exact error text quoted in the finding: `"download failed (HTTP {}). Private repo? Install gh CLI and \`gh auth login\`, or set GITHUB_TOKEN."` (line 141-143), which is what `cmd_update`/`self update` (line 218-229, which calls `target_triple()` then `cli_asset_name`) surfaces to a Linux/aarch64 user instead of an honest "not built for this platform" message — misattributing a missing-asset problem to auth/repo-visibility.

  5. `crates/interaction-cli/src/selfmgmt.rs:497-500` — `cmd_install_desktop` hardcodes Linux to `format!("interaction-control-center_{bare}_amd64.AppImage")` regardless of `std::env::consts::ARCH`, so an ARM64 Linux user gets the x86_64 AppImage (which the desktop job on `ubuntu-latest` does build, so this particular download succeeds but yields a non-executing binary on that architecture — a related but distinct failure mode from the CLI's 404, both stemming from the same root cause of untracked ARM64-Linux support).

  6. `docs/releases/v0.5.1-release-readiness.md:19` corroborates historical Release composition as "4 個 CLI 封裝＋4 個 .sha256＋6 個桌面 bundle" — consistent with the 4-target matrix, no aarch64-linux CLI asset ever shipped.

  Net effect matches the claim: Linux/ARM64 (Raspberry Pi, Graviton, ARM containers) is declared "supported" by both the installer script and the CLI's own target-triple resolver, but release.yml never builds that target, so real users on that platform hit a download failure with a misleading message instead of an honest unsupported-platform notice. This is a genuine, currently-present provenance/consistency gap between what the code claims to support and what CI actually produces.

  </details>

**Fix**

二擇一：在 release.yml matrix 補 `aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu`（cross 或 ARM runner），或把 `target_triple()` 與 `get.sh` 的 Linux/aarch64 分支改成明確 `bail!`／`exit 1` 並指向從原始碼建置。無論哪一種，都值得加一個測試把「`target_triple()` 能回傳的 triple 集合」與 release.yml matrix 交叉比對。

**Regression test**

A CI check that diffs the target triples enumerated in `release.yml`'s CLI matrix against the set of triples `target_triple()` returns `Ok` for (and the set of `uname` combos `get.sh` maps to a non-error TRIPLE) and fails the build if `target_triple()`/`get.sh` claims support for a triple absent from the matrix.

---

### Severity: low（低）— 18 則

#### MAX_ID_CHARS and MAX_STRING_CHARS are counted in grapheme clusters on iOS but in scalars/code points in Rust and TypeScript

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-aip-protocol-037`
- **維度**：`aip-protocol`
- **Severity**：`low`
- **file:line**：`apps/interaction-ios/InteractionCompanion/Models/AIPEnvelope.swift:378`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

Swift's `String.count` counts grapheme clusters, so the iPhone accepts identifiers and payload strings roughly an order of magnitude larger than the §11 limits the Rust host enforces: a `messageId` of 128 family-emoji (896 Unicode scalars, 3200 UTF-8 bytes) passes Swift's `checkId` but is rejected by Rust and TS as >128 chars; a payload string of 2000 `e`+combining-acute pairs (4000 scalars) passes Swift's string check and fails in Rust/TS. Same envelope, opposite verdicts — the parity docs/aip/conformance.md §1 claims.

**Evidence**

AIPEnvelope.swift:378 `guard !value.isEmpty, value.count <= AIPLimits.maxIdChars` and AIPEnvelope.swift:408 `if text.count > AIPLimits.maxStringChars`; Rust uses scalars — envelope.rs:257 `value.chars().count() > limits::MAX_ID_CHARS` and envelope.rs:291 `s.chars().count() > limits::MAX_STRING_CHARS`; TS uses code points — envelope.ts:199-201 `[...value].length` and envelope.ts:220 `[...value].length`. Measured under Swift 6.3.3: `String(repeating: "👨‍👩‍👧‍👦", count: 128)` → graphemes=128, scalars=896, utf8=3200; `String(repeating: "e\u{0301}", count: 2000)` → graphemes=2000, scalars=4000.

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `low`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  驗證屬實。三處程式碼逐行核對: apps/interaction-ios/InteractionCompanion/Models/AIPEnvelope.swift:378 `guard !value.isEmpty, value.count <= AIPLimits.maxIdChars` 與 :408 `if text.count > AIPLimits.maxStringChars`——Swift 的 String.count 是 grapheme cluster 計數。crates/interaction-aip/src/envelope.rs:257 `value.chars().count() > limits::MAX_ID_CHARS` 與 :291 `s.chars().count() > limits::MAX_STRING_CHARS`——Rust 用 Unicode scalar (char) 計數。apps/interaction-desktop/src/aip/envelope.ts:199-201 `[...value].length` 與 :220 `[...value].length`——JS 展開運算子按 code point（等同 scalar）迭代。三者計數單位確實不同。用 Swift 6.2.3 實測完全重現 finding 給的數字：128 個 family emoji → graphemes=128（<=128 通過 Swift）、scalars=896（>128，Rust/TS 拒絕）、utf8=3200 bytes；2000 個 e+combining-acute → graphemes=2000（<=2000 通過 Swift）、scalars=4000（>2000，Rust/TS 拒絕）。額外查核：docs/aip/conformance.md §1 確實主張「同一則訊息，三個實作必須得到同一個結論」的 parity 要求，與 finding 引用一致。又查 crates/interaction-aip/tests/fixtures/manifest.json 中僅有的兩則邊界 fixture（invalid-message-id-too-long.json、invalid-payload-string-too-long.json）只用純 ASCII 字元頂到超限長度，三種計數法在純 ASCII 下結果相同，因此現有 conformance 測試套件並未覆蓋到這個 grapheme/scalar 分歧，不是「已被測試覆蓋的防護」。severity 維持 low 合理：Swift 端檢查只是 client-side 預檢（送出前的本地驗證），Rust host 仍是唯一權威且會在收到 envelope 時獨立重新驗證，所以此落差不構成安全繞過（不會讓超限訊息真的被 Runtime 接受），影響僅止於 client 誤判「可送出」與三語言 conformance 承諾不一致（違反 docs/aip/conformance.md §1 的字面 parity 保證），屬正確但低嚴重度的一致性缺陷。

  </details>

**Fix**

Use `value.unicodeScalars.count` in Swift's `checkId`, string-length walk and the 200-char error-message truncation, and add a fixture with a multi-scalar oversized messageId expecting `schema-invalid`.

**Regression test**

在 crates/interaction-aip/tests/fixtures/manifest.json 新增一則含 128 個 family-emoji 的 messageId（或 2000 個 e+combining-acute 的 payload 字串）fixture，expect=error/code=schema-invalid；pnpm aip:codegen 重生 AIPFixtures.swift 後，Swift conformance 測試會因為目前 checkId/walk 用 grapheme count 而誤判為 ok，暴露與 Rust/TS 的分歧。

---

#### Swift (and TS) silently corrupt large integers in unknown top-level fields, breaking AIP §1's round-trip guarantee, and the conformance round-trip test cannot detect it

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-aip-protocol-038`
- **維度**：`aip-protocol`
- **Severity**：`low`
- **file:line**：`apps/interaction-ios/InteractionCompanion/Models/Protocol.swift:52`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`AIPEnvelope.extra` and `payload` are typed `JSONValue`, whose only numeric case is `Double`. AIP §1 requires unknown top-level optional fields to be preserved 「round-trip 不遺失」, but a value such as `9007199254740993` comes back as `9007199254740992`, and any integer ≥ 1e15 is re-emitted in exponent form (`1000000000000000001` → `1e+18`), which a Rust `u64` field would then refuse to parse. The Swift and TS conformance round-trip tests compare parsed-to-parsed values, never parsed-vs-original bytes, so the loss is structurally undetectable (the Rust test does compare against the original JSON, which is why only Rust actually pins the guarantee).

**Evidence**

Protocol.swift:20 `case number(Double)`, decode at Protocol.swift:31-32, re-encode at Protocol.swift:52-58 (`if value.rounded() == value && abs(value) < 1e15 { Int64(value) } else { Double }`). Ran the verbatim enum under Swift 6.3.3: input `{"traceId":9007199254740993,"big":1000000000000000001,"f":0.1,"exp":1e3}` → output `{"big":1e+18,"f":0.1,"exp":1000,"traceId":9007199254740992}`. AIPConformanceTests.swift:162-168 compares `parsed.extra[key]` with `reparsed.extra[key]`; aip-conformance.test.ts:143-145 compares `reparsed.value[key]` with `original[key]` where both sides came from `JSON.parse` (also doubles). Contrast conformance.rs:209-212, which compares against the raw fixture JSON.

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `low`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Verified every load-bearing claim directly against current file contents (no drift):

  1. Protocol.swift:20 `case number(Double)`, decode at Protocol.swift:31-32 (`container.decode(Double.self)`), encode at Protocol.swift:52-58 — exact match to citation.
  2. Ran the verbatim JSONValue enum (decode+encode) under local Swift 6.2.3 with the exact input from the finding: `{"traceId":9007199254740993,"big":1000000000000000001,"f":0.1,"exp":1e3}` and got byte-for-byte the claimed corrupted output: `{"exp":1000,"f":0.1,"traceId":9007199254740992,"big":1e+18}`. Confirms silent loss of precision (traceId off by 1) and exponent re-emission for the large `big` value.
  3. `AIPGenerated.swift:386` and `:381` confirm `extra: [String: JSONValue]` and `payload: JSONValue?` are exactly the types the claim targets, and this file is codegen'd (auto-generated client), not hand-maintained.
  4. docs/aip/README.md:51 confirms the exact spec text: "未列出的頂層選填欄位：接收端必須保留（round-trip 不遺失）且忽略；不得因此拒絕" — the round-trip-without-loss guarantee is real and explicit in AIP §1.
  5. AIPConformanceTests.swift:164-166 confirms the comparison is `parsed.extra[key]` vs `reparsed.extra[key]` (both derived from the same lossy Double pipeline), plus only `XCTAssertNotNil(original[key], …)` for presence — never a value-equality check against the original. Since both parsed and reparsed independently round the same way, this comparison structurally cannot detect the corruption.
  6. aip-conformance.test.ts:143-144 confirms TS compares `reparsed.value[key]` to `original[key]`, but `original` itself comes from `JSON.parse(read(entry.file))` — JS numbers are IEEE754 doubles too, so `original[key]` is already equally lossy by the time it's compared; the comparison is trivially satisfied and doesn't pin the byte-for-byte guarantee either.
  7. crates/interaction-aip/tests/conformance.rs:199-210 and crates/interaction-aip/src/envelope.rs:41 confirm Rust's `extra: serde_json::Map<String, Value>` uses serde_json's default Number representation (exact u64/i64 for integer-shaped JSON literals, no arbitrary_precision needed), so its `original[key]` really is the untouched source value — making Rust's test the only one of the three that actually pins the guarantee, exactly as claimed.
  8. Checked the two current `roundTrip: true` fixtures (`roundtrip-unknown-optional-field.json`, `version-minor-1-3.json`) — neither contains an integer anywhere near 2^53, so this bug is real but currently latent/untriggered by the fixture set, consistent with "structurally undetectable" rather than "currently causing a visible red test."

  Every citation (file:line) matches the current code with only the ordinary few-line tolerance. All numeric reproduction claims verified empirically by executing the actual enum code, not just reasoning about it. This is a genuine, narrow, reproducible violation of AIP §1's round-trip guarantee for unknown top-level fields (and payload), and the conformance suite's methodology in both derived clients (Swift, TS) is structurally blind to it because both sides of each comparison pass through the same lossy Double representation before being compared — only the Rust suite compares against a precision-preserving original. Severity "low" is fair: it requires an unlisted top-level (or payload) field carrying an integer beyond 2^53, a narrow/uncommon case with no evidence it currently occurs in shipped traffic, and does not affect known/typed fields.

  </details>

**Fix**

Keep the original literal for numbers on the wire path (the repo already has `SemanticJSON` doing exactly this for the hash path) or add an `.int(Int64)`/`.uint(UInt64)` case; add a fixture whose unknown top-level field carries an integer > 2^53 and assert the round-trip against the original bytes in all three suites.

**Regression test**

Add a `roundTrip: true` fixture whose unknown top-level field (or payload) carries an integer beyond 2^53 (e.g. 9007199254740993) and, in the Swift/TS conformance round-trip tests, assert byte-for-byte against the raw source text (or a big-int-safe parse) rather than comparing two values that both passed through the same lossy JS/Double pipeline — mirroring how crates/interaction-aip/tests/conformance.rs:199-210 compares against a precision-preserving serde_json::Value.

#### Integer-typed envelope fields written as `1.0` are rejected by Rust but accepted by TypeScript and Swift

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-aip-protocol-040`
- **維度**：`aip-protocol`
- **Severity**：`low`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-aip/src/envelope.rs:216`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

Rust requires JSON integer syntax: `sequence`/`baseRevision` deserialize into `u64` (a float literal fails with an invalid-type error → `schema-invalid`) and `payload.revision` is read with `Value::as_u64()`, which returns None for a float-encoded `1.0`. TypeScript accepts it (`Number.isInteger(1.0)` is true — JSON.parse cannot distinguish `1` from `1.0`) and Swift accepts it (`JSONValue.intValue` returns 1 for 1.0; `JSONDecoder` decodes `1.0` into `UInt64`). So a `state` envelope with `"sequence": 1.0, "payload": {"revision": 1.0}` is rejected by the authoritative host and accepted by both clients — the exact asymmetry docs/aip/conformance.md §1 says must not exist, and again uncovered by any fixture.

**Evidence**

envelope.rs:214-220 `self.payload.get("revision").and_then(Value::as_u64).is_some()` (serde_json returns None for `Number::Float`); envelope.rs:30-32 `pub sequence: Option<u64>` / `pub base_revision: Option<u64>`. envelope.ts:360-365 accepts any `Number.isInteger(payload.revision) && >= 0`, envelope.ts:180-189 accepts any `Number.isInteger(value) && value >= 0` for sequence/baseRevision. AIPEnvelope.swift:349 `body["revision"]?.intValue` with Protocol.swift:78-81 accepting any Double whose `rounded() == value`; AIPGenerated.swift:414/420 `decodeIfPresent(UInt64.self, …)` accepts the exactly-representable 1.0.

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `low`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Verified empirically in all three languages, not just by reading code.

  Rust (crates/interaction-aip/src/envelope.rs): Wrote and ran two throwaway `cargo test -p interaction-aip` probes (removed afterward, repo left clean — `git status --short crates/interaction-aip/` shows nothing).
  1. A full envelope with `"sequence": 1.0` and `"payload":{"revision":1.0}` fails `Envelope::parse` itself: `Err(AipError { code: SchemaInvalid, message: "invalid envelope (data at line 9 column 23)" })` — column 23 is the `sequence` field. An integer `1` in the same position parses fine. This confirms `sequence`/`base_revision: Option<u64>` reject JSON float syntax during serde deserialization, before `validate()` even runs.
  2. Isolating the other half: valid integer `sequence`, but `"payload":{"revision":1.0}` — this parses fine (payload is untyped `Value`), but `env.validate()` returns `Err(AipError { code: SchemaInvalid, message: "state requires payload.revision" })`, confirming `Value::as_u64()` (envelope.rs:216, no line drift) returns `None` for a JSON float literal even when it's integral (matches serde_json's `Number::as_u64` only matching the `N::PosInt` variant).

  TypeScript (apps/interaction-desktop/src/aip/envelope.ts): Read the code — `sequence`/`baseRevision` (lines ~178-186) and `payload.revision` (lines ~356-364) both gate only on `typeof value === "number" && Number.isInteger(value) && value >= 0`. Ran `node -e 'JSON.parse(...)'` directly: `JSON.parse('{"sequence":1.0,"payload":{"revision":1.0}}')` yields `sequence: 1, isInteger: true` and `revision: 1, isInteger: true` — JS has one number type, so `1.0` and `1` are indistinguishable post-parse and both checks pass.

  Swift: Read `Protocol.swift:78-81` (`JSONValue.intValue` returns `Int(value)` whenever `value.rounded() == value`, used at `AIPEnvelope.swift:349` for `payload.revision`) and `AIPGenerated.swift:414/420` (`decodeIfPresent(UInt64.self, ...)` for `sequence`/`baseRevision`). Then actually compiled and ran a minimal Swift script (`swift <script>.swift` with `DEVELOPER_DIR` set) mirroring the real `Codable` decode path: `JSONDecoder().decode` of `{"sequence": 1.0}` into a `UInt64?` field succeeds and yields `1`, while `{"sequence": 1.5}` correctly fails with `dataCorrupted` — confirming Foundation's JSONDecoder accepts exactly-representable float literals for fixed-width integers.

  Checked `docs/aip/conformance.md` §1: it explicitly states the three implementations must reach the *same* conclusion for every fixture, framing divergence as a security-boundary leak (Rust rejects → WebView accepts → attack surface moves to JS). Grepped `crates/interaction-aip/tests/fixtures/` and the three languages' unit/conformance test files for any float-literal-in-sequence/revision case: none exists, confirming the "uncovered by any fixture" claim.

  No file-content drift: the cited `envelope.rs:216` line (`.and_then(Value::as_u64)`) matches exactly what's in the file today. All cited evidence lines in envelope.ts, AIPEnvelope.swift, and AIPGenerated.swift also match current content. This is a genuine, reproducible, currently-unfixed cross-language asymmetry.

  </details>

**Fix**

Either relax Rust (accept integral floats via a custom deserializer) or tighten TS/Swift (reject when the raw token contains `.`/`e`), then pin the choice with a fixture carrying `"sequence": 1.0` and `"revision": 1.0`.

**Regression test**

Add fixture(s) to crates/interaction-aip/tests/fixtures/ with a `state` envelope where `sequence`, `baseRevision`, and `payload.revision` are encoded as JSON float literals (e.g. `1.0`) with an expected `error`/`schema-invalid` verdict, referenced from manifest.json so all three conformance suites (Rust `conformance.rs`, TS `aip-conformance.test.ts`, Swift `AIPConformanceTests.swift`) must agree — today TS and Swift would fail such a fixture by accepting it.

#### 未成為成員的裝置送出的第一則 capability frame 完全跳過 §8 第 1 關（schema／payload 上限／深度／字串長度）

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-identity-binding-008`
- **維度**：`identity-binding`
- **Severity**：`low`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs:776（announcement 反序列化在 804-816 行；character_session_join 呼叫在 817 行）`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`character_session_device_capability` 對「還不是成員」的 party 直接 `serde_json::from_value::<CapabilityAnnouncement>(envelope.payload)` 後呼叫 `character_session_join`，整條路徑沒有任何一處呼叫 `Envelope::validate()`。`docs/aip/character-session.md` §8 規定每則來自 device／renderer 的訊息第一關就是「schema／profile 驗證 → payload ≤ 32 KiB、深度、字串長度」，這條 join 路徑只剩 `Envelope::parse` 的 64 KiB 整包上限。

**Evidence**

character_session.rs:776-830：成員走 `character_session_submit`（→ gate 第 1 關 `envelope.validate()`，session.rs:696-699），非成員則落到 :810-830 直接 `character_session_join(party, &announcement)`。`Envelope::parse`（crates/interaction-aip/src/envelope.rs:110-122）註解自陳「不做 profile 驗證（見 Envelope::validate）」，只檢查 `MAX_MESSAGE_BYTES = 65536`。因此第一則 capability 的 payload 可達約 64 KiB（>`MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES = 32768`）、巢狀深度可超過 `MAX_JSON_DEPTH = 8`、單一字串可超過 `MAX_STRING_CHARS = 2000`，`messageId` 可超過 `MAX_ID_CHARS = 128`、`name` 可完全不符 `is_valid_name` 文法，而且這些值會被當成 `causationId` 回送（見另一則 finding）。`negotiate_capabilities` 也不限制 `announcement.inputs` 的筆數與長度，未被 host 接受的 name 會整批留在 `NegotiatedCapabilities.unsupported_inputs`（capability.rs:117-125）並存進成員紀錄。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `low`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  逐層讀碼證實整條 claim。(1) crates/interaction-aip/src/envelope.rs:110-122 `Envelope::parse` 只檢查 `bytes.len() > MAX_MESSAGE_BYTES`（65536），註解明講「不做 profile 驗證」；schema/profile/payload≤32768/深度≤8/字串≤2000/check_id 全部只存在於 `Envelope::validate()`（envelope.rs:141-249，呼叫 `check_payload` 於 217 行）。(2) `character_session_device_frame` 的共用前段（character_session.rs 671-716）只呼叫 `Envelope::parse`＋`negotiate_version`＋`message_type.is_known()`＋`bind_identity`，全程未呼叫 `validate()`。(3) `character_session_device_capability`（776 行起）在 `character_session_is_member(party)` 為 false 時，804-816 行直接 `serde_json::from_value::<CapabilityAnnouncement>(envelope.payload.clone())` 再呼叫 817 行 `character_session_join`；全函式沒有 `validate()`／`check_payload()`。(4) `character_session_join`（381-399）→ `session.join()`（interaction-session/src/session.rs 315-383）只呼叫 `negotiate_capabilities`，同樣沒有大小/深度檢查；`negotiate_capabilities`（interaction-aip/src/capability.rs 94-141）對 `announcement.inputs/intents/profiles/features` 的筆數與長度完全不設限。(5) 對照組：member 走的 `character_session_submit`→`session.submit`→`gate()`，gate 第 1 關（session.rs 703-706）明確 `envelope.validate()`；這是 join 路徑唯一沒有複製的一關。(6) 全 repo grep `\.validate\(\)|check_payload` 只在 session.rs:704（gate）與 character_session.rs:944（另一條與此無關的路徑）出現各一次，join 路徑零覆蓋。(7) docs/aip/character-session.md §8（133-140 行）明文規定「每則來自 device／renderer 的訊息」順序固定為 bytes≤64KiB→JSON 解析→schema/profile 驗證→payload≤32KiB/深度/字串長度→…→membership，並未把「第一則 capability（未 join）」排除在外，故此為對 spec 的明確違反。(8) crates/interaction-session/tests/security_matrix.rs 80-95 行的 `oversized_payloads_are_rejected_before_anything_is_applied` 用的是 `joined_session()`（測試輔助函式直接呼叫 `session.join()` 先建好會員），驗證的是『已是會員後』`submit` 的 gate，完全沒有涵蓋『第一則 capability join 本身』的大小/深度/字串上限——所以這不是既有測試涵蓋、也不是已修復的缺陷。所有常數（MAX_MESSAGE_BYTES=65536／MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES=32768／MAX_ID_CHARS=128／MAX_STRING_CHARS=2000／MAX_JSON_DEPTH=8，limits.rs 1-14 行）與 finding 描述完全吻合，行號未漂移。影響範圍如 finding 所述：已通過 transport／TLS 身分綁定但尚未成為 session 成員的裝置，其第一則 capability envelope 可在 64KiB 總上限內夾帶 >32KiB payload、>8 層巢狀、>2000 字元字串、>128 字元 messageId（且可含控制字元）、不符文法的 name，且 messageId 會被原樣塞進錯誤/成功回覆的 causationId。嚴重度確實偏低（需要已認證裝置、非任意匿名者；不會 panic；只是浪費頻寬/記憶體並違反規格保證的不變量，而非直接可用於接管會話），故 low 合理。

  </details>

**Fix**

在 `character_session_device_frame` 解析成功後、分派到 capability／query／submit 之前統一呼叫 `envelope.validate()`，讓非成員的第一則 capability 與其他訊息共用同一條固定管線；並對 `CapabilityAnnouncement.intents／inputs` 加上筆數與單項長度上限。

**Regression test**

在 interaction-session crate 建一個全新 CharacterSession，直接呼叫 session.join(stranger_party, &CapabilityAnnouncement{ inputs: vec!["x".repeat(40_000)], ..Default::default() }, now)（不透過 joined_session 這種先 join 好的輔助函式），斷言回傳 Err 而不是 Ok——目前會是 Ok，因為 negotiate_capabilities 不檢查長度；同樣可用巢狀 9 層以上的 extra 欄位或 200 字元 messageId 驗證深度／id 檢查也同樣缺席。

#### 重複 messageId 的 query 會被再次執行（去重環對 resume／snapshot 失效）

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-identity-binding-009`
- **維度**：`identity-binding`
- **Severity**：`low`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs: resume() at 577 (bump(C_RESUMES) at 585); snapshot_envelope_inner() at 1252 (next_sequence() at 1253, bump(C_SNAPSHOTS) at 1270). character_session.rs:864 citation is exact, no drift.`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`character_session_device_query` 只在 `submission.error.is_some()` 時短路返回；`gate` 的去重命中走的是 `Gate::Duplicate` 分支，回傳 `Submission { outcome: Accepted, error: None, reply: true }`（session.rs:456-472），因此重播同一個 `messageId` 的 `query character.session.resume`／`snapshot` 會被**再執行一次**，而且原本該回給對方的 `accepted{duplicate:true}` result 被丟掉、改回一則全新的 `response`。這違反 AIP §7「重複回 accepted{duplicate:true} 不重套用」。

**Evidence**

character_session.rs:864-866 `if submission.error.is_some() { return AipFrameOutcome::reply(submission.result); }`，之後 :867-926 依 `name` 執行 resume／snapshot。session.rs:456-472 `Ok(Gate::Duplicate)` 分支明確設 `error: None`。`snapshot_envelope_inner`（session.rs:1248-1277）每次都 `self.next_sequence()` 並 `bump(C_SNAPSHOTS)`，`resume`（session.rs:570-576）`bump(C_RESUMES)`，所以重播會消耗 sequence 並灌大 diagnostics 計數器。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `low`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Traced the full path and it holds exactly as claimed.

  1. `crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs:844-926` `character_session_device_query` is the live handler for `MessageType::Query` frames (dispatched from `character_session_frame` at line 723-724, reachable for any paired device sending a `query` envelope over the WS transport).

  2. It calls `character_session_submit` (character_session.rs:453-466), which forwards straight to `CharacterSession::submit` in `crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:452-473`.

  3. In `submit`, the dedup check happens generically inside `gate()` step 12 (session.rs:783-785, comment: "去重（重複回 accepted{duplicate:true}，不重套用）") — this applies to every allowed `MessageType` including `Query` (step 9 at session.rs:757-764 explicitly allows `Query` through the pipeline). On a repeat `messageId`, `gate` returns `Ok(Gate::Duplicate)` and `submit` short-circuits *before* ever calling `dispatch`, producing `Submission { outcome: Accepted, error: None, reply: true, result: <accepted/duplicate:true envelope> }` (session.rs:460-472, confirmed verbatim, only ~2 lines off the cited 456-472).

  4. Back in `character_session_device_query`, the only guard is `if submission.error.is_some() { return AipFrameOutcome::reply(submission.result); }` at character_session.rs:864 (exact line match, no drift). Since duplicates carry `error: None`, this guard does not fire, and execution falls through to `match name.as_str()`, which unconditionally calls `character_session_resume(...)` or `character_session_snapshot_envelope(...)` again (character_session.rs:867-903).

  5. Those wrappers (character_session.rs:504-527) call straight into `session.resume(...)` / `session.snapshot_envelope(...)`, which are NOT dedup-aware: `resume()` unconditionally calls `self.bump(C_RESUMES)` (session.rs:585, function starts at 577 — cited range 570-576 is ~7-9 lines off but the substance is right), and `snapshot_envelope_inner` unconditionally calls `self.next_sequence()` (consuming a real monotonic sequence number) and `self.bump(C_SNAPSHOTS)` (session.rs:1253 and 1270, function starts at 1252 — cited range 1248-1277 correctly encloses both).

  6. Confirmed against contract: `docs/aip/README.md` §7 (line 195-196) states plainly "去重：host 對每個 (session, source) 保留 256 筆 messageId 環；重複回 accepted{duplicate:true} 不重套用" with no message-type carve-out, and the dedup check inside `gate()` is applied uniformly regardless of message type. So a replayed `character.session.resume` / `character.session.snapshot` query genuinely: (a) discards the correct `accepted{duplicate:true}` result the session layer already computed, (b) re-executes resume/snapshot logic a second time, (c) consumes an extra real sequence number and inflates `resumes`/`snapshots` diagnostic counters — which is observable via the public `diagnostics()` accessor (session.rs:682).

  7. Checked for existing coverage: `crates/interaction-runtime/tests/character_session_loop.rs:748-768` has `duplicate_touch_is_accepted_once_and_never_reapplied`, but it only exercises an `event` (touch) duplicate through the capability/report path, not a `query` (resume/snapshot) duplicate through `character_session_device_query`. No test in the runtime crate covers this path, so the gap is real and unguarded.

  Net: this is not fixed, not already tested, and not a matter of style — it is a genuine idempotency/dedup-bypass bug on a reachable code path, with only minor (non-substantive) line-number drift on two of the four citations.

  </details>

**Fix**

在 `character_session_device_query` 改用 `submission.outcome`／`result.payload["duplicate"]` 判斷：命中去重就直接回 `submission.result`（`accepted{duplicate:true}`），不再重跑 resume／snapshot。

**Regression test**

Over a paired device WS connection, send the same `character.session.snapshot` query envelope (identical messageId) twice; assert the second `response` payload's `sequence`/`revision` snapshot fields differ from the first (proving it was recomputed rather than replayed) and/or that `session.diagnostics().counters["snapshots"]` increments by 2 instead of 1 — where AIP §7 requires the duplicate to be answered from the cached `accepted{duplicate:true}` result with no re-execution.

---

#### 去重環在 emergency 關卡之前就被消耗：被拒的訊息重送會拿到 accepted{duplicate:true} 卻從未被套用

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-session-integrity-062`
- **維度**：`session-integrity`
- **Severity**：`low`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:788`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`gate` 第 12 關 `self.members[index].dedupe.note(&envelope.message_id)` 排在第 13 關 emergency 檢查之前，`handle_event` 內部的 schema／unknown-name 拒絕也在它之後。於是一則因為緊急停止而被 `rejected{scope-denied}` 的訊息已經佔用了 messageId；解除緊急停止後用同一個 messageId 重送，host 回 `accepted{duplicate:true}` 但完全沒有套用——回覆的語意（accepted）與實際發生的事（什麼都沒做、而且上一次是被拒絕）不一致。

**Evidence**

session.rs:787-796：`if !self.members[index].dedupe.note(&envelope.message_id) { return Ok(Gate::Duplicate); }` 之後才是 `if self.state.truth.state == TruthState::Emergency && ...`。實測：
  during estop           : outcome=Rejected err=Some(ScopeDenied)
  resent after clear     : outcome=Accepted err=None dup=Some(Bool(true))
  activity after resend  : Idle   // 從未套用

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `low`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Code inspection confirms the exact ordering cited: `crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:788` calls `self.members[index].dedupe.note(&envelope.message_id)` (gate step 12) strictly before the emergency check at line 792 (gate step 13, `TruthState::Emergency && name.starts_with("character.interaction.")` → `ScopeDenied`). `DedupeRing::note` (`crates/interaction-aip/src/envelope.rs:365`) unconditionally records the id and returns `true` the first time it is seen, with no rollback path anywhere in `rejection()` (session.rs:1095-1123) — a later rejection from *any* downstream check (emergency, and also the post-gate schema/unknown-name checks inside `handle_event`, which run after `Gate::Proceed`) does not un-note the id.

  I wrote a standalone reproduction crate (path-depending on the real `interaction-session`/`interaction-aip`/`interaction-character` crates, no repo files modified) that joins a device, engages `RuntimeFact::Emergency{engaged:true}`, sends `touch("m2", ...)`, clears the emergency, then resends the *same* `touch("m2", ...)`. Output:
  ```
  during estop           : outcome=Rejected err=Some(ScopeDenied)
  resent after clear     : outcome=Accepted err=None dup=Some(Bool(true))
  activity after resend  : Idle
  fresh id after clear    : outcome=Applied err=None
  activity after fresh id : Reacting
  ```
  This exactly reproduces the finding's cited evidence: the id-reused resend is silently swallowed (`Accepted`+`duplicate:true`, activity stays `Idle`), while a fresh-id resend (`m3`) applies normally (`Applied`, activity `Reacting`), proving the failure is specifically the dedupe-before-emergency-gate ordering and not e.g. residual frozen state.

  I also checked `docs/aip/README.md:114/195` and `docs/aip/character-session.md:55`, which document duplicate semantics as "same messageId sent again → `accepted{duplicate:true}`, not reapplied" — i.e. the spec's mental model is that the *first* delivery was accepted/applied, so telling the caller "duplicate" is honest only when something actually happened the first time. The existing regression test `duplicate_message_id_is_accepted_once_and_never_reapplied` (`crates/interaction-session/tests/session.rs:391`) only covers first=Applied/second=duplicate; it does not cover first=Rejected/second=duplicate, so this gap is real and untested. This is a genuine, reproducible honesty-ladder inconsistency: a rejected (never-applied) message permanently burns its messageId, so an idempotent retry after conditions change (e.g. emergency cleared) gets a misleading `accepted{duplicate:true}` instead of being reprocessed.

  </details>

**Fix**

只在訊息真的通過所有關卡、準備進入 `dispatch` 時才 `note()`（或在被拒時把該 messageId 從環裡撤回），讓 `accepted{duplicate:true}` 只用在「上一次真的被套用過」的重複訊息上。

**Regression test**

In `crates/interaction-session/tests/session.rs`, add a test that: joins a device, engages emergency, submits `touch("m2", ...)` and asserts `Rejected`/`ScopeDenied`, clears emergency, resubmits `touch("m2", ...)` (same id) and asserts it is `Applied` (not `Accepted` with `duplicate:true`) with the character's activity actually transitioning — i.e. gate() must only consume the dedupe slot for messages that make it all the way to being applied/handled, not ones rejected by a later gate step or by `handle_event`'s own schema/unknown-name checks.

#### ConsentVerifier port 宣告為安全管線的一環卻從未接線，inbound envelope 的 consentGrantId 完全不驗證

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-capability-consent-055`
- **維度**：`capability-consent`
- **Severity**：`low`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-session/src/ports.rs:42`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

ports.rs 定義了 `ConsentVerifier`（「`consentGrantId` 是否有效（1.0 只對帶 grant 的 command）。AI／adapter 不能授予 consent」）與 fail-closed 的 `DenyAllConsent`，但 rg 全庫顯示這兩個型別除了定義與自身單元測試之外**沒有任何使用者**：CharacterSession 的 gate()／dispatch() 從頭到尾沒有讀過 `envelope.consent_grant_id`。契約 §1 把「consent／scope」列為 host 職責，docs/aip/README.md §8 也把任何帶 consentGrantId 的訊息歸為 require-reconfirmation，但 session 管線對這個欄位既不驗證也不拒絕，只是原樣收下。同理 `interaction_aip::offline_policy()`（offline.rs:17）在 Rust／TS／Swift 三邊都只有 fixture 測試在呼叫，沒有任何真正的離線佇列會查它（目前實際行為是「送不到就丟」，character_session.rs:1090-1096，結果剛好不違規，但那不是這張表在把關）。

**Evidence**

`rg -n "consent_grant_id|ConsentVerifier" crates/ --type rust` 的結果只有 crates/interaction-aip/src/envelope.rs（欄位定義與 check_id 驗長度）與 crates/interaction-session/src/ports.rs:42/72（trait 與 DenyAllConsent 定義），session.rs 一次都沒有出現。`rg -n "offlinePolicy\(|aipOfflinePolicy\("` 在 apps/ 只命中定義與 test/fixture 檔。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `low`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Verified every load-bearing part of the claim by reading source directly, not just grepping.

  1. crates/interaction-session/src/ports.rs:42 defines `pub trait ConsentVerifier { fn is_valid(&self, grant_id: &str, now: Timestamp) -> bool; }` with doc comment exactly as quoted ("`consentGrantId` 是否有效…AI／adapter 不能授予 consent"), and `DenyAllConsent` (line 70/72) as the fail-closed default impl.

  2. `rg -n "consent_grant_id|ConsentVerifier|DenyAllConsent" --type rust` across crates/ returns only: the field definition in interaction-aip/src/envelope.rs (struct field + constructor + canonical-JSON key list), the trait/impl definitions in ports.rs, and `DenyAllConsent.is_valid(...)` inside ports.rs's own unit test (line 216) — `is_valid(` has zero other call sites anywhere in the workspace.

  3. Read `CharacterSession::gate()` (session.rs:697-786) in full: it is the documented "十三關固定順序的安全管線" (13 numbered steps, matching the CHANGELOG's own description), and none of the 13 steps reference `envelope.consent_grant_id` or any ConsentVerifier. Read `dispatch()` (session.rs:800+) too — same result, zero consent handling.

  4. `CharacterSession` (session.rs:168) holds no port dependencies at all — no `ConsentVerifier`/`IdentityVerifier` field, `SessionConfig`/`new()` take none — so the port structurally cannot be wired into gate/dispatch as the type is currently built. `IdentityVerifier`'s underlying check (`bind_identity`) is at least inlined directly into gate() step 2; ConsentVerifier's underlying check has no such inlined equivalent anywhere.

  5. Checked `crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs` (the actual "Runtime 接線"/Session Host per docs/aip/character-session.md) — zero occurrences of "consent" in the whole 1217-line file, and zero references to ConsentVerifier/DenyAllConsent anywhere in interaction-runtime.

  6. Checked whether this could be by-design (consent validation living elsewhere per architecture-boundaries.md/character-session.md, which say "Consent／single-use grant" owner is "Consent Service（policy.rs／executor.rs）" and "Session 只帶 consentGrantId 參照"): confirmed there IS a real, fully-wired legacy consent system (`interaction_core::ConsentScope`, `Session::has_consent`, used throughout interaction-runtime/executor.rs, runtime.rs, human.rs, sensors.rs, mobile.rs) — but that system is entirely separate from AIP's `consentGrantId` field, and nothing in the codebase ever constructs an outbound `command` Envelope with a non-None `consent_grant_id`, nor does anything validate one on receipt. So the AIP-layer consent story (both stamping and verifying) is completely unimplemented, not merely delegated to a wired sibling component — the "reference" the docs describe is never actually produced or checked by anyone.

  7. The finding's secondary point about `offline_policy()`/`aipOfflinePolicy` is also verified: defined identically in Rust/TS/Swift, but every non-test call site is only in conformance-fixture test files (crates/interaction-aip/tests/conformance.rs, apps/interaction-ios/…Tests, apps/interaction-desktop/src/test/*.test.ts) — no production reconnect/offline-queue code calls it. The finding correctly caveats that this doesn't itself constitute a violation (character_session.rs:1090-1096 just drops undeliverable messages, which happens to be compliant by accident), only that it corroborates the same "declared-but-unwired safety surface" pattern.

  8. No existing known-limitation entry in CHANGELOG.md or docs/acceptance-evidence.md documents this gap for v0.6.0 (v0.6.0 is still "開發中"/未經 closing audit), so this is new, actionable information rather than a duplicate of an already-tracked limitation.

  Severity "low" is well-calibrated: because `command` (the only AIP message type that can legitimately carry `consentGrantId`) is host→device only and the host never currently constructs one with a real grant, there is no live exploitable path today where an external actor's message bypasses consent — this is an unwired/dead safety port (a Foundation-phase gap), not an active hole letting attacker-supplied envelopes through. That matches the finding's own framing and its "low" self-rating.

  </details>

**Fix**

要嘛把 ConsentVerifier 真的接進 gate（帶 consentGrantId 的 inbound 訊息在 1.0 一律 `rejected{scope-denied}`，因為成員本來就不該帶），要嘛把它與 offline_policy 的角色在文件裡降級為「純分類函式／未接線」，不要讓契約讀起來像是 host 會驗。

**Regression test**

In crates/interaction-session, construct a CharacterSession, submit an Envelope carrying a non-empty `consent_grant_id` for a name class that AIP's offline table marks `require-reconfirmation` and assert it is rejected/ignored unless a wired ConsentVerifier says the grant is valid — this test would fail to compile/pass today because there is no verifier hook to inject and gate() never inspects the field.

#### Rust 與 TS 的 §2.1 assets 驗證雙向分岔：mediaType 必填性與 asset id 字元集不一致

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-character-package-020`
- **維度**：`character-package`
- **Severity**：`low`
- **file:line**：`apps/interaction-desktop/src/companion/gatewayWiring.ts:242`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

CPP §2.1 明訂「Rust 與 TS 都要做」同一套匯入／載入驗證，golden schema（由 Rust 型別產生）把 `assets[].mediaType` 列為 required，但 TS 鏡射把它當選填；反向地，TS 用 `ASSET_ID_RE`（只允許小寫）限制 asset id，Rust 核心 `validate_manifest` 對 asset id 完全沒有字元集規則。結果：(a) 少了 `mediaType` 的 manifest 在匯入對話框驗證為合法、按鈕可按，送到 Tauri 才被 serde 以 schema 錯誤打回；(b) asset id 含大寫（例如 `"Sheet"`）能通過 Rust 匯入並實際寫成檔案，但之後 `character_list_imported` 夾帶的 manifest 被 TS 驗證擋下，角色被列為 valid 卻永遠退回文字角色。

**Evidence**

golden：schemas/character-protocol.schema.json 的 `AssetDecl.required = ["id","path","mediaType"]`；Rust：manifest.rs:84-93 `pub media_type: String`（無 `#[serde(default)]`）＋ :1004-1010 `if !is_media_type(&asset.media_type)`。TS：manifest.ts:571 `if (a.mediaType !== undefined) { ... }` —— 缺欄位直接跳過。反向：manifest.ts:76 `const ASSET_ID_RE = /^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9._-]{0,63}$/;` 與 :557 `if (typeof a.id !== "string" || !ASSET_ID_RE.test(a.id))`；Rust manifest.rs:990-997 只檢查 `asset.id.is_empty() || char_len(&asset.id) > MAX_ID_CHARS || !asset_ids.insert(&asset.id)`，沒有字元集檢查（host 端 character_store.rs:151-159 `is_safe_segment` 允許大寫）。附帶後果：gatewayWiring.ts:241 `if (typeof asset.mediaType === "string" && !asset.mediaType.startsWith("image/")) return null;` 在 mediaType 缺席時整條影像型別檢查被略過。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `low`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  逐一沿程式碼路徑重現，兩個方向都證實存在：

  (a) mediaType 必填性分岔
  - golden schema schemas/character-protocol.schema.json 的 AssetDecl.required = ["id","path","mediaType"]（實測輸出確認）。
  - Rust crates/interaction-character/src/manifest.rs:84-93 AssetDecl.media_type: String 無 #[serde(default)]；parse_manifest（同檔 1251-1275）用 serde_json::from_slice 反序列化，缺 mediaType 欄位會在此直接失敗（ManifestErrorCode::Json，classify_message 對應 "data does not match the manifest schema"），連 validate_manifest 都進不去。
  - TS apps/interaction-desktop/src/character/manifest.ts:571 `if (a.mediaType !== undefined) { ... }`——缺欄位時整段驗證直接跳過，asset.mediaType 留空但不算錯。
  - UI 端 apps/interaction-desktop/src/pages/character/CharacterLibrary.tsx：canSubmit = ... validation?.ok === true ...，validation 來自 validateImportedManifestText → validateCharacterManifest（同一份寬鬆邏輯），畫面上 decl.mediaType ?? "類型未標示" 印證 UI 本身知道 mediaType 可能缺席。故「少了 mediaType 的 manifest 匯入按鈕可按、送到 Tauri 才被 serde 打回」路徑成立。

  (b) asset id 字元集分岔
  - TS ASSET_ID_RE（manifest.ts:76）只允許 ^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9._-]{0,63}$，manifest.ts:557 用它擋大寫。
  - Rust 核心 validate_manifest（manifest.rs:990-1010，含 :1010 is_media_type 檢查行）對 asset id 只查空字串／長度／去重，無字元集規則。
  - Host 層 apps/interaction-desktop/src-tauri/src/character_store.rs：is_safe_segment（151-158）用 is_ascii_alphanumeric()，大寫可通過；validate_import（212-286）宣告端與輸入端都只呼叫 is_safe_segment，沒有額外擋大寫；write_tree（351-372）直接用 decl.id 當檔名寫入磁碟。故含大寫 id（如 "Sheet"）的第三方 manifest 可完整走完 Rust 匯入並寫成檔案。
  - 之後 character_list_imported（lib.rs:1808 → character_store::list → load_entry，452-500）對 valid=true 的角色一律把重新解析出的 manifest（Rust 驗證通過、含大寫 id）整包送回前端。
  - apps/interaction-desktop/src/companion/gatewayWiring.ts 的載入路徑（約 188-193）對 entry.manifest 再跑一次 validateCharacterManifest（TS 版，ASSET_ID_RE 擋大寫）→ 驗證失敗回傳 loadFailure（153-155）→ { kind: "text", characterId: "plain-text", failed: true }，即永遠退回純文字角色，即使 entry.valid === true。
  - 附帶後果：gatewayWiring.ts:242（finding 標 241，實際 242，屬行號漂移非本質差異）`if (typeof asset.mediaType === "string" && !asset.mediaType.startsWith("image/")) return null;`——mediaType 缺席時 typeof undefined === "string" 為假，整條影像型別檢查被略過，與 (a) 的推論一致。

  CPP §2.1 標題本身即載明「（匯入／載入時，Rust 與 TS 都要做）」（docs/character-protocol/README.md:80），故此雙向分岔確為對契約的違反，而非單純風格差異。行號僅 gatewayWiring.ts 有 1 行漂移（241→242），其餘全部精確命中目前檔案內容，不影響論證。

  </details>

**Fix**

TS `checkAssets` 把 `mediaType` 改為必填並套用與 Rust `is_media_type` 相同的正則；Rust `validate_manifest` 補上 asset id 的字元集規則（與 `ASSET_ID_RE` 一致）。再加一組跨語言 conformance fixture（缺 mediaType／大寫 id）確認兩邊給出相同的 accept/reject。

**Regression test**

對兩份第三方 manifest 各寫一條端對端測試：①assets[0] 缺 mediaType，斷言 TS `validateImportedManifestText` 回 ok:true 但 `character_store::import`（或 `parse_manifest`）回 Err；②assets[0].id 為 `"Sheet"`（含大寫），斷言 Rust `character_store::import` 成功寫檔且 `character_store::list` 回傳 `valid:true`，但把該 `entry.manifest` 餵給 TS `validateCharacterManifest` 回 `ok:false`，進而 `resolveCharacterSource`／`loadFailure` 回退為 `kind:"text"`。

#### character_import 在任何數量檢查之前就解碼全部 IPC 資產

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-character-package-021`
- **維度**：`character-package`
- **Severity**：`low`
- **file:line**：`apps/interaction-desktop/src-tauri/src/lib.rs:1785`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`character_import` 收到的 `assets: Vec<ImportAssetArg>` 沒有任何筆數上限：Tauri 命令先對**每一筆**呼叫 `decode_asset_base64`（單筆容許到 ~32 MB 解碼後位元組）並全部收進 `Vec`，之後才呼叫 `validate_import`，而 assets ≤ 64 筆與總量 ≤ 32 MB 的檢查都在 `validate_import` 裡。host 端「有界集合」這條不變量在這條路徑上是在解碼之後才生效，記憶體用量由呼叫端決定而不是由 host 決定。

**Evidence**

lib.rs:1785-1801：`let mut decoded = Vec::with_capacity(assets.len()); for asset in assets { let bytes = character_store::decode_asset_base64(&asset.base64)?; decoded.push(...); } character_store::import(&root, &manifest_text, &decoded)`。character_store.rs:180-195 `decode_asset_base64` 只限制**單筆** `max_encoded = (MAX_TOTAL_IMPORT_BYTES/3+1)*4`，不累計。上限檢查位置：character_store.rs:216（parse_manifest→assets ≤ 64）與 :305-311（`total > MAX_TOTAL_IMPORT_BYTES`），都在全部解碼之後。UI 路徑（CharacterLibrary.tsx:359-362 `declared.map(...)`）確實有界，所以這是 IPC 介面本身少了防線，而不是 UI 的問題。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `low`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Read the exact code paths cited. `character_import` (lib.rs:1785-1804) iterates the full caller-supplied `Vec<ImportAssetArg>` and calls `character_store::decode_asset_base64` (character_store.rs:184-197) on every element, appending each decoded buffer to `decoded: Vec<ImportAssetInput>`, before ever calling `character_store::import` → `validate_import`. `decode_asset_base64` only bounds a single base64 string against `(MAX_TOTAL_IMPORT_BYTES/3+1)*4` (~42.7MB encoded / ~32MB decoded); it has no running total and no count limit. The two bounds the claim says are missing — asset count ≤ 64 (enforced inside `parse_manifest` via `crates/interaction-character/src/manifest.rs:985`, `MAX_ASSETS = 64`, called from `character_store.rs:216`) and total bytes ≤ 32MB (`character_store.rs:308-311`, computed over the *input* assets) — are indeed only reached from inside `validate_import`, i.e. strictly after the lib.rs loop has finished decoding everything. The frontend (`desktop.ts:107-111`) passes `input.assets` through to `invoke` with no cap of its own; the only existing bound on count is that `CharacterLibrary.tsx` happens to build its payload from the already-validated `declared` (≤64) list — a UI convention, not a host-enforced guarantee on the `#[tauri::command]` itself, which any other caller of the same IPC entry point (devtools console, a future code path, or a compromised renderer, which the CSP `default-src 'self'` restricts to the app's own origin but does not eliminate) could bypass. This is a real ordering gap: unbounded-count, unbounded-running-total decode work happens before the host-side bounded-collection checks fire, matching the CLAUDE.md invariant on bounded sets being host-enforced. All cited line numbers (lib.rs:1785-1801, character_store.rs:180-195/216/305-311, CharacterLibrary.tsx:359-362) matched the current file contents exactly — no drift.

  </details>

**Fix**

在解碼迴圈之前先擋 `assets.len() > MAX_ASSETS`，並在迴圈內累加已解碼位元組、超過 `MAX_TOTAL_IMPORT_BYTES` 就立刻中止，讓有界性由 host 而非呼叫端決定。

**Regression test**

Call the (currently private-visibility-permitting) equivalent of `character_import`'s decode loop with e.g. 200 `ImportAssetArg` entries each carrying a ~1MB valid-base64 payload (all under the per-string cap but manifest declares far fewer assets); assert that decoding is rejected (or bounded) before more than `MAX_ASSETS`×`max_asset` bytes worth of base64 have been decoded, e.g. by asserting an early length/count check runs before the per-item decode loop rather than only inside `validate_import`.

---

#### e2e「鍵盤到得了五個主入口／Reduced Motion」的斷言幾乎不可能失敗，證據強度低於測試名稱的宣稱

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-general-mode-ux-027`
- **維度**：`general-mode-ux`
- **Severity**：`low`
- **file:line**：`apps/interaction-desktop/e2e/character-session.spec.ts:324`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

測試名稱宣稱「鍵盤到得了五個主入口」與「減少動態時卡片仍讀得到」，但五入口那一段只斷言五個文字可見（沒有任何 focus／鍵盤操作），Reduced Motion 那一段的文字比對用了 `/尚未連接 iPhone|iPhone|角色同步目前關閉/` 這種含空泛 `iPhone` 分支的 alternation 再加 `innerText.length > 0`——即使卡片在 reduced-motion 下錯誤地顯示「iPhone 已連接，角色狀態已同步」也照樣通過。這是 claimed-completed 被寫成 verified 的證據問題（同一檔 :328-338 對「重新檢查」按鈕的 Tab 斷言則是真的有驗）。

**Evidence**

e2e/character-session.spec.ts:323-326 `const nav = page.getByRole("navigation", { name: "主要導覽" }); for (const item of PAGES) { await expect(nav.getByText(item.label, { exact: true })).toBeVisible(); }`；:344-350 `await page.emulateMedia({ reducedMotion: "reduce" }); ... await expect(card.getByText(/尚未連接 iPhone|iPhone|角色同步目前關閉/).first()).toBeVisible(...); expect((await card.innerText()).trim().length).toBeGreaterThan(0);`。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `low`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Read the actual test file at apps/interaction-desktop/e2e/character-session.spec.ts:316-352 (test "角色同步：鍵盤到得了五個主入口與同步卡；減少動態時卡片仍讀得到"). Verified line-by-line against the claim:

  1. Five-entries section (lines 322-326): `const nav = page.getByRole("navigation", ...); for (const item of PAGES) { await expect(nav.getByText(item.label, { exact: true })).toBeVisible(); }` — this is purely a visibility assertion on nav.getByText for each of the 5 PAGES entries (confirmed PAGES has exactly 5 items in helpers.ts:56-62: 現在/小樞/工作/連接與權限/更多). No `page.keyboard.press` or focus check touches these five items anywhere in this block or elsewhere in the file — the only Tab-loop in this test (lines 328-338) targets the sync card's "重新檢查" button, an unrelated element. So the test name's claim that "keyboard can reach the five main entries" is not exercised by this section at all — a real claimed-vs-verified gap.

  2. Reduced-motion section (lines 344-350): ran the regex directly with `node -e`: `/尚未連接 iPhone|iPhone|角色同步目前關閉/.test("iPhone 已連接，角色狀態已同步")` → `true`. Because bare `iPhone` is one of the alternation branches, it subsumes the other two branches and matches any text containing the substring "iPhone" regardless of connection/sync state — including a hypothetical wrong string like "iPhone 已連接，角色狀態已同步" appearing under reduced motion. The trailing `expect((await card.innerText()).trim().length).toBeGreaterThan(0)` only checks non-emptiness. Neither assertion pins down which specific safety/status text reduced-motion is supposed to render, so a regression that shows the wrong (even contradictory) status text would still pass.

  Checked for mitigating coverage elsewhere: grepped e2e/*.ts for other keyboard tests of the five nav entries or stricter reduced-motion content checks on this card — a11y.spec.ts has unrelated Tab tests (skip-to-content, emergency-stop button), not this one. No other test closes this gap. The finding's cited evidence lines (323-326, 344-350) match current file content with only trivial line-number reflow (test currently starts at line 316, nav loop at 323-326, reduced-motion block at 344-350) — well within normal drift tolerance, not a stale reference. This is a genuine test-rigor/evidence-strength defect matching the project's honesty-ladder concern (test name implies verified keyboard-reachability and content-correctness that the assertions don't actually establish), correctly scoped as low severity since it's a test-quality issue, not a production runtime bug.

  </details>

**Fix**

五入口改成逐一 keyboard.press("Tab")／press("Enter") 並斷言 aria-current 與 topbar 標題變化；Reduced Motion 改成先由後端狀態決定期望的那一句固定文案再精確比對（去掉空泛的 `iPhone` 分支），或斷言與非 reduced-motion 模式取得的同一句文字相等。

**Regression test**

Force the sync card to render a wrong/contradictory string containing "iPhone" during reduced-motion (e.g. stub the character-session state so the card shows "iPhone 已連接" while the backend is actually disconnected) and confirm the existing assertion at spec.ts:347-349 still passes — proving the regex doesn't discriminate correct vs. incorrect status text; separately, add an actual `page.keyboard.press("Tab")` walk that asserts each of the 5 PAGES nav items receives focus in turn, and show today's version has no such loop.

#### threat-model.md 的 13 步安全管線行號整體漂移約 55 行，指向 diagnostics() 等無關程式碼

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-evidence-honesty-014`
- **維度**：`evidence-honesty`
- **Severity**：`low`
- **file:line**：`docs/aip/threat-model.md:38 (also its :48-58 step table, and docs/aip/renderer-adapter.md:44); actual gate() is now crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:697-798`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

威脅模型文件把 `gate()` 的逐步行號當成安全邊界的證據鏈引用（`session.rs::gate()`（`:642-737`），以及每一關的 `:648-651`…`:730-735`），但 HEAD commit `6683403` 在 `session.rs` 前段插入程式碼後，`gate()` 實際位於 `:697-798`。文件標注的區間現在落在 `diagnostics()` 的結尾，讀者按圖索驥核實安全關卡時會讀到錯的程式碼；`renderer-adapter.md:44` 引用的 `session.rs::gate`（:690-693）同樣落在 `diagnostics()` 的 struct 欄位上。

**Evidence**

`grep -n "fn gate" crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs` → `697:    fn gate(`；`sed -n '688,696p'` 顯示 690-693 是 `members: self.state.members.clone(), counters: …, event_log_len: …, event_log_cap: …`（`diagnostics()` 的回傳），不是 verified 的 scope 檢查；`sed -n '685,705p'` 顯示第 1 關 `envelope.validate()` 實際在 :704-706 而非文件寫的 :648-651。13 個步驟註解（`// 1.` … `// 13.`）實際落在 :703-791。相對地，同組文件裡不在該檔的行號引用仍正確（`outcome.rs:73` 的 `is_runtime_only`、`security_matrix.rs:216/259` 的兩個測試皆命中）。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `low`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Verified by direct inspection. `grep -n "fn gate"` shows `fn gate(` at session.rs:697, and its closing (`Ok(Gate::Proceed)` / `}`) is at line 798 — the 13 numbered step comments run from `:703` (`// 1.`) through `:791` (`// 13.`). The doc (threat-model.md:38) claims `gate()` is at `:642-737`. Reading session.rs:648-651 (the doc's cited location for step 1, `envelope.validate()`) actually falls inside the presence-timeout loop of a different function (`tick()`'s `AUDIT_PRESENCE` / "presence: offline" handling) — confirming the doc's own step-1 citation points to unrelated code. Likewise renderer-adapter.md:44 cites `session.rs::gate` at `:690-693`; those lines are verifiably the tail of `diagnostics()` (`event_log_len`, `event_log_cap`, and its closing braces — `pub fn diagnostics(&self)` starts at line 682), exactly as the finding describes, not the verified-scope check (the real step 8 is at :754-759). The drift is 697-642=55 lines, matching the finding's "about 55 lines" claim precisely. I also confirmed the audited commit (6683403, the run's stated HEAD) is byte-identical to current HEAD (8c5ea58) for both docs and session.rs (`git diff 6683403 8c5ea58 -- <files>` is empty) — this is not a case of the file having since been fixed; the drift is live in the exact commit under audit. This is a real, reproducible documentation-accuracy defect: a reader following the threat model's path:line citations to verify the 13-step gate() pipeline lands on unrelated diagnostics()/tick() code instead. Severity stays low as labeled — it is a stale-citation issue in a markdown doc, not a functional or security-enforcing code defect; the gate() pipeline itself is intact and correctly ordered (verified separately by reading its live source), only the line-number pointers in two markdown files are stale.

  </details>

**Fix**

把 threat-model.md §3 與 renderer-adapter.md §「決定 verified」的 `session.rs` 行號重新對齊到 HEAD（gate 697-798），或改為只引用函式名／步驟註解字串以免再度漂移；並在 release-verify 或既有的 docs-claims 測試裡加一條「文件引用的 `session.rs::gate` 行號區間必須包含 `fn gate`」的檢查。

**Regression test**

Not applicable to code (doc-only defect); mitigation would be a CI check that greps the current `fn gate(`/`fn diagnostics(` line numbers in session.rs and fails if they no longer fall within the ranges hardcoded in docs/aip/threat-model.md and docs/aip/renderer-adapter.md.

#### DESKTOP-GUIDE 的同步狀態表與 narrow.spec 的允許清單都漏掉「角色同步紀錄曾損毀」這一態

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-evidence-honesty-015`
- **維度**：`evidence-honesty`
- **Severity**：`low`
- **file:line**：`docs/DESKTOP-GUIDE.md:230`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`CHARACTER_SYNC_PROJECTION` 有十種狀態（`general-mode-ux.md` §4 也自稱「十種狀態」），但使用者指南的表格只列了九種，漏掉 `store-reset`「角色同步紀錄曾損毀，已重新開始」——那正是最需要對使用者誠實的一態（持久化紀錄壞掉、裝置得重新對齊）。同樣的九項清單也被複製進 `narrow.spec.ts` 的斷言允許集合，因此若該狀態在測試期間出現，e2e 會以「不在允許清單」誤判為失敗，而不是驗證它。

**Evidence**

`apps/interaction-desktop/src/statusProjection.ts:807-861`：`synced／reconnecting／offline／partial-capability／syncing／unrecoverable／needs-reconfirmation／no-device／disabled／store-reset` 共 10 筆，`satisfies Record<CharacterSyncState, …>` 窮舉。`docs/DESKTOP-GUIDE.md:230-241` 的表格只有 9 列，且第 243-250 行的補充說明也未提及 store-reset。`apps/interaction-desktop/e2e/narrow.spec.ts` 新增的斷言 `expect([...9 句...]).toContain(headline.trim())` 同樣缺這一句。對照之下 `docs/aip/character-session.md` §11 的文案表**有**列出 storeNote 那一列，可見是抄寫時漏掉而非刻意省略。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `low`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Verified directly against source. `apps/interaction-desktop/src/statusProjection.ts:786-861` defines `CharacterSyncState` as a 10-member union and `CHARACTER_SYNC_PROJECTION` (a `satisfies Record<CharacterSyncState, …>` exhaustive map) with all 10 keys including `store-reset` → headline `"角色同步紀錄曾損毀，已重新開始"`. `docs/aip/general-mode-ux.md:24,38,91-93` self-describes this as "十種狀態" and correctly lists `store-reset` in its own table. `docs/DESKTOP-GUIDE.md:230-241` (the user guide table) has only 9 rows and omits `store-reset` entirely; the surrounding explanatory bullets (242-253) never mention it either, even though it's arguably the state most requiring user honesty (persisted sync record was corrupted and reset). The same 9-item list is duplicated as the allowlist in `apps/interaction-desktop/e2e/narrow.spec.ts:104-113` (confirmed by reading the array), whose own comment says "九句人話之一" (one of nine) — so if `store-reset` ever renders during that shared-daemon e2e run, `expect([...9 items]).toContain(headline.trim())` would fail the test as an unrecognized string rather than validate the state, i.e. a false-negative test failure instead of coverage. Cross-checked `docs/aip/character-session.md:172-175`, which does carry the `storeNote`/store-reset row, supporting the "copy-paste omission, not a deliberate design decision" reading. All cited line numbers match current file contents exactly — no drift, not already fixed.

  </details>

**Fix**

在 DESKTOP-GUIDE 的表格補上「角色同步紀錄曾損毀，已重新開始」列（並說明它為何不是綠色也不是紅色），同時把該句加進 `narrow.spec.ts` 的允許清單；或直接讓測試從 `CHARACTER_SYNC_PROJECTION` 匯出 headline 集合，避免第三份手抄清單。

**Regression test**

Add the missing 10th row/detail to the DESKTOP-GUIDE table and add `"角色同步紀錄曾損毀，已重新開始"` to the narrow.spec.ts allowlist; a unit/lint check asserting `Object.keys(CHARACTER_SYNC_PROJECTION).length` matches the count of headlines parsed out of DESKTOP-GUIDE.md's sync table would catch future drift.

#### AIP §10 宣稱 EvidenceClass「用於 diagnostics」，但它在整個 repo 沒有任何生產端或消費端

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-evidence-honesty-016`
- **維度**：`evidence-honesty`
- **Severity**：`low`
- **file:line**：`docs/aip/README.md:234`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

AIP 1.0 §10 把 `EvidenceClass`（unit｜contract｜fixture｜simulator｜…｜real-device）描述成「用於 diagnostics、文件與 acceptance evidence」，並以此支撐「fixture／simulator 永遠不得標成 real-device」這條不變量。實際上這個列舉只存在於型別與 schema／codegen 產物中：Runtime、API、CLI、桌面 TS、iOS App 沒有任何一處產生或讀取它，`GET /v1/character-session/diagnostics` 的回傳裡也沒有任何證據等級欄位。因此這條不變量目前完全靠人工文件紀律維持，文件用語卻讀起來像已經有機制在執行。

**Evidence**

`crates/interaction-aip/src/evidence.rs:8-40` 定義 `EvidenceClass` 與 `ALL`；`schemas/aip-1.0.schema.json:235,498` 與 `apps/interaction-ios/.../AIPGenerated.swift:303`、`apps/interaction-desktop/src/aip/generated.ts:137,149` 都只是鏡射型別。`grep -rniE "evidenceclass|evidence_class" crates/interaction-runtime/src crates/interaction-api/src crates/interaction-cli/src` → 0 命中。`crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs:334-359` 的 `character_session_diagnostics_value()` 回傳只有 `sessionId／sessionEpoch／revision／sequence／members／counters／eventLog／storeNote`，沒有任何 evidence 欄位。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `low`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Verified every claim in the finding directly against the current repo state:

  1. docs/aip/README.md:234 reads exactly as quoted: "`EvidenceClass` ∈ `unit | contract | fixture | simulator | integration | browser | real-agent | real-device | real-hardware | unverified`。用於 diagnostics、文件與 acceptance evidence；**fixture／simulator 永遠不得標成 real-device**。"

  2. crates/interaction-aip/src/evidence.rs:8-40 defines the `EvidenceClass` enum, its `ALL` const, and an `is_real()` helper — confirmed present exactly as cited.

  3. `grep -rniE "evidenceclass|evidence_class" crates/interaction-runtime/src crates/interaction-api/src crates/interaction-cli/src` → 0 hits (exit code 1), confirming no runtime/API/CLI code references the type by name.

  4. A repo-wide grep (excluding target/node_modules) for the same pattern turns up matches only in: evidence.rs itself, crates/interaction-aip/src/schema.rs (schema-generation registration, lines 18/47), apps/interaction-desktop/src/aip/generated.ts:137/149 (generated TS mirror), and apps/interaction-ios/.../AIPGenerated.swift:303 (generated Swift mirror) — i.e., exactly the type/schema/codegen artifacts the finding describes, nothing else.

  5. `crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs:334-359` — read the actual body of `character_session_diagnostics_value()`. It returns exactly `{sessionId, sessionEpoch, revision, sequence, members[], counters, eventLog:{len,cap}, storeNote}` — no evidence-class field anywhere, confirmed by direct inspection.

  6. Confirmed the `GET /v1/character-session/diagnostics` route (crates/interaction-api/src/routes.rs:1856, wired in lib.rs:128-129) just forwards that same value — no separate evidence-class production path exists in the API layer either.

  7. As an extra check, `VerificationEvidence` in runtime/executor.rs is a distinct, unrelated interaction-core type (verification receipts), not AIP's `EvidenceClass` — it doesn't rebut the claim; the grep pattern correctly excludes it.

  8. docs/acceptance-evidence.md has zero mentions of EvidenceClass, so even the "文件" (documentation) half of the claimed usage isn't fulfilled outside the AIP spec's own §10 text.

  Every piece of cited evidence matches current file content and line numbers with no drift. The finding's core claim — EvidenceClass is a documented/typed enum with no producer or consumer anywhere in Runtime/API/CLI/desktop-TS/iOS runtime code, so the fixture-vs-real-device invariant it's meant to support is enforced purely by human documentation discipline rather than any mechanism — is accurate and reproducible. This is a real (if low-severity, since the invariant itself is separately enforced via other means like ProviderTested/pairingUnverified per the same README line) documentation-vs-reality gap: the wording "用於 diagnostics" reads as if a diagnostics mechanism already consumes it, when no such mechanism exists.

  </details>

**Fix**

要嘛把 §10 的措辭改成「1.0 只定義詞彙，尚未接進 diagnostics（implemented-unverified）」，要嘛真的把 `EvidenceClass` 接上——例如在 diagnostics 的 `members[]` 帶上該成員的來源等級（fixture 由 `fake_iphone` 自報不算數，需由 host 依 transport／pairing 事實決定），並補一則測試斷言 fixture 來源不得被標成 `real-device`。

**Regression test**

Add an integration test that calls the character-session diagnostics endpoint/value and asserts an `evidenceClass` (or equivalent) field is present and populated for at least one known-fixture and one known-real-device scenario; today the test would fail since the returned JSON has no such field at all, proving the enum is unconsumed.

#### presence 逾時的成員在同一個 tick 內就被 leave 掉，§11 的「iPhone 暫時離線」在逾時路徑上看不到

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-reconnect-recovery-046`
- **維度**：`reconnect-recovery`
- **Severity**：`low`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs:592`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`character_session_tick_at` 先呼叫 `session.tick(now)`（把 `now - last_seen_at >= presence_timeout` 的成員標成 Offline），接著用**同一個 `now`、同一個門檻**挑出 stale 成員並 `leave`。判定條件完全相同，所以任何「因逾時而 offline」的成員在同一次 tick 就被移出成員名單，presence=offline 這個狀態一個 tick 都活不過。只有 transport 主動偵測到斷線那條路（`character_session_presence(Offline)` 會把 `last_seen_at` 重設成 now）才會讓 offline 顯示滿 45 秒；而 mobile 的 idle timeout 與 presence timeout 都是 45 000 ms，兩條路誰先觸發是競態。結果是契約 §11「遠端成員 offline → 『iPhone 暫時離線』」在殭屍連線／host 先逾時的情況下被直接跳過，UI 從「已連接」跳到「沒有裝置」。

**Evidence**

character_session.rs:590 `outputs.extend(session.tick(now));` 之後 :592-601 `let timeout = ...presence_timeout_ms; let stale = members.filter(|m| m.presence == Presence::Offline && now.signed_duration_since(m.last_seen_at) >= timeout)` → :602-607 `session.leave(&party, now)`；crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs:637-644 `tick` 用完全相同的 `now.signed_duration_since(entry.member.last_seen_at) >= timeout` 設 Offline。常數：session/types.rs:76 `presence_timeout_ms: 45_000`；mobile.rs:59 `IDLE_TIMEOUT_DEFAULT_MS: u64 = 45_000`。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `low`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Verified by direct code reading, no repro script needed given the logic is fully synchronous and deterministic.

  1. crates/interaction-runtime/src/character_session.rs:565-608 (`character_session_tick_at`): inside a single `{ let mut session = host.session(); ... }` block that holds a `std::sync::MutexGuard` for the whole scope (session() at line 235-239 returns `MutexGuard`, blocking, no await point inside the block), the code does:
     - line 590: `outputs.extend(session.tick(now));`
     - line 592: `let timeout = ChronoDuration::milliseconds(session.config().presence_timeout_ms);`
     - lines 593-601: `session.members()` (queried AFTER tick) filtered by `member.presence == Presence::Offline && now.signed_duration_since(member.last_seen_at) >= timeout`
     - lines 602-606: for each match, `session.leave(&party, now)` — which does `self.members.remove(index)` immediately (interaction-session/src/session.rs:386-407).

  2. crates/interaction-session/src/session.rs `tick()` (starts ~line 624): the loop that flips a member to Offline uses the exact same threshold and reference point: `entry.member.presence != Presence::Offline && now.signed_duration_since(entry.member.last_seen_at) >= timeout` → sets `entry.member.presence = Presence::Offline`. Critically, it does **not** touch `last_seen_at`.

  3. Because `last_seen_at` is untouched by the tick-triggered Offline transition, and character_session_tick_at's stale filter reuses the identical `now` and identical `timeout` against the identical `last_seen_at`, any member whose presence just flipped to Offline via this path mathematically also satisfies the stale filter in the same synchronous call, and gets `leave()`-d before the lock is ever released — so no external reader (HTTP diagnostics, SSE/state broadcast) can ever observe that member as `presence: offline` in the roster; it goes straight from online/reconnecting to entirely absent (`no-device` per the desktop's `projectCharacterSession` in apps/interaction-desktop/src/statusProjection.ts:1024-1052, which falls through to `no-device` once `remote.length === 0`).

  4. This is a genuinely different code path from the one the existing tests cover. Explicit transport-detected disconnect (crates/interaction-runtime/src/mobile.rs:3746-3750, "斷線＝presence offline（成員保留，等 tick 逾時才清）") calls `character_session_presence(Offline)`, whose underlying `session.presence()` (interaction-session/src/session.rs:429-435) *does* reset `last_seen_at = now`. That reset is exactly why the tested/documented path (crates/interaction-runtime/tests/character_session_loop.rs:1013-1030, `reconnecting_fixture_iphone_resumes_with_patches`) genuinely shows a visible `offline` grace window before the later tick removes the member. No existing test exercises the *other* path — a member that goes silent without an explicit disconnect event ever being fired (e.g. a network drop that never surfaces a close/read-error, only detected by the host's own inactivity timeout in `tick()`). For that silent/zombie case, the "iPhone 暫時離線" text from §11 (docs/aip/character-session.md:167) is provably skippable in the exact way the finding describes.

  5. Config check: `interaction-session/src/types.rs` defines a single `presence_timeout_ms` (default 45_000, types.rs:76) used for both "mark offline" and "how long offline before leave" — there is no separate grace-period constant, so the two decisions collapse onto the same instant for this specific transition path. This matches the finding's evidence exactly.

  Severity: keeping it at low is reasonable — the end state (member removed, "沒有裝置") is still an honest reflection that the member is gone (no false "online"/no fabricated verified-state), it just skips an intermediate UI wording for one specific silent-disconnect sub-case; it's a UX/spec-conformance gap, not a safety or truth-ladder violation.

  </details>

**Fix**

stale 清除用比 presence timeout 更長的寬限期（例如 `presence_timeout_ms * 2` 或獨立的 `member_eviction_ms`），讓「暫時離線」至少存在一個可觀察的區間，再進入 leave；或改成記錄 `offline_since` 並以它計算清除門檻。

**Regression test**

Join a member, advance time only past `presence_timeout_ms` without ever calling `character_session_presence`/closing a transport (i.e. simulate a silent/zombie connection whose `last_seen_at` is simply stale), call `character_session_tick_at(now)` once, and assert the member briefly existed with `presence: offline` in an intermediate observation — then show it currently jumps straight to absent from `character_session_diagnostics_value()`/`members` with zero observable offline window, unlike `reconnecting_fixture_iphone_resumes_with_patches` which does observe the grace window via the explicit-disconnect path.

#### 活連線被新連線取代（superseded）的分支仍然沒有任何測試——v0.6.0 恢復矩陣第 11 條列為必補，本輪仍為 0 命中

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-reconnect-recovery-047`
- **維度**：`reconnect-recovery`
- **Severity**：`low`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-runtime/src/character.rs:1445`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

CPP 端 `character_ws_attach` 在同一 adapter 的舊連線仍在時送 `Goodbye{reason:"superseded"}` 並 cancel 舊連線；mobile 端同 device_id 新連線取代時走 `closed_by_server = Some("superseded")`（靜默關閉、不誤報撤銷、不把 provider 轉 Disconnected、不停高風險受器）。這兩條分支在 crates/*/tests、apps/interaction-desktop/e2e、iOS 測試裡都沒有任何斷言，`docs/releases/v0.6.0-recovery-matrix.md` §2 第 11 條已把它列為「受保護但無測試，v0.6.0 應優先補上」，本輪重新搜尋仍是 0 命中。這代表「舊連線收到 Goodbye」「superseded 不被誤判成斷線／撤銷」這些會影響誠實顯示與感測停用的行為，任何重構都不會被既有套件攔下。

**Evidence**

`grep -rn 'superseded' --include='*.rs' --include='*.ts' --include='*.tsx' --include='*.swift' .`（排除 target）只命中生產程式碼：character.rs:1446 `reason: Some("superseded".into())`、mobile.rs:3264 `closed_by_server = Some("superseded")`、mobile.rs:3782 `Some("superseded") => {}`、runtime.rs:1179、curator.rs:246（知識版本，無關）；測試側唯一命中是 apps/interaction-ios/InteractionCompanionTests/AIPFixtures.swift:54 的 cancel payload fixture（`{"reason":"superseded"}`，是 CPP cancel 的理由字串，不是連線取代測試）。crates/interaction-runtime/tests/ 內 `superseded` 只出現在 knowledge_loop.rs（知識版本語意）。docs/releases/v0.6.0-recovery-matrix.md:110 明列此缺口。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `low`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Verified directly against source and tests, independent of the recovery-matrix doc's own claim.

  1. crates/interaction-runtime/src/character.rs:1443-1448 — `character_ws_attach` removes any existing connection for the instance and, when one was found (`if let Some(prev) = previous`, line 1444, anchor at 1445), sends `WireMessage::Goodbye { reason: Some("superseded".into()) }` and cancels its close token. This is the live-supersede branch under test.

  2. `grep -rn 'superseded' --include='*.rs' --include='*.ts' --include='*.tsx' --include='*.swift' .` (excluding target/) reproduces exactly the hit list in the finding's evidence: production-only hits at character.rs:1446, mobile.rs:3264 (`closed_by_server = Some("superseded")`), mobile.rs:3782 (`Some("superseded") => {}`), runtime.rs:1179, curator.rs:246 (unrelated knowledge-version state), plus the unrelated knowledge.rs state-machine hits. The only non-knowledge test-side hit is apps/interaction-ios/InteractionCompanionTests/AIPFixtures.swift:54, which is a CPP *cancel* payload fixture (`{"reason":"superseded"}`), not a connection-supersede test — confirmed by reading it, it's just a fixture literal, not an assertion about attach/replace behavior.

  3. Checked every test in crates/interaction-runtime/tests/character_loop.rs that calls `character_ws_attach` more than once for state relevant to this claim:
     - The reattach at line 1559 happens only *after* the prior session was already disconnected by `character_sweep_at` (heartbeat timeout, line ~1538) and `character_ws_closed` was explicitly called (line ~1549) — so `hub.connections` no longer holds an entry for that instance_id by the time attach runs; the `Some(prev)` branch is not exercised, matching the doc's own note that this test covers reconnect-after-timeout, not supersede-of-a-live-connection.
     - The second `character_ws_attach` at line 2016 uses a *different* adapter (`adapter2`/`instance2`), not the same adapter_id as `session`, so it cannot hit the same-instance supersede path either.
     No test constructs two concurrently-live connections to the same adapter_id/instance_id to assert the Goodbye-with-superseded-reason send or the close-cancel side effect.

  4. Checked crates/interaction-runtime/tests/mobile_loop.rs: `token_reconnect_works_until_revoked` explicitly `drop(ws)`s the old connection and sleeps 300ms before reconnecting — the old connection is already gone (not a live-supersede race), so it cannot exercise the `still_paired => closed_by_server = Some("superseded")` branch at mobile.rs:3264. No test name or body found that connects two live sockets for the same device_id concurrently to hit that branch.

  5. docs/releases/v0.6.0-recovery-matrix.md §2.4 item 11 (around line 110) independently documents this exact gap ("無測試：需在 v0.6.0 補"), citing the same rg search and the same distinction between the timeout-reconnect test and a true live-supersede test — corroborating rather than being the sole source of the claim, since I verified the test bodies myself rather than trusting the doc's assertion.

  The claim's technical description (Goodbye+cancel on the CPP side, silent closed_by_server on the mobile side, zero test hits for the live-supersede branch specifically) is accurate and reproducible. Severity "low" is reasonable — it's a test-coverage gap on a currently-correct, already-documented-as-known behavior, not a live functional defect.

  </details>

**Fix**

在 character_loop.rs 補一個「同一 adapter_id 在舊連線仍活著時再 attach」的測試：斷言舊連線先收到 `Goodbye{reason:"superseded"}`、其 pending intent 轉 uncertain、新連線第一則 outbound 是 Hello；在 mobile_loop.rs 補一個「同 device_id 第二條連線」的測試：斷言舊連線被關但 provider 不轉 Disconnected、高風險受器不被當成斷線停用、character session 的 presence 不被誤標 Offline。

**Regression test**

In character_loop.rs, attach adapter_id once to get session A (do NOT let it time out or close), then call character_ws_attach(&adapter_id) again while A is still live to get session B; assert A.rx receives WireMessage::Goodbye{reason: Some("superseded")}, assert A.close.is_cancelled(), and assert B.rx receives Hello — proving live supersede (not post-timeout reconnect) is exercised.
