#### `cargo metadata` 失敗時遞移邊界檢查靜默通過（綠燈≠已驗證）

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-runtime-boundaries-064`
- **維度**：`runtime-boundaries`
- **Severity**：`low`
- **file:line**：`tests/e2e/tests/dependency_boundaries.rs:102`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

遞移依賴檢查在 `cargo metadata --locked` 無法執行或非零離開時只 `eprintln!("SKIPPED …")` 然後 `return`，測試仍算 pass。`cargo test` 預設吃掉通過測試的 stderr，所以 CI／發布驗證會看到「dependency_boundaries 2 passed」而完全不知道邊界檢查沒有跑——這正是誠實階梯要禁止的「未驗證卻宣稱通過」。

**Evidence**

line 95 `.args(["metadata", "--format-version", "1", "--locked"])`；line 99-106 `Err(e) => { eprintln!("SKIPPED pure_crates_do_not_pull_tokio_transitively: cannot run `cargo metadata` ({e}); …"); return; }`；line 108-115 對 `!output.status.success()` 同樣 `eprintln!` + `return`。兩條路徑都沒有 `panic!`／`assert!`。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `low`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Read tests/e2e/tests/dependency_boundaries.rs directly: lines 94-115 exactly match the finding's evidence. `pure_crates_do_not_pull_tokio_transitively` calls `cargo metadata --format-version 1 --locked` (line 95) and on either a spawn `Err(e)` (lines 99-106) or non-zero exit status (lines 108-115) does `eprintln!(\"SKIPPED …\")` then a bare `return` — no `panic!`/`assert!`/`Result::Err` on either path, so the test function returns normally and the Rust test harness records it as passed. Ran `cargo test -p interaction-e2e --test dependency_boundaries -- --nocapture` locally: both tests pass, and `cargo metadata --locked` currently succeeds here (no SKIPPED output), confirming the fallback is a real but currently-dormant branch rather than something already neutralized. Since Rust's default test harness captures stdout/stderr for non-failing tests, a plain `cargo test` in CI would show `... ok` with the SKIPPED diagnostic invisible, exactly as claimed. This is not covered by the sibling direct-dependency test, which only parses Cargo.toml and never exercises the transitive walk. Severity `low` is appropriate — it's a CI-robustness/honesty-signal gap in test tooling, not a runtime safety violation, and it does not currently misfire since cargo metadata succeeds in normal conditions; it's a latent risk that CI could report false confidence if `cargo metadata --locked` ever fails (e.g. stale Cargo.lock, offline runner, cargo missing from PATH).

  </details>

**Fix**

把兩條 skip 路徑改成 `panic!`（或至少在 CI 用環境變數 `STRICT_DEP_BOUNDARY=1` 強制 fail），讓「無法驗證」與「驗證通過」在退出碼上可區分。

**Regression test**

In a CI-boundary test, spawn a `cargo metadata --locked` in a directory with a deliberately corrupted/out-of-sync Cargo.lock (or PATH stripped of `cargo`) and assert that `pure_crates_do_not_pull_tokio_transitively` fails/panics instead of silently returning Ok — or, simpler, replace the two skip branches with `panic!`/`assert!(false, ...)` so an inability to run `cargo metadata` fails the test loudly rather than passing.

#### 架構守門測試的 entrypoint 分岔偵測只涵蓋 `===`／`case`，且例外規則會遮蔽真正的分岔

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-runtime-boundaries-067`
- **維度**：`runtime-boundaries`
- **Severity**：`low`
- **file:line**：`apps/interaction-desktop/src/test/architecture-no-entrypoint-switch.test.ts:35`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

`literalBranches()` 只用兩條 regex 抓 `=== "id"`／`"id" ===`／`case "id":`。`entrypoint.startsWith("shu")`、`includes("shu-rig")`、以 id 當 key 的物件查表、以及鬆散 `== "shu-rig"` 全都逃過。更嚴重的是 `stripSourceDiscriminants()` 會把**任何** `\w+.kind === "<小寫-減號>"` 整段抹掉，`entrypoint.kind === "shu-rig"` 這種真正的 entrypoint 分岔也會被當成 CharacterSource 判別標籤而放行。守門測試綠燈不代表 host 真的沒有角色分岔。

**Evidence**

line 35 `const comparison = new RegExp("[!=]==\\s*[\"'`]${id}[\"'`]|[\"'`]${id}[\"'`]\\s*[!=]==", "g");`（`[!=]==` 需要三個字元，`== "shu-rig"` 不匹配）；line 36 只有 `case\s+"id"\s*:`；line 56 `return source.replace(/\w+\.kind\s*[!=]==\s*["'`][a-z-]+["'`]/g, "SOURCE_KIND_CHECK");` — `[a-z-]+` 同時匹配 `shu-rig`／`sprite`／`text`／`shape`。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `low`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Read apps/interaction-desktop/src/test/architecture-no-entrypoint-switch.test.ts in full; line numbers (35, 36, 56) and quoted regex source match the finding exactly, no drift. Extracted the three functions (literalBranches, stripSourceDiscriminants) verbatim and ran them in Node against contrived inputs to empirically test each claim rather than reason about the regex abstractly:\n\n1. `entrypoint == "shu-rig"` (loose equality) -> literalBranches returns [] (escapes). Confirmed: `[!=]==` needs 3 contiguous chars (class + 2 literal '='), so a 2-char `==` never matches.\n2. `entrypoint.startsWith(\"shu\")` -> [] (escapes; not `===`/case shape at all).\n3. `entrypoint.includes(\"shu-rig\")` -> [] (escapes, same reason).\n4. `entrypoint.kind === \"shu-rig\"` -> literalBranches alone finds it (`=== \"shu-rig\"`), but the test pipeline applies stripSourceDiscriminants first, which erases it to `SOURCE_KIND_CHECK` before literalBranches ever sees it, producing zero hits. Verified stripSourceDiscriminants's regex `\\w+\\.kind\\s*[!=]==\\s*[\"'`][a-z-]+[\"'`]` matches ANY `<ident>.kind === \"<lowercase-id>\"`, not only `source.kind` as the adjacent comment claims it's scoped to justify.\n\nAlso checked: no ESLint config (no eslint.config.*, .eslintrc*, no eqeqeq rule) exists anywhere in the repo, so nothing structurally forbids a future loose-`==` entrypoint comparison from silently passing this guard.\n\nGrepped `.kind` usage across src/character and src/companion (80+ hits) to check whether any CURRENTLY-existing entrypoint dispatch is being masked today: found none — today's real `.kind` comparisons (source.kind, entrypoint.kind==='builtin'/'process', pack kind checks, transient kind checks) are legitimate source/type discriminators, not adapter-id literal branches, so the finding's own framing (a coverage-gap in what the test *guarantees*, not a claim of a live undetected violation today) holds and is not overstated. This is a genuine, reproducible imprecision in the guard test's exception carve-out and detection regex, correctly scoped as low severity (test-quality gap, not a production security hole).

  </details>

**Fix**

改成先移除註解與字串以外再對每個 id 做「出現即違規（白名單化允許檔）」的檢查，而不是列舉比較運算子；`stripSourceDiscriminants` 收緊成只允許實際的 source kind 值（`index|legacy-pack|imported|text`）。

**Regression test**

Add cases to literalBranches()/stripSourceDiscriminants() unit-level tests (or extend the existing describe block) asserting that `entrypoint.kind === "shu-rig"`, `entrypoint == "shu-rig"`, and `entrypoint.startsWith("shu")` each still produce a detected offender — they currently all produce zero hits after the strip/branch pipeline.

#### `providers.rs` 引入 `COMPANION_CAPABILITY_PREFIX` 後，同檔四處仍寫死 `"companion."` 字面值

- **Finding ID**：`F-6683403-20260904T161327Z-runtime-boundaries-068`
- **維度**：`runtime-boundaries`
- **Severity**：`low`
- **file:line**：`crates/interaction-runtime/src/providers.rs:460`
- **Verdict**：已確認（`confirmed`）

**Claim**

v0.6.0 重構在 line 236 新增 `COMPANION_CAPABILITY_PREFIX` 作為「呈現面前綴的唯一產生點」並用於 `companion_capability_declaration()`，但同一檔案裡切分 builtin provider 與 companion provider 能力清單的四個 filter 仍直接寫 `"companion."`。前綴一旦調整，宣告表會跟 provider 能力歸屬瞬間不一致（能力被同時算進 builtin 又不被視為呈現面），而沒有任何測試會擋。

**Evidence**

line 236 `pub const COMPANION_CAPABILITY_PREFIX: &str = "companion.";`；line 241 `.with_presentation_surface(CapabilitySelector::prefix(COMPANION_CAPABILITY_PREFIX))`；line 460 / 468 `.filter(|id| !id.starts_with("companion."))`、line 506 / 514 `.filter(|id| id.starts_with("companion."))` 全為字面值。

**Verifier 視角與 verdict**

- **可重現性（`reproducibility`）** — ✔ confirmed，severity `low`

  <details><summary>推理全文</summary>

  Verified directly in crates/interaction-runtime/src/providers.rs. Line 236 defines `pub const COMPANION_CAPABILITY_PREFIX: &str = "companion.";` and it is used at line 241 inside `companion_capability_declaration()` via `CapabilitySelector::prefix(COMPANION_CAPABILITY_PREFIX)`. However, the same file's builtin/companion provider construction (in the provider-bootstrap logic, ~lines 440-520) still filters receptor/actuator manifest ids with four literal `"companion."` string checks: lines 460 and 468 (`!id.starts_with("companion.")`, used for the builtin provider's receptors/actuators) and lines 506 and 514 (`id.starts_with("companion.")`, used for the companion/presentation provider's receptors/actuators). None of these four sites reference `COMPANION_CAPABILITY_PREFIX`. `grep -rn "COMPANION_CAPABILITY_PREFIX"` across the crate shows only two use sites (definition + the one use in `companion_capability_declaration`), confirming the four filters are independent literals. I found no test in the file's `mod tests` (starting line 1241) or elsewhere that asserts consistency between the prefix constant and these four literal filters — a change to the constant would silently desync capability partitioning from the presentation-surface declaration used elsewhere (e.g. AIP capability selector logic), exactly as the finding describes. This is a real, low-severity maintainability/consistency defect (magic-string duplication that could cause data to silently misclassify if the prefix ever changes), not a style nit that's already covered.

  </details>

**Fix**

四處改用 `COMPANION_CAPABILITY_PREFIX`。

**Regression test**

Add a unit test in providers.rs that asserts, for a set of synthetic receptor/actuator ids, the ids partitioned into the builtin provider (lines ~460/468) and the companion provider (lines ~506/514) exactly match `CapabilitySelector::prefix(COMPANION_CAPABILITY_PREFIX)`'s match set — or better, refactor all four filters to call a shared `is_companion_capability(id: &str) -> bool` helper built on `COMPANION_CAPABILITY_PREFIX`, then have the test change the constant's value and confirm all five call sites (declaration + 4 filters) move together.
